Experiments on nuScenes across state-of-the-art online map construction models show that TwinIR reduces mAP by 8.18-8.96 percentage points under RSA and 2.84-5.62 points under ETA, while increasing the unreachable-goal rate by 25-28 points and the unsafe-planned-trajectory rate by 19-20 points over clean inputs. These attacks are also validated on a real-world testbed AV, where TwinIR successfully induces both road straightening and early-turn deformations while remaining inconspicuous in full-color views.

</details>


### [34] [Beyond Global Routing Aggregation: Phase-Aware Expert Merging for MoE Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04454)
*Hongyu Zhang,Cheng Yan,Xiang Xia,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RoleMerge，一种基于相位条件专家角色（phase-conditioned expert roles）的训练免费专家合并方法，用于MoE-VLM模型。通过构建专家路由角色画像（RRP）和基于专家-相位信息损失的合并策略，在保留回答解码专家差异的同时合并兼容专家，在三个模型和多个基准上实现了最高9.6%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-VLM模型部署需要存储全部专家参数，训练免费专家合并可降低负担。现有路由聚合方法对所有token的路由统计进行全局聚合来判断合并兼容性，但MoE-VLM推理具有相位结构（图像上下文、问题、答案三种token类型），不同相位的token数量与路由分布差异巨大。全局聚合会过多强调图像上下文处理，掩盖了各相位下专家角色的差异，导致服务于不同相位的专家被错误视为可互换，降低模型性能。

Method: RoleMerge包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建每个专家的路由角色画像（RRP），基于相位归一化的路由统计捕捉专家的相对相位偏好；(2) 以专家-相位信息损失为指导标准，合并具有兼容RRP画像的专家及其对应路由器条目；(3) 保留回答解码阶段专家的区分度，确保输出生成质量不受影响。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在三个MoE-VLM模型和多个基准上的实验表明，在相同专家保留比例下，RoleMerge相比其他专家合并方法保留了更多完整模型性能，在六任务宏平均性能上相对提升最高达9.6%。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了相位条件专家角色比全局路由聚合更适合作为MoE-VLM专家合并的依据，为高效部署MoE-VLM提供了更有效的模型压缩方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts vision-language models (MoE-VLMs) increase model capacity with sparse expert activation, yet deployment requires storing the full expert pool. Training-free expert merging reduces this burden, and many routing-based methods aggregate routing statistics across all tokens to determine merge compatibility. However, MoE-VLM inference is phase-structured: image-context tokens carry visual content, question tokens specify the query, and answer tokens produce the output, with different counts and routing distributions. Because image-context tokens are far more numerous, global aggregation can overemphasize image-context processing and obscure phase-conditioned expert roles, making experts serving different phases appear interchangeable and degrading model performance. We therefore argue that MoE-VLM expert merging should preserve phase-conditioned expert roles, judging compatibility by how experts serve different phases rather than globally aggregated routing statistics. Based on this view, we propose RoleMerge, a training-free method that constructs each expert's Routing Role Profile (RRP) from phase-normalized routing statistics, capturing its relative phase preference. Guided by expert-phase information loss, RoleMerge merges experts with compatible profiles and their corresponding router entries while preserving answer-decoding expert distinctions. Experiments on three models and multiple benchmarks show that RoleMerge preserves more of the full model's performance than alternative expert-merging methods at matched expert-retention ratios, with relative improvements of up to 9.6 percent in six-task macro-average performance. These results validate phase-conditioned expert roles as a more effective basis than global routing aggregation for MoE-VLM expert merging.

</details>


### [35] [Overcoming Statistical Bias in Action-Controllable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04653)
*Yuhong Shi,Zhenhao Chu,Jie Wei,Jun Hao,Jianyi Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（反事实一致性框架），通过多步反事实一致性和动作-空间反事实一致性两种约束，减少动作条件世界模型对统计捷径的依赖，增强动作可控性。在Mini-SSMB、VP2等基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型旨在预测环境在智能体动作下的演化，但未来帧往往可由视觉惯性和重复运动模式高度预测，导致模型可以通过利用统计偏差拟合数据，而无需让可见动态真正依赖动作。这种捷径导致不同动作产生相似未来、零动作下运动仍持续等问题。核心问题是减少动作条件预测中对统计捷径的依赖。

Method: 提出CoCo反事实一致性框架，包含两个互补约束：(1) 多步反事实一致性：约束参考轨迹、逆动作轨迹和零动作轨迹之间的一致性；(2) 动作-空间反事实一致性：在镜像场景和变换动作下强制执行一致的预测。此外引入Action Response Consistency (ARC)和Drift Energy (DE)两个评估指标来度量动作可控性，以及Mini-SSMB用于同状态多动作反事实评估。

Result: 在Mini-SSMB上，完整模型ARC_inv达到0.412、ARC_ref达到0.483，相对于基线DE降低17.07%；在VP2视觉规划任务上达到73.1%的平均成功率，为SOTA最佳；在BAIR和RoboNet上的实验表明这些增益能保持视频预测质量并跨模型设置迁移。

Conclusion: 动作控制不仅需要注入动作特征，更需要通过反事实改变动作和观察来强制执行一致性约束。CoCo框架通过减少对统计捷径的依赖，有效提升了动作条件世界模型的动作可控性，同时不牺牲视频预测质量，并在多项基准上取得SOTA性能。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models aim to predict how visual environments evolve under an agent's actions. Yet future frames are often highly predictable from visual inertia and recurring motion patterns alone. This creates a shortcut: models can fit the data by exploiting statistical biases without making their visible dynamics meaningfully depend on the action. As a result, different actions may produce similar futures, while motion may persist even under zero action. The key question is how to reduce reliance on statistical shortcuts from dominating action-conditioned prediction. We argue that action control requires more than injecting action features; it requires enforcing consistency under counterfactual changes to actions and observations. Based on this insight, we introduce CoCo, a Counterfactual Consistency framework to enhance action controllability through two complementary constraints. Multi-step counterfactual consistency constrains reference, inverse-action, and zero-action rollouts, while action-spatial counterfactual consistency enforces consistent predictions under mirrored scenes and transformed actions. Together, they reduce reliance on statistical shortcuts from substituting for action-dependent dynamics. We further introduce Action Response Consistency (ARC) and Drift Energy (DE) to assess action controllability, together with Mini-SSMB for same-state, multi-action counterfactual evaluation. On Mini-SSMB, our full model achieved ARC_inv of 0.412 and ARC_ref of 0.483, while reducing DE by 17.07% relative to the baseline. On VP2 visual planning, it achieves the highest average success rate among SOTA models, at 73.1%. Experiments on BAIR and RoboNet further show that these gains preserve video prediction quality and transfer across model settings.

</details>


### [36] [EndoVLM: An Endoscopy Vision-Language Pre-training Model via Anatomy-Guided Sparsity and Progressive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04472)
*Zhenyu Yi,Jianwei Xu,Yue Hu,Zhongwei Qiu,Sijing Li,Liang Huang,Bin Lv,Ling Zhang,Yingda Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EndoVLM是一种针对内窥镜图像分析的大规模视觉-语言基础模型，预训练于超过34.8万对内窥镜检查和临床报告，通过解剖引导的稀疏池化、渐进式语义感知对齐和语义集中的掩码自编码器三个创新模块，弥合了结构化解剖描述与高冗余视觉帧之间的模态鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内窥镜基础模型主要依赖单模态图像或视频的自监督学习，忽略了临床报告中蕴含的丰富语义知识。同时，结构化解剖描述与高冗余、未筛选的视觉流之间存在根本性的模态鸿沟，难以充分利用临床记录。

Method: 包含三个关键组件：1) Anatomy-Guided Sparse Pooling——利用文本描述作为查询驱动稀疏注意力，高效地将语义显著的帧聚合为解剖特定的视觉表示；2) Progressive Semantic-Aware Alignment——通过结构化软目标建模临床分类体系（解剖和病理状态），弥合从全局患者级匹配到细粒度局部对齐的差距；3) Semantic-Concentrated Masked Autoencoder——仅应用于语义丰富的帧，整合低层视觉精度与高层语义表示。

Result: 在多种下游任务的广泛实验中，EndoVLM优于现有基础模型，且与任务专用方法具有竞争力。此外，EndoVLM展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: EndoVLM通过将临床报告语义引入基础模型预训练，有效解决了内窥镜图像分析中的模态鸿沟问题，展示了在更广泛临床应用中作为基础模型的潜力。

Abstract: The development of foundation models (FMs) is crucial for advancing endoscopic image analysis. However, existing endoscopy FMs mainly rely on self-supervised learning from uni-modal images or videos, overlooking the rich semantic knowledge contained in clinical reports. Furthermore, effectively leveraging these records is hindered by a fundamental modality gap: structured anatomical descriptions are not naturally mapped to specific frames within the high-redundancy, uncurated visual streams. In this paper, we present EndoVLM, a novel vision-language FM pre-trained on over 348K endoscopic examinations, each pairing a clinical report with its corresponding image collection. An Anatomy-Guided Sparse Pooling mechanism utilizes textual descriptions as queries to drive sparse attention, efficiently aggregating semantically salient frames into anatomy-specific visual representations across redundant image-sets. Next, a Progressive Semantic-Aware Alignment strategy models clinical taxonomy (anatomy and pathological status) via structured soft targets, bridging the gap from global patient-level matching to fine-grained localized alignment. Finally, a Semantic-Concentrated Masked Autoencoder is applied exclusively to these semantic-rich frames, integrating low-level visual precision with robust high-level semantic representation. Extensive experiments across various downstream tasks demonstrate that EndoVLM outperforms existing foundation models and remains competitive with task-specific methods. Remarkably, EndoVLM also exhibits robust zero-shot generalization capabilities, highlighting its potential for broader clinical application.

</details>


### [37] [Differential 6-DOF Pose Estimation with Provable First-Order Immunity to Camera Calibration Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04673)
*Yueqiang Zhang,Liang Deng,Yi Zhang,Baoqiong Wang,Wenjun Chen,Shuixin Pan,Yulan Guo,Qifeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种微分位姿估计方法，通过差分透视投影方程直接从帧间图像位移和已知3D控制点恢复平台6-DOF运动，避免了传统3D-2D方法中独立绝对位姿估计对外参标定误差的敏感性。理论证明了平移外参误差完全抵消，旋转误差仅产生有界扰动，并给出了可观测性条件、Cramer-Rao下界和偏差消除估计器。实验表明该方法在微运动估计中优于PnP和广义PnP方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D-2D方法在每一时刻独立估计绝对相机位姿，再通过相机到平台的外参恢复平台运动，这种方法对外参标定误差高度敏感，尤其在微运动场景下误差会被显著放大。作者希望直接差分帧间图像位移来估计平台运动，从而绕开独立绝对位姿估计这一步骤，提高微运动估计的鲁棒性和精度。

Method: 该方法通过差分手写透视投影方程，利用深度不变近似，在SE(3)流形上对运动进行建模。主要技术要素包括：(1) 差分投影方程避免独立绝对位姿估计；(2) 证明平移外参误差在差分中完全抵消，旋转外参误差只引入有界扰动；(3) 推导通用可观测性条件、Cramer-Rao下界以及偏差消除的一致估计器；(4) 支持单目和多相机系统。该方法的理论基础完整，包括近似有效域的刻画。

Result: 在五个控制点和0.5像素图像噪声条件下，单目求解器获得俯仰-偏航组合旋转RMSE为10.09角秒，平移RMSE为3.70毫米，运行时间0.34毫秒；双目求解器旋转RMSE为10.58角秒，平移RMSE为3.91毫米，运行时间0.27毫秒。在精度、标定鲁棒性和计算效率方面均优于代表性PnP和广义PnP方法，建立了6-DOF平台微运动估计的新sota。

Conclusion: 微分位姿估计方法通过直接差分帧间图像位移，从根本上避免了对绝对位姿估计的依赖和对外参标定的敏感性，理论上保证了平移外参误差的精确抵消和旋转误差的有界性。该方法在精度、鲁棒性和效率上都达到了新sota，适用于机器人操作、自主系统和结构位移监测等微运动场景，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Accurate six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) motion estimation is essential for robotic manipulation, autonomous systems, and structural displacement monitoring. Conventional 3D-2D methods estimate absolute camera poses independently at each time and recover platform motion through camera-to-platform extrinsics, making them sensitive to extrinsic calibration errors, especially for micromotion. We present a differential pose estimation method that directly recovers platform motion from inter-frame image displacements and known 3D control points. By differencing perspective projection equations, using a depth-invariance approximation, and modeling motion on SE(3), the method avoids independent absolute-pose estimation and supports both monocular and multi-camera systems. We prove that translational extrinsic errors cancel exactly, while rotational errors induce a bounded perturbation determined by calibration error, motion magnitude, and observation geometry. We also derive generic observability conditions, a Cramer-Rao lower bound, and a bias-eliminated consistent estimator, and characterize the validity limits of the approximations. Extensive synthetic and real-world experiments establish a new state of the art for 6-DOF platform micromotion estimation, outperforming representative PnP and generalized-PnP methods in accuracy, calibration robustness, and computational efficiency. With five control points and 0.5-pixel image noise, the monocular solver obtains a combined pitch-yaw rotation RMSE of 10.09 arcsec, a translation RMSE of 3.70 mm, and a runtime of 0.34 ms. The binocular solver achieves a rotation RMSE of 10.58 arcsec, a translation RMSE of 3.91 mm, and a runtime of 0.27 ms. Code will be released upon publication at https://github.com/zyoungszu/pami2026.

</details>


### [38] [REZE: Recognition-Based Zero-Shot Extraction for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04480)
*Boyang Li,Chenhui Gou,Jianfei Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出REZE（Recognition-based Zero-shot Extraction），一种无需训练的零样本视频时间定位方法。它将视频分割成短片段，让视觉语言模型（VLM）对每个片段输出查询相关的置信度分数，再用确定性算法把分数曲线转换成任务所需的输出。该方法在QVHighlights基准上将无训练时刻检索mAP从38.23提升至40.32，高亮检测达到44.18 mAP和73.41 HIT@1，其HIT@1甚至超过所有全监督SOTA方法。REZE在七种骨干网络上验证，并在所有可用对比中优于直接时间戳生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本视频时间定位方法通常直接让视觉语言模型（VLM）生成起止时间戳，导致结果高度依赖模型设计和训练，不同VLM之间的定位精度差异悬殊。作者旨在提出一种模型无关、无需训练的通用方法，消除VLM之间的性能差异，同时适应多种下游任务（单/多区间时刻检索、高亮检测）。

Method: REZE将视频分割为短片段，对每个片段询问模型的查询相关置信度分数，生成一条时间置信度曲线，随后使用确定性算法将曲线转换为任务要求的输出格式。时间聚合在模型外部完成，因此无需训练即可适配不同任务输出类型。方法具有即插即用特性，可在多种VLM骨干网络上部署。

Result: 在QVHighlights上，REZE将无训练时刻检索mAP从38.23提升至40.32；高亮检测达到44.18 mAP和73.41 HIT@1，创下无训练方法的新SOTA。其HIT@1超越了QVHighlights测试集上所有全监督SOTA方法。在三个模型家族的七种骨干网络上评估，於Charades-STA和QVHighlights上均优于直接时间戳生成方法。此外，使用REZE使旧代模型可接近同系列新一代模型的原生性能。

Conclusion: REZE通过将时序聚合从模型内部移到外部，以简单、训练免费的方式显著提升零样本视频时间定位性能，不仅跨任务类型具备通用性，还能弥合不同代际VLM之间的性能差距，为零样本VTG提供了一种轻量且高效的范式。

Abstract: Video temporal grounding (VTG) refers to the task of identifying the time interval in a video that corresponds to a given natural-language query. A common zero-shot strategy asks a large vision-language model (VLM) to generate the start and end timestamps directly, so the result depends heavily on the design and training of the model, and grounding accuracy differs widely from one VLM to another. We therefore propose REcognition-based Zero-shot Extraction (REZE), a simple training-free method that splits the video into short clips, asks the model for a clip-level confidence score for the query, and uses a deterministic algorithm to convert the resulting score curve into the output required by the task. Because temporal aggregation is performed outside the model, REZE adapts to different task outputs, from single- and multi-interval moment retrieval to highlight detection. On QVHighlights, REZE improves the best reported training-free moment-retrieval mAP from 38.23 to 40.32, while on highlight detection it reaches 44.18 mAP and 73.41 HIT@1, establishing a new state of the art among training-free methods. Its HIT@1 also outperforms all fully supervised SoTAs on the QVHighlights test split. We evaluate REZE on seven backbones from three model families. On Charades-STA and QVHighlights, it outperforms direct timestamp generation in every available comparison. We further observe that with REZE an earlier-generation model can approach the native performance of a newer model in its family.

</details>


### [39] [A Multi-Sensor Dataset for Monitoring the Operational Environment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04704)
*Claudio Diotallevi,Rodrigo Gudiño,Zaharia Pachalieva,Philipp Neumaier,Patrick Naumann,Erik Bochinski,Volker Eiselein,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了德国铁路数字化项目（DSD）下，DB InfraGO AG与understandAI GmbH联合开发的多传感器铁路环境感知数据集，包含超过700万个高质量标注，覆盖铁路专用和通用感知对象，可在多种运营场景下支持自动化轨道交通（GoA2至GoA4）中基于AI的环境监控系统研发与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化轨道交通系统（从GoA2部分自动化到GoA4全自动运行）的安全高效运营依赖于可靠的环境监控，而基于AI的感知系统需要大量精确标注的数据进行训练和验证。当前铁路领域缺乏符合实际运营需求的综合多传感器标注数据集。

Method: 在Digitale Schiene Deutschland（DSD）计划框架下，DB InfraGO AG与understandAI GmbH合作开发了一套面向铁路环境感知需求的多传感器数据集。数据集涵盖多种运营场景，包含铁路专用对象和通用感知对象的标注，共超过700万个高质量标注。

Result: 构建完成了包含超过700万个高质量标注的综合多传感器数据集，覆盖铁路专用和通用感知对象，并在多种运营场景下采集。该数据集现已正式开放，可通过DB InfraGO AG申请获取。

Conclusion: 该数据集为铁路领域基于AI的环境监控研究提供了宝贵的公共资源，有望推动自动化轨道交通感知系统的研发与部署。

Abstract: Reliable environment monitoring is essential for the safe and efficient operation of automated railway systems, covering all Grades of Automation (GoA), from partially automated (GoA2) to fully automated operation (GoA4). Artificial Intelligence (AI) plays a central role in enabling these systems to detect, classify, and react to potential hazards in real time. The development of such AI-based perception systems requires large volumes of accurately annotated data for training and validation.
  Within the Digitale Schiene Deutschland (DSD) program, DB InfraGO AG and understandAI GmbH have developed a comprehensive multi- sensor dataset tailored to the needs of railway environment perception. This dataset contains over 7 million high-quality annotations of both railway-specific and general perception objects, captured under varying operational scenarios. The finalized dataset can now be requested at the DB InfraGO AG and serve as a valuable resource for advancing AI-driven environment monitoring in the railway domain.

</details>


### [40] [Not All Redundant Tokens Are Alike: Analyzing Visual Token Pruning through Token Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04483)
*Hyeonyu Kim,Sehwan Lim,Youngwon Choi,Taeyoun Kwon,Jaejin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过EmbedLens标识的token角色来分析视觉语言模型（VLM）中的视觉token剪枝方法，发现不同剪枝方法具有不同的token角色偏好，但这些偏好与下游性能无直接相关性。通过改进token角色分配并评估角色保护的剪枝变体，发现保留non-alive token有时能维持甚至提升性能，表明语义对齐弱的token在剪枝下仍可能影响模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉token剪枝方法被广泛用于缓解VLM推理时的计算瓶颈，但这些剪枝决策与视觉token的功能角色（如alive/non-alive）之间的关系尚不清楚，缺乏从token角色角度对剪枝方法进行系统性分析的工作。

Method: 本文利用EmbedLens定义的token角色对代表性剪枝方法进行分析；首先检测各剪枝方法的token角色偏好及其与性能的关联；然后对token角色分配流程进行细化，设计并评估角色保护的剪枝变体（role-protected pruning variants），以验证不同角色token在剪枝过程中的重要性。

Result: 实验结果揭示了不同剪枝方法在token角色上存在明显偏好，但这种偏好与下游性能没有直接相关性；进一步发现保留non-alive token在某些情况下可以维持甚至提升性能，说明即使语义直接对齐较弱的token在剪枝场景下仍可能对模型行为产生重要影响。

Conclusion: 并非所有冗余token都是相同的——token的角色差异对剪枝效果有潜在影响，需要更精细地理解视觉token的功能角色来指导VLM的token剪枝策略设计。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) process an image as a sequence of visual tokens, which creates a substantial computational bottleneck during inference. Recent visual token pruning methods address this issue by removing seemingly redundant tokens, yet it remains unclear how these pruning decisions relate to the functional roles of visual tokens. In this work, we analyze visual token pruning through the lens of token roles identified by EmbedLens. We first show that representative pruning methods exhibit distinct token-role biases, but these biases do not directly correlate with downstream performance. To better understand this behavior, we refine the token-role assignment procedure and evaluate role-protected pruning variants. Our results show that preserving non-alive tokens can sometimes maintain or improve performance, suggesting that tokens with weak direct semantic alignment may still affect model behavior under pruning. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jaykim9870/Not_All_Redundant_Tokens_Are_Alike.

</details>


### [41] [DIVE: Dynamic Iterative Visual Evidence Construction for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04496)
*Chen Zhong,Xiao An,Zijie Wang,Jiepan Li,Guangyi Yang,Wei He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DIVE提出一种无需训练的动态迭代视觉证据构建框架，通过"选择-更新-再评估"的循环过程替代单次评分剪枝，以残差条件化分数逐步选择互补且与提示相关的视觉token，在八项图像理解基准上以88.9%的token压缩率保持了98.2%的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）中，视觉输入被编码为比文本更长的token序列，成为推理效率的瓶颈。现有方法采用单次评分并剪枝低分token的方式，但单次评分不足，因为token对提示的有用性取决于已保留的证据，即token的重要性是条件依赖的。

Method: DIVE是一个无需训练的训练后框架，将视觉token剪枝重新定义为动态证据构建。它迭代地执行三步：(1) 选择剩余token中残差条件化分数最高的token；(2) 更新视觉与提示残差，以折扣已解释的证据；(3) 重新评估剩余token。该选择-更新-再评估过程构建出一个互补且提示相关的保留token集合。

Result: 在八个图像理解基准上，DIVE在不同token预算下均一致地保持性能。当视觉token减少88.9%时，DIVE仍保留未压缩模型平均性能的98.2%。

Conclusion: DIVE证明了动态迭代式选择方法优于传统的单次贪心评分剪枝，通过考虑token间的互补性能够更高效地压缩视觉token而不显著损失模型性能，为VLM高效推理提供了一种有效且免训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual inputs in vision-language models (VLMs) are often encoded into substantially longer token sequences than text, making visual tokens a major bottleneck for efficient inference. Abundant recent methods address this bottleneck by scoring token importance and pruning low-scoring tokens in a single pass. However, one-shot scoring is insufficient because a token's prompt-relevant usefulness depends on the evidence already retained. Motivated by this insight, we introduce DIVE (Dynamic Iterative Visual Evidence Construction), a training-free framework that recasts visual-token pruning as dynamic evidence construction. DIVE repeatedly selects the remaining token with the highest residual-conditioned score, updates the visual and prompt residuals to discount the evidence already explained, and re-evaluates the remaining tokens. This select-update-re-evaluate process builds a retained set of complementary, prompt-relevant evidence. Experiments across eight image-understanding benchmarks show that DIVE consistently preserves performance across token budgets. With an 88.9% reduction in visual tokens, DIVE retains 98.2% of the uncompressed model's average performance. Code is available at https://github.com/Zhong-Chenchen/DIVE.git.

</details>


### [42] [Privacy-Preserving Action Recognition: Taxonomy, Methods, and Privacy-Utility Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04501)
*Sareer Ul Amin,Muhammad Ayaz,Muhammad Munsif,Sanghyun Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过PRISMA指南对2018-2026年间32篇经同行评审的隐私保护动作识别（PPAR）论文进行系统综述，提出二维隐私空间分类法、正式威胁模型、比较权衡分析以及统一的评估协议（PPAR Unified Evaluation Protocol），指出现有文献存在碎片化、威胁模型不兼容、评估标准缺失等核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频监控在公共安全、医疗和智能环境中已使连续人体监控成为常态，对个人身份和外观构成真实隐私风险。然而现有关于隐私保护动作识别的综述过于狭窄：多数仅归类单一机制家族，未能涵盖最新对抗性和混合方法，且几乎不涉及评估问题，导致文献碎片化、威胁模型不兼容、指标不一致且缺乏统一评估标准。

Method: 采用PRISMA指南，从885篇筛选记录中选出32篇同行评审论文（2018-2026）进行系统综述；将方法分为五个家族——对抗学习（52%）、基于骨架（20%）、密码学（12%）、差分隐私（8%）和混合方法（8%）；提出二维隐私空间分类法、正式威胁模型、比较权衡分析及PPAR统一评估协议。

Result: 方法家族具有不同的隐私/效用/效率权衡：骨架方法可达约85%准确率但丢弃外观信息；对抗方法在中等隐私（cMAP 0.9到0.3-0.5）下保持近80%效用；差分隐私经常低于70%。评估是薄弱环节：仅10%的论文采用正式隐私定义，65%依赖临时指标，40%报告不一致定义的cMAP。困难条件下的测试严重不足：不到15%的论文检查跨数据集泛化，低于10%测试自适应攻击者，实时边缘部署几乎未被触及。

Conclusion: 作者认为通过统一评估协议和基准标准化，PPAR可以从原型迈向实际部署，其经验教训可推广到人脸识别和医学影像等相邻领域。

Abstract: Video surveillance in public safety, healthcare, and smart environments has made continuous human monitoring routine, raising real risks to personal identity and appearance. Privacy-preserving action recognition (PPAR) tackles the tension between the utility of video understanding and this exposure, and has drawn fast-growing interest. However, existing surveys remain narrow. Most catalog a single mechanism family, predate recent adversarial and hybrid work, or barely address evaluation. The result is a fragmented literature with incompatible threat models, inconsistent metrics, and no shared evaluation standard. We address this with a PRISMA-guided review of 32 peer-reviewed papers (2018--2026) drawn from 885 screened records. Methods sort into five families, namely adversarial learning (52%), skeleton-based (20%), cryptographic (12%), differential privacy (8%), and hybrid (8%), each with distinct privacy, utility, and efficiency trade-offs. Evaluation is the weak point. Only 10% of papers adopt a formal privacy definition, 65% rely on ad-hoc metrics, and 40% report an inconsistently defined cMAP. The trade-offs are steep. Skeleton methods reach about 85% accuracy but drop appearance, adversarial methods hold near 80% utility at moderate privacy (cMAP 0.9 to 0.3--0.5), and differential privacy often falls below 70%. Harder conditions stay under-tested, with fewer than 15% of papers checking cross-dataset generalization, under 10% testing adaptive attackers, and real-time edge deployment nearly untouched. We contribute a two-dimensional privacy-space taxonomy, a formal threat model, a comparative trade-off analysis, the PPAR Unified Evaluation Protocol, and a roadmap centered on benchmark standardization. With this grounding, we argue PPAR can move from prototypes toward deployment, with lessons extending to face recognition and medical imaging.

</details>


### [43] [GeoReward: Mitigating Contextual Variable Overestimation in Vision-Language Models for Cross-Market Preference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04504)
*Shuo Liu,Huixiang Cai,Weiru Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文诊断了视觉语言模型（VLM）中的一种系统性偏差——上下文变量过度估计（CVE），即模型过度依赖主导性的视觉-文本线索而忽视稀疏但关键决策的上下文变量。为应对该问题，作者收集了跨国广告创意与点击率的多模态数据集，并提出了GeoReward奖励模型，集成市场感知检索增强、上下文引导视觉调制和选择性敏感性损失三种机制，可用于引导VLM的RL微调以生成市场感知的广告创意，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在多模态任务中表现优异，但在真实场景（如预测不同地理市场的广告偏好）中存在系统性失败模式：过度估计主导视觉-文本线索，低估稀疏但决策关键的上下文变量，导致模型忽略地域差异而输出一致结果。

Method: 首先构建包含真实广告创意及多国点击率表现的新多模态数据集；然后提出GeoReward奖励模型，集成三个机制：市场感知检索增强（Market-Aware Retrieval Augmentation）、上下文引导视觉调制（Context-Guided Visual Modulation）、选择性敏感性损失（Selective Sensitivity Loss）；最后利用GeoReward引导VLM的强化学习微调，使文本到图像模型生成市场感知的广告背景设计。

Result: 实验验证所提出的框架能够缓解CVE问题，并在预测广告图像偏好任务中优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅诊断了VLM对主导感知特征的系统性偏差，还为稀疏上下文变量主导决策的应用场景提供了针对性解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models excel in many multimodal tasks but remain prone to a subtle yet impactful failure mode: they tend to overestimate dominant visual-textual cues while underestimating sparse but decision-critical contextual variables. This issue, which we term Contextual Variable Overestimation (CVE), becomes particularly evident in real-world applications such as predicting advertisement image preferences across diverse geographic markets. For instance, when a VLM is asked to choose between two product images tailored for different countries, it often defaults to a consistent output, ignoring ground-truth regional variations. This collapse occurs because pervasive high-volume signals, such as product attributes and dense image patches, overwhelm the few but critical tokens that encode market-specific context. To address CVE, we first collect a new multimodal dataset of real advertising creatives and their click-through performance across multiple countries. We then introduce GeoReward, a reward model designed to predict ad image preferences across diverse geographic markets. GeoReward integrates three purpose-built mechanisms: (1) Market-Aware Retrieval Augmentation, (2) Context-Guided Visual Modulation, (3) Selective Sensitivity Loss. Furthermore, we demonstrate how GeoReward can guide the fine-tuning of RL for a VLM to generate background designs for text-to-image models, producing market-aware advertising creatives. Experiments validate that our framework mitigates CVE and outperforms existing baselines. This work not only diagnoses a systematic bias in VLMs toward dominant perceptual features but also delivers a targeted solution for applications where sparse contextual variables govern decision-making.

</details>


### [44] [CARVE: Cross-Slice Anisotropic Reallocation of Visual Evidence for Efficient 3D Medical Volume Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04515)
*Zhenyu Yi,Qiang Hu,Zhenhao Li,Jiaxuan Zhao,Yusong Sun,Lichi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CARVE框架，一种免训练的视觉令牌压缩方法，针对3D医学图像多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中切片式表示的令牌冗余问题，将令牌削减建模为预算约束的2.5D分配问题，沿深度轴分层分配令牌预算，在保留约20%令牌的情况下超越所有压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切片式3D MLLM将三维体数据表示为2D切片序列，产生数千个视觉令牌，相邻切片间存在大量重叠的视觉证据，加重了LLM骨干网络的负担。扩展分析发现增加令牌预算的收益递减：成本持续上升而准确率饱和，且提升面内分辨率比增加切片更有效。因此需要选择性分配预算而非简单扩大，但现有令牌压缩方法主要针对2D图像或视频，不适配于沿深度轴近乎重复内容导致的3D医学图像冗余。

Method: CARVE是一个免训练框架，在LLM推理前压缩视觉令牌，将令牌削减形式化为预算约束的2.5D分配问题。具体包括：沿深度轴将体积划分为连贯窗口；根据归一化的跨切片证据非均匀分配令牌；在代表性切片上构建空间锚点，从全体积检索局部变化的证据；在每个窗口内将剩余合格令牌合并到邻近锚点。整体移除约80%的视觉令牌。

Result: 在Hulu-Med-7B上移除约80%视觉令牌后，CARVE在AMOS-MM所有报告生成指标上优于所有压缩基线，全令牌质量保留率比最强基线高6.2个百分点；在三个VQA基准上保留98.1%的全令牌性能。

Conclusion: 通过将预算非均匀地分配到信息量更大的切片区域而非简单扩大令牌预算，CARVE在显著减少计算开销的同时有效保留3D医学MLLM的视觉理解能力，表明面向3D医学场景的令牌压缩应关注深度轴上的跨切片冗余特性。

Abstract: Slice-based MLLMs leverage mature 2D encoders by representing 3D volumes as sequences of 2D slices. However, this slice-wise formulation produces thousands of visual tokens that burden the LLM backbone, many of which capture overlapping visual evidence across adjacent slices. To understand how effectively a growing visual token budget improves performance, we perform scaling analyses on two 3D medical VQA benchmarks and find diminishing returns: cost keeps rising while accuracy saturates, and improving in-plane resolution is more effective than adding slices at comparable budgets. The budget should therefore be allocated more selectively rather than simply enlarged, yet most token compression methods are designed for 2D images or videos, where redundancy arises from spatial layout or temporal motion rather than from near-duplicate content along the depth axis. We present CARVE, a training-free framework that compresses visual tokens prior to LLM inference and casts token reduction as budget-constrained 2.5D allocation. CARVE partitions the depth axis into coherent windows and allocates tokens non-uniformly according to normalized cross-slice evidence. Under a shared budget, CARVE builds spatial anchors on representative slices and retrieves locally varying evidence from the full volume, then merges remaining eligible tokens into nearby anchors within each window. Removing roughly 80% of the visual tokens on Hulu-Med-7B, CARVE leads all compression baselines on every AMOS-MM report-generation metric, with 6.2 points higher retention of full-token quality than the strongest baseline, and preserves 98.1% of full-token performance across three VQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [45] [Coupled Continuous-Discrete Generation for Scene Text Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04525)
*Axi Niu,Knag Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Hao Jin,Jinqiu Sun,Yanning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualTSR是一个统一的场景文本图像超分辨率框架，将STISR建模为耦合的连续-离散生成过程：条件流匹配恢复图像潜变量，吸收态离散扩散重建文本令牌，二者共享多模态Transformer主干并在生成过程中交互，无需外部OCR先验。在CTR-TSR和RealCE数据集上取得最佳FID、LPIPS、ACC和NED，相比DiffTSR在X4下ACC提升12.78个百分点，参数量从1.23B降至203M，端到端延迟从13.3s降至132ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STISR系统通常依赖外部生成的先验或将图像与文本模型分离，导致错误传播和多阶段推理的高成本。作者旨在构建一个无需外部OCR先验的统一框架，同时兼顾超分辨率的视觉质量和文本语义保真度。

Method: DualTSR将STISR形式化为耦合的连续-离散生成任务。连续图像潜变量由条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）恢复，文本令牌通过吸收态离散扩散（absorbing-state discrete diffusion）重建。两个生成过程共享一个多模态Transformer主干，使图像和文本的演化状态在生成过程中持续交互，推理时无需外部OCR先验。

Result: 在CTR-TSR数据集上，DualTSR在X2和X4两种尺度下均取得最好的FID、LPIPS、ACC和NED；在对齐的RealCE子集上取得最佳FID、ACC和NED以及有竞争力的LPIPS。与DiffTSR相比，在X4尺度下ACC提升12.78个百分点，参数量从1.23B降至203M，端到端延迟从13.3s降至132ms。

Conclusion: DualTSR通过耦合的连续-离散统一生成框架，在场景文本图像超分辨率任务上同时实现了更高的准确性和效率，确立了其作为精准且高效的STISR方法的地位。

Abstract: Scene text image super-resolution (STISR) aims to recover visually plausible appearance while preserving character semantics from degraded inputs. Existing STISR systems often rely on externally generated priors or separate image and text models, resulting in error propagation and costly multi-stage inference. We present DualTSR, a unified framework that formulates STISR as coupled continuous-discrete generation. Conditional flow matching restores continuous image latents, while absorbing-state discrete diffusion reconstructs text tokens. Both processes share a multimodal transformer backbone, allowing the evolving image and text states to interact throughout generation without an external OCR prior at inference. On CTR-TSR, DualTSR achieves the best FID, LPIPS, ACC, and NED among the compared methods at both X2 and X4. On an aligned RealCE subset, it obtains the best FID, ACC, and NED with competitive LPIPS. Compared with DiffTSR at X4, DualTSR improves ACC by 12.78 percentage points while reducing the parameter count from 1.23B to 203M and end-to-end latency from 13.3s to 132ms. These results establish DualTSR as an accurate and efficient method for STISR.

</details>


### [46] [FocusMem: Factorizing Content, Readout, and Trust in Latent GUI Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04530)
*Zhuoran Zhang,Bowen Li,Jingcheng Ju,Yang Shi,Qixun Wang,Haotian Wang,Wei Chen,Tengjiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FocusMem，一种用于GUI智能体的紧凑潜在记忆接口，通过角色感知内容基分离情景记忆和工作记忆的责任，利用状态条件读出生成决策特定视图，并使用轻量信任门控抑制不相关记忆块。在五个GUI智能体基准上，FocusMem一致优于完全匹配的仅动作固定记忆基线和先前的潜在记忆改编方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在记忆方法通常将每条轨迹映射为一个固定记忆块，且主要通过下一步动作监督训练，存在三个实际问题：压缩过程中重要细节可能丢失；同一个记忆块需要服务于不同决策阶段；检索到的不相关轨迹仍可能误导智能体。

Method: FocusMem在紧凑潜在记忆接口内分离不同责任：(1) 角色感知内容基促使情景记忆保留可复用经验、工作记忆保留任务进度；(2) 状态条件读出为同一存储证据生成决策特定视图；(3) 轻量信任门控可抑制与当前步骤不相关的记忆块。所有组件在GUI策略冻结的条件下训练。

Result: 在五个GUI智能体基准上，FocusMem一致优于完全匹配的仅动作固定记忆基线和先前的潜在记忆改编方法。进一步分析表明：语义与功能监督保留互补信息；状态条件读出在周围轨迹上下文增长时更鲁棒；信任门控能减少注入不相关情景证据带来的危害。

Conclusion: 有效的潜在记忆不仅取决于对过去交互的压缩，还取决于保留什么、暴露什么以及允许什么（即内容、读出方式与门控机制三个层面），这为GUI智能体的记忆设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: GUI agents must remember both useful experience from earlier tasks and unfinished progress in the current interaction. Latent memory offers a compact solution by compressing multimodal trajectories into a few continuous tokens. Existing methods, however, usually map each trajectory to one fixed memory block and train it mainly through next-action supervision. This creates three practical problems: important details may be lost during compression, the same memory block must serve different decision stages, and irrelevant retrieved trajectories may still mislead the agent. We introduce FocusMem, which separates these responsibilities within a compact latent-memory interface. A role-aware content basis encourages episodic memory to retain reusable experience and working memory to retain task progress. A state-conditioned readout generates a decision-specific view of the same stored evidence, while a lightweight trust gate can suppress memory blocks that appear irrelevant to the current step. All components are trained while the GUI policy remains frozen. Across five GUI-agent benchmarks, FocusMem consistently outperforms a fully matched action-only fixed-memory baseline and prior latent memory adaptations. Further analysis shows that semantic and functional supervision preserve complementary information, state-conditioned readout is more robust as surrounding trajectory context grows, and the trust gate reduces the harm caused by injected irrelevant episodic evidence. These results show that effective latent memory depends not only on compressing past interaction, but also on what is retained, what is exposed, and what is allowed.

</details>


### [47] [EgoAfford: Task-Oriented Affordance Grounding via Egocentric Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04533)
*Xinyuan Guan,Feifan Chen,Xinyu Zhan,Fu-Cheng Zhang,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoAfford基准和EgoLens模型，用于在自顶向下视角下联合研究多步桌面任务的视觉感知与规划，涵盖直接物体、工具和目的地的功能区域分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的部件级可供性基础定位仅限于将功能区域与单一动作关联，缺乏将对象语义角色、任务状态对齐的视觉观测与多步规划三者连接起来的能力。为弥补这一空白，需要一个新的基准来支持复杂任务中的联合感知与规划研究。

Method: 引入EgoAfford基准：包含约15.5k张人工验证图像（来自2000个生成的多步场景，按语义对齐、任务完整的图像序列组织）和EgoAfford-Real（102张人工采集图像，覆盖26个任务）。同时提出EgoLens，一个3B参数的多模态大语言模型，配备角色特定的掩码解码器，用于联合推理下一步动作并分割直接对象、工具和目的地三个组件的功能区域。

Result: 对近期参照分割MLLM、商业VLM-SAM2流水线和EgoLens的评估显示，下一步推理和动作角色条件部件接地存在互补性挑战。EgoLens在生成数据和人工采集数据上均建立了强参考性能。

Conclusion: EgoAfford和EgoLens共同为多步桌面任务中联合感知与规划研究提供了基础，填补了部件级可供性接地从单一动作扩展到复杂任务的关键空白。

Abstract: Part-level affordance grounding has advanced the localization of functional object regions associated with elemental actions. Extending this capability to complex tasks calls for connecting the semantic roles of participating objects with task-state-aligned visual observations and multi-step planning. We introduce EgoAfford, a benchmark designed to connect these three aspects. Given an egocentric observation and a high-level tabletop task, a model must generate the remaining plan and segment the functional regions of up to three components of the next action: the direct object, instrument, and destination. EgoAfford comprises approximately 15.5k human-verified images from 2,000 generated multi-step scenes, organized as semantically aligned, task-complete image series, together with EgoAfford-Real, 102 manually captured images spanning 26 tasks. We further present EgoLens, a 3B multimodal large language model with role-specific mask decoders, as an in-domain reference model for this joint task. Evaluations of recent referring-segmentation MLLMs, commercial-VLM--SAM2 pipelines, and EgoLens highlight the complementary challenges of next-step inference and action-role-conditioned part grounding. EgoLens establishes strong reference performance on both generated and manually captured observations. Together, EgoAfford and EgoLens provide a foundation for jointly studying perception and planning in multi-step tabletop tasks. Our project page is available at: https://egoafford.github.io

</details>


### [48] [VoxStruct3D: Structure-Leading Flow Matching for Voxel-Space 3D MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04557)
*Fang Li,Yang Gao,Shihao Zou,Weixin Si,Hongyu Wu,Qing Xia,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VoxStruct3D，一个直接在体素空间中建模全分辨率MRI体积的流匹配框架，通过干净数据预测目标、因子化3D patch嵌入、重叠上采样和结构引导策略，克服了潜在扩散模型中图像自编码器的重建瓶颈，在病理和健康大脑T1加权MRI数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真3D MRI合成需要同时具备全局解剖一致性和细粒度体素级细节。虽然潜在扩散使体积生成变得可行，但其图像自编码器引入了重建瓶颈，限制了最终体积中可恢复的精细细节。因此需要一种能直接建模全分辨率体积、同时保留显式解剖先验的生成框架。

Method: 1) 体素空间流匹配框架：采用干净数据预测目标直接建模全分辨率MRI体积。2) 体积体素生成器（VVG）：结合因子化3D patch嵌入、重叠上采样、时间调制残差细化和跳跃融合，使相邻token共同重建共享体素区域并抑制patch边界伪影。3) 结构先行-图像跟随（SFIF）策略：使用冻结的预训练3D医学编码器和StructVAE提取紧凑结构token保留主导解剖结构，结构引导调度使结构轨迹领先于图像轨迹。4) Patch对齐RoPE实现不等token网格的空间对齐，非对称注意力实现从结构到图像的单向引导。

Result: 在病理和健康的T1加权大脑MRI数据集上，VoxStruct3D在特征分布对齐、样本多样性和感知质量方面均取得最强综合性能，能够生成解剖一致且视觉逼真的体积。

Conclusion: 体素空间流匹配结合显式结构先验可以克服潜在扩散方法的重建瓶颈，实现高保真3D MRI合成，兼顾全局解剖一致性与细粒度细节。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis requires both globally coherent anatomy and fine-grained voxel-level detail. Although latent diffusion makes volumetric generation tractable, its image autoencoder introduces a reconstruction bottleneck that can limit the fine detail recoverable in the final volume. We present VoxStruct3D, a voxel-space flow-matching framework that directly models full-resolution MRI volumes using a clean-data prediction objective. Its Volumetric Voxel Generator (VVG) combines factorized 3D patch embedding with overlapping upsampling, time-modulated residual refinement, and skip fusion, enabling neighboring tokens to jointly reconstruct shared voxel regions and suppress patch-boundary artifacts. To complement direct voxel-space modeling with an explicit anatomical prior, we further introduce a Structure-First, Image-Follows (SFIF) strategy. A frozen pretrained 3D medical encoder and a StructVAE extract compact structure tokens that preserve dominant anatomy, while a structure-leading schedule keeps their trajectory ahead of the image trajectory. Patch-Aligned RoPE spatially aligns the unequal token grids, and asymmetric attention enforces one-way guidance from structure to image. Experiments on pathological and healthy T1-weighted brain MRI datasets show that VoxStruct3D achieves the strongest overall performance across feature-distribution alignment, sample diversity, and perceptual quality, producing anatomically coherent and visually realistic volumes.

</details>


### [49] [ColorFD: A Finite-Difference Guided Black-Box Physical Adversarial Attack for Remote Sensing Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04559)
*Tiannuo Guo,Guhang Qiu,Yuzhen Xie,Rui Feng,Ligang Li,Deliang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ColorFD，一种基于多个纯色补丁的黑盒物理攻击方法，利用差分进化联合优化补丁位置和颜色参数，并通过关键区域定位和共同特征提取两种引导策略约束搜索空间，在数字和物理世界中均表现出优异的攻击性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感目标检测器的对抗攻击研究主要集中在数字域或白盒设定下，而黑盒物理攻击研究不足，且面临物理可行性受限和高维搜索空间优化效率低下的挑战。

Method: ColorFD采用多个纯色补丁进行黑盒物理攻击：使用差分进化（DE）联合优化补丁位置和颜色参数；提出目标级适应度与选择机制评估各目标攻击状态并保留目标特定改进；引入关键区域定位（通过有限差分颜色探测识别敏感区域）和共同特征提取（提供类别级空间先验并避免重复定位）两种引导策略约束搜索空间。

Result: 在YOLOv3u、YOLOv5u和Faster R-CNN上的实验表明，ColorFD在所有测试检测器上均优于对比的黑盒补丁攻击方法，并与强白盒基线表现相当；物理世界实验验证了纯色补丁从数字域到真实成像条件的可迁移性。方法虽在航空器类别上评估，但公式不限于该类别。

Conclusion: ColorFD通过纯色补丁与差分进化优化有效解决了黑盒物理攻击的可行性和效率问题，兼具跨检测器的通用性以及数字到物理的迁移能力，为遥感领域的对抗攻击提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Although deep neural network-based remote sensing object detectors have achieved strong performance, they remain vulnerable to adversarial perturbations. Existing studies mainly focus on digital or white-box settings, whereas black-box physical attacks remain underexplored. These attacks are often constrained by limited physical feasibility and inefficient optimization in high-dimensional search spaces. To address these challenges, this paper proposes ColorFD, a black-box physical attack based on multiple pure-color patches. The patch positions and color parameters are jointly optimized using Differential Evolution (DE). A target-wise fitness and selection mechanism evaluates the attack state of each target and preserves target-specific improvements during evolution. Two guidance strategies further constrain the patch search space. Key-region localization identifies sensitive regions through finite-difference color probing. Common-feature extraction provides category-level spatial priors and avoids repeated localization. Although evaluated on aircraft, the formulation is not inherently restricted to this category. Experiments on YOLOv3u, YOLOv5u, and Faster R-CNN show that ColorFD outperforms the tested black-box patch method across all evaluated detectors and remains competitive with strong white-box baselines. Physical-world experiments further demonstrate that the optimized pure-color patches can be transferred from the digital domain to real imaging conditions.

</details>


### [50] [OutLangSplat: 3D Language Gaussian Splatting for UAV Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04560)
*Xia Yan,He Wu,Yanghui Xu,Zizhao Wu,Jiazhou Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OutLangSplat，将3D语言高斯泼溅（3D Language Gaussian Splatting）扩展到UAV室外场景，通过2D-3D双分支特征表示和训练无关的贡献与一致性感知的特征聚合策略，解决现有方法在遮挡严重、远距离视角下语义激活错误和目标响应缺失的问题，并在四个真实UAV室外数据集上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语言高斯泼溅方法仅限于室内或小规模场景，在UAV室外场景中由于严重遮挡和远距离视角导致语义激活错误和目标响应缺失，缺乏适用于UAV室外开放词汇3D场景理解的方法。

Method: 1) 特征表示方面：设计2D-3D双分支表示架构，通过区域级别的对齐与融合提升空间一致性，减少目标响应不完整和背景误激活。2) 特征聚合方面：提出一种训练无关的贡献和一致性感知高斯特征聚合策略，利用像素贡献可靠性和跨视角语义一致性抑制来自噪声视角的不可靠响应。3) 数据集构建：在四个真实UAV室外公共数据集上手动标注多种物体，提供首个UAV室外场景开放词汇3D场景理解数据集。

Result: 在开放词汇语义分割和实例定位两项任务上，OutLangSplat均优于现有SOTA方法，并通过定量评估和消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: OutLangSplat通过改进特征表示和聚合可靠性，成功将语言高斯表示适配到UAV室外场景，且数据集和代码将开源，为该领域后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: 3D Language Gaussian Splatting embeds open-vocabulary language features into 3D Gaussian Splatting, providing an efficient explicit representation for text-driven 3D scene understanding. However, existing methods are limited to indoor or small-scale scenes, and tend to fail in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) outdoor scenes, where severe occlusions and long distance viewpoints often lead to incorrect semantic activations and missing target responses. In this paper, we present OutLangSplat which adapts language Gaussian representations to UAV outdoor scenes by improving feature representation and aggregation reliability. For the feature representation, a 2D-3D dual-branch representation with region-based alignment and fusion is designed to improve spatial consistency, reducing incomplete target responses and background misactivations. For the feature aggregation, we introduce a training-free contribution and consistency-aware Gaussian feature aggregation strategy that leverages pixel contribution reliability and cross-view semantic consistency to suppress unreliable responses from noisy viewpoints. A new dataset is provided by manually annotating various objects on four real-world public UAV outdoor scene datasets. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first accessible dataset of open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding for UAV outdoor scenes. Quantitative evaluations and ablation studies demonstrate that OutLangSplat outperforms SOTA methods on both open-vocabulary semantic segmentation and instance localization tasks. The datasets and codes will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [51] [Talk2Sensors: 3D Visual Grounding in Autonomous Driving via Sensor-Adaptive Physical Cue Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04568)
*Runwei Guan,Di Tian,Ningwei Ouyang,Ruixiao Zhang,Shaofeng Liang,Haocheng Zhao,Lianqing Zheng,Xiaokai Bai,Guotao Wang,Daizong Liu,Henghui Ding,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍Talk2Sensors——首个基于摄像头、LiDAR和4D雷达的多传感器3D视觉定位数据集，以及TSFormer——一个统一Transformer框架，通过粗到细的属性感知融合策略实现自动驾驶场景下的语言引导3D视觉定位，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉定位（3DVG）研究主要集中在室内场景（RGB-D或点云），室外扩展大多依赖单目图像，均未充分利用异构传感器捕获的互补物理属性（视觉纹理、3D几何、物体运动学），而这些对于灵活鲁棒的查询自适应定位至关重要。

Method: 提出TSFormer框架，采用粗到细的属性感知融合策略：(1) Language-Routed Property Sampler通过查询级语言线索调节传感器采样权重，执行粗粒度文本条件特征检索；(2) Sparse-Preserving Modality Arbiter模块进行细粒度模态仲裁和文本引导精炼，确定精确的被指空间位置。该设计可根据每个提示的语义需求动态路由外观、几何和运动线索，防止密集模态淹没稀疏但关键的传感器信号。

Result: TSFormer在Talk2Sensors上比最强基线提升8.05 mAP，在单目Mono3DRefer基准上达到53.05% Acc@0.5，均取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过构建首个多传感器3D视觉定位数据集并设计属性感知的多模态融合框架，证明了异构传感器信息（外观、几何、运动）的动态路由可以显著提升自动驾驶场景下的3D视觉定位性能，为室外3DVG提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: As a key capability for embodied intelligence, 3D visual grounding (3DVG) has been predominantly studied in indoor scenes with RGB-D or point-cloud inputs, while existing outdoor extensions largely rely on monocular images alone. Both settings fall short of real-world outdoor perception, where heterogeneous sensors capture complementary yet distinct physical properties, such as visual texture, 3D geometry, and object kinematics, that are indispensable for flexible and robust query-adaptive grounding but remain under-exploited. To bridge this gap, we introduce Talk2Sensors, the first multi-sensor 3D visual grounding dataset built upon camera, LiDAR, and 4D radar. It contains 8,682 language instructions and 20,558 referred objects, with diverse prompts explicitly aligned with sensor-specific physical cues. Furthermore, we propose TSFormer, a unified Transformer-based framework for language-guided 3D visual grounding in autonomous driving. TSFormer adopts a coarse-to-fine property-aware fusion strategy: the Language-Routed Property Sampler first performs coarse text-conditioned feature retrieval by modulating sensor sampling weights with query-level linguistic cues, while the subsequent Sparse-Preserving Modality Arbiter module conducts fine-grained modality arbitration and text-guided refinement to determine the precise referred spatial location. This design enables dynamic routing of appearance, geometry, and motion cues according to the semantic requirements of each prompt, preventing dense modalities from overwhelming sparse but critical sensor signals. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TSFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks: it improves over the strongest baseline by 8.05 mAP on Talk2Sensors, and transfers to the monocular Mono3DRefer benchmark with 53.05\% Acc@0.5.

</details>


### [52] [PhysMind: From Video to Executable Worlds for Training-Free Physical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04575)
*Chen Yang,Shenxiang Zeng,Haoyang Zhao,Zhouyuan Xu,Youquan He,Haoyu Li,Mingyi Deng,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysMind是一个无需训练的智能体框架，通过为每个视频构建一个可复用的、与问题无关的可执行世界模型，实现可靠的物理推理。该方法在CLEVRER和Physion++基准上分别比直接链式推理提升38.23和8.08个百分点，在反事实问题上超越GPT-5.5达19.25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）难以从视频中理解物体的运动、交互和对干预的响应，尤其在未来预测和反事实推理方面表现不佳。作者旨在克服这一局限，构建一个能够进行可靠物理推理的框架。

Method: PhysMind采用训练无关的智能体框架，核心步骤包括：(1) 通过目标分割、网格重建和6D姿态跟踪恢复时间上一致的动态场景；(2) 在不展开时间步进模拟器的情况下，拟合解析连续时间动力学模型并估计潜在物理参数；(3) 针对给定问题，检查、延续或编辑所构建的世界模型，并从生成的轨迹和交互中获取答案。

Result: 在CLEVRER基准上提升38.23个百分点，在Physion++上提升8.08个百分点（相对同一VLM的直接链式推理）；在反事实问题上，超越最强评估VLM基线GPT-5.5达19.25个百分点。

Conclusion: PhysMind通过构建可执行的世界模型而非直接依赖VLM的隐式推理，显著提升了视频物理推理能力，尤其在前馈预测和反事实推理等复杂任务上展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Reliable physical reasoning from video requires understanding how objects move, interact, and respond to interventions. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) often struggle to interpret these dynamics and reason reliably about future and counterfactual outcomes. We introduce PhysMind, a training-free agentic framework that constructs one reusable, question-agnostic executable world per video. PhysMind recovers a temporally consistent dynamic scene through object segmentation, mesh reconstruction, and 6D pose tracking, then fits analytic continuous-time dynamics and latent physical parameters without unrolling a time-stepped simulator. Given a question, it inspects, continues, or edits the world and answers from the resulting trajectories and interactions. Relative to direct chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning with the same VLM, PhysMind improves accuracy by 38.23 points on CLEVRER and 8.08 points on Physion++. On counterfactual questions, it exceeds the strongest evaluated VLM baseline, GPT-5.5, by 19.25 points.

</details>


### [53] [ACA-GS: Adaptive-Capacity Anchored Gaussian Splatting for Compact Dynamic Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04581)
*Seunghyeon Song,Joo Chan Lee,Chanung Park,Jun Young Jeong,Minseo Lee,Eunbyung Park,Jong Hwan Ko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应容量的锚点式4D高斯溅射框架，通过动态分配每个锚点的Neural Gaussian数量和特征通道容量，解决传统锚点式方法参数量固定导致的存储浪费问题，在保持视觉质量的同时实现更高的压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管4D高斯溅射（4DGS）在高质量实时时空渲染方面取得进展，但锚点式设计存在刚性问题：每个锚点使用固定的Neural Gaussian数量和特征预算，导致在面对复杂运动时只能通过增加锚点密度来弥补表达力不足，而非灵活地增加单个锚点的容量，造成内存浪费。

Method: 提出两个互补的自适应机制：(1) 自适应锚点基数（Adaptive Anchor Cardinality）——根据局部空间和时间复杂度动态调整每个锚点的Neural Gaussian数量，在几何或运动复杂区域集中图元，在简单区域抑制冗余；(2) 自适应锚点特征掩蔽（Adaptive Anchor Feature Masking）——动态调整锚点特征通道，为复杂区域分配丰富特征，为简单区域分配轻量表示。

Result: 在MPEG、Panoptic Sports和N3DV数据集上的实验表明，该方法在不损失视觉质量的前提下显著降低存储需求。在具有复杂运动的MPEG序列上，相比于最先进的锚点式方法实现了最高1.5倍更高的压缩率，同时保持相当的渲染质量。

Conclusion: 通过打破锚点式4DGS中固定的参数分配范式，自适应容量分配能够更高效地利用存储资源，在保证渲染质量的同时实现更高的压缩性能，为4D动态场景表示提供了更灵活、紧凑的方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) enable high-fidelity, real-time spatiotemporal rendering, but expose a fundamental trade-off between motion expressiveness and storage efficiency. While anchor-based designs achieve compactness through anchor-level parameter sharing, their rigid uniform parametrization enforces fixed Neural Gaussian counts and feature budgets per anchor. Consequently, insufficient fidelity is addressed by excessive anchor density, rather than lightweight, targeted increases in Neural Gaussian count or feature capacity, resulting in memory waste. To overcome this rigidity, we introduce an adaptive-capacity anchor-based framework that dynamically allocates the representational capacity based on local spatiotemporal demands. Adaptive Anchor Cardinality varies the number of Neural Gaussians per anchor, concentrating primitives in regions of high geometric or motion complexity while suppressing redundancy. In parallel, Adaptive Anchor Feature Masking modulates anchor-level feature channels, assigning rich features to complex regions and lightweight representations to simpler ones. Experiments on MPEG, Panoptic Sports, and N3DV datasets demonstrate substantial storage reduction without degrading visual quality. Notably, on challenging MPEG sequences with complex motion, our method achieves up to 1.5x higher compression than state-of-the-art anchor-based methods while preserving comparable quality.

</details>


### [54] [MetaVideoAgent: Automated Video-Agent Evolution for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04587)
*Benlei Cui,Ruize Wang,Junjie Li,Jinhao Chen,Longtao Huang,Yinghao Chen,Yuwen Zhai,Jingqun Tang,Ruijian Jia,Weiwei Wu,Pengfei Sun,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaVideoAgent，一个能够针对目标视频分布自动演化视频智能体的框架，以及VA-EvoBench基准。通过四个演化迭代，MetaVideoAgent在八个视频分布上将宏平均准确率从38.44%提升至51.47%，超越最强固定设计视频智能体6.39个百分点，且使用最少的token和视频帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要从长多模态视频中定位稀疏且与问题相关的证据，但真实世界视频分布在模态信息密度、内容结构和证据模式上存在差异，导致固定的视频智能体设计在处理不匹配的数据时产生冗余计算或性能失效。从文本领域扩展到视频领域的自动智能体演化面临三大挑战：全长视频执行导致候选验证成本高昂、跨耦合证据处理阶段的错误传播、以及复杂预处理/感知工具/定位策略使代码级更新难以可靠实施。

Method: MetaVideoAgent通过以下步骤自动演化视频智能体：(1) 从稀疏采样帧和相关查询中刻画信息密度与证据需求以指导初始设计；(2) 将局部化失败压缩为可独立执行的最小验证任务；(3) 构建基于证据的Gold Paths，审计Student轨迹，跨样本聚合重复失败并归因到负责模块；(4) 采用模块化智能体表示，将每次更新约束到主要责任模块及其必要依赖。同时提出VA-EvoBench基准，覆盖八个视频分布，包含独立的演化和留出（held-out）划分。

Result: 在VA-EvoBench的八个视频分布上，每个分布经过四次演化迭代，MetaVideoAgent均改进了初始智能体，宏平均准确率从38.44%提升到51.47%，平均演化成本为每分布354万token。演化后的智能体相比最强的先验固定设计视频智能体高出6.39个百分点，同时在比较的视频智能体中使用的token和每问题视频帧数最少。

Conclusion: MetaVideoAgent证明通过自动化的、基于证据归因的模块化演化，可以针对不同视频分布自适应地优化视频智能体，在显著提升准确率的同时降低推理成本。该工作为视频智能体的自动演化提供了可行框架，并将开源全部代码和数据以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding requires locating sparse, question-relevant evidence in long, multimodal videos. Real-world video distributions differ in modality-specific information density, content structure, and evidence patterns, causing fixed video-agent designs to incur redundant processing or fail when mismatched. Extending automated agent evolution from text to video is challenging because full long-video execution makes candidate validation expensive, failures propagate across coupled evidence-processing stages, and complex preprocessing, perception tools, and localization strategies make code-level updates difficult to implement reliably.
  We introduce MetaVideoAgent, a framework that automatically evolves a video agent for a target distribution. It profiles information density and evidence requirements from sparsely sampled frames and associated queries to guide initial design, then compresses localized failures into independently executable minimal validation tasks. It constructs evidence-grounded Gold Paths, audits Student trajectories, aggregates recurring failures across samples, and attributes them to responsible modules. A modular agent representation constrains each update to the primary responsible module and its necessary dependencies.
  We further introduce VA-EvoBench, covering eight video distributions with separate evolution and held-out splits. With four evolution iterations per distribution, MetaVideoAgent improves every initial agent and raises macro-average accuracy from 38.44% to 51.47%, at an average evolution cost of 3.54M tokens per distribution. The evolved agents outperform the strongest prior fixed-design video agent by 6.39 percentage points while using the fewest tokens and video frames per question among the compared video agents. We will release all code and data to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [55] [The First EgoCross Challenge at EgoVis 2026: Cross-Domain Egocentric Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04589)
*Yuqian Fu,Tianwen Qian,Yanjun Li,Yu Li,Kunyu Peng,Xu Zheng,Yongqin Xian,Alessio Tonioni,Yanwei Fu,Xiaoling Wang,Danda Paudel,Federico Tombari,Luc Van Gool,Leyi Wu,Yifan Zhao,Jinjie Zhang,Yinchuan Li,Yingcong Chen,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Wenbo Wang,Yupeng Hu,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie,Takuya Murakawa,Toru Tamaki,Yi Wen,Zhenglin Du,Zhengyang Li,Lingling Li,Licheng Jiao,Wenping Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoCross是一个跨域第一人称视频问答基准，旨在评估多模态大语言模型在日常生活场景之外的泛化能力，涵盖手术、工业装配、极限运动和动物视角四个目标域。首届挑战赛在CVPR 2026的Third EgoVis Workshop上举办，设立了Source-Limited和Open-Source两个官方赛道，共收到超1500份提交，来自130多名参与者，并发布了官方排行榜及优胜方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称视频理解基准多聚焦于日常场景，缺乏对模型在专业和特殊领域（如手术、工业、动物视角）泛化能力的系统评估。EgoCross旨在填补这一空白，通过构建跨域基准来检验多模态大语言模型能否超越日常场景实现真正的泛化。

Method: 构建包含四个目标域（手术、工业装配、极限运动、动物视角）的跨域测试数据集，每个测试样例由第一人称视频片段、问题和四个候选答案组成。通过Codabench平台设立两个官方赛道：Source-Limited Track限制使用官方基线模型和小型支持集；Open-Source Track允许更广泛地选择模型和训练数据，但禁止手动构建目标域训练数据。

Result: 挑战赛共收到超过1500份提交，来自130多名参与者，其中19支团队参加Open-Source Track，38支团队参加Source-Limited Track。官方发布了两个赛道的排行榜结果，并总结了优胜团队的解决方案。

Conclusion: EgoCross为跨域第一人称视频理解提供了标准化评估基准和挑战资源，可作为推动该领域研究进展的参考。所有资源（挑战数据、基线实现、优胜团队代码）均已公开发布，以促进后续研究。

Abstract: EgoCross is a cross-domain egocentric video question answering benchmark designed to evaluate whether multimodal large language models can generalize beyond common daily-life scenarios. The first EgoCross Challenge was hosted at the Third EgoVis Workshop at CVPR 2026 and evaluated models on first-person videos from four target domains: surgery, industrial assembly, extreme sports, and animal perspectives. Each test example consists of an egocentric video clip, a question, and four candidate answers, from which the model must select the correct option. This technical report introduces the challenge task, benchmark resources, and two official Codabench tracks. The Source-Limited Track restricts participants to the official baseline model and a small support set, whereas the Open-Source Track permits broader choices of models and training data under rules that prohibit the manual construction of target-domain training data. In total, the challenge received more than 1,500 submissions from over 130 participants, with 19 teams participating in the Open-Source Track and 38 teams in the Source-Limited Track. We further present the official leaderboard results and summarize the winning solutions from both tracks. We hope that this report will serve as a useful technical reference for advancing cross-domain egocentric video understanding. All resources, including the challenge data, baseline implementation, and code released by the winning teams, are made publicly available.

</details>


### [56] [COSMO: Consensus-Driven Shift Modulation for Source-Free Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04604)
*Bo Li,Junjie Peng,Xiaohua Xie,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COSMO将VLM引导的源-free域适应（SFDA）形式化为样本级置信度分配问题，通过锚定共享共识替代专家间的直接引导，实现源模型与VLM证据的协同适应，在四个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFDA中模型的自生成监督在领域偏移较大时会强化源偏差；虽然预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）可提供补充语义知识，但源模型与VLM的相对可靠性在不同目标样本间差异显著，现有跨模型引导方法未显式建模这种差异，可能在冲突时覆盖有效源证据，导致"源证据遗忘"问题。

Method: COSMO将问题建模为样本级可靠性分配，用锚定共享共识替代专家到专家的直接引导。首先构造偏向更集中预测的样本特定初始共识；在适应过程中，重新聚合两支（源模型与VLM）演化的证据，并根据共识不确定性与训练进度调节共识偏离初始锚点的程度，使共享监督保持锚定又具适应性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，在匹配VLM骨干网络条件下取得SOTA性能；进一步分析表明COSMO能更好地平衡保留有效源证据与吸收VLM互补证据之间的关系。

Conclusion: COSMO通过锚定-自适平衡机制有效缓解了源证据遗忘问题，证明了在SFDA中显式建模跨模型证据可靠性与共享共识的重要性。

Abstract: Source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) adapts a source-trained model to an unlabeled target domain without source data, a practical setting under privacy or storage constraints. Yet its self-generated supervision can reinforce source bias under substantial domain shifts. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) offer complementary semantic knowledge, but the relative reliability of the source model and VLM varies across target samples. Existing cross-model guidance does not explicitly account for this variation and may overwrite valid source-derived evidence under conflict, a failure we term source-derived evidence forgetting. We formulate VLM-guided SFDA as a sample-wise reliability-allocation problem and propose Consensus-Driven Shift Modulation (COSMO). COSMO replaces expert-to-expert guidance with co-adaptation through an anchored shared consensus. It first forms a sample-specific initial consensus that favors the more concentrated prediction. During adaptation, COSMO re-aggregates both branches' evolving evidence and regulates how far the resulting consensus moves from its initial anchor based on consensus uncertainty and training progress. This keeps the shared supervision anchored yet adaptive. Across four benchmarks, COSMO achieves state-of-the-art performance under matched VLM backbones. Further analyses indicate that it better balances the retention of valid source-derived evidence with the absorption of complementary VLM evidence.

</details>


### [57] [TRCoRSurg: Temporal-Relational Co-Reasoning for Surgical Video Triplet Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04606)
*Fang Li,Shihao Zou,Weixin Si,Yang Gao,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，整合空间、关系和时间线索用于手术三元组识别，通过标签相关性建模模块和多尺度类激活图引导的关系提取来捕获成分间的静态共现与动态上下文依赖，并通过双向时间-关系融合注意力模块协调时间与关系表示，同时提出新评估指标TCER度量三元组因果与语义一致性，在CholecT45和ProstaTD数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术三元组识别方法难以在统一框架中联合建模帧内标签依赖和帧间时间语义，缺乏对仪器、动作和目标等相互依赖实体之间关系一致性的时空协同建模能力。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 多尺度编码器提取类特定空间先验；(2) 标签相关性建模模块（MS-CAMRE）用于捕获三元组成分间的静态共现模式和动态上下文依赖；(3) 双向时间-关系融合注意力模块（BTRFA）协调时间与关系表示以实现连贯的时间推理。此外引入新评估指标TCER量化模型保持三元组因果和语义一致性的能力。

Result: 在CholecT45和ProstaTD数据集上均取得最优性能，AP_IVT分别提升5.1%和7.8%；根据TCER指标，两个数据集上的相对误差分别降低超过36%和25%，验证了框架在时间-关系协同推理中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过统一整合空间、关系和时间线索，所提出框架能够有效建模手术三元组中帧内标签依赖与帧间时间语义，在时序-关系协同推理方面取得显著优势，并通过新指标TCER更全面地评估模型的一致性保持能力。

Abstract: Understanding complex surgical scenes requires recognizing multiple interdependent entities, such as instruments, actions, and targets, while maintaining their relational consistency across time. Existing surgical triplet recognition methods struggle to jointly model intra-frame label dependencies and inter-frame temporal semantics in a unified manner. To address these limitations, we propose a unified framework that integrates spatial, relational, and temporal cues for robust surgical triplet recognition. Specifically, class-specific spatial priors are first extracted through a multi-scale encoder. These priors are then refined by a Label Correlation Modeling module with multi-scale class activation map-guided relational extraction (MS-CAMRE), enabling the model to capture both static co-occurrence patterns and dynamic contextual dependencies among triplet components. Furthermore, a Bidirectional Temporal-Relational Fusion Attention (BTRFA) module harmonizes temporal and relational representations to achieve coherent temporal reasoning. We also introduce a new evaluation metric, the Triplet Consistency Error Rate (TCER), which quantitatively measures the model's ability to preserve causal and semantic consistency across triplets. Extensive experiments on the CholecT45 and ProstaTD datasets show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving AP_IVT by 5.1 percent and 7.8 percent, respectively. Moreover, according to TCER, our approach achieves relative reductions of more than 36 percent and 25 percent on the two datasets, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of our framework in temporal-relational co-reasoning.

</details>


### [58] [HiSC: Hierarchical Spatial Clustering Token Compression for Efficient 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04610)
*Jiuhe Qu,Yingping Liang,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiSC是一个无需训练的3D视觉语言模型（3D VLM）层级空间聚类token压缩框架，通过空间图融合（SGraM）和空间聚类剪枝（SCluP）两阶段策略，将token压缩从token级选择提升到簇级处理，在保持空间完整性和细粒度细节的同时实现超过90%的token缩减且性能下降极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D VLMs在多变场场景中执行空间推理时，由于重复观测和大量无信息区域导致严重的token冗余和高计算成本。虽然2D VLM的视觉token压缩方法表现良好，但它们无法捕捉3D场景的结构化特性，导致空间覆盖不完整和细粒度细节丢失。

Method: HiSC包含两个核心策略：（1）空间图融合（SGraM）：在LLM推理之前，将跨视角冗余建模为空间连通性，融合物理一致的区域，合并极度相似的冗余token；（2）空间聚类剪枝（SCluP）：在LLM推理过程中，利用联合几何和语义线索组织token为空间聚类，跨簇和簇内进行层级压缩，保留对象实例完整性以及重要区域的细粒度细节。

Result: 在多个3D推理基准上的大量实验验证了HiSC的有效性，特别是在高视觉token剪枝率下表现突出。HiSC实现了超过90%的token缩减，且仅有极小的性能下降。

Conclusion: HiSC通过将token压缩从token级选择提升到簇级处理，有效解决了3D VLM中的token冗余问题，在保持空间覆盖和细节保留的同时实现了极高的token压缩率，为3D VLM的高效推理提供了可行的训练-free解决方案。

Abstract: 3D vision-language models (3D VLMs) enable spatial reasoning over multi-view scenes but suffer from substantial token redundancy due to duplicated observations and large uninformative regions, leading to high computational cost. Although visual token compression has shown promise in accelerating 2D VLMs, it fails to capture the structured nature of 3D scenes and leads to incomplete spatial coverage and loss of fine-grained details. In this paper, we propose \textbf{HiSC}, a training-free framework for hierarchical spatial clustering token compression in 3D VLMs. HiSC lifts token compression from token-level selection to cluster-level processing by organizing tokens into spatially grounded clusters using joint geometric and semantic cues. Specifically, we first introduce a \textbf{spatial graph-based merging (SGraM) strategy} that models cross-view redundancy as spatial connectivity and consolidates physically consistent regions, effectively merging extremely similar redundant tokens prior to LLM inference. We then propose a \textbf{spatial clustering-based pruning (SCluP) paradigm} within LLM inference, which performs hierarchical compression across clusters and within clusters, preserving object instance completeness while retaining fine-grained details for important regions. Extensive experiments on diverse 3D reasoning benchmarks show validate the effectiveness of HiSC, particularly under high visual token pruning ratios. Besides, HiSC achieves over 90\% token reduction with minimal performance degradation. Code is accessible at https://github.com/elecreak/HiSC.

</details>


### [59] [DAC-Pose: Dual-Agent Collaborative Framework for Pose-Guided Human Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04622)
*Haotian Yang,Zhile Yang,Huiyu Zhou,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAC-Pose提出了一种基于双智能体的多模态框架，用于姿态引导的人体生成任务。该系统通过语义推理智能体（PSR）和视觉感知智能体（DAVE）协同工作，解决了大视角变换下传统方法产生严重视觉伪影的问题。在DeepFashion和Market-1501基准上的实验验证了该方法在纹理对齐和身份一致性方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统姿态引导人体生成方法本质上是被动的像素级映射，缺乏认知能力，在剧烈视角变化下无法逻辑推理未可见区域并建模复杂的空间形变，从而不可避免地产生严重的视觉伪影。作者旨在通过引入智能体驱动的多模态框架来解决这一根本性局限。

Method: 提出DAC-Pose双智能体协同框架：(1) Prior Semantic Reasoning（PSR）智能体作为认知引擎，通过协同推理推断未可见区域的细粒度属性；(2) Discrepancy-Aware Visual Encoding（DAVE）智能体作为视觉感知模块，量化并编码视角引起的空间错位，持续向生成过程反馈稳健的空间约束。两者之间形成语义推理与视觉感知的自主反馈闭环。

Result: 在DeepFashion和Market-1501两个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了该智能体驱动范式的优越性。DAC-Pose在剧烈视角变化下在纹理对齐保持和身份一致性方面表现出色。

Conclusion: DAC-Pose通过将单视角人体生成重构为协同双智能体系统，实证表明智能体驱动的认知-感知闭环范式能有效克服传统方法在大视角变换下的局限性，为该任务提供了新的研究范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: AI agents have emerged as a powerful new paradigm in generative image synthesis, enabling systems to perform complex semantic reasoning rather than passive pixel-level mapping. In pose-guided human generation, conventional methods inevitably produce severe visual artifacts under drastic viewpoint shifts, fundamentally because they lack the cognitive capacity to logically deduce unseen regions and model complex spatial deformations. To bridge this gap, we propose DAC-Pose, a novel agent-driven multimodal framework that reformulates single-view human generation as a collaborative dual-agent system. DAC-Pose integrates two complementary components, namely, the Prior Semantic Reasoning (PSR) agent and the Discrepancy-Aware Visual Encoding (DAVE) agent. Functioning as a cognitive engine, PSR utilizes collaborative reasoning to deduce the fine-grained attributes of unseen regions. Concurrently, acting as a specialized visual perception agent, DAVE quantifies and encodes viewpoint-induced spatial misalignments, continuously feeding robust spatial constraints back into the generative process. This autonomous feedback loop between semantic deduction and visual perception ensures high-fidelity detail synthesis. Extensive experiments on the DeepFashion and Market-1501 benchmarks validate the superiority of our agent-driven paradigm. Notably, DAC-Pose excels in preserving texture alignment and identity consistency under drastic viewpoint changes. The code is available at https://github.com/AIVRC/DAC-Pose.

</details>


### [60] [Visual Anchoring in Diffusion: Multimodal Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04623)
*Zehao Bao,Shujun Guo,Bruce X. B. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出TDSM-MM，一种基于扩散生成范式的多模态零样本骨架动作识别方法。通过"按生成分类"（classify-by-generation）的思想，用文本和RGB联合条件去噪器来打分类别，避免了传统独立分支融合中固定权重无法适应类别对的问题。在NTU-60/120数据集上达到最优的归纳式准确率，并在NTU-120 96/24拆分上超越直推式SOTA（71.3% vs 69.1%），且无需测试时适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本骨架动作识别（ZSAR）在未见动作类别共享相似的骨架关节动力学但物体或场景上下文不同时会产生歧义。RGB模态可以提供这些缺失的线索，但现有多模态方法通常保持骨架和RGB独立打分分支并融合输出，存在两个关键问题：(1) 固定融合权重无法捕捉类别对依赖的模态可靠性；(2) 自适应的融合规则在无标签测试数据反馈的情况下难以决定哪个分支应该主导。作者希望绕过这个权重选择问题。

Method: 采用"按生成分类"（classify-by-generation）范式，每个类别通过一个文本条件去噪器预测添加到骨架特征上的噪声的准确度来打分。该公式将逐步被破坏的骨架特征与固定条件分离，使RGB和文本能够联合条件化一个单一的类别打分函数，而非产生独立分数。具体实现为Multimodal Triplet Diffusion for Skeleton-Text Matching (TDSM-MM)，在文本条件去噪Transformer上增加一个非扩散的RGB条件token，作为骨架数据重建过程中的稳定视觉锚点。

Result: 在NTU-60/120数据集中四个划分中的三个上取得最优的归纳式准确率，并在NTU-120 96/24划分上超越直推式SOTA（71.3% vs 69.1%），且无需测试时适应。

Conclusion: 扩散式方法可以作为零样本学习的一个有前景的方向。通过生成范式联合条件化多模态线索，可以避免传统融合权重选择问题，在没有测试时适应的情况下实现更强的零样本骨架动作识别性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot Skeleton Action Recognition (ZSAR) remains ambiguous when unseen actions share similar skeleton joint dynamics but differ in objects or scene context. RGB provides these missing cues, yet existing multimodal methods typically maintain independent skeleton and RGB scoring branches and fuse their outputs. Without using unlabeled test data for adaptation or fusion calibration, a fixed fusion weight cannot capture class-pair-dependent modality reliability, while an adaptive rule lacks target-side feedback for deciding which branch should dominate. We bypass this weight-selection problem via the classify-by-generation paradigm, where each class is scored by how accurately a text-conditioned denoiser predicts the noise added to the skeleton feature. This formulation separates the progressively corrupted skeleton from fixed conditioning, allowing RGB and text to jointly condition a single class-scoring function rather than produce independent scores. We instantiate this idea as Multimodal Triplet Diffusion for Skeleton-Text Matching (TDSM-MM), augmenting a text-conditioned denoising Transformer with a non-diffused RGB condition token that serves as a stable visual anchor during skeleton data reconstruction. Our proposed TDSM-MM has been ablated via extensive experiments and achieved the best inductive accuracy on three of four NTU-60/120 splits and surpasses the transductive state-of-the-art on NTU-120 96/24 (i.e., 71.3% vs. 69.1%), without test-time adaptation, suggesting that diffusion-based methods can be a promising direction for zero-shot learning.

</details>


### [61] [YOLO-PVC: 2D-to-3D Consolidation of Slice-wise Detections for Volumetric Liver Tumor Localization in MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04642)
*Talha Waqas,Mounir Lahlouh,Kawther Taibouni,Mahnoor Waqas,Salar Ahmed,Sébastien Mulé,Yasmina Leroul-Chenoune*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YOLO-PVC，一种轻量级、与模型无关的框架，用于将切片级别的2D检测结果整合为3D体积预测。该方法通过深度连续性约束、稳健百分位统计聚合边界框坐标，以及基于MLP的轻量级校准模块来细化轴向范围。在三维肝脏MRI肿瘤定位任务中，校准变体达到0.710的IoU_3D和约0.78的BEV IoU，显著优于朴素堆叠和平均等聚合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切片式2D目标检测器在处理体积数据时具有计算高效和可扩展性优势，但沿深度轴常常产生碎片化和不稳定的预测结果。现有的朴素堆叠或平均策略无法有效处理缺失检测和异常切片问题，因此需要一种结构化的几何整合方法来实现可靠的2D到3D检测转换。

Method: YOLO-PVC包含三个核心组件：(1)深度连续性约束，确保相邻切片间的预测保持一致性；(2)稳健百分位统计聚合，用于整合各切片边界框坐标；(3)基于MLP的轻量级校准模块，细化轴向范围。该框架是模型无关的，可即插即用，且计算开销低。

Result: 在三维肝脏MRI体积上的三种肿瘤类别的实验中，YOLO-PVC一致优于多个聚合基线。启发式PVC的IoU_3D为0.665，校准变体提升至0.710，且平面重叠度高（BEV IoU约0.78），证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化几何整合为临床MRI中的体积肝脏肿瘤定位提供了一种有效且实用的解决方案，能够在不增加显著计算成本的前提下，显著提升切片式2D检测器在体积数据上的3D定位性能。

Abstract: Slice-wise 2D object detectors are increasingly applied to volumetric data due to their computational efficiency and scalability, yet they often yield fragmented and unstable predictions along the depth axis. We propose YOLO-PVC, a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for 2D-to-3D consolidation of slice-wise detections. The method enforces depth continuity, aggregates bounding box coordinates using robust percentile statistics, and further refines axial extent through a lightweight MLP-based calibration module. Unlike naïve stacking or averaging strategies, YOLO-PVC explicitly addresses missing detections and outlier slices along the depth dimension. Experiments on 3D liver MRI volumes across three tumor categories demonstrate consistent improvements over multiple aggregation baselines. The heuristic PVC achieves an overall $\mathrm{IoU}_{3D}$ of $0.665$, while the calibrated variant further improves performance to $0.710$, with high planar overlap ($\mathrm{BEV\ IoU} \approx 0.78$). These results demonstrate that structured geometric consolidation provides an effective and practical solution for volumetric liver tumor localization in clinical MRI.

</details>


### [62] [DisMix: Order-Aware Mixup for Medical Imaging via Disentangling Ordinal and Non-Ordinal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04652)
*Dileepa Pitawela,Gustavo Carneiro,Hsiang-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DisMix，一种面向序数分类任务（如医学疾病分级）的序数感知混合数据增强框架。DisMix通过双码本VQ-VAE将序数特征与非序数特征解耦，分别对两类特征进行独立混合：序数编码插值生成有意义的中间等级，非序数编码变化引入外观多样性，从而避免标准mixup破坏序数结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图像mixup数据增强在序数分类任务（如医学疾病严重程度分级）中不适配，因为标签编码的是严重程度的递进关系（序数属性），而标准mixup会不加区分地混合疾病严重度线索（序数）与外观层面变化（非序数），从而扭曲临床严重程度分级所依赖的序数结构。

Method: DisMix通过双码本VQ-VAE将图像特征分解为序数特征和非序数特征两个子空间；随后在各子空间内独立进行混合操作——序数编码通过插值生成具有中间语义等级的样本，非序数编码通过变化引入外观多样性，两者结合使混合样本既能体现等级递进又不破坏序数信号。

Result: 在四个医学影像数据集上，DisMix与六种图像mixup基线方法搭配六种序数分类器进行对比，取得了最佳的综合性能；并且在数据稀缺和临床分级变异性场景下依然保持有效性。

Conclusion: DisMix通过序数感知的混合策略，有效解决了标准mixup在序数分类中扭曲标签结构的问题，为医学疾病分级等序数分类任务提供了一种鲁棒且通用的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Image mixup is a widely adopted data augmentation strategy, yet it is ill-suited for ordinal classification tasks such as medical disease grading, where labels encode a progression of severity. By indiscriminately blending disease-severity cues (ordinal) with appearance-level variation (non-ordinal), standard mixup produces samples that distort the very ordinal structure that underpins clinical severity grading. We introduce DisMix, an order-aware mixup framework for ordinal classification. DisMix disentangles ordinal and non-ordinal features via a dual-codebook VQ-VAE, allowing each subspace to be mixed independently: ordinal codes are interpolated to produce meaningful intermediate ranks, while non-ordinal codes are varied to introduce appearance diversity without corrupting the ordinal signal. Across four medical imaging datasets, DisMix shows the best aggregate performance among six image mixup baselines paired with six ordinal classifiers and remains effective under data scarcity and clinical grading variability.

</details>


### [63] [CSGen: A Multi-Domain Curvilinear Structure Generation Model via Hierarchical Multimodal Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04655)
*Zhe Shan,Ziming Yang,Lei Zhou,Wenwen Zhang,Cong Lin,Xia Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CSGen是一个分层多模态扩散模型，通过构建24K+多域数据集、层次渐进式控制策略和稀疏感知损失重加权机制，实现了对曲线结构图像的高保真可控生成，并显著提升了下游分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线结构分析是多媒体中的重要基础任务，但精确控制图像生成中曲线结构对象仍是一个开放挑战。现有方法难以在多个控制条件下保持曲线结构的拓扑完整性和视觉保真度。

Method: CSGen包含三个核心创新：1) 构建跨5个领域、7种标注类型、超24K样本的多域多模态数据集以训练统一生成模型；2) 提出分层渐进式控制策略，通过分阶段信号注入将拓扑线索与视觉上下文解耦，缓解语义漂移并保证稀疏结构的拓扑完整性；3) 设计稀疏感知损失重加权机制，针对曲线结构的极端稀疏性增强优化中对细薄脆弱结构的关注。

Result: 大量实验表明CSGen生成了结构精度和视觉真实感均更优的图像，显著提升了下游分割性能，并在多样提示下保持鲁棒性。代码和数据集已公开。

Conclusion: CSGen被证实是一种可扩展的、数据驱动的复杂曲线结构分析范式，适用于多种多媒体应用场景。

Abstract: Curvilinear structure analysis is an important and fundamental task in multimedia. However, the controllable generation of images with precise curvilinear structure objects remains an open challenge. To address this, we propose CSGen, a hierarchical multimodal diffusion model that synthesizes high-fidelity images precisely aligned with multiple control conditions. The CSGen is built upon three key innovations: 1) We construct a multi-domain and multimodal dataset, including over 24K samples from 5 domains and 7 different types of annotations, to train the unified generation model. 2) We propose a novel hierarchical progressive control strategy that decouples topology clues from visual context by a phased signal injection, mitigating semantic drift while ensuring the topological integrity of sparse structures. 3) We design a sparsity-aware loss re-weighting mechanism to address the extreme sparsity of curvilinear structures, significantly enhancing the attention on thin and fragile structures during optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CSGen generates images with superior structure accuracy and visual realism, significantly improving downstream segmentation performance while maintaining robustness across diverse prompts. Our results confirm CSGen as a scalable, data-centric paradigm for the analysis of complex curvilinear structures in diverse multimedia applications. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/ShanZard/CSGen.

</details>


### [64] [MobileWAM: Bridging World Action Models to Mobile Manipulation with Chain-of-Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04657)
*Zehua Fan,Junjie He,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Wenqi Lyu,Linge Zhao,Fuhao Li,Zihan You,Yifei Yang,Kaiming Xu,Qi Jiang,Yue Jiang,Haoang Li,Cheng Chi,Bailin Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MobileWAM是一种基于视频生成先验的移动操作世界动作模型（WAM），通过混合Transformer架构将预训练视频扩散Transformer与轻量动作专家融合，并引入Chain-of-Foresight（CoF）中间表示进行链式未来潜在块预测以稠密化监督，在ManiSkill-HAB基准上超越现有方法，并成功微调到真实ARX Lift2移动机械臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频生成的世界动作模型（WAM）仅局限于桌面操作，而移动操作需要同时处理全身运动与场景级动态，但主流方法仍使用缺乏动态感知的视觉编码器和手工设计的协调机制，无法充分利用互联网规模的运动先验。

Method: MobileWAM采用混合Transformer架构：将预训练的视频扩散Transformer与轻量动作专家通过逐层联合注意力融合，将互联网规模运动先验迁移到全身控制。动作专家的每个前馈层被设计为三专家混合（共享、运动、操作专家），由动作令牌中的运动意图进行软路由，以调和运动与操作的异构动态。同时提出Chain-of-Foresight（CoF）：中间表示顺序预测未来潜在块链，每步条件于前驱，并与解耦的视频-动作去噪方案自然配合。部署时仅保留WAM作为当前帧编码器，预测链和视频生成在推理时被丢弃。

Result: 在ManiSkill-HAB基准上超越现有最先进的移动操作策略，并能成功微调到真实ARX Lift2移动机械臂，在多种任务上展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MobileWAM成功弥合了世界动作模型从桌面操作到移动操作的鸿沟，验证了视频生成先验结合混合专家架构与链式未来预测在移动操作中的有效性，且推理时开销仅相当于策略级成本。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) built on video generation backbones are a rising recipe for robot learning, yet remain confined to tabletop manipulation. Mobile manipulation demands simultaneous locomotion and whole-body manipulation amid scene-scale dynamics, yet is still dominated by dynamics-blind visual encoders with hand-crafted coordination. We bridge this gap with MobileWAM, a mixture-of-transformers architecture that fuses a pretrained video diffusion transformer with a lightweight action expert through layerwise joint attention, translating internet-scale motion priors into whole-body control. To reconcile the heterogeneous dynamics of moving and manipulating, each feed-forward layer of the action expert becomes a three-expert mixture of shared, locomotion, and manipulation experts, softly routed by the motion intent in the action tokens. To densify supervision, we further propose Chain-of-Foresight (CoF): intermediate representations sequentially predict a chain of future latent chunks, each step conditioned on its predecessor. CoF pairs naturally with our decoupled video--action denoising scheme. At deployment, the WAM serves as a pure current-frame encoder; foresight acts only through gradients, so at inference the foresight chain and video generation are discarded, leaving only policy-level cost. MobileWAM surpasses state-of-the-art mobile manipulation policies on ManiSkill-HAB and fine-tunes to a real ARX Lift2 mobile manipulator across diverse tasks with strong generalization. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [65] [SurgNarrator: A Generative Retrieval Framework for Surgical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04676)
*Yuqing Feng,Jiawei Ma,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Kun Yuan,Nicolas Padoy,Daniel S. Elson,Anh Nguyen,Stamatia Giannarou,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgNarrator，一种面向手术视频理解的生成式检索框架。通过构建手术专属词汇表、微调Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B学习带时间感知的判别性临床表征，并采用层级化、手术流程感知的检索策略，在零样本设置下于12个基准上取得一致性能提升，同时相较生成式基线将输出阶段延迟降低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法面临权衡困境：自回归视频-语言模型支持全面推理但不适合时间敏感的临床场景（高延迟），对比学习模型延迟低但难以处理复杂场景理解。而通用领域的生成式检索方法无法直接迁移到手术领域，因为近似的视觉外观可能对应语义上截然不同的手术事件，且手术术语高度领域特有。因此需要一个兼顾延迟与理解能力的手术视频理解框架。

Method: SurgNarrator包含三个核心组件：1）从手术字幕中构建精心策划的手术中心词汇表，定义临床有意义的检索空间；2）适配预训练的Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B模型，使用时间感知的对比学习目标学习判别性临床表征；3）推理阶段采用层级化、手术流程感知的检索策略，将搜索空间缩小到相关手术类型，实现快速有效的响应。

Result: 该方法在12个基准的零样本设置下进行了全面评估，相较最先进的基线方法取得一致性能提升，同时与生成式基线相比，将输出阶段延迟降低了两个数量级以上（超过100倍）。

Conclusion: SurgNarrator证明了生成式检索框架在手术视频理解领域的有效性，通过手术专用词汇构建和时间感知对比学习，成功在低延迟条件下实现了复杂手术场景的准确理解，为临床实时辅助决策提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Surgical procedures unfold as structured and recurring clinical events, whose real-time understanding via intraoperative surgical videos is critical for intraoperative decision-making and support. However, existing video understanding methods force a trade-off: autoregressive video-language models support comprehensive reasoning but are not practical for time-sensitive clinical applications, whereas contrastive models offer low latency but struggle with complex scene understanding.
