Large feature sets not only increase computational burden and overfitting risk, but also make model interpretation difficult, leading to limited usefulness in clinical settings. In this study, we focus on diagnosis-related features and compare five feature selection paradigms for opioid use disorder (OUD) prediction: recurrence enrichment, NTK-motivated early gradient sensitivity, LightGBM-SHAP, Elastic Net, and large language model (LLM)-guided semantic selection. We use a unified preprocessing and evaluation framework and assess each method by downstream predictive performance, resampling stability, and representation of infrequent diagnosis codes. Our results demonstrate that performance improves with larger feature budgets with diminishing returns beyond a moderate size. NTK sensitivity provides the best overall balance of accuracy and stability, and LLM-guided selection contributes complementary clinically meaningful signals despite lower standalone performance.

</details>


### [133] [Unscented KalmanNet: a hybrid deep learning filter with calibrated posterior covariance for nonlinear state estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04201)
*Minhyeok Ko,Abdollah Shafieezadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unscented KalmanNet (UKN)，一种混合递归估计器，在保留UKF显式sigma点协方差递归的基础上，通过NoiseNet和GainNet两个学习组件分别预测噪声协方差和修正增益偏差。UKN在三个合成系统及UZH-FPV真实飞行数据上均取得最低状态估计误差，相比UKF在合成案例中RMSE降低26.4%-49.7%，在真实飞行数据上位置和速度RMSE分别降低22.4%和34.3%，且协方差校准最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UKF虽广泛用于非线性系统状态估计，但实际应用中未知时变的噪声统计和模型失配会降低估计精度与协方差校准。现有学习型滤波器多基于EKF，要么放弃显式协方差输出，要么学习不确定性却未修正失配导致的增益偏差，缺乏一种既能保持UKF协方差递归结构、又能同时解决噪声统计未知和增益偏差问题的混合估计器。

Method: UKN在UKF框架上集成两个结构不同的学习组件：NoiseNet预测时变的过程和测量噪声协方差，作为固定基线的有界乘性修正，保证正定性；GainNet对解析卡尔曼增益施加有界残差修正。训练目标采用校准感知的联合损失，融合状态误差项、协方差一致性项和创新一致性项，并通过自适应权重联合优化精度与校准。

Result: UKN在全部四个基准测试（三个合成系统及UZH-FPV真实飞行数据）中取得最低聚合状态估计误差。合成案例中相比UKF降RMSE达26.4%-49.7%；11次飞行的留一序列交叉验证中，位置和速度RMSE分别降低22.4%和34.3%。UKN的折间变异性最低，归一化NEES和经验覆盖率最接近名义值。

Conclusion: UKN通过保留UKF的sigma点协方差递归并结合学习型噪声协方差预测与增益残差修正，同时提升了状态估计精度和协方差校准能力，在合成和真实场景中均优于UKF、KalmanNet和Bayesian KalmanNet，证明了混合学习-模型驱动方法在非线性状态估计中的有效性。

Abstract: State estimation for nonlinear dynamical systems is commonly performed with the Unscented Kalman filter (UKF), which propagates the state moments through deterministic sigma points and reports a posterior covariance at every step. In practice, however, unknown and time-varying noise statistics and model mismatch degrade both estimation accuracy and covariance calibration. Existing learned filters improve accuracy but are largely built on the extended Kalman filter and either forgo an explicit covariance or learn uncertainty without correcting mismatch-induced gain bias. This paper introduces the Unscented KalmanNet (UKN), a hybrid recursive estimator that augments the UKF with two structurally distinct learned components while preserving its explicit sigma-point covariance recursion. NoiseNet predicts time-varying process and measurement covariances as bounded multiplicative corrections to fixed baselines, guaranteeing positive definiteness, while GainNet applies a bounded residual correction to the analytical gain. A calibration-aware training objective combines state error with covariance- and innovation-consistency terms through adaptive weights, jointly optimizing accuracy and calibration. UKN is benchmarked against UKF, KalmanNet, and Bayesian KalmanNet on three synthetic systems and UZH-FPV real-flight data. It achieves the lowest aggregate state-estimation error in all four examples and reduces RMSE by $26.4$-$49.7\%$ compared to UKF in the synthetic cases. Leave-one-sequence-out cross-validation over 11 flights shows $22.4\%$ and $34.3\%$ reductions in mean position and velocity RMSE, respectively. UKN also yields the lowest fold-to-fold variability, with normalized NEES and empirical coverage closest to nominal values among the other filters.

</details>


### [134] [From Non-Convex Self-Concordant Regularization to Scalable Quasi-Newton Training of PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04206)
*Chenhao Si,Kang An,Shiqian Ma,Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCORE方法，一种面向PINN训练的自一致启发式准牛顿优化算法。它利用从学习到的逆度量中计算的单一准牛顿decrement，同时确定候选步长和用于构建下一割线几何的自适应shift，无需构造Hessian或其向量积。在Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、KdV和复Ginzburg-Landau方程上验证了其优于BFGS和自缩放Broyden基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN训练通常需要高精度的准牛顿细化来获得可靠的PDE解，但其残差目标函数可能表现出不定性、近似奇异性以及病态缩放的局部曲率。现有正则化准牛顿方法虽能稳定割线模型，但缺乏对曲率相关步长选择的局部度量规则；自一致方法提供了局部度量规则但尚未与准牛顿框架有效结合。

Method: SCORE将自一致启发与准牛顿方法结合：从学习到的逆度量计算单一准牛顿decrement，该decrement同时决定强Wolfe准则检验的候选步长和用于定义下一割线几何的自适应shift。位移表示沿接受步长的平均移位度量作用，既不需要Hessian构建也不需要Hessian-向量乘积。通过强Wolfe接受、回退线搜索和标准曲率保障实现全局化。

Result: 在局部谱等价条件下，SCORE的准牛顿decrement和候选步长与正移位度量中的对应量保持可比，并在匹配度量情形下恢复了归一化自一致规则。在粘性Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、KdV和复Ginzburg-Landau方程上，SCORE获得了比BFGS和自缩放Broyden基线更低的最终误差。Burgers消融实验表明移位曲率稳定化和基于decrement的步长选择对高精度细化有互补贡献。

Conclusion: SCORE通过将自一致思想的局部度量规则与准牛顿的割线模型稳定机制相结合，为PINN高精度训练提供了新的优化框架。移位曲率稳定与decrement步长选择的互补效应表明，这两种机制分别解决了不定的局部曲率和不可靠的步长尺度问题，值得在更广泛的PDE问题上进一步探索。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often require high-accuracy quasi-Newton refinement to obtain reliable partial differential equation solutions, but their residual objectives can exhibit indefinite, nearly singular, and poorly scaled local curvature. Regularized quasi-Newton methods provide established mechanisms for stabilizing secant models, while self-concordant methods provide local-metric rules for curvature-dependent step selection. Building on these two lines of work, we propose SCORE, a self-concordance-inspired quasi-Newton method with decrement-coupled shifted secant geometry for PINN training. Its distinguishing mechanism is that a single quasi-Newton decrement computed from the learned inverse metric jointly determines a strong-Wolfe-tested candidate step and an adaptive shift used to define the next secant geometry. The shifted displacement represents the action of an averaged shifted metric along the accepted step, while requiring neither Hessian construction nor Hessian-vector products. Under a local spectral-equivalence condition, we show that the quasi-Newton decrement and candidate step remain comparable to their counterparts in a positive shifted metric, and recover the normalized self-concordant rule in the matched-metric case. Strong Wolfe acceptance, fallback line search, and standard curvature safeguards provide globalization without modifying the underlying PINN objective. Experiments on the viscous Burgers, Kuramoto--Sivashinsky, Korteweg--de Vries, and complex Ginzburg--Landau equations show that SCORE attains lower final errors than the tested BFGS and self-scaled Broyden baselines. The Burgers ablation further indicates that shifted curvature stabilization and decrement-based step selection make complementary contributions to high-accuracy refinement.

</details>


### [135] [Attention-Only White-Box Transformer via LeJEPA-Based Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04213)
*Yang Bai,Linyuan Wang,Haoyang Jiang,Nuolin Sun,Libin Hou,Bin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从联合视角重新审视白盒网络推导与自监督学习，基于LeJEPA框架与稀疏率减少目标中R(Z)项的等价性，用ADMM方法将白盒Transformer优化为仅含注意力模块的结构（去掉ISTA结构和MLP层）。该注意力白盒Transformer在CIFAR-10/100上达到88.88%/63.54%的准确率，与原始CRATE（89.18%/63.56%）性能相当，但参数量减少约31%。此外，研究发现标准ViT中用ReLU替换所有MLP块可在知识蒸馏下移除约66%参数并保持竞争性精度，暗示MLP模块可能存在冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将白盒网络的推导（通过优化算法）与自监督学习范式分开处理，本文旨在从联合视角重新审视这两个组件，探索它们之间的内在联系，并进一步减少白盒Transformer的参数量和结构复杂度。

Method: 1) 识别LeJEPA自监督框架中假设的各向同性高斯嵌入分布与稀疏率减少目标中扩展项R(Z)的概念等价性；2) 用LeJEPA范式优化R(Z)项；3) 通过ADMM推导剩余项R^c(Z|U_{[K]})+λ∥Z∥_0，得到纯注意力白盒Transformer（去除ISTA结构和MLP层）；4) 进一步在标准ViT上用ReLU替换所有MLP块进行知识蒸馏实验。

Result: 注意力白盒Transformer在Base规模下，LeJEPA自监督范式下CIFAR-10准确率88.88%、CIFAR-100准确率63.54%，与原始CRATE（89.18%/63.56%）性能接近，参数减少约31%。标准ViT中MLP块全部替换为ReLU后在知识蒸馏下移除约66%参数仍保持竞争性精度。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化自监督学习目标与白盒网络架构推导，可以构建更轻量化的纯注意力白盒Transformer而不损失显著性能，同时标准ViT中MLP模块的冗余性值得进一步探究。

Abstract: Existing studies on self-supervised learning for white-box networks typically decouple the derivation of white-box networks via optimization algorithms from self-supervised learning paradigms. In this work, we instead revisit the two components from a joint perspective. The LeJEPA-based self-supervised framework assumes an isotropic Gaussian distribution as the optimal embedding distribution for downstream tasks, which is conceptually equivalent to the expansion term $R(Z)$ in the sparse rate reduction objective guiding white-box Transformer optimization. Building on this observation, we use the LeJEPA self-supervised paradigm to optimize $R(Z)$, and derive the remaining terms $R^{c}(Z\mid U_{[K]})+λ\lVert Z\rVert_{0}$ via the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) into an attention-only Transformer that dispenses with the ISTA structure or MLP layers of the original design. Experimental results demonstrate that our attention-only white-box Transformer achieves classification accuracies of $88.88\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $63.54\%$ on CIFAR-100 at the Base scale under the LeJEPA self-supervised paradigm, while the original white-box Transformer CRATE achieves classification accuracies of $89.18\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $63.56\%$ on CIFAR-100. Our model achieves competitive performance while reducing the parameter count by roughly $31\%$. Beyond the white-box setting, we further investigate standard ViTs and find that replacing all MLP blocks with ReLU activations under knowledge distillation removes approximately 66\% of the parameters while preserving competitive accuracy, motivating further investigation into the potential redundancy of MLP modules in standard ViT architectures.

</details>


### [136] [Random features for Grassmannian kernel approximation with bounded rank-one projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04227)
*Rémi Delogne,Laurent Jacques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族用于Grassmannian流形（低维子空间）上可扩展核机器的随机特征映射方法。该方法基于子空间投影矩阵的秩一投影和受限非线性变换（周期或二值），使得特征空间中的内积能够以高概率均匀逼近仅依赖于主子角的旋转不变Grassmannian核。相比需要完整Gram矩阵的经典Grassmannian核（如投影核和Binet-Cauchy核），该方法大幅降低了计算和存储成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Grassmannian核（投影核、Binet-Cauchy核）需要计算完整Gram矩阵，在大规模高维子空间数据集上计算和内存开销过大，难以扩展。作者旨在通过随机特征映射绕过对Gram矩阵的显式构造，实现可扩展的Grassmannian核机。

Method: 1) 提出基于秩一投影的随机特征族：对子空间投影矩阵进行秩一随机投影，再施加周期（periodic）或二值（binary）非线性变换以控制结果分布。2) 理论分析证明特征空间内积逼近旋转不变的Grassmannian核，该核仅依赖主子角，且当特征数相对内禀子空间维数足够大时，逼近对所有固定维子空间一致成立（高概率）。3) 周期变换情形下，逼近的核具有闭式表达式，可在逆Binet-Cauchy型与高斯型核之间调节行为。4) 二值变换产生紧凑的一比特子空间特征（无闭式核）。5) 采用基于随机化快速傅里叶变换的结构化秩一投影进一步降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在合成数据和ETH-80分类任务上的实验表明，所提的秩一嵌入特征能够准确保持Grassmannian几何结构，同时显著降低计算、内存和存储开销，验证了其作为经典Grassmannian核的实用可扩展替代方案的效力。

Conclusion: 秩一嵌入为大规模高维子空间数据上的核方法提供了实用且可扩展的替代方案，兼顾了几何保真度与计算效率。周期变换给出可调行为的闭式核逼近，二值变换则提供极紧凑的存储表示，结合结构化投影进一步提升了实际可行性。

Abstract: We propose a family of random feature maps for scalable kernel machines on low-dimensional subspaces, ie on the Grassmannian manifold. Such representations are useful when data classes or clusters are well described by the span of a few samples. Classical Grassmannian kernels, including the projection and Binet-Cauchy kernels, require full Gram matrices, which leads to prohibitive computational and memory costs for large high-dimensional subspace datasets. We address this limitation using random features based on rank-one projections of subspace projection matrices followed by bounded non-linear transforms, either periodic or binary, to control the resulting distributions.
  We show that inner products in the random feature space approximate well-defined rotation-invariant Grassmannian kernels that depend only on the principal angles between subspaces. When the number of features is sufficiently large relative to the intrinsic subspace dimension, the approximation holds uniformly over all fixed-dimensional subspaces with high probability. For periodic transforms, the approximated kernel has a closed-form expression with tunable behaviour between inverse Binet-Cauchy and Gaussian-type regimes. Binary transforms yield compact one-bit subspace features, although no closed-form kernel is known. Structured rank-one projections based on randomised fast Fourier transforms further reduce computation without sacrificing practical accuracy. Experiments on synthetic data and ETH-80 classification tasks show that these features accurately preserve Grassmannian geometry while reducing computation, memory, and storage. Rank-one embeddings therefore provide a practical and scalable alternative to classical Grassmannian kernels.

</details>


### [137] [Transferable Dual-Stream Representations for Mesoscale-Preserving Sea Surface Temperature Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04230)
*Parth Doshi,Priyanka Aravindan,Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EddyFlow是一个面向千米级海表温度降尺度的表示学习框架，通过物理信息约束同时优化预测精度、多尺度结构和区域泛化能力，在零样本和少样本设置下于多个未见区域中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习降尺度模型仅最小化重建误差，导致输出过于平滑，丢失对区域海洋动力学至关重要的中尺度变率；同时像素级目标或单上下文条件限制了谱保真度和跨区域泛化能力。

Method: 提出EddyFlow框架，融合物理信息的表示学习，在圣劳伦斯湾训练，并在芬迪湾和墨西哥湾进行零样本与少样本评估，以平衡预测精度、尺度相关结构和区域泛化。

Result: 零样本RMSE降低21%，在未见域上相对持续性预报的技巧分数最高达85.6%，PSD比值约为1.00，保持近理想谱保真度。

Conclusion: 物理信息引导的表示学习能在保持谱保真度的同时有效提升跨区域降尺度泛化能力，为海洋科学降尺度任务提供了一种更可靠的深度学习范式。

Abstract: Deep learning models for scientific spatio-temporal downscaling often minimize reconstruction error while failing to preserve physically meaningful multi-scale structure. For sea surface temperature prediction, this can yield outputs that are numerically plausible yet overly smooth, missing mesoscale variability critical to regional ocean dynamics. Existing methods often focus on pixel-wise objectives or single-context conditioning, which limits their ability to preserve spectral fidelity and generalize across regions. To address this, we propose EddyFlow, a representation learning framework for kilometer-scale sea surface temperature downscaling that balances predictive accuracy, scale-dependent structure, and regional generalization. EddyFlow is trained on the Gulf of St.~Lawrence and evaluated in zero-shot and few-shot settings on the Bay of Fundy and the Gulf of Mexico. EddyFlow demonstrates that physics-informed representation learning reduces zero-shot RMSE by 21%, achieves up to 85.6% skill relative to persistence on unseen domains, and maintains near-ideal spectral fidelity with a PSD ratio of $\approx 1.00$.

</details>


### [138] [Physics-informed reduced-order modelling with equivariant spectral submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04239)
*Georg Maierhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等变谱子流形（eSSM）约简方法，将全阶模型的对称性显式融入谱子流形（SSM）约简框架中，通过利用系统对称性显著加速高维非线性系统的降阶模型计算，同时提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM约简虽比DMD等线性方法更可靠，但其计算成本在高维系统中仍十分昂贵，因此需要一种能够利用系统固有对称性来加速计算的方法。

Method: 首先证明SSM是天然的等变子流形，且相关的坐标卡和降阶动力学继承相应的诱导群作用；在此基础上开发等变SSM约简算法，利用对称性实现更快的计算并提升模型鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个基准问题（包括科学共同任务框架CTF中的测试）上验证了该方法的优势，实现了更快的计算速度和更高的模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 等变SSM约简是一种新颖的SSM框架扩展，通过显式利用全阶模型的对称性，为高维非线性系统的可靠约简提供了更高效、更鲁棒的路径。

Abstract: Spectral submanifold (SSM) reduction has emerged as a mathematically principled route to reliable nonlinear reduced-order models, capturing dynamics beyond the reach of linear techniques such as Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD). The computation of SSMs, however, remains computationally expensive, particularly for high-dimensional systems. In this work, we introduce equivariant spectral submanifold (eSSM) reduction, a novel extension of the SSM framework that explicitly incorporates symmetries of the full-order model into the reduction process. We establish the mathematical foundations of this approach by showing that SSMs are naturally equivariant submanifolds and that the associated charts and reduced dynamics inherit the appropriate induced group actions. Building on this framework, we develop a novel equivariant SSM reduction algorithm that exploits these symmetries to achieve substantially faster computations while also improving model robustness. We demonstrate the advantages of this approach on several benchmark problems including a test from the Common Task Framework for Science.

</details>


### [139] [Attention-based representations for multi-task computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04243)
*Daniel Hsu,Mingyue Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多头注意力层在完成多个下游任务时所需头数的下界与构造。在第一个场景中，证明单头注意力需要指数级更高的嵌入维度或精度才能同时计算列表中的最小值和最大值，而两个头即可高效完成。在第二个场景中，证明了n位XOR函数需要"头数×多项式度数"的乘积至少为n，并构造了达到此下界的多头注意力层，结果推广到任意对称布尔函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力层能够产生支持多个下游任务的向量表示，但关于需要多少个头才能高效支持多任务的问题缺乏理论保证。此前已知两个头可以以较小的嵌入维度和精度完成特定任务，但单头是否可行、多任务的一般性下界是什么尚不清楚，本文旨在建立这些界限。

Method: 本文采用理论分析的方法，针对两类具体的多任务场景建立注意力头数的下界与上界。第一类场景通过注意力头的向量表示支持线性预测器同时计算列表的最大值和最小值，证明单头需要指数级更高的嵌入维度或位精度。第二类场景研究多项式阈值函数计算n位XOR，建立头数×多项式度数≥n的下界，并构造达到该下界的多头注意力层，并推广到对称布尔函数（以阈值度数为界）。

Result: 证明了两头注意力在低嵌入维度和小位精度下即可完成最大/最小值计算，而单头需要指数级更高的资源；对于n位XOR，给出了"头数×多项式度数≥n"的必要条件，并构造了匹配该下界的注意力层。这些结果进一步推广到了任意对称布尔函数，其界限由阈值度数决定。

Conclusion: 多头注意力层的头数在表示能力上具有本质意义：增加头数可以指数级降低嵌入维度或精度需求，且多任务表示能力与多项式度数之间存在精确的权衡关系（乘积下界）。这为理解注意力机制的多任务表示能力提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Multi-head attention layers produce vector representations that support multiple downstream tasks. We establish bounds on the number of heads required in two simple and concrete multi-task scenarios. In the first scenario, a vector representation is sought so that linear predictors can compute both the smallest and largest numbers in a given list. In this case, it is known two attention heads with small embedding dimension and bit precision level suffice. We prove that a single attention head requires exponentially higher embedding dimension or precision level. In the second scenario, a vector representation is sought so that a polynomial threshold function can compute the XOR of a given string of $n$ bits. This scenario is analogous to the first one for $n=2$, since XOR is readily computed by a linear function using a vector representation that encodes both the AND and the OR of the two bits. We observe that $n$-bit XOR requires the product of the number of heads and the polynomial degree to be at least $n$, and we construct multi-head attention layers that match this lower bound. These results generalize to arbitrary (symmetric) Boolean functions, where the bound is given in terms of the threshold degree.

</details>


### [140] [Geometry-Informed Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Pre-trained Molecular GNNs for Blood-Brain Barrier Permeability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04257)
*Marco Vieto Vega,Long D. Nguyen,Binh P. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BBBP-GeoPEFT，一种基于几何信息的参数高效微调框架，用于预训练分子图神经网络的血脑屏障渗透性预测。通过多截断距离图和线图编码空间原子与二阶边交互，仅更新10.1%的参数即达到与全量微调相当的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBB渗透性预测是中枢神经系统药物发现中的关键筛选任务，但面临数据量有限、类别不平衡和分子结构敏感等挑战。虽然预训练分子GNN提供了可迁移知识，但全量微调参数效率低且易过拟合，现有PEFT方法主要适配节点特征或二维共价图，无法捕获三维几何构象和第二阶交互信息。

Method: BBBP-GeoPEFT框架包含：(1) 从分子构象构建多个截断距离下的距离图和对应的线图，以捕获空间原子交互和二阶边交互；(2) 轻量级辅助几何图编码器生成截断特定的表示；(3) 通过节点级截断注意力机制和门控残差连接，将几何信息注入预训练模型的每一层；(4) 保持预训练知识的同时仅更新少量可训练参数。

Result: 在整理的BBBP数据集上，BBBP-GeoPEFT在随机划分和骨架划分下均取得了与全量微调及代表性PEFT基线相当或更优的ROC-AUC和准确率，且仅更新模型参数的10.1%。

Conclusion: 几何信息增强的PEFT策略能够以极低的参数预算有效提升BBB渗透性预测性能，验证了将三维几何信息和二阶交互编码融入预训练GNN微调过程的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Blood-brain barrier permeability (BBBP) prediction is a critical screening task in central nervous system drug discovery, where candidate molecules must be assessed for whether they can cross, or should be prevented from crossing, the blood-brain barrier. However, this task remains challenging because of limited, class-imbalanced datasets and sensitivity to molecular structure. Recent advances in deep learning have established graph neural networks (GNNs) as a powerful approach for molecular representation learning, while pre-trained molecular GNNs provide transferable knowledge for downstream tasks. However, full fine-tuning is often parameter-inefficient and prone to overfitting, whereas existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods mainly adapt node features or the two-dimensional covalent graph, limiting their ability to capture three-dimensional geometry and second-order interactions. To address these limitations, we propose BBBP-GeoPEFT, a geometry-informed PEFT framework for pre-trained molecular GNNs. BBBP-GeoPEFT constructs distance-based graphs at multiple cutoffs and their corresponding line graphs from molecular conformers to capture spatial atom and second-order edge interactions. Lightweight auxiliary geometric graph encoders generate cutoff-specific representations, which are incorporated into each pre-trained layer through node-wise cutoff attention and gated residual connections. This design preserves pre-trained knowledge while incorporating permeability-relevant geometric information with a small trainable-parameter budget. Experiments on a curated BBBP dataset show that BBBP-GeoPEFT achieves competitive performance compared with full fine-tuning and representative PEFT baselines. Under both random and scaffold splitting, BBBP-GeoPEFT achieves competitive or improved ROC-AUC and accuracy in most experiments while updating only 10.1% of the model parameters.

</details>


### [141] [Sample Complexity of Multicalibration for Multilevel Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04288)
*Jiuyao Lu,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Aleksandr Podkopaev,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多校准（multicalibration）在序列属性（sequential properties）上的样本复杂度，其中每个属性在前序属性固定后可被识别（包括方差、偏度、CVaR等）。作者建立了匹配的上下界：对于任意k≥2，达到ε多校准误差需要Ω̃(ε^{-(k+2)})样本；同时给出了使用O(ε^{-(k+2)}+ε^{-2}log|G|)样本的随机学习器，证明多项式规模群体族的样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-(k+2)})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实预测任务中常需同时预测同一条件分布多个相关属性（如均值、方差、偏度、分位数等），这些属性之间存在嵌套依赖关系（方差依赖均值、偏度依赖均值和方差）。现有多校准理论主要针对单一属性或Bayes对，缺乏对序列属性关系的系统研究，且其样本复杂度特征尚不明确。

Method: 作者提出了一个统一的序列属性多校准框架，其中每个属性在前序属性固定后可识别（不要求由单一损失函数导出）。通过组合学习（composition learning）的理论分析，建立了样本复杂度的匹配上下界：下界通过构造多项式级别二元群体（仅polylog个群）证明需要Ω̃(ε^{-(k+2)})样本；上界则给出一个随机化学习器，样本复杂度为O(ε^{-(k+2)}+ε^{-2}log|G|)。

Result: 对于任意固定k≥2，在正则条件下，多校准误差ε所需的样本复杂度匹配至对数因子：下界为Ω̃(ε^{-(k+2)})（即使仅有polylog多个二元群体），上界为O(ε^{-(k+2)}+ε^{-2}log|G|)。对于多项式规模群体族，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-(k+2)})。该理论在三个规范示例（方差、偏度、条件风险价值）上得到具体验证。

Conclusion: 序列属性的多校准样本复杂度随属性数量k呈现ε^{-(k+2)}的指数级增长，说明校准的属性维度越高，所需样本越多。框架比Bayes对更通用（不要求单一损失来源），为多属性预测的样本复杂度提供了精确刻画。

Abstract: Calibration requires a predictor to be unbiased after conditioning on its own predictions. Multicalibration asks for this guarantee simultaneously across a collection of groups. Many prediction tasks ask for several related features of the same conditional outcome distribution: variance is defined relative to the mean, skewness relative to both mean and variance, and conditional value at risk relative to a quantile. We study multicalibration for a sequence of $k$ properties in which each property is identifiable once the preceding properties are fixed. This framework includes Bayes pairs but does not require the properties to arise from a single loss.
  For every fixed $k\ge2$, we establish matching upper and lower sample-complexity bounds up to logarithmic factors under regularity conditions. Even with only polylogarithmically many binary groups, achieving multicalibration error $\varepsilon$ requires $\widetildeΩ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ samples. Conversely, for any finite group family $\mathcal G$, we give a randomized learner using $O(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)}+\varepsilon^{-2}\log|\mathcal G|)$ samples. Thus the sample complexity is $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ for polynomial-size group families. We instantiate the theory for three canonical examples.

</details>


### [142] [Adaptive Finite-Budget Training for CVaR Risk-Aware Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04305)
*Yifan Wu,Junjie Lei,Wenjie Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应训练控制器，用于条件风险价值（CVaR）风险感知Q学习（RaQL），在不改变原有CVaR估计器和Bellman不动点的前提下，通过六种协调机制重新设计训练过程。在每日比特币交易任务中，该方法将经验CVaR Bellman残差降低约85%，并在交易成本后实现了0.9281的Sharpe比率和6.46%的最大回撤，显著改善了风险调整后收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准风险感知Q学习（RaQL）的有限预算行为脆弱：固定的内循环超参数会导致不稳定的价值估计、持续的Bellman残差和低效的样本复用，这限制了其在金融等实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 提出自适应训练控制器，通过六个协调机制重新设计训练流程：（1）逐单元内步长调整；（2）外部速率匹配的衰减同步；（3）对类似VaR的内部变量进行早终止修正；（4）覆盖优先然后贪心的样本分配规则；（5）成熟内部估计的渐进后缀聚合；（6）基于在线可观测量的数据驱动关键尺度校准。该方法保留了原始的CVaR估计器和Bellman不动点不变。

Result: 在20个随机种子和856,000个内部转移样本上，该方法将经验CVaR Bellman残差从MeanBEQ 1.2202降至0.1854（约85%），MeanBEV从1.1624降至0.0535，并在不同CVaR水平、折扣因子和训练预算下保持稳定性。在时序外样本测试中，策略实现Sharpe比率0.9281和最大回撤6.46%（扣除交易成本后），波动率9.57%远低于买入持有策略的47.93%。

Conclusion: 仅通过训练过程的适应性设计而不改变风险目标本身，即可显著提高风险感知Q学习在金融应用中的可靠性和风险调整后性能，对抗了固定超参数基线在有限预算下的脆弱性。

Abstract: Risk-aware Q-learning (RaQL) provides a model-free, two-timescale estimator for dynamic risk objectives, but its finite-budget behavior remains fragile: fixed inner-loop hyperparameters can produce unstable value estimates, persistent Bellman residuals, and inefficient sample reuse. This paper proposes an adaptive training controller for Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) RaQL and evaluates it on a daily Bitcoin trading task. The controller preserves the original CVaR estimator and Bellman fixed point; instead, it redesigns the training procedure through six coordinated mechanisms: per-cell inner-step sizing, outer-rate-matched decay synchronization, a short early correction for the VaR-like inner variable, a coverage-first-then-greedy sample allocation rule, progressive suffix aggregation of mature inner estimates, and data-driven calibration of key scales from online-observable quantities. Across 20 random seeds and 856,000 inner-transition samples, the controller reduces the mean empirical CVaR Bellman residual by approximately 85% relative to the fixed-parameter baseline (MeanBEQ: 1.2202 to 0.1854; MeanBEV: 1.1624 to 0.0535) and maintains stability across CVaR levels, discount factors, and training budgets. On the chronological out-of-sample test set, the learned policy attains a Sharpe ratio of 0.9281 with a maximum drawdown of 6.46% after transaction costs. Although buy-and-hold yields a higher cumulative return (35.43% vs. 23.61%), the adaptive policy achieves far lower volatility (9.57% vs. 47.93%), drawdown, and CVaR loss. These results demonstrate that adaptive finite-budget training design, applied solely to the training procedure without altering the risk objective, can materially improve the reliability and risk-adjusted performance of risk-aware Q-learning in financial applications.

</details>


### [143] [ArborEnum: Decision Tree Rashomon Sets over Continuous Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04310)
*Zakk Heile,Hayden McTavish,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能够在连续特征上精确枚举决策树Rashomon集的算法，并开发了近似枚举的松弛方法和逐步细化阈值的任意时间算法，相比现有方法实现了数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rashomon集（在正则化损失上接近最优的所有模型集合）对解释性、鲁棒性和自定义化具有重要意义。决策树是最少数能被完全枚举Rashomon集的模型类别之一，但现有方法受限于对原始数据的二值化处理，这会限制分裂选择或显著增加组合问题的复杂度，无法充分利用连续特征的有序结构。

Method: 提出了三个核心算法：(1) 首个利用连续特征有序结构精确枚举决策树Rashomon集的算法；(2) 用于近似枚举的松弛方法；(3) 任意时间算法，能够逐步细化候选阈值集合，产生的近似结果逐步收敛到连续特征Rashomon集。

Result: 实验表明，粗粒度的二值化会遗漏大量树、重要特征和预测多样性；所提算法相比现有枚举方法实现了数量级的加速，近似方法在保持近乎完美召回率的同时进一步提升了速度。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了连续特征下决策树Rashomon集精确枚举的空白，证明利用连续特征的有序结构可以显著提升Rashomon集计算效率，为鲁棒性和可解释性分析提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: The Rashomon effect describes the phenomenon that many models can achieve nearly equivalent performance on the same learning task, with significant ramifications for robustness, feature importance, and customizability. These use cases motivate the computation of Rashomon sets: the set of all models whose regularized loss is near-optimal. Decision trees are one of the few model classes for which Rashomon sets can be fully enumerated, but this computation has always been conditional on a binarization of the original data, either restricting which splits each tree is allowed to make or substantially increasing the complexity of an already difficult combinatorial problem. We introduce the first algorithm that exactly enumerates decision-tree Rashomon sets while exploiting the ordered structure of continuous features. We further develop a relaxation for approximate enumeration and an anytime algorithm that progressively refines the set of candidate thresholds, producing increasingly detailed approximations that converge to the continuous-feature Rashomon set. Experiments show that coarse binarization can miss many trees, important features, and predictive multiplicity; our algorithms achieve orders-of-magnitude speedups over existing enumeration methods, with approximations providing further speedups while maintaining near-perfect recall.

</details>


### [144] [Efficient Online Lexicographic Generalized Low-Rank Matrix Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04324)
*Bo Xue,Ji Cheng,Haodong Jing,Hongzong Li,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lexi-LowGLM算法，用于求解具有多个优先级目标的广义低秩矩阵老虎机问题，采用在线牛顿步替换批量估计器，将估计器更新复杂度从O(T²)降至O(T)，并建立了依赖于有效低秩维度(d₁+d₂)r而非环境维度d₁d₂的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩矩阵老虎机研究主要针对单目标场景，但现实应用中往往存在多个具有不同优先级的并列目标（如多目标推荐系统）。同时，已有的单目标算法需要反复求解批量的广义线性估计器，计算复杂度高。因此需要设计既能处理多优先级目标又具备计算高效性的算法。

Method: 提出Lexi-LowGLM算法，包含两个阶段：首先估计各目标特定的低秩子空间，然后在降维后的特征空间中进行字典序学习。每个目标对应的估计器通过在线牛顿步（online Newton step）更新，而非使用所有历史观测反复求解批量GLM估计器，从而显著降低计算复杂度。

Result: 每个目标i的遗憾界为Õ(W_i^lex·√m·(d₁+d₂)r·√T)，其中W_i^lex描述字典序权衡效应。遗憾界依赖于有效低秩维度(d₁+d₂)r而非环境维度d₁d₂。估计器更新复杂度从O(T²)降低到O(T)。数值实验验证了方法的有效性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将广义低秩矩阵老虎机拓展到多优先级目标设定的字典序学习框架，在理论上实现了与低秩维度相关的遗憾界，在计算上将更新复杂度提升了一个数量级，为多目标在线决策问题提供了高效且可理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper studies generalized low-rank matrix bandits with multiple prioritized objectives. At each round, the learner selects a matrix-valued arm and observes a vector-valued reward, whose components correspond to multiple objectives with different priority levels. Each objective is governed by an objective-specific generalized low-rank matrix model, and the learner evaluates arms according to a lexicographic preference order, prioritizing higher-level objectives before lower-level ones. We propose \textsc{Lexi-LowGLM}, an efficient online algorithm that first estimates objective-specific low-rank subspaces and then performs lexicographic learning in the reduced feature spaces. Unlike existing single-objective algorithms that repeatedly solve a batch generalized linear estimator using all historical observations, \textsc{Lexi-LowGLM} updates each objective-specific estimator via an online Newton step, reducing the estimator-update complexity over $T$ rounds from $O(T^2)$ to $O(T)$. We establish a regret bound of $\widetilde O\left(W_i^{\rm lex}\sqrt{m}\,(d_1+d_2)r\sqrt{T}\right)$ for each objective $i\in[m]$, where $r$ is an upper bound on the ranks of the objective-specific parameter matrices and $W_i^{\rm lex}$ characterizes the lexicographic trade-off effect. This bound depends on the effective low-rank dimension $(d_1+d_2)r$ rather than the ambient dimension $d_1d_2$. Numerical experiments further validate the effectiveness and computational efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [145] [Real-time probabilistic tsunami forecasting via generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04327)
*Yusuke Oishi,Takashi Furumura,Fumihiko Imamura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件扩散模型（生成式AI）的概率集成模型，用于近场海啸的陆上淹没预测。该模型在校准精度与准确度之间取得平衡，通过2011年东北地方太平洋近海地震数据进行验证，能够随震后时间推移忠实追踪不确定性的衰减，同时准确预测淹没深度和范围，将海啸预警从确定性范式转向概率性范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性海啸淹没预测在高不确定性条件下（如巨型逆冲型地震引发的近场海啸）可能给出虚假的安全边界，导致公众误判。现有机器学习方法虽能实现即时淹没预测，但仅输出确定性结果，缺乏不确定性量化，无法支撑可靠的风险决策。因此需要一种既能保持精度又能提供校准不确定性评估的预测框架。

Method: 提出基于条件扩散模型的概率集成方法。该生成式AI模型通过条件化机制将地震与海啸源参数作为条件输入，生成淹没深度与范围的概率分布样本。通过在多个采样结果间集成以获得后验不确定性估计，同时保持预测精度（校准与准确性之间的权衡）。（基于论文摘要可推断其核心方法为条件扩散概率模型）。

Result: 利用2011年东北地方太平洋近海地震数据进行验证，模型能够准确预测淹没深度与淹没范围，并随着震后时间推进，忠实追踪并反映不确定性的逐步衰减过程。模型同时满足预测精度与统计校准要求。

Conclusion: 该研究表明生成式AI能够将海啸预报从确定性范式转向概率性范式，为下一代海啸早期预警系统提供了方法论基础。概率性淹没预测有望在保持公众安全警觉的同时，避免确定性边界带来的误导性安全隐患。

Abstract: Explicit onshore tsunami inundation forecasting can improve public risk awareness, but deterministically predicted inundation boundaries under highly uncertain conditions, such as near-field tsunamis generated by megathrust earthquakes, may falsely imply safety outside the boundaries. Consequently, current warnings primarily target coastal tsunami height, not onshore inundation. Although machine learning enables instant inundation predictions, they remain deterministic, lacking uncertainty quantification. Here, we develop a probabilistic ensemble model based on a conditional diffusion model (a type of generative AI) that reconciles accuracy with calibration. Validated with the 2011 Tohoku-oki earthquake data, our model faithfully tracks the postearthquake uncertainty decreasing over time while accurately predicting inundation depth and extent. Our framework shows that generative AI can shift tsunami forecasting from determinism to probabilism, providing a foundation for next-generation early warning.

</details>


### [146] [Learning When to Stop: Prefix-Optimal Dynamic Diffusion Policies for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05084)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Manoj Saravanan,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出前缀最优生成策略（POGP），通过在去噪链的每个中间步骤学习前缀值函数（基于Bellman风格的递归），实现自适应早期停止去噪，在保持任务性能的同时将去噪迭代次数减少约2.7倍，并在动态扩散基线基础上提升约3.5%的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在连续控制中表现强大，但其迭代去噪过程造成显著的计算瓶颈。减少计算成本需要根据每个动作的难度自适应调整去噪步数，同时保持任务性能，而现有方法缺乏有效的中间步骤监督和停止准则。

Method: POGP框架在去噪链的每个中间步骤学习前缀值函数，通过Bellman风格的递归进行训练。前缀值函数有两个用途：(1) 作为辅助训练目标，鼓励中间输出成为高质量动作；(2) 作为测试时的停止规则，当额外去噪步骤不太可能带来有意义的改进时终止去噪过程。

Result: 在四个MuJoCo环境和12个基线的比较中，POGP将所需去噪迭代次数减少约2.7倍，同时保持接近完整的任务性能。与最先进的动态扩散基线相比，前缀训练还使最终任务性能提升约3.5%。

Conclusion: 监督中间去噪步骤不仅是实现自适应早期停止的有用工具，还可以作为辅助目标来改进学习到的策略，这为扩散策略的计算效率优化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are a powerful policy class for continuous control, but their iterative denoising process creates a substantial computational bottleneck. Reducing this cost requires adapting the number of denoising steps to the difficulty of each action while preserving task performance. We introduce Prefix-Optimal Generative Policies (POGP), a framework that learns a prefix value function at every intermediate denoising step through a Bellman-style recursion over the denoising chain. The prefix value function serves two purposes: it provides an auxiliary training objective that encourages intermediate outputs to become high-quality actions, and it enables a test-time stopping rule that terminates denoising when additional steps are unlikely to produce meaningful improvement. Across four MuJoCo environments and comparisons with 12 baselines, POGP reduces the required number of denoising iterations by approximately 2.7-fold while retaining near-full task performance. Compared with state-of-the-art dynamic diffusion baselines, prefix training also improves final task performance by approximately 3.5%. These results indicate that supervising intermediate denoising steps is useful not only for adaptive early stopping, but also as an auxiliary objective that improves the learned policy.

</details>


### [147] [Cost-Aware Multi-Objective Bandits: Theory and Application to Budgeted LLM Configuration Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04333)
*Bo Xue,Zhi Hong,Jiayi Li,Yuanyu Wan,Ji Cheng,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将大规模语言模型（LLM）配置评估形式化为成本感知的多目标老虎机（bandit）问题，并针对在线配置选择和Pareto配置识别两个核心问题，提出了基于超体积（hypervolume）的UCB算法和成本感知的经验间隙消除算法，分别实现了对数级遗憾界和指数级衰减的错误概率，并通过实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM配置评估面临评估预算有限、评估成本随配置不同而变化以及多个相互竞争的优化目标等挑战。现有的评估方法未能统一处理这些因素，需要一种能够兼顾成本和多目标特性的形式化框架来高效地进行LLM配置的选择与识别。

Method: 1) 将LLM配置评估建模为成本感知的多目标老虎机问题，每个配置评估产生依赖于配置的成本和带噪的向量值回报；2) 针对在线配置选择，提出基于超体积的UCB算法，优化一个乐观的超体积-成本效率指标；3) 针对固定预算Pareto识别，开发了成本感知的经验间隙消除算法。

Result: 1) 在线配置选择算法实现了O(Σ_{i≠i*} log B / Δ_i)的预算遗憾界，保留了经典单目标预算老虎机的对数预算依赖性；2) Pareto识别算法实现了O(exp(−B/H_{μ,c}))的错误概率，随评估预算呈指数衰减，且当所有配置成本相同时可恢复标准Pareto集识别保证；3) 在LLM配置评估任务上的实验验证了框架在有限预算下实现高效在线决策和准确成本感知Pareto识别的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM配置评估提供了统一的理论框架和算法保障，在理论上实现了对数级遗憾和指数级错误概率衰减，在实践中证明了其在有限预算下进行高效决策和准确Pareto识别的能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) configuration evaluation is challenging due to limited evaluation budgets, varying costs, and multiple competing objectives. In this paper, we formulate LLM configuration evaluation as a cost-aware multi-objective bandit problem, where each configuration evaluation incurs a configuration-dependent cost and yields a noisy vector-valued outcome. Under this framework, we study two fundamental problems: online configuration selection and Pareto configuration identification. For online configuration selection, we propose a hypervolume-based UCB algorithm that optimizes an optimistic hypervolume-per-cost index. We establish a budgeted regret bound of order $O\bigl(\sum_{i\ne i^\star}\frac{\log B}{Δ_i}\bigr)$, where $B$ is the evaluation budget, $i^\star$ is the optimal configuration in terms of hypervolume efficiency, and $Δ_i$ is the corresponding efficiency gap of configuration $i$. This bound retains the logarithmic budget dependence of classical single-objective budgeted bandits. For fixed-budget Pareto identification, we develop a cost-aware empirical gap elimination algorithm and prove that its error probability is of order $O\bigl(\exp(-\frac{B}{H_{μ,c}})\bigr)$, where $H_{μ,c}$ is a cost-aware Pareto identification complexity depending on configuration costs and Pareto classification gaps. This error probability decays exponentially with the evaluation budget and recovers the standard Pareto set identification guarantee when all configuration costs are identical. Experiments on LLM configuration evaluation tasks demonstrate that the proposed framework enables efficient online decision-making and accurate cost-aware Pareto identification under limited budgets.

</details>


### [148] [ATLAS: Adaptive Topological Learning with Abstract Successors for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04334)
*R. Blake Lawlor,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS框架，结合Grow When Required网络与Successor Features，在实现高样本效率的同时，通过将转移动态与奖励信号结构解耦，实现了对非平稳环境中新目标的近瞬时适应和正向反向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无模型强化学习算法性能高但样本效率低且对环境变化不鲁棒，而基于模型的算法样本效率较高但在环境漂移时仍会失效。论文旨在同时解决样本效率低和灾难性遗忘两大挑战。

Method: ATLAS采用Grow When Required（GWR）网络与Successor Features相结合，将转移动态从奖励信号中结构性地解耦，从而实现对环境变化的高效适应。

Result: 在空间导航任务中，ATLAS在非平稳环境下显著优于常见的on-policy和off-policy基线算法，实现了近瞬时适应新目标的能力，并表现出正向反向迁移（positive backward transfer）。

Conclusion: 通过结构解耦转移动态与奖励信号，ATLAS在保持高样本效率的同时增强了非平稳环境下的鲁棒性，为应对强化学习中的样本效率与灾难性遗忘问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Contemporary model-free reinforcement learning algorithms can achieve very high performance, but have low sample efficiency and are not robust to changes in the environment. Model-based algorithms have much higher sample efficiency, but still fail when the environment shifts. This paper introduces Adaptive Topological Learning with Abstract Successors (ATLAS) to combat these challenges. ATLAS uses a Grow When Required network with Successor Features in order to achieve high sample efficiency while also robustly tackling catastrophic forgetting. We evaluate ATLAS in spatial navigation tasks, benchmarking its performance against common on-policy and off-policy algorithms. Our empirical results demonstrate that by structurally decoupling transition dynamics from the reward signal, ATLAS achieves near-instantaneous adaptation to new goals and can exhibit positive backward transfer, significantly outperforming baseline methods in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [149] [Looking in the Mirror: Introspecting Side-Effect Misalignments Induced by Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04347)
*Kotaro Yoshida,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Yukinori Yamamoto,Yuji Naraki,Ryotaro Shimizu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"副作用内省"（side-effect introspection）这一新问题设置，针对微调导致的非预期对齐退化（而非显式植入的行为）进行内省分析，并构建了相应的数据集。同时提出Delta-Aware内省适配器（DAIA），通过显式处理基座模型激活与微调引起的激活差异来增强对内部模型变化的敏感性。实验表明内省学习可泛化到未见过的微调模型和安全类别，且DAIA显著优于现有内省适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRA的内省适配器（IA）研究主要针对显式植入特定行为的数据集进行微调后解释植入行为，这与实际部署场景不符。实际部署中核心关注的是副作用不对齐（side-effect misalignment），即在与安全或对齐无关的任务上微调时导致的非预期对齐退化。现有方法无法有效处理这一问题设置。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了"副作用内省"（side-effect introspection）这一新问题设置；2) 构建了针对该设置的数据集；3) 提出Delta-Aware内省适配器（DAIA），该机制显式处理基座模型激活和微调引起的激活差异，增强对模型内部变化的敏感性。

Result: 实验评估表明：内省学习能够泛化到未见过的微调模型和安全类别；DAIA在性能上一致优于现有内省适配器。

Conclusion: 本文填补了内省学习研究与实际部署场景之间的空白，将内省分析从显式植入行为扩展到微调导致的副作用对齐退化，并通过DAIA显著提升了对内部模型变化的感知能力，为更实用的模型对齐监控提供了新方向。

Abstract: Fine-tuning enables a source model to acquire desired capabilities and behaviors in a target domain while retaining much of its general-purpose competence. However, this adaptation process can also degrade alignment properties that were present in the source model. Recent work has shown that large language models can be trained using LoRA-based modules known as introspection adapters (IAs) to describe behavioral changes induced by fine-tuning. However, existing studies primarily consider settings in which the model is fine-tuned on datasets explicitly designed to implant a specific behavior and is then asked to explain the implanted behavior. This differs from practical deployment scenarios, where the central concern is often side-effect misalignment: unintended degradation of alignment caused by fine-tuning on tasks that are not obviously related to safety or alignment. To bridge this gap, we formulate a novel problem setting called \emph{side-effect introspection}, in which the target of introspection is not a behavior explicitly implanted through fine-tuning, but rather alignment shifts that emerge as unintended side effects, and we construct a dataset for this setting. Furthermore, to enhance sensitivity to internal model changes, we propose the Delta-Aware Introspection Adapter (DAIA), a novel mechanism designed to explicitly process both base-model activations and activation differences induced by fine-tuning. Our empirical evaluation shows that introspection learning generalizes to unseen fine-tuned models and safety categories, and that DAIA consistently outperforms existing introspection adapters.

</details>


### [150] [Manipulation-Proof Oblivious Audits against Deceptive Model Providers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04365)
*Augustin Godinot,Sofiane Azogagh,Julien Ferry,Sébastien Gambs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于隐私信息检索(PIR)的新型审计协议，使审计者能够以 oblivious 方式查询模型，显著提高审计后对操纵行为的可检测性，防止模型提供者在公平性审计中策略性地操纵结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法审计是算法治理的关键手段，但在监管场景中审计通常可被检测，导致模型提供者可以操纵审计过程（如策略性地均衡不同群体间的分配率以满足公平性指标），从而规避公平性审查。现有审计方法的脆弱性使得确保审计完整性成为一个挑战性问题。

Method: 引入了基于私有信息检索（PIR）机制的审计协议，使审计者能够以 oblivious 方式查询模型：要求提供者标注大量实例，但阻止其知晓哪些子集将最终用于审计。该协议高效、对审计者施加最小开销，且无需修改被审计模型、训练过程或推理管线，并提供了理论保证。

Result: 理论证明表明，在该协议下，试图隐藏不公平性的提供者必须伪造显著更大数量的响应，从而同时增加了操纵的难度和被检测的可能性。实验覆盖多种代表性审计场景，验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实用的、可部署的审计协议，通过在审计过程中引入 obliviousness 来显著增强后审计阶段操纵检测能力，为算法公平性治理提供了新的工具和理论依据。

Abstract: Audits have emerged as a critical instrument for algorithmic governance, providing a mechanism for external scrutiny and governance of machine learning models. However, ensuring the integrity of such assessments remains a challenging issue. For instance in regulatory contexts, audits are typically declared or easily detected, thus enabling model providers to manipulate the process, whether intentionally or inadvertently. This vulnerability is particularly acute in the context of fairness evaluations, in which providers can often infer sensitive attributes and strategically equalize allocation rates between groups to satisfy fairness metrics.
  In this paper, we introduce a novel audit protocol designed to significantly increase the post-audit detectability of such manipulations by enabling the auditor to query the model in an oblivious manner. Our approach leverages a Private Information Retrieval mechanism to require the provider to label a large set of instances, while preventing it from knowing which subset will ultimately be used for the audit. The protocol is efficient, imposes minimal overhead on the auditor, and requires no modification to the audited model, its training procedure, or its inference pipeline. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that, under this protocol, a provider attempting to hide unfairness must falsify a significantly larger number of responses, thereby increasing both the difficulty and the likelihood of detection of manipulation. Experimental results across representative audit scenarios confirm the effectiveness and practicality of our approach.

</details>


### [151] [EvtGraph: Event-Adaptive Compression for Sparse Temporal Graph Learning in Multimodal Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04368)
*Ziqian Wang,Tingxiong Xiao,Yuxiao Cheng,Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvtGraph，一个统一的预算约束框架，通过事件自适应压缩（EAMC）、节点预算选择（NBC）和时序约束稀疏图推理（T2SG）将多模态时序数据转化为事件级token上的结构化计算，在固定预算下分配表示容量，实现性能与效率的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态时序数据天然具有不规则性和不均匀的信息密度，但现有模型依赖均匀离散化，导致表示效率低下。需要一种能将计算与时间显著性对齐、在显式预算约束下工作的统一框架。

Method: EvtGraph包含三个核心组件：1）事件自适应压缩（EAMC），将序列重参数化为事件级token；2）节点预算选择（NBC），在预算约束下选取紧凑子集；3）时序约束稀疏图推理（T2SG），在受限时序条件下执行稀疏图计算。整体将密集序列转化为对显著事件的结构化计算。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV+CXR多模态临床数据和跨域基准上，EvtGraph在显著提升效率的同时，性能优于基于Transformer和循环神经网络（RNN）的基线模型。且较小的预算在实际中通常就足够，展示了稳定的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 预算约束的、以事件为中心的表示学习为处理高冗余时序数据提供了一种通用范式，能够在固定计算预算下有效分配表示容量，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Multimodal temporal data are inherently irregular and uneven in information density, yet most models rely on uniform discretization, leading to inefficient representations.
  We propose \textbf{EvtGraph}, a unified framework that aligns computation with temporal salience under explicit budget constraints. EvtGraph reparameterizes sequences into event-level tokens via event-adaptive compression (EAMC), selects a compact subset with a node budget (NBC), and performs temporally constrained sparse graph reasoning (T2SG). This transforms dense sequences into structured computation over salient events, reducing complexity while preserving critical transitions.
  We show that this design provides a practical mechanism for allocating representational capacity under a fixed budget, yielding a consistent performance--efficiency trade-off, where a small budget is often sufficient in practice. Experiments on multimodal clinical (MIMIC-IV + CXR) and cross-domain benchmarks demonstrate that EvtGraph outperforms both Transformer-based and recurrent baselines while significantly improving efficiency.
  These results suggest that budget-constrained event-centric representation provides a general paradigm for learning from high-redundancy temporal data.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Trustworthy Hypergraph Neural Networks under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04377)
*Mengyao Zhou,Zhiheng Zhou,Xiao Han,Guiying Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了超图节点分类中的标签噪声问题，提出了名为HyperTrust的鲁棒框架，通过基于预训练与熵感知的策略估计超边可信度，并利用HyperedgeBoost和HyperedgePrune两个模块协同调整超图结构以增强可靠监督、抑制噪声传播，在多种噪声设置下验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图神经网络（HGNNs）在复杂高阶关系处理上表现出色，但其性能高度依赖带标签数据，对标签噪声十分脆弱。尽管LLN和GLN领域已有进展，超图上的噪声标签学习仍未被充分探索，现有鲁棒学习策略在超图中的适用性和局限性尚不明确。

Method: 首先将代表性的LLN和GLN方法适配到超图场景并构建统一基准进行评测，揭示现有方法的不足。然后提出HyperTrust框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于预训练和熵感知策略估计超边可信度；(2) HyperedgeBoost模块通过将未标注节点连接到可信超边来增强可靠监督，以及HyperedgePrune模块通过移除不可信的节点-超边关联来抑制噪声传播。两个模块协同工作以调整超图结构并生成最终预测。

Result: 在多个超图数据集和多种噪声设置下的大量实验和理论分析证明了HyperTrust在鲁棒性方面的优越性，超过现有适配后的LLN和GLN方法，为超图噪声标签学习提供了统一基线和有效解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文填补了超图噪声标签学习研究的空白，提出了系统性的评测基准和有效的鲁棒学习框架HyperTrust，为该领域的后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Hypergraph neural networks (HGNNs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in processing complex higher-order relationships. However, their performance is highly dependent on labeled data, making them vulnerable to label noise. Despite advances in learning with label noise (LLN) and graph learning with label noise (GLN), noisy-label learning on hypergraphs remains underexplored. In this paper, we present a systematic study of hypergraph node classification under label noise. First, we adapt representative LLN and GLN methods to hypergraphs and evaluate them under a unified benchmark, revealing the limitations of existing robust learning strategies for hypergraphs. Building on this, we propose a new hypergraph robust framework, HyperTrust, which first estimates hyperedge trustworthiness through a pretraining-based, entropy-aware strategy, and then incorporates the HyperedgeBoost module to enhance reliable supervision by connecting unlabeled nodes to trustworthy hyperedges, as well as the HyperedgePrune module to suppress noisy propagation by removing untrustworthy node-hyperedge incidences. Finally, two modules work collaboratively to adjust the hypergraph structure and generate final predictions. Extensive experiments and theoretical analysis demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of HyperTrust on multiple hypergraph datasets under various noisy settings. Our work provides a unified benchmark and an effective solution for hypergraph learning with label noise and lays a foundation for future research in this direction.

</details>


### [153] [NodeJEPA: Structure-Conditioned Latent Prediction for Node-Level Graph Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04381)
*Tinghe Zhang,Jian Xu,Jiaheng Chen,Jiaxing Li,Yucheng Xiao,Qiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NodeJEPA，一种用于节点级图自监督学习的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），通过掩码结构感知的k跳自我子图，并用结构条件预测器在潜在空间中预测被掩码节点的表示，避免输入重建和图增强，结合EMA目标编码器及多种谱正则化器实现稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图自监督学习方法主要有两类：对比学习方法依赖精心设计的图增强，生成式方法在输入空间重建节点属性。二者都容易将表示与低层输入统计量纠缠，而非与关系结构关联。JEPA范式通过预测潜在目标而非重建输入来学习，但已有工作主要聚焦于图级表示学习，节点级JEPA目标设计和预测器应依赖何种结构信号尚不清晰。

Method: NodeJEPA包含以下核心组件：1）掩码结构感知的k-hop自我子图，训练上下文编码器预测被掩码节点的潜在表示；2）目标编码器通过EMA更新并采用stop-gradient；3）结构条件预测器通过交叉注意力整合谱特征和中心性描述符；4）使用方差、协方差和拉普拉斯谱正则化器稳定嵌入几何；5）可选课程学习逐步增加掩码难度。

Result: 在标准节点分类基准上采用线性探测和微调两种协议评估了NodeJEPA，并进行了掩码、预测和正则化设计选择的消融实验，验证了各组件有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为图上的节点级JEPA式潜在预测提供了实用配方，阐明了结构条件化何时有助于表示学习，并开源了代码和配置。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning on graphs is largely shaped by contrastive methods that depend on carefully designed augmentations, and by generative methods that reconstruct node attributes in the input space. Both paradigms can entangle representations with low-level input statistics rather than with relational structure. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPA) instead learn by predicting latent targets rather than reconstructing inputs. Recent work has explored this idea for graph-level representation learning, but how to design JEPA-style objectives for node-level tasks, and which structural signals the predictor should condition on, remains less clear. We present NodeJEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture for node-level graph self-supervised learning. NodeJEPA masks structure-aware k-hop ego-subgraphs and trains a context encoder to predict the latent representations of the masked nodes. These targets come from an EMA-updated target encoder with stop-gradient. A structure-conditioned predictor integrates spectral and centrality descriptors through cross-attention. Variance, covariance, and Laplacian spectral regularizers help stabilize the embedding geometry, and an optional curriculum gradually increases masking difficulty during training. Because prediction occurs in latent space, NodeJEPA does not rely on input reconstruction or hand-crafted graph augmentations. We evaluate NodeJEPA on standard node classification benchmarks under linear probing and fine-tuning protocols, and conduct ablations on masking, prediction, and regularization design choices. Our study offers a practical recipe for node-level JEPA-style latent prediction on graphs, and clarifies when structural conditioning helps representation learning. Code, configurations, and evaluation scripts are publicly available at https://github.com/OliverZ-dot/Node-Jepa.

</details>


### [154] [Non-asymptotic implicit bias of logistic regression at early-stage gradient descent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04382)
*Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从新视角研究梯度下降在分类问题中的隐式偏差（max-margin隐式偏差），重点理解早期阶段参数向量与最大间隔方向之间的"弱对齐"现象，证明在O(exp(exp(-δ)))次迭代内即可发生弱对齐（δ为容许对齐误差），并通过追踪径向和切向流直接在对齐动力学上操作，避免了渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的max-margin收敛结果是渐近性的，且渐近收敛速率远慢于纯凸优化。然而经验上参数向量在远少于渐近速率所需迭代次数内就与最大间隔方向呈正相关。作者希望从新的角度理解这种早期对齐现象的机制。

Method: 通过追踪参数向量的径向流（radial flow）和切向流（tangential flow），直接在对齐动力学上进行分析，结合数据集几何信息，绕开了传统的渐近展开方法，证明了弱对齐的快速收敛上界O(exp(exp(-δ)))并证明了其紧性。

Result: 理论结果表明，参数向量在O(exp(exp(-δ)))次迭代内即可与最大间隔方向弱对齐，其中δ是容许对齐误差且该界是紧的（tight）。这远快于渐近收敛速率。

Conclusion: 这项工作为"训练更久、泛化更好"的经验现象提供了理论解释：即使max-margin需要渐近时间才能精确达到，参数向量在极早期就已与最大间隔方向产生实质性的弱对齐，从而解释了梯度下降在极少迭代内就能获得良好泛化性能的原因。

Abstract: Gradient descent has been of particular interest in modern machine learning beyond sole focus on optimization. Implicit bias emerging from optimization, though not being encoded by the learning objective, often prevents from overfitting to spurious patterns. A typical instance is the max-margin implicit bias of a linear classifier, widely established for exponentially tailed loss functions. Even after having a given dataset separated, the parameter vector continues to evolve towards the max-margin direction asymptotically along the gradient descent dynamics. This phenomenon corroborates a frequent empirical observation of "train longer, generalize better." However, the max-margin convergence is an asymptotic phenomenon, and what is worse, this asymptotic convergence rate is significantly slower than pure convex optimization. Even so, the parameter vector along gradient descent dynamics commonly correlates with the max-margin direction positively (though not exactly) within considerably fewer iterations than the asymptotic rate. By shedding another light on this classical problem, this work aims to understand the mechanism of this early-stage alignment phenomenon. Our theoretical results demonstrate that the parameter vector weakly aligns with the max-margin direction within $O(\exp(\exp(-δ)))$ iterations, where $δ>0$ is the permissible alignment error, which is shown to be tight. By tracking the radial and tangential flows, our proof operates on the alignment dynamics directly with dataset geometry and gets rid of the asymptotic expansion, which is a key insight to establishing faster weak alignment.

</details>


### [155] [Wiring Beats Blending: What Transfers Between Transformer Sizes -- and What Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02829)
*Ravi Satya Durga Prasad Yenugula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了将预训练大模型转化为更小"兄弟姐妹"模型的可能性，以Pythia家族1.4B→410M转换为例，发现表示层对齐强而参数层对齐弱，直接权重投影在功能上是破坏性的；转换价值主要存在于初始化中。在匹配预算的持续预训练下，转换可分解为最小二乘补偿和方差保持重缩放两个独立杠杆，前者在低预算下是token高效的优势，但在大规模预算下两者收敛至平价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型家族通常从零训练每个规模的模型，这造成了巨大的计算浪费。作者质疑：能否将预训练的大模型转换为更小的兄弟模型，从而避免从零训练？

Method: 以Pythia 1.4B→410M转换为主线，利用ridge回归分析表示对齐；通过置换控制实验证明权重投影的破坏性源于基混合破坏rotary、per-head、GELU和LayerNorm结构；将转换分解为最小二乘补偿（面向零样本性能）和方差保持重缩放（面向训练动力学）两个独立杠杆，在匹配预算的持续预训练下评估。

Result: 表示层ridge R²=0.84高度对齐，参数层对齐弱；稠密权重投影功能上具破坏性且非装配伪影。在30M token低预算下，补偿方法在宽度缩减对（84.0±1.8 vs 89.7±3.7）和深度缩减对（109.3 vs 117.9）均3/3种子胜出；在33倍预算下两者收敛至平价（40.0 vs 40.0），且始终优于从零训练（低预算时优势高达18倍）。在6.9B→1.4B（约5倍供体规模）时叠加两杠杆会过度校正，归因于大宽度下补偿求解的病态条件。

Conclusion: 大模型转小的价值核心在于初始化而非直接参数投影；最小二乘补偿是token高效的低预算策略而非普适优势；需引入维度感知正则化以解决大宽度下的病态问题。

Abstract: Model families train every size from scratch. Can a pretrained large model be converted into a smaller sibling? We characterize the 1.4B->410M conversion in the Pythia family end-to-end: (i) representations align strongly across sizes (ridge R^2=0.84) while parameters align weakly; (ii) dense weight projection is functionally destructive -- provably not an assembly artifact -- because basis mixing breaks rotary, per-head, GELU, and LayerNorm structure; (iii) after the best-fit linear operator, weight residuals are statistically indistinguishable from noise under shuffle controls; (iv) conversion value therefore lives in initialization. In matched-budget continued pre-training we decompose conversion into two independent levers -- least-squares compensation (function: best zero-shot) and variance-preserving rescale (dynamics: best endpoints). Compensation is a token-efficient, low-budget win rather than a universal one: at 30M tokens it beats the strongest subcloning variant on both a width-reduced pair (84.0 +/- 1.8 vs. 89.7 +/- 3.7, 3/3 seeds) and a held-out depth-reduced pair (109.3 vs. 117.9, 3/3 seeds), reaching a given quality with fewer tokens; at a 33x larger budget the two converge to parity (40.0 vs. 40.0), both far ahead of from-scratch, which transfer initialization always beats -- by up to 18x at low budget, the margin narrowing at convergence and at the largest scale. We further map the method's boundary: at ~5x the donor scale (6.9B->1.4B) stacking both levers over-corrects, which we trace to ill-conditioning of the compensation solve at large width, pointing to dimension-aware regularization as the fix. Code, checkpoints, and the frozen evaluation corpus are released.

</details>


### [156] [NeuroPB: Scaling Neural Decoding with Pretrained Behavioral Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04389)
*Luyao Jin,Yonghao Song,Huan Zhao,Vincent C. K. Cheung,Wei-Hsin Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroPB框架，通过大规模行为数据预训练运动编码器，再将神经活动与行为表征空间对齐，从而在有限神经数据条件下提升连续运动轨迹解码性能，并在多个猕猴数据集上验证了显著的R²提升和跨会话/跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经解码器受限于神经记录数据的规模有限和异质性，而行为数据可以更大规模、更容易地获取。当前方法缺乏利用大规模行为数据来提升神经解码能力的手段，需要一种可扩展的方案来突破神经数据瓶颈。

Method: NeuroPB包含三个步骤：(1) 在大规模运动行为数据上预训练运动编码器；(2) 使用有限配对的神经-行为数据将神经活动对齐到预训练的行为表征空间；(3) 联合优化神经编码器和轻量级运动解码器，从对齐的神经表征中重建连续运动轨迹。

Result: 在多个猕猴运动数据集上，行为预训练显著提升轨迹解码性能：中心外出任务R²提升11%，随机目标任务提升8%；机器人轨迹预训练可达与猕猴轨迹预训练相当的性能；解码性能随机器人预训练数据的规模与多样性增加而提升；仅需10%的校准数据即可达到从头训练的性能。

Conclusion: 行为预训练为神经解码提供了一种可扩展的知识来源，在有限神经数据条件下能实现高性能、低校准成本的脑机接口，并证明生物与非生物（机器人）运动之间存在可迁移的运动学结构。

Abstract: Decoding continuous motor trajectories from neural activity is essential for developing practical brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). However, current neural decoders are constrained by the limited scale and heterogeneity of neural recordings. In contrast, behavioral data can be collected more readily and at substantially larger scale from humans, animals, simulations, and robotic systems. Here, we introduce NeuroPB, a framework that scales neural decoding by transferring knowledge from pretrained behavioral representations. NeuroPB first pretrains a motor encoder on large-scale motor behavior data and then aligns neural activity with the resulting behavioral representation space using a limited set of paired neural-behavioral recordings. A neural encoder and lightweight motor decoder are subsequently optimized to reconstruct continuous movement from the aligned neural representations. Across multiple macaque motor datasets, behavioral pretraining improves trajectory decoding, including an 11% $R^2$ increase on center-out and 8% on random-target compared with training the motor encoder from scratch. Notably, pretraining on robotic trajectories achieves performance comparable to pretraining on macaque trajectories, demonstrating that transferable kinematic structure is shared across biological and artificial models. Moreover, decoding performance improves as the scale and diversity of robotic pretraining data increase, when the amount of neural data is fixed. Pretraining also enhances generalization across recording sessions, subjects, and motor tasks, with only 10% calibration needed to match training from scratch. Overall, these results establish behavioral pretraining as a scalable source for neural decoding and provide a promising route toward high-performance and calibration-efficient BCIs under limited neural data.

</details>


### [157] [When Proxy Prediction Becomes Equation Reconstruction: Diagnostics and Residual Learning for Factor-Derived Proxy Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04393)
*Chayan Lahiri,Ahmed Shafee,Cody Fehringer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RASPL框架，在土壤流失预测中通过保留退化公式估计作为预测锚点并学习自适应门控上下文修正，显著优于直接将退化公式作为普通输入特征的直接预测方法，确立了公式保留作为因子派生代理目标鲁棒学习核心设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习常依赖由已知域因子计算得到的代理目标，但当这些因子同时作为模型输入时，高预测精度可能只是反映了对代理生成方程的重构，而非对因子信息退化的鲁棒性。作者在RUSLE土壤流失预测场景下，系统研究了对土壤可蚀性因子K进行受控退化时这一问题的严重性。

Method: 提出一个诊断框架，包括退化公式参考、经典树基线、匹配的直接预测与公式特征预测、上下文消融、尾部误差分析和退化鲁棒性评分。在此基础上提出RASPL（公式保留残差框架），将退化公式估计作为预测锚点，学习自适应门控的上下文修正。比较了紧凑统计编码器和卷积编码器两种实现。

Result: RASPL显著优于匹配的直接预测方法，相比将公式估计作为普通输入特征，提供了更强的退化鲁棒性和尾部鲁棒性。紧凑统计编码器实现最高宏平均R²和最低计算成本；卷积编码器实现最强退化鲁棒性和最低Tail95 MAE。

Conclusion: 公式保留是因子派生代理目标鲁棒学习的核心设计原则。RASPL框架通过锚定退化公式估计而非视其为普通特征，成功兼得高精度与强退化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning often relies on proxy targets computed from known domain factors when direct observations are limited. When those same factors are used as model inputs, however, high predictive accuracy may reflect reconstruction of the proxy-generating equation rather than robustness to degraded factor information. We study this problem in RUSLE-derived soil-loss proxy prediction under controlled degradation of the soil-erodibility factor $K$. We introduce a diagnostic framework that combines degraded-formula references, classical tree-based baselines, matched direct and formula-feature predictors, contextual ablations, tail-error analysis, and degradation robustness scoring. We then propose RASPL, a formula-preserving residual framework that retains the degraded formula estimate as the prediction anchor and learns an adaptively gated contextual correction. RASPL substantially outperforms matched direct prediction and provides stronger degradation and tail robustness than treating the formula estimate as an ordinary input feature. Within RASPL, a compact statistical encoder achieves the highest macro-averaged $R^2$ and lowest computational cost, whereas a convolutional encoder achieves the strongest degradation robustness and lowest Tail95 mean absolute error (MAE). These results establish formula preservation as the central design principle for robust learning from factor-derived proxy targets.

</details>


### [158] [Elbow-Based MoE Routing: A Training-Free Inference Time Plugin for Expert Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04401)
*Robin Pan,Raymond Liu,Daniel Fang,Adelina Andrei,Rosa Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"elbow-based routing"的训练免推理时改进方法，通过动态调整每个token激活的专家数量来优化MoE模型的推理效率。该方法在保持精度的同时，平均降低5.3%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的MoE模型使用固定的top-k专家选择机制，无论每个token实际需要多少专家，都消耗相同的计算量。这种固定的策略在推理时存在计算浪费，因为不同token对专家的需求程度不同。

Method: 该方法是一种训练免的推理时修改方案，通过检查排序后的路由器概率分布，识别出一个"elbow点"（肘部点）来区分高概率和低概率的专家，从而在每token基础上动态调整激活的专家数量。

Result: 在先进的MoE模型上，该方法在六个基准测试中保持准确率的同时，实现了平均5.3%的延迟降低。实验还表明大多数路由器分布具有适合该策略的明显拐点，并从理论和实证上证明elbow-based routing保持了专家负载均衡。

Conclusion: elbow-based routing作为一种简单而有效的训练免方法，能够在不牺牲模型精度的前提下提升MoE模型的推理效率，且能保持专家负载均衡，是一种实用的推理优化方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable model scaling while maintaining low inference-time compute by activating only a subset of experts per token. However, conventional routing relies on a fixed top-k selection, forcing the model to spend the same compute regardless of how many experts are relevant. We introduce elbow-based routing, a training-free inference-time modification that dynamically adjusts the number of experts on a per-token basis. Our method examines the sorted router probability distribution and identifies an elbow point that separates high- and low-probability experts. We find that most router distributions exhibit clear inflection points suitable for this strategy, and we show both theoretically and empirically that elbow-based routing preserves expert load balance. Experiments on a state-of-the-art MoE model demonstrate an average latency reduction of 5.3% while maintaining accuracy across six benchmarks.

</details>


### [159] [Training-Free Hashing-Based Attention via Binary Principal Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04405)
*Daohai Yu,Zhanpeng Zeng,Keyu Chen,Wenhao Li,Zhifeng Shen,Luxi Lin,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BinaryPC是一种免训练、基于数据感知哈希的稀疏注意力机制，通过计算数据的二值主成分来构建紧凑的二值哈希码和哈希函数，在不损失精度的前提下显著提升长上下文LLM的解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM在实际部署中面临自注意力效率瓶颈，尤其是在解码阶段需要反复处理不断增长的KV缓存。现有稀疏注意力方法要么精度下降明显，要么需要额外训练，要么依赖昂贵的哈希计算，缺乏一种既免训练又高效且能保持精度的解决方案。

Method: BinaryPC通过计算数据的二值主成分（binary principal components）来构建紧凑的二值哈希码和对应的哈希函数。与依赖数据无关随机投影的LSH或需要梯度训练的非线性哈希方法不同，BinaryPC显式保留数据的结构信息，且无需任何基于梯度的训练。

Result: 在多个模型家族和长上下文基准上的综合实验表明，BinaryPC在保持与全注意力相当精度的同时，在稀疏注意力和基于哈希的基线中取得了最优性能。在现代GPU上，BinaryPC相比FlashAttention内核实现了3.56倍的整体解码吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: BinaryPC提供了一种免训练、数据感知的哈希稀疏注意力方案，在精度和效率之间取得了优异平衡，为长上下文LLM的高效解码提供了一条实用路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-context large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, yet self-attention remains a major efficiency bottleneck -- especially during decoding -- due to the necessity of repeatedly processing ever-growing key-value (KV) caches. Existing sparse attention reduce computation by attending to fewer KV pairs, but often suffer from substantial accuracy degradation, require additional training, or rely on expensive hashing. In this work, we present BinaryPC, a training-free, data-aware hashing-based sparse attention for long-context LLMs. BinaryPC constructs compact binary hash codes and corresponding hash function by computing binary principal components of data. Unlike Locality-Sensitive Hashing (LSH) with data-independent random projections or learned non-linear hashing methods, BinaryPC constructs binary codes that explicitly preserve the structural information of data without requiring gradient-based training. Comprehensive experiments across multiple model families and long-context benchmarks show that BinaryPC preserves accuracy relative to full attention while achieving superior performance among sparse and hashing-based baselines. On modern GPUs, BinaryPC improves end-to-end decoding throughput by 3.56$\times$ over the FlashAttention kernel. Our code is available at https://github.com/yudaohai666/BPC.

</details>


### [160] [MESH: Memory-Efficient Sinkhorn Optimization for Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04407)
*Masato Fujitake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MESH（隐动量Sinkhorn更新）用于MoE训练中的专家矩阵优化，通过梯度缓冲区生命周期恢复时间一阶动量信号，无需存储专家一阶矩作为优化器状态，在110M参数DeepSeek风格MoE预设中减少62.5%优化器状态内存和约12.6%的峰值CUDA分配，同时保持接近AdamW的评估损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存高效矩阵优化器（如Sinkhorn梯度下降）可去除稠密Transformer矩阵的大部分AdamW优化器状态，但直接应用于Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）训练并不可靠——路由专家矩阵的梯度是条件性的、时间变化的，无状态的Sinkhorn归一化难以处理，导致评估损失显著退化（3.8265 vs AdamW基线3.58-3.64）。

Method: 提出MESH（hidden-Momentum Sinkhorn update）：通过梯度缓冲区生命周期恢复时间一阶动量信号，无需将专家一阶矩存储为优化器状态。另有MESH-B块预条件变体，添加粗粒度神经元/块逆RMS乘子。核心思想是先用时间平滑（隐动量）处理梯度，再进行矩阵归一化（Sinkhorn）。

Result: 在110M参数nanowhale DeepSeek风格MoE设定中，MESH和MESH-B减少优化器状态内存62.5%，峰值PyTorch CUDA分配约12.6%（相对AdamW），评估损失差距较小。SAGE/Sinkhorn混合方案虽将状态从0.883GB降至0.331GB但损失退化至3.8265。消融实验表明时间平滑是主要因果成分；块/神经元预条件可改善内存-质量前沿但非普遍必要；全状态诊断变体可恢复AdamW级别性能。

Conclusion: MoE专家矩阵需要时间平滑（隐动量），但不一定需要完整的逐坐标AdamW状态。MESH成功在消除专家第一矩存储的同时保留时间动量信号，实现了内存效率与训练质量的良好平衡，验证了"时间平滑必要、逐坐标状态非必要"的假设。

Abstract: Memory-efficient matrix optimizers such as Sinkhorn gradient descent remove most AdamW optimizer state for dense Transformer matrices, but direct application to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training is unreliable. We study this failure in a controlled 110M-parameter nanowhale DeepSeek-style MoE pretraining setting. A SAGE/Sinkhorn hybrid reduces optimizer state from 0.883GB to 0.331GB but degrades evaluation loss to 3.8265, far above the AdamW baselines observed in the same setup (3.58--3.64 across the seeds we study). We show that routed MoE expert matrices are the dominant failure point: their gradients are conditional, temporally varying, and poorly served by stateless Sinkhorn normalization. We propose MESH, a hidden-momentum Sinkhorn update for MoE experts. MESH restores a temporal first-moment signal through the gradient-buffer lifecycle, without storing the expert first moment as optimizer state. MESH is an optional block-preconditioned variant that adds a coarse neuron/block inverse-RMS multiplier. Across ablations, temporal smoothing before matrix normalization is the primary causal ingredient; block/neuron preconditioning can improve the memory-quality frontier, but is not established as universally necessary. In two additional seeds, MESH and MESH-B reduce optimizer-state memory by 62.5\% and peak PyTorch CUDA allocation by about 12.6\% relative to AdamW, with a modest evaluation-loss gap. Full-state diagnostic variants recover AdamW-like performance in ablations, supporting the conclusion that MoE experts need temporal smoothing, but not necessarily full coordinate-wise AdamW state.

</details>


### [161] [Not Every Divergence Should Be Suppressed: Counterfactual Recoverability in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04408)
*De Jiang,Zhengyang Zhang,Kehong Yuan,Shaohua Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于反事实可恢复性（counterfactual recoverability）的在线蒸馏选择性监督方法，通过预算匹配的教师延续分支和回滚分支来判断错误前缀是否可纠正，据此将状态分为可恢复、不可逆但可避免和模糊三类，分别指导轨迹的保留、回滚或常规监督，在AIME和GPQA-Diamond基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）需要对 student 访问的轨迹进行监督，但现有的基于散度（divergence）的规则无法判断一个错误的轨迹前缀是否仍然可以被纠正。作者将这一决策形式化为反事实可恢复性问题，旨在提供一种以结果为导向的决策变量来替代传统的散度规则。

Method: 对每个错误状态，通过预算匹配的教师延续分支（teacher-continuation）和回滚分支（rollback）进行重放，根据两个分支的相对成功情况将状态分类为可恢复（recoverable）、不可逆但可避免（irreversible-but-avoidable）或模糊（ambiguous）。这些标签指导训练策略：保留（retain）、回滚（rollback）或常规监督（conventional supervision）相应的轨迹。

Result: 在AIME分支诊断中，可恢复状态的延续减回滚效应均值为0.185，不可逆但可避免状态为-1.000，展示了相反的干预偏好。基于分支的可恢复性代理指标AUC达1.000，远超散度指标0.392。在冻结评估中，可恢复性感知控制在AIME2025上达0.578的成功率（基线最佳0.517）；AIME2024-2025平均@32从0.2656提升至0.3125，GPQA-Diamond平均@32从0.2702提升至0.3070。消融实验表明保留教师可纠正前缀贡献最大。

Conclusion: 反事实可恢复性是一种有效的、以结果为基础的决策变量，可用于OPD中的选择性监督。该研究表明，相比传统的散度度量，基于分支诊断的可恢复性判断能更准确地指导干预决策，从而显著提升模型在下游推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises student-visited trajectories, yet divergence-based rules cannot determine whether an erroneous prefix remains correctable. We formulate this decision as counterfactual recoverability and replay each error state through budget-matched teacher-continuation and rollback branches. Based on their relative success, states are categorized as recoverable, irreversible-but-avoidable, or ambiguous, and these labels guide whether training retains, rolls back, or conventionally supervises the corresponding trajectory. On AIME branch diagnostics, the mean continuation-minus-rollback effect is 0.185 for recoverable states and -1.000 for irreversible-but-avoidable states, demonstrating opposite intervention preferences. A branch-derived recoverability proxy achieves an AUC of 1.000, substantially outperforming divergence alone at 0.392. Across frozen evaluations, recoverability-aware control achieves the strongest recorded performance, reaching 0.578 success on held-out AIME2025 compared with 0.517 for the best baseline. It also improves AIME2024-2025 average@32 from 0.2656 to 0.3125 and GPQA-Diamond average@32 from 0.2702 to 0.3070. Component ablations further show that retaining teacher-correctable prefixes provides the largest individual contribution. These findings establish recoverability as an outcome-grounded decision variable for selective supervision in OPD.

</details>


### [162] [SPOT: Sparse Probing and Outcome Calibration for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04419)
*Zikun Qu,Min Zhang,Mingze Kong,Zhiwei Shang,Yikun Ban,Shuang Qiu,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPOT（Sparse Probing and Outcome-calibrated Targets OPD）方法，通过获取-探索-利用三步流程解决在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中两个耦合决策：在何处探测（where to probe）和蒸馏什么（what to distill）。该方法在多个学生模型和推理基准上显著提升推理性能，平衡了解决方案质量与覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准反向KL训练在老师蒸馏中学生可能无法获得足够概率分配给其他合理延续。仅靠老师熵无法区分不确定性是集中在少数几个合理next token上还是分散在长尾概率中，也无法判断学生是否已能代表这些候选；且局部老师概率未必能预测下游成功。因此需要解决两个耦合问题：在哪探测和蒸馏什么。

Method: SPOT采用获取-探索-利用（acquisition-exploration-exploitation）流程。获取阶段：使用位置级分数（由归一化老师熵、top-k候选集捕获的概率质量、学生-老师不匹配度组合而成）来分配有限的探测预算。探索阶段：通过验证器评分的学生延续来评估老师提出的候选。利用阶段：根据探索结果产生闭式KL正则化目标，该目标偏向具有更好下游结果的候选，同时锚定在老师分布上。

Result: 在多个学生模型和推理基准上的大量实验表明，SPOT在提升推理性能方面有效，同时平衡了解决方案质量和覆盖度。

Conclusion: SPOT通过结合位置级探测策略和结果校准的蒸馏目标，有效解决了OPD中探测位置与蒸馏内容两个耦合问题，在不牺牲覆盖度的前提下提升了推理性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories, but standard reverse-KL training can assign insufficient probability to other plausible continuations. Teacher entropy alone does not reveal whether uncertainty is concentrated among a few plausible next tokens or dispersed over a long probability tail, nor whether the student already represents those candidates well. Moreover, local teacher probabilities may not predict downstream success. We introduce Sparse Probing and Outcome-calibrated Targets OPD (SPOT), which addresses two coupled decisions, where to probe and what to distill, through an acquisition--exploration--exploitation procedure. During acquisition, a position-level score combines normalized teacher entropy, the probability mass captured by a small top-$k$ candidate set, and student--teacher mismatch to allocate a limited probing budget. During exploration, SPOT evaluates teacher-proposed candidates through verifier-scored student continuations. During exploitation, these outcomes produce a closed-form, KL-regularized target that favors candidates with better downstream outcomes while remaining anchored to the teacher distribution. Extensive experiments across multiple student models and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of SPOT in improving reasoning performance while balancing solution quality and coverage.

</details>


### [163] [Generative Optimization for Incentivized Advertising with Global Level Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04421)
*Gege Chen,Ning Luo,Hao Jiang,Da Li,Wenzheng Shu,Teng Sha,Yanxiang Zeng,Wenxin Tai,Fan Zhou,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GOAL框架，将激励分配问题形式化为条件序列生成任务，结合SCPO算法实现单一生成策略在不同ROI约束下的泛化，在真实数据和合成疲劳环境中提升了长期收益和用户留存，同时显著降低ROI违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激励广告中的激励发放优化面临连续激励幅度下严格全局约束的挑战，现有uplift建模和约束强化学习方法因高频交互、延迟反馈和用户非马尔可夫动态（如疲劳效应）而效果受限，亟需新的建模框架来应对这些问题。

Method: 提出约束感知的生成式框架GOAL，将激励分配建模为条件序列生成问题，直接生成以用户历史和系统全局压力为条件的激励幅度；集成分层因果状态编码器以捕获局部行为动态和长程依赖；提出Safe Constrained Policy Optimization (SCPO)算法，学习单一生成策略以在无重训练情况下泛化多种ROI约束。

Result: 在大规模真实数据和合成疲劳感知环境上的实验表明，GOAL相比强基线在长期收益和用户留存方面均有提升，同时大幅降低了ROI违规率。

Conclusion: GOAL框架有效解决了激励广告中连续激励分配的全局约束优化难题，通过生成式建模和约束感知的策略优化，在保持合规性的同时提升了业务核心指标。

Abstract: Incentivized advertising allocates monetary or virtual rewards to drive user engagement, where a key challenge is optimizing continuous incentive magnitudes under strict global constraints. This problem is complicated by high-frequency interactions, delayed feedback, and non-Markovian user dynamics such as fatigue, which limit the effectiveness of existing uplift modeling and constrained reinforcement learning approaches. To address these challenges, we propose GOAL, a constraint-aware generative framework that formulates incentive allocation as a conditional sequence generation problem. GOAL directly generates incentive magnitudes conditioned on user histories and system-level global pressure, and integrates a hierarchical causal state encoder to capture both local behavioral dynamics and long-range dependencies. To enable flexible constraint control, we introduce \textbf{S}afe \textbf{C}onstrained \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (SCPO), which learns a single generative policy that generalizes across a spectrum of ROI constraints without retraining. Experiments on large-scale real-world data and a synthetic fatigue-aware environment show that GOAL improves long-term revenue and user retention while substantially reducing ROI violation rates compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [164] [Robustness Emerges Early in Training Dynamics, but Is Not Preserved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04442)
*Jiangang Yang,Wenhui Shi,Lu Hu,Jing Xing,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度神经网络在训练早期浅层会自发形成鲁棒表示和平坦损失景观，但这一特性在标准收敛过程中会消失（即"鲁棒性衰减"现象）。为此提出一个框架，通过两种无参数策略（Early-Phase Stabilization 和 Asymmetric Weight Reversion）来稳定或恢复训练早期涌现的鲁棒先验，在不修改架构或引入可学习参数的情况下显著提升了下游迁移、动态适应和多种计算机视觉应用的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络对自然损坏（natural corruptions）的鲁棒性仍然是一个根本性挑战。已有研究表明训练早期可能涌现某些有价值的表现，但浅层在早期训练中发展出的鲁棒表示与平坦损失景观在标准收敛过程中未被保留，导致最终模型的鲁棒性受限。作者旨在利用这一早期涌现的鲁棒先验来改善模型最终性能。

Method: 提出两种参数无关（parameter-free）的训练干预策略：(1) Early-Phase Stabilization（EPS）：在训练早期稳定浅层的鲁棒配置，防止其在后续训练中被破坏；(2) Asymmetric Weight Reversion（AWR）：在训练过程中非对称地将权重回退/恢复到早期鲁棒状态。两者均不需要修改模型架构或引入额外可学习参数，仅对训练动态进行战略性干预。

Result: 在多种基准测试和不同架构上进行了广泛实验，该框架在下游迁移学习、动态适应以及多种计算机视觉应用中均取得了显著的性能提升，验证了所提策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过战略性干预训练动态以保留早期涌现的鲁棒先验，可以有效对抗鲁棒性衰减现象，从而在不增加模型复杂度或额外参数的前提下显著提升深度神经网络的鲁棒性和下游任务表现。

Abstract: Robustness to natural corruptions remains a fundamental challenge for deep neural networks. In this paper, we identify a robustness fading phenomenon where shallow layers spontaneously develop robust representations and flat loss landscapes in early training, yet these properties are not preserved during standard convergence. To address this, we propose a framework that performs strategic interventions on training dynamics to stabilize the empirically identified early-emergent robust priors. Our approach includes two parameter-free strategies: Early-Phase Stabilization~(EPS) and Asymmetric Weight Reversion~(AWR), which stabilize or recover robust shallow configurations without modifying the model architecture or introducing learnable parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy of our framework across various benchmarks and architectures, yielding significant gains in downstream transfer, dynamic adaptation, and diverse computer vision applications.

</details>


### [165] [Tropical Algebraic Geometry for Neuronal Representations: An Arakelov-Green Measure Based Descriptor for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04460)
*Yuyang Zhang,Weihan Xu,Xuehai Zhou,Shucheng Cao,Qihuang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于热带代数几何的训练无关几何先验，通过热带Abel-Jacobi变换和极化距离将树状3D神经元形态转换为循环度量图，并嵌入热带Jacobian中。为避免NP-Hard的格上最近向量问题（CVP），作者采用Albanese环面上通用覆盖的连续松弛，利用图拉普拉斯广义逆闭式求解离散Arakelov-Green测度，得到节点级特征向量和置换不变的谱签名。在BREC基准上超越1-WL表达力，在3D形态数据集上无缝集成到VAE/GNN/Tree-LSTM等架构中提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有消息传递GNN受限于1-WL测试，无法捕捉由空间邻近性诱导的环结构，难以充分表达3D神经元形态中既包含图拓扑又包含空间几何的复杂信息。需要一种训练无关、表达能力超越1-WL的几何先验来弥补这一不足。

Method: 提出结构变换流水线：(1) 环空间增强与商空间构造，将空间树转换为循环度量图；(2) 利用热带Abel-Jacobi变换嵌入热带Jacobian；(3) 通过在Albanese环面的通用覆盖上进行连续松弛，避免求解NP-Hard的CVP整数格问题；(4) 通过图拉普拉斯广义逆闭式计算离散Arakelov-Green测度，精确分解为内蕴路径度量减去非量化的极化距离，从而生成节点级特征向量和置换不变的图级谱签名。

Result: 在BREC基准上，特征向量公式展示了超越1-WL表达力的能力。在ACT-4、JML-4、BIL-6三维形态数据集上，谱签名无需额外可训练参数即可无缝集成到VAE、GNN、Tree-LSTM等标准架构中，优于显式格近似方法，并在现有空间模型基础上提升了分类精度。

Conclusion: 热带代数几何（热带Abel-Jacobi变换与极化距离）为树结构化数据的GNN提供了有效的训练无关几何先验，通过连续松弛规避了CVP的NP-hardness，同时实现了超越1-WL的表达力和实际的分类性能提升，为3D神经元形态分析提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: The quantitative analysis of 3D neuronal morphologies requires capturing both graph topology and spatial geometry. Current message-passing Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bounded by the 1-Weisfeiler-Lehman (1-WL) test, limiting their ability to capture cycles induced by spatial proximities. To address this, we propose a training-free geometric prior based on tropical algebraic geometry. We apply the recently established tropical Abel-Jacobi transform and polarization distances to machine learning on tree-structured data. We introduce a structural transformation pipeline, comprising cycle space augmentation and quotient space construction, to convert spatial trees into cyclic metric graphs suitable for embedding into the Tropical Jacobian.
