Computing exact tropical polarization distances requires solving the NP-Hard Closest Vector Problem (CVP) on integer lattices. Instead of relying on explicit approximations with quantization errors (e.g., Babai's rounding), we adopt a continuous relaxation on the universal cover of the Albanese torus. We show that the discrete Arakelov-Green measure, computed in closed form via the graph Laplacian's generalized inverse, decomposes exactly into the intrinsic path metric minus the unquantized polarization distance on this cover, avoiding integer lattice searches. This metric yields two descriptors: eigenvectors provide node-level structural coordinates, and the permutation-invariant eigenvalue spectrum provides a graph-level signature. On the BREC benchmark, the eigenvector formulation demonstrates expressivity beyond the 1-WL limit. On 3D morphology datasets (ACT-4, JML-4, BIL-6), the spectrum seamlessly integrates into standard architectures (VAEs, GNNs, Tree-LSTMs) without additional trainable parameters, outperforming explicit lattice approximations and improving classification accuracy over existing spatial models.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Linear Dynamics: Neural Bilinear Dynamical Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04471)
*Mengzhou Gao,Huangqian Yu,Pengfei Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经双线性动力学模型（NBDM），通过Koopman理论将非线性动力学提升到高维潜在空间，构建双线性潜在动力学模型，并引入参数化误差补偿项和记忆增强控制器来处理控制输入缺失问题，在五个真实世界数据集上显著提升了多步和长时程预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列常由非线性动力系统生成，现有显式建模系统动力学的方法依赖线性假设或Koopman线性化，难以充分捕捉复杂非线性行为，导致长时程预测中误差累积的问题。

Method: NBDM利用Koopman理论将原始非线性动力学提升到高维潜在空间，在该空间中构建双线性动力学模型刻画状态演化；引入参数化误差补偿项以缓解双线性表示的近似误差；将控制输入显式集成到动力学中（有控制输入时用辅助变量，缺失时用学习到的反馈信号）；设计记忆增强控制器，通过历史状态与控制信号之间的乘法交互推断潜在控制输入。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上，NBDM在给定控制和缺失控制两种设置下均一致优于竞争基线，特别是在多步和长时程预测任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: NBDM通过双线性潜在动力学建模结合误差补偿和记忆增强控制机制，有效解决了非线性系统长时程预测中的误差累积问题，在多种实际场景中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Time series in real-world applications are often generated by nonlinear dynamical systems, making accurate forecasting challenging. Existing approaches that explicitly model system dynamics typically rely on linear assumptions or Koopman-based linearizations, which may inadequately capture complex nonlinear behaviors and lead to error accumulation in long-horizon prediction. To address this limitation, we propose the Neural Bilinear Dynamical Model (NBDM), which models nonlinear system dynamics through a bilinear latent dynamical formulation. Specifically, NBDM leverages Koopman theory to lift the original nonlinear dynamics into a higher-dimensional latent space, where a bilinear dynamical model is constructed to characterize state evolution. To mitigate the approximation error introduced by bilinear representations, we further incorporate a parameterized error compensation term. Within this formulation, control inputs are explicitly integrated into the dynamics, using auxiliary variables when available and learned feedback signals otherwise. To handle scenarios with missing control inputs, we design a memory-enhanced controller that infers latent controls through multiplicative interactions between historical states and control signals. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that NBDM consistently outperforms competitive baselines in both given-control and missing-control settings, particularly for multi-step and long-horizon forecasting.

</details>


### [167] [Local Violation Certification for Linear Predict-Then-Optimize Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04474)
*Ş. İlker Birbil,Wenhao Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对线性决策管道（预测模型+下游优化）的局部违反认证框架，通过分析部署模型的固定决策边界，用单次优化求解闭式计算局部风险，并提供精确采样程序和闭式风险统计以实现特征级归因，避免了传统随机采样在罕见故障场景下的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的决策管道在高层级运营中越来越常见，但传统场景生成方法依赖重复随机测试，当故障事件罕见时计算代价高昂，且难以揭示故障发生的根本原因，无法有效认证决策的安全性、公平性和可靠性。

Method: (1) 数学证明标准采样方法在罕见违规场景下效率低下；(2) 通过分析部署管道的固定决策边界，证明局部风险可用单一优化求解以闭式形式直接计算；(3) 引入精确采样程序和闭式风险统计，提供特征层面的归因分析，识别对潜在不合规贡献最大的输入特征。

Result: 在受排放法规约束的经济电力调度系统中验证了该方法，实现了精确、可审计的风险评估，且计算成本仅为传统方法的极小部分。

Conclusion: 本文确立了针对线性决策管道局部违规认证的结构化方法，克服了标准采样方法在罕见事件下的低效问题，并在保持计算效率的同时提供了特征级的可解释风险归因。

Abstract: Data-driven decision pipelines combining predictive machine learning models with downstream optimization software are increasingly used to make high-stakes operational decisions. Certifying the safety, fairness, and reliability of these decisions is essential, yet traditional scenario generation methods rely on repeated random testing, which becomes computationally prohibitive when failure events are rare and offers little insight into why failures occur. We present a framework for local violation certification designed specifically for linear decision pipelines under input uncertainty. We mathematically demonstrate that standard sampling methods fail efficiently for rare violations, motivating a direct structural approach. By analyzing the fixed decision boundary of a deployed pipeline, we show that the local risk of failure can be calculated directly in closed form using a single optimization solve. Furthermore, we introduce an exact sampling procedure and closed-form risk statistics that provide feature-level attributions (identifying which input characteristics contribute most to potential non-compliance) without requiring repetitive random trials or complex sampling algorithms. We demonstrate our approach on an economic power dispatch system subject to emissions regulations, delivering precise, auditable risk assessments at a fraction of the traditional computational cost.

</details>


### [168] [Discretization and Statistical Consistency of Functional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04531)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数分布上的函数流匹配（functional flow matching）在无限维极限下的理论保证，证明了对于任意强一致的有限秩重构序列，有限条件速度目标具有强L²收敛性，并给出了定量界。文章还建立了学习流到群体叠加路径的端到端Wasserstein界，并通过非交换迹类高斯例子展示了投影限制与精确条件化之间的边界乘子差异，最后给出了显式的端到端收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数流匹配虽然定义在函数分布上，但实际实现仅基于有限个系数或点值。在散乱或自适应细化条件下，相应的条件σ-代数未必是嵌套的，因此经典的鞅收敛理论无法证明传感器极限的有效性。这构成了理论与实现之间的根本性gap，需要严格的收敛性证明来支撑方法在实际应用中的合理性。

Method: 本文从理论分析入手，主要方法包括：(1) 对任意强一致的有限秩重构序列，证明有限条件速度目标的强L²收敛性，并对正交投影情形给出定量收敛界，通过正则空间推广到点传感器情形；(2) 对学习流，直接将耦合对准群体叠加路径，获得端到端Wasserstein界，无需假定群体有限维ODE的唯一性；(3) 对归一化求积神经算子验证传感器独立常数，利用显式幅值递推处理全局Lipschitz激活函数；(4) 使用空间正则性-立方体（cubature）证书封闭算子实现项，通过Bernstein论证得到固定模型维数和包络下的Õ(n^{-1})超额风险项。

Result: 主要结果包括：任意强一致有限秩重构序列下有限条件速度目标的强L²收敛性；端到端Wasserstein界；非交换迹类高斯例子中，投影限制情形下边界乘子为0，而精确条件化情形下为0.72，揭示了两种实现方式的本质差异；裁剪高斯缩放特例下给出了显式的端到端收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文为函数流匹配在无限维极限下的理论合理性提供了严格的收敛性保证，消解了散乱/自适应细化下鞅收敛失效的问题。所得传感器无关常数和显式收敛速率表明，在适当的实现条件下，函数流匹配具有稳定的无限维极限行为，为后续算法设计与理论深化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Functional flow matching is posed on distributions of functions but implemented from finitely many coefficients or point values. Under scattered or adaptive refinement, the resulting conditioning sigma-algebras need not be nested, so martingale convergence does not justify the sensor limit. We prove strong $L^2$ convergence of finite conditional velocity targets for every strongly consistent sequence of finite-rank reconstructions, with quantitative bounds for orthogonal projections and a point-sensor extension through a regularity space. For learned flows, coupling directly to a population superposition path yields an end-to-end Wasserstein bound without assuming uniqueness of the population finite-dimensional ODE. We verify sensor-independent constants for a normalized quadrature neural operator, including globally Lipschitz activations through an explicit magnitude recurrence. A noncommuting trace-class Gaussian example gives boundary multiplier $0$ under projected restriction and $0.72$ under exact conditioning. A spatial regularity--cubature certificate closes the operator-realization term, a Bernstein argument gives a $\widetilde{O}(n^{-1})$ excess-risk term for fixed model dimension and envelopes, and an exactly realizable clipped Gaussian scaling specialization yields an explicit end-to-end rate.

</details>


### [169] [Learning Compression Rules for Network Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04545)
*Quentin Lampin,Éloi Sainte-Beuve,Louis-Adrien Dufrène,Guillaume Larue,Massih-Reza Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RECAP（Robust Entropy Clustering for Adaptive comPression），一种用于学习紧凑规则压缩器的框架，用于结构化网络流量（数据包头部字段）压缩。方法分为两阶段：基于归一化熵比准则的递归无监督结构发现，以及基于动态规划在硬预算约束下选择最优规则子集。应用于SCHC（IETF标准），在四个真实IoT和5G核心网数据集上超越专家设计的规则集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化网络流量（如数据包头部字段）存在高度冗余，规则式压缩器（如SCHC）能有效压缩，但传统规则依赖专家手工设计，费时费力且难以适应不同网络场景。现有方法缺乏从数据中自动学习紧凑规则的能力。

Method: 将规则学习建模为两阶段问题：(i) 无监督结构发现阶段，使用对小型样本稳健的归一化熵比准则递归划分训练数据包；(ii) 约束选择阶段，使用动态规划在可安装规则数量硬预算下选取使期望压缩增益最大化的规则子集。将该框架实例化到SCHC标准。

Result: 在四个真实世界的IoT和5G核心网络数据集上，RECAP用少量学习得到的规则超越了专家手工设计的规则集，并且消除了手动规则设计的需要。

Conclusion: RECAP证明了数据驱动的规则学习可以替代专家手工规则设计，在受限网络环境下实现高效且自适应的头部压缩，且仅需少量规则即可获得优于专家的压缩性能。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning compact rule-based compressors for structured network traffic. Each packet is a record of header fields that are highly redundant within a flow, and a compressor is a small set of rules matching such records and replacing predictable fields with short codes. We cast rule learning as a two-stage problem: (i) an unsupervised structure-discovery stage that recursively partitions training packets using a normalized entropy-ratio criterion robust to small samples, and (ii) a constrained selection stage that uses dynamic programming to pick the rule subset maximizing expected compression gain under a hard budget on the number of installable rules. We instantiate the framework on Static Context Header Compression (SCHC), the IETF standard for rule-based header compression in constrained networks, and evaluate it on four real-world Internet-of-Things and 5G core-network datasets. Our method, Robust Entropy Clustering for Adaptive comPression (RECAP), surpasses expert-engineered rule sets with a small number of learned rules and removes the need for manual rule design.

</details>


### [170] [A Model Merging Approach for Continual MLLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04548)
*Yuhang Wang,Linlin Zhang,Haoxuan Ji,Xianmin Ye,Zhenxing Niu,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCU方法，通过动态合并多个一次性遗忘适配器来解决多模态大语言模型的持续遗忘问题，利用leave-one-out合并分析揭示适配器间的跨任务依赖，并通过共享表示空间投影、主方向保留、过度集中坐标抑制和跨任务依赖重构来兼顾遗忘效果与知识保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM遗忘方法仅针对一次性请求设计，无法处理持续遗忘场景。反复应用一次性操作会导致累积效用退化、遗忘回弹和知识保持漂移等严重问题，亟需一种能动态适应持续遗忘请求的解决方案。

Method: MCU方法在收到每个新的遗忘请求时，将多个一次性遗忘适配器动态合并为统一适配器。首先通过leave-one-out合并分析发现遗忘适配器间存在强跨任务依赖，这些依赖兼具正面（促进跨任务遗忘迁移）和负面（引入干扰降低遗忘效果并损害保留知识）效应。MCU将适配器投影到共享表示空间，保留主导方向，抑制过度集中的坐标，并重构跨任务依赖以减轻干扰同时增强迁移能力。

Result: 在ICU-Bench和MLLMU-Bench基准上的实验表明，MCU在遗忘有效性方面表现优越，同时有效保留了已学知识和通用多模态能力。

Conclusion: MCU通过智能合并遗忘适配器并重构跨任务依赖，成功解决了MLLM持续遗忘中的关键挑战，在保证遗忘效果的同时维护了模型的其他知识不受损害，为持续遗忘场景提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) unlearning methods have been proposed to remove private, sensitive, or proprietary information from well-trained models. However, most existing MLLM unlearning methods are designed for one-shot requests and fail to adequately address continual scenarios, as repeatedly applying one-shot operations leads to cumulative utility degradation, unlearning rebound, and retention drift. We introduce Merging for Continual Unlearning (MCU), an approach that dynamically merges multiple one-shot unlearning adapters into a unified adapter upon receiving each new unlearning request.Through a leave-one-out merging analysis, we reveal that these unlearning adapters exhibit strong cross-task dependencies. Such dependencies have two contrasting effects: they can facilitate cross-task unlearning transferability, but they can also introduce severe interference that degrades unlearning effectiveness and compromises retained knowledge. To address this challenge, MCU projects the adapters into a shared representation space, preserves their dominant directions, suppresses over-concentrated coordinates, and reconfigures cross-task dependencies to mitigate interference while enhancing transferability. Experiments on ICU-Bench and MLLMU-Bench demonstrate that MCU achieves superior unlearning effectiveness while preserving both retained knowledge and general multimodal utility.

</details>


### [171] [Rethinking Reservoir Pruning: A Dynamical Perspective for Echo State Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04593)
*Sudip Laudari,Puspa Raj Adhikari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dynamical Mode Pruning (DMP)方法，通过轨迹平均Jacobian Gramian识别主导转移模式，按神经元对动态模式的贡献进行储层剪枝，仅重训读出层，在混沌和真实时间序列基准上改善或保持预测精度同时减少冗余储层组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回波状态网络（ESN）虽然为时序预测提供了高效框架，但其随机初始化的储层往往过参数化且存在动态冗余。现有剪枝方法多依赖静态连接性或激活统计，可能忽略那些塑造输入驱动状态转移的神经元，需要一种基于动态影响的剪枝标准。

Method: 从轨迹平均的Jacobian Gramian中获取主导转移模式，据此对储层神经元按其对模式的贡献度进行排序；移除低贡献单元后仅对读出层进行重训练，不改变其余储层结构。

Result: 在混沌系统和真实世界时间序列基准上，DMP能够改善或保持预测准确率，同时有效减少冗余储层组件数量。

Conclusion: 动态影响力是储层精炼中超越静态结构重要性的有效剪枝准则，DMP提供了一种在保持预测性能的同时压缩储层规模的新途径。

Abstract: Echo State Networks (ESNs) offer an efficient framework for temporal prediction, but their randomly initialized reservoirs are often over-parameterized and dynamically redundant. Existing pruning methods largely rely on static connectivity or activation statistics, which may overlook neurons that shape input-driven state transitions. We propose Dynamical Mode Pruning (DMP), a reservoir pruning method that ranks neurons by their contribution to dominant transition modes obtained from a trajectory-averaged Jacobian Gramian. DMP removes low-impact units and retrains only the readout. Experiments on chaotic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that DMP improves or preserves forecasting accuracy while reducing redundant reservoir components. Our results suggest that dynamical influence is a useful criterion for reservoir refinement beyond static structural importance alone.

</details>


### [172] [Why Ranking Anomaly Detection Algorithms Isn't as Reliable as You May Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04613)
*Simon Klüttermann,Jérôme Rutinowski,Frederik Polachowski,Alice Kirchheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了异常检测基准测试中常见选择对算法排名稳定性的影响，提出了一种排名不稳定度量方法，并使用7种代表性算法和690个数据集进行大规模实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测是安全关键的机器学习问题，但大量新算法声称达到最优性能时，使用的基准设置各不相同且互不一致。这种缺乏可比性的现状引发了对异常检测基准测试可重复性和可靠性的担忧。

Method: 使用7种代表性异常检测算法和690个来自OddBench基准套件的数据集，分析算法排名在不同数据集选择、评估指标、超参数配置和随机种子下的变化情况，并引入一种排名不稳定度量来量化算法排名在基准设置间的变异性。

Result: 异常检测中的算法排名高度不稳定，几乎每种有竞争力的算法都可以在某些基准配置下成为最佳方法。其中数据集选择和超参数选择对排名不确定性的贡献最大，而随机种子和评估指标的影响相对有限。可靠的基准测试需要比以往研究更大更多样化的数据集集合。

Conclusion: 异常检测领域的基准测试存在严重的排名不稳定问题，当前常用的基准设置不足以支撑可靠的方法比较，需要采用更大规模、更多样化的数据集集合来进行可靠的算法评估。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is a safety-critical machine learning problem with applications ranging from fraud detection to network intrusion prevention and industrial monitoring. Despite the large number of proposed anomaly detection algorithms, many novel methods claim state-of-the-art performance. However, many authors do so under benchmark settings that are not aligned with one another. This lack of comparability raises concerns regarding the reproducibility and reliability of anomaly detection benchmarks.
  In this work, we study the impact of common benchmarking choices on the stability of algorithm rankings. Using seven representative anomaly detection algorithms and 690 datasets from the OddBench benchmark suite, we analyze how rankings change under varying dataset selections, evaluation metrics, hyperparameter configurations, and random seeds. To quantify this effect, we introduce a rank instability metric measuring the variability of algorithm rankings across benchmark settings.
  Our results show that algorithm rankings in anomaly detection are highly unstable. In many cases, almost every competitive algorithm can appear as the best-performing method under some benchmark configuration. Among the studied factors, dataset selection and hyperparameter choice contribute most strongly to ranking uncertainty, while random seeds and evaluation metrics have a comparatively limited impact. We also observe that reliable benchmarking requires substantially larger and more diverse dataset collections than the ones commonly used in prior work.

</details>


### [173] [An entropic explanation of insistence on sameness in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04616)
*Przemysław Śliwiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息论的框架，将自闭症中对相同性的坚持解释为个体减少意外和不确定性的一般行为模式的具体体现。该框架将自闭症重新定义为认知功能仅局限于对环境中可感知属性的辨别、记忆和预测的障碍，并通过条件熵度量D_H(R,M)来形式化分析这一行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图为自闭症中对相同性的坚持（insistence on sameness）提供一个统一的形式化解释框架。现有的自闭症理论和解释多为描述性或定性，缺乏可量化和可计算的理论基础。作者希望借助信息论，将这一现象纳入更一般的行为模式中，并最终为自闭症的治疗和护理提供可操作的设计原则。

Method: 作者采用信息论方法，定义了一个条件熵度量D_H(R,M)=H(R|M)+H(M|R)，其中R为随机刺激序列，M为存储和检索刺激的记忆。该度量分别以条件熵的两个方向解释"惊喜"（surprise）和"不确定性"（uncertainty）。作者论证最小化该度量有两条路径：(1)个体学习关于R的知识并存入M；(2)个体将R限制为已知的M。并得出结论，自闭症中对相同性的坚持是后者的表现。此外，框架还可用于量化多个相关概念，并可操作化为优化算法，用于设计和实现机器人护理人员的程序。

Result: 该框架能够：(1)量化惊喜、不确定性、感觉过载/剥夺、焦虑、舒适区、失望、迷失、迂腐、刻板等概念；(2)生成学习疗法和日常护理指南，并可将其定义为优化算法、实现为机器人生活护理人员的程序；(3)可通过类图灵测试的方法进行验证，无需涉及自闭症患者的实验。

Conclusion: 若该框架获得正向验证，将为改善自闭症个体基本日常生活自理能力的疗法提供形式化基础与设计指南。

Abstract: An information theory-based framework is proposed in attempt to explain insistence on sameness in autism as an instance of a general behavior pattern in which an individual tries to reduce surprise and uncertainty. It offers a new definition of autism as an impairment in which cognitive functions are restricted to discrimination, memorization and prediction of tangible properties of the environment. An analogy between insistence on sameness and constrained minimization of the entropy metric is observed and examined for a set of assumptions that describe cognitive limitations of a person with autism. The metric is given by the formula $D_H(R, M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R)$, where $R$ represents sequences of random stimuli, $M$ is a memory that stores and retrieves them, and where $H(.|.)$ denotes their conditional entropies interpreted as surprise and uncertainty, respectively. It is first inferred that to minimize the metric an individual can learn about $R$ (and store that knowledge in $M$) or can restrict $R$ to the already known $M$. Then, it is concluded that insistence on sameness is a manifestation of the latter. Moreover, it is shown that the proposed framework: (1) Helps to quantify the concepts of surprise, uncertainty, sensory overload and deprivation, anxiety, comfort zone, disappointment, disorientation, pedantry, rigidness, observance or aberrant precision. (2) Leads to a list of guidelines for learning therapies and daily care routines, and allows them to be defined as optimization algorithms and implemented as programs for robotic live-in caregivers. (3) Can be validated with the help of a Turing test-like approach that requires no experiments involving individuals with autism. The framework-if positively validated-will provide formal foundations and design guidelines for therapies aimed at improving self-reliance of individuals with autism in basic activities of daily living.

</details>


### [174] [Active Learning Guided Design Space Refinement for Scalable Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization in Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04651)
*Alexandros Ntagiantas,Panagiotis Tsilimidos,George Giannakopoulos,Christoforos Rekatsinas,Panagiotis Krokidas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种主动学习引导的自适应搜索空间细化框架，结合多目标贝叶斯优化（BO）加速材料发现。在CH4/N2分离的共价有机框架和压力容器设计中验证，候选空间缩减约一半，同时保留超过99%的原始超体积，并改善早期收敛和累积Pareto前沿发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化在候选空间庞大时效率低下，往往在抵达高价值区域前就评估了较多低价值区域，限制了在有限评估预算下的大规模材料发现。

Method: 提出主动学习（AL）引导的自适应搜索空间细化框架，与多目标贝叶斯优化相结合。通过主动学习识别并剔除低价值设计区域，在保持Pareto相关区域的同时缩小候选空间，再在多目标BO框架中执行优化。

Result: 在CH4/N2分离（COF材料）和压力容器设计两个案例中，AL引导的细化使候选空间缩减约50%，同时保留超过99%的原始超体积；细化后的空间策略改善了BO的早期收敛和累积Pareto前沿发现。

Conclusion: 主动学习引导的搜索空间细化能够有效提升大规模、受约束的自主材料发现场景中多目标贝叶斯优化的效率和收敛表现。

Abstract: Advanced materials discovery increasingly relies on machine learning and Bayesian optimization to explore large discrete design spaces under limited evaluation budgets. However, conventional Bayesian optimization (BO) can become inefficient as candidate spaces grow, often evaluating low-value regions before reaching informative areas. We propose an active-learning (AL)-guided adaptive search-space refinement framework combined with multi-objective BO to accelerate materials optimization while preserving Pareto-relevant regions. We evaluate the approach on CH4/N2 separation in covalent-organic frameworks and pressure-vessel design with material-direction stress components and thickness objectives. Results show that the AL-guided refinement reduces the candidate space by approximately half while preserving more than 99 percent of the original hypervolume. The reduced-space strategy improves early convergence and cumulative Pareto-front discovery from the BO, demonstrating efficient large-scale materials optimization across constrained autonomous materials discovery settings.

</details>


### [175] [Differentiating Through Dual Prices: End-to-End Policy Learning Under Capacity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04669)
*Mohammadsaeed Haghi,Mahdi Salmani,Nima Kelidari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从日志数据中学习稀缺资源在线分配策略的问题。传统"决策盲"流程先分别拟合每个行动的结果模型，再通过回归定价和容量约束来分配资源；作者提出端到端训练方法，通过离线策略估计值对对偶价格进行微分。论文提出一个精确非凸公式和一个凸松弛公式，后者在期望上满足容量约束且次优性有界。在六个数据集（含七万患者住院队列）的队列模拟中，端到端变体在部署调整价值指标上领先，容量紧张环境下决策盲基线频繁违约容量并产生更长排队延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多社会服务（如住房援助、医院干预）需要将稀缺资源分配给逐个到达的个体，每次到达需立即决策且长期资源使用须在容量内。标准做法是"决策盲"管道：先分别回归拟合每个臂的结果模型，再对模型定价分配。这种方法将模型学习与决策优化割裂，可能导致容量违约和较差的部署价值。作者希望训练端到端的目标模型，通过区分对偶价格来直接优化部署策略价值。

Method: 作者提出两种端到端公式化：(1) 精确非凸公式，通过离线策略估计值对双价格进行微分来端到端训练结果模型；(2) 凸松弛，其最优解在期望上满足容量约束，次优性与平滑温度和臂数量的对数成正比。实验中在资源以容量速率补充的队列仿真中评估，比较了端到端变体与决策盲基线（含容量匹配的神经网络基线）。

Result: 在六个数据集上，两种端到端变体在每种延迟成本（包括零）下占据部署调整价值指数前两名。当容量紧张时，决策盲基线频繁违反容量约束并产生更长的排队延迟。在最大的七万患者医院队列数据集中，端到端训练显著获得更高策略价值，且优势在面对容量匹配的神经网络基线时依然存在。然而在可测量真实标签的纯预测任务上，灵活的决策盲回归仍是更强的预测器。

Conclusion: 端到端训练最适合资源真正稀缺、可行性至关重要的场景；决策盲回归在纯预测场景中仍具优势。本研究为稀缺资源在线分配提供了新的端到端学习范式，既保证了容量可行性又有界次优性保证。

Abstract: Many social services assign scarce resources, such as housing assistance or hospital interventions, to people who arrive one at a time: each arrival must receive a decision immediately, and the long-run usage of every resource must stay within its capacity. We study how to learn such an assignment policy from logged observational data. The standard pipeline is decision-blind: fit one outcome model per arm by regression, price each capacitated resource from the fitted models, and assign each arrival the arm whose predicted outcome minus price is largest. We instead train the outcome models end-to-end, differentiating an off-policy estimate of the deployed policy's value through the dual prices themselves. We study two formulations: an exact nonconvex one, and a convex relaxation whose optimum always satisfies the capacity constraints in expectation and which is suboptimal by at most a term linear in the smoothing temperature and logarithmic in the number of arms. Every method is evaluated in a queueing simulation with resources replenished at their capacity rates. Across six datasets, the two end-to-end variants take the top slots on a deployment-adjusted value index at every delay cost, including zero; when capacities are binding, decision-blind baselines frequently violate them and incur much longer queueing delays. On the largest dataset, a hospital cohort of seventy thousand patients, end-to-end training also achieves significantly higher policy value, a margin that survives a capacity-matched neural baseline. Flexible decision-blind regression remains the stronger pure predictor where ground truth is measurable; end-to-end training is best suited to settings where resources are genuinely scarce and feasibility matters.

</details>


### [176] [Diverse and Plausible Algorithmic Recourse via Tractable Recourse Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04677)
*Anagha Sabu,Hrithik Suresh,Narayanan C. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可处理的追索分布（Tractable Recourse Distributions），一个概率框架，将给定事实实例的可行替代方案空间表示为有利结果上的概率分布。该分布通过指数倾斜概率电路得到精确表示，无需重新训练模型即可获得每个个体的闭合形式分布，采样可自然产生多样且合理的追索建议，实验证明该方法能同时兼顾多样性、合理性和可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法将问题构建为优化问题，仅生成一个或少量反事实，而非建模底层可行解空间。实践中这些方法往往在多样性、合理性和可行性之间相互牺牲，无法为不同个体提供多个现实可行的替代路径。

Method: 提出概率框架，将可行替代方案空间表示为有利结果上的概率分布。对于基于邻近性和特征变化数的常用成本函数，证明该分布可通过指数倾斜概率电路获得精确表示；每个个体的分布以闭合形式可用，无需重新训练模型；倾斜参数提供对邻近性和稀疏性的显式控制。

Result: 在标准算法追索基准数据集上，该框架能同时获得多样性、合理性和可行性，并在可行反事实上保留足够概率质量使拒绝采样切实可行。MNIST上的可视化研究表明倾斜强度可权衡邻近性与有效性。

Conclusion: 本文展示了通过概率建模而非优化求解来建模追索空间的可能性，避免了现有方法在多样性、合理性和可行性之间的权衡困境。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse seeks to help individuals reverse unfavorable automated decisions by recommending actionable changes that achieve a desired outcome. As an individual usually has several distinct routes to a favorable decision, and different people can act on different ones, a recourse system should offer multiple realistic alternatives rather than one. Existing approaches formulate recourse as an optimization problem that constructs one or a small set of counterfactuals rather than modeling the underlying space of feasible solutions, and in practice each sacrifices diversity, plausibility, or feasibility to secure the others. We propose Tractable Recourse Distributions, a probabilistic framework that represents the space of feasible alternatives for a given factual instance as a probability distribution over favorable outcomes. For commonly used cost functions based on proximity and the number of feature changes, we show that this distribution admits an exact representation as a probabilistic circuit, obtained by exponentially tilting the circuit; each individual's distribution is therefore available in closed form, without retraining the model. Sampling from these distributions naturally produces diverse and plausible recourses, while the tilting parameters provide explicit control over their proximity and sparsity. Experiments on standard algorithmic recourse benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework attains diversity, plausibility, and feasibility simultaneously, while retaining sufficient probability mass over feasible counterfactuals for rejection sampling to be practical. A visual study on MNIST illustrates how the tilt strength trades proximity against validity.

</details>


### [177] [The Sample Complexity of Distributionally Robust PAC Learning under Cressie--Read Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04686)
*Elad Aigner-Horev,Daniel Rosenberg,Roi Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究0-1损失下的分布鲁棒PAC学习，在Cressie-Read散度（阶数k>1）约束的对抗性分布扰动下，建立了可实现和不可知两种情形下紧至常数/对数因子的样本复杂度界，并揭示了鲁棒性导致的ε依赖指数转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布鲁棒学习在对抗鲁棒性中具有重要意义，但现有工作主要集中在χ²散度特例上，且其边界在ρ→0极限下不能正确退化为标准PAC学习率。本文旨在将结果推广到所有Cressie-Read阶数k>1，并消除上下界之间的差距。

Method: 基于鲁棒0-1风险到普通分类误差的已知标量约化，分析分类误差统计估计与其被鲁棒性放大之间的尺度敏感交互作用；对VC维为d的假设类建立可实现与不可知两种情形下的样本复杂度界，并证明普通经验风险最小化（ERM）可达到该界（至多对数因子）。

Result: 建立了紧至常数因子（可实现情形）和对数因子（不可知情形）的样本复杂度界。对于固定ρ>0，可实现情形下ε依赖从ε^{-1}变为ε^{-k_*}(k_*=k/(k-1))；不可知情形下，当1<k<2时从ε^{-2}变为ε^{-k_*}，当k≥2时指数保持经典值2但ρ依赖非平凡。此外，当ρ→0时恢复标准PAC学习率。

Conclusion: 本文将分布鲁棒PAC学习的分析从χ²散度特例推广到所有Cressie-Read阶数k>1，封闭了上下界间隙并正确插值到标准PAC率，同时揭示了不可知率转变背后的尺度敏感统计-放大交互机制。

Abstract: We study distributionally robust PAC learning for the $0$--$1$-loss, where adversarial perturbations of the data distribution are constrained by a Cressie--Read divergence of order $k>1$ and radius $ρ\geq 0$. For hypothesis classes with VC dimension $d$, we establish realizable and agnostic sample-complexity bounds tight up to constant and logarithmic factors, respectively; ordinary empirical risk minimization attains both rates up to logarithmic factors. For target accuracy $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$ and confidence $δ\in(0,1)$, their respective orders are \[ \max\!\left\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}, \frac{ρ^{\frac 1{k-1}}}{\varepsilon^{k_\star}} \right\}\cdot(d+\log δ^{-1}) \qquad\text{and}\qquad \max\!\left\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}, \frac{ρ^{\frac1{k-1}}}{\varepsilon^{k_\star\vee 2}} \right\}\cdot(d+\log δ^{-1}), \] where $k_\star={k}/{(k-1)}$. For every fixed $ρ>0$, robustness changes the realizable $\varepsilon$-dependence from $\varepsilon^{-1}$ to $\varepsilon^{-k_\star}$ as $\varepsilon\downarrow0$. In the agnostic case, for $1<k<2$, robustness changes the $\varepsilon$-dependence from $\varepsilon^{-2}$ to $\varepsilon^{-k_\star}$, whereas for $k\geq2$ the exponent remains the classical $2$, with nontrivial $ρ$-dependence.
  Building on the known scalar reduction of robust $0$--$1$ risk to ordinary classification error, our analysis reveals a scale-sensitive interaction between the statistical estimation of classification error and its amplification by robustness, sharply explaining the transition in the agnostic rate. We extend the previously studied $χ^2$-divergence case to every Cressie--Read order $k>1$, close its upper--lower gaps, and recover standard PAC learning rates as $ρ\to0$, unlike previous bounds that fail to interpolate correctly in this limit.

</details>


### [178] [Personalized Federated Sparse Adaptation of Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04695)
*Priyanka Nihalchandani,Naman Srivastava,Varun Ojha,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向建筑能耗预测的个性化联邦稀疏适配框架，将基于异构时间混合专家（MoE）的适配器置于预训练时间序列基础模型（TSFM）之后，通过序列级路由器将每个168小时上下文窗口映射到针对周期性、长程交互、局部变化、趋势残差和多分辨率行为特化的top-k专家子集上。在50栋建筑和三种TSFM骨干上的实验表明，个性化方案一致优于全局FL-MoE和局部MoE，且最佳稀疏适配策略因骨干网络和评估指标而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦适配TSFM用于建筑能耗预测具有吸引力（仪表数据私密、分布且高度非IID），但单一的参数共享策略难以适用于所有预训练TSFM或建筑客户端：完全共享适配器会抑制建筑特定的时间行为，而完全局部适配则丢弃跨建筑迁移能力。

Method: 提出个性化联邦稀疏适配框架，包含放置在预训练TSFM表示之后的异构时间MoE适配器。采用序列级路由器将每个168小时上下文窗口路由到top-k个专家，这些专家分别特化于周期性、长程交互、局部变化、趋势残差结构及多分辨率行为。比较了全局FL、局部训练以及使用全局共享或客户端私有专家库的个性化FL变体。

Result: 在50栋建筑和三种TSFM骨干上的实验中，个性化方法一致优于全局FL-MoE和局部MoE。最佳稀疏适配策略因骨干网络和指标而异，路由行为揭示了客户端级专家特化、专家集中度以及跨骨干网络接近均匀的路由模式。

Conclusion: 联邦TSFM适配应当同时是客户端感知和骨干感知的，不能采用一刀切的参数共享策略。

Abstract: Federated adaptation of time-series foundation models (TSFMs) is attractive for building energy forecasting because meter data are private, distributed, and highly non-IID. However, a single parameter-sharing strategy is unlikely to serve all pretrained TSFMs or building clients: fully shared adapters can suppress building-specific temporal behavior, while fully local adaptation discards cross-building transfer. We propose a personalized federated sparse adaptation framework with a heterogeneous temporal mixture-of-experts (MoE) adapter placed after the pretrained TSFM representation. A sequence-level router maps each 168-hour context window to a top-$k$ subset of experts specialized for periodicity, long-range interactions, local variation, trend-residual structure, and multi-resolution behavior. We compare global FL, local training, and personalized FL variants with globally shared or client-private expert banks. Across 50 buildings and three TSFM backbones, personalization consistently outperforms Global FL-MoE and Local MoE, while the best sparse-adaptation strategy varies by backbone and metric. Routing behavior further reveals client-level expert specialization, expert concentration, and near-uniform routing across backbones, showing that federated TSFM adaptation should be both client-aware and backbone-aware.

</details>


### [179] [Design Choices That Matter: A Functional ANOVA Analysis for Remote Sensing Multi-Label Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04702)
*Maryam Gholami Shiri,Eva Tuba,Sašo Džeroski,Tome Eftimov,Ana Nikolikj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用功能方差分析（fANOVA）取代传统的模型排名，系统量化遥感图像多标签分类中不同设计选择（网络架构、微调策略、学习策略、初始化）及其交互作用对模型性能的贡献，并通过数据集元表征聚类揭示数据集内在属性与设计选择敏感性之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习模型在遥感图像多标签分类任务上的基准测试排名无法在不同数据集间泛化，缺乏对模型性能差异来源的系统性归因分析。作者希望超越简单的模型排名，量化不同设计选择及其交互作用对性能变异的具体贡献，从而为模型选择和设计提供更可迁移的指导。

Method: 采用功能方差分析（fANOVA）作为核心方法，对48个和20个深度学习模型分别进行两组实证分析，覆盖网络架构、微调策略、学习策略和初始化等设计维度。在七个多标签遥感数据集上应用fANOVA，构建数据集级元表征（即设计选择敏感性画像），并利用层次聚类分析这些元表征以揭示数据集之间的分组关系。

Result: 研究发现数据集会根据其对设计决策的响应模式自然分组，这种分组与数据集的内在属性（如尺度、空间分辨率和标签空间复杂度）密切相关。具体而言：大规模数据集上微调策略和架构是主导因素；数据受限场景下初始化起决定性作用；中间规模场景下架构与学习策略的交互主导性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过fANOVA框架能够获得跨数据集可迁移的设计洞察，为不同数据条件下的遥感图像多标签分类模型构建提供了基于数据的、可解释的设计选择指导，超越了单一基准排名所提供的有限信息。

Abstract: Benchmarking deep learning (DL) models for multi-label classification (MLC) of remote sensing images (RSI) typically yields rankings that do not generalize beyond the evaluated datasets. In this work, we move beyond rankings by employing functional analysis of variance (fANOVA) to systematically quantify the contributions of individual design choices and their interactions to performance variability. We conduct two empirical analyses covering 48 and 20 DL models, respectively, spanning design choices such as network architecture, fine-tuning strategy, learning strategy, and initialization. By applying fANOVA across seven MLC RSI datasets, we construct dataset meta-representations that capture design-choice sensitivity profiles. Hierarchical clustering of these meta-representations reveals that datasets naturally group according to how they respond to design decisions, with patterns strongly linked to intrinsic dataset properties such as scale, spatial resolution, and label space complexity. Our findings show that for large-scale datasets, fine-tuning strategy and architecture are dominant factors, while in data-limited regimes, initialization becomes decisive. For intermediate regimes, the interaction between architecture and learning strategy governs performance.

</details>


### [180] [Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Dense Event Classification of Offshore Wind Infrastructure in Sentinel-1 Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04706)
*Thorsten Hoeser,Felix Bachofer,Claudia Kuenzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用ESA Sentinel-1 SAR数据，系统比较了十种深度学习模型变体（LSTM、Transformer、全连接网络，结合单时相、单向、双向上下文感知及自监督预训练）用于海上风电基础设施时序数据的密集分类。有监督BiLSTM表现最佳，将AUC从0.7853提升至0.8509，完美匹配率从0.3508提升至0.5063，并结合标签转移最小化集成进一步提升效果，最终实现全球海上风机部署阶段的区域级分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风电基础设施生命周期的监测（尤其是部署阶段）对利益相关者的决策至关重要。Sentinel-1 SAR任务产生海量数据档案，但将这些全球尺度的时序数据转化为信息需要能够自动从密集时序中提取单一事件标签的算法。现有基于规则的事件分类方法性能有限，亟需更先进的深度学习方法来推进这一任务。

Method: 对十种深度学习模型训练变体进行结构化比较：包括LSTM、Transformer和全连接网络，每种模型分别配置单时相、单方向和双向上下文感知，各含/不含自监督预训练。采用有监督BiLSTM模型，并设计标签转移最小化集成策略，将BiLSTM预测与现有规则基线标签结合，以改善与测试数据的一致性。

Result: 有监督BiLSTM表现最佳，目标AUC从基线的0.7853提升至0.8509，完美匹配率从0.3508提升至0.5063。利用改进标签在全球尺度隔离个体风机部署阶段，覆盖2016-01-01至2025-03-31的分析显示：中位部署持续时间为中国84天、欧盟242天、英国258天，并在多空间尺度上清晰呈现了补贴等法律监管和自然环境条件等部署驱动因素。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型（尤其是有监督BiLSTM）结合集成策略能够显著超越基于规则的基线方法，实现海上风电基础设施时序事件的自动化分类。该方法成功实现了全球尺度风机部署阶段的识别与区域分析，并揭示部署时长与法律监管和自然环境条件之间的关联，为利益相关者决策提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Monitoring of offshore wind energy infrastructure life cycles, especially during the deployment phase, is an important contribution for stakeholders to make informed decisions in a phase of increasing deployment activities. ESA's Sentinel-1 Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) mission produces large data archives that enable the global monitoring of offshore wind infrastructure. Turning these high-volume archives into information requires algorithms that automatically extract single event labels from dense time series at a global scale. In this study, we present a structured comparison of ten deep learning model-training variants for the dense classification of Sentinel-1 based offshore wind infrastructure time series, aiming to advance rule-based event classification of this task. We trained LSTM, Transformer, and fully connected model variants with monotemporal, unidirectional, and bidirectional context awareness, each with and without self-supervised pretraining. Among these, the supervised BiLSTM performs best, raising the target AUC score from 0.7853 for the rule-based baseline to 0.8509, and the perfect match rate from 0.3508 to 0.5063. Combining the BiLSTM predictions with the existing baseline labels in a label-transition-minimising ensemble further improves agreement with the test data. Using these improved labels, we isolate the deployment phase of individual turbines at a global scale and conduct a regional and subregional analysis covering 2016-01-01 to 2025-03-31, reporting median deployment durations of 84 d (China), 242 d (EU), and 258 d (UK). Deployment-related drivers, including legal regulations such as subsidies, and environmental conditions, emerge clearly from the analysed results across multiple spatial scales.

</details>


### [181] [A 6G Integrated Sensing and Communication Framework for Railway Intrusion Detection and Collision Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04710)
*Ajeet Kumar Yadav,Sankaran Balasubramaniam,Aritra Chatterjee,Vinod Aduru,Yogesh Simmhan,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对3GPP Release 19定义的"铁路入侵检测"ISAC用例，利用Sionna仿真器在3D渲染铁路环境中生成22,695个CSI矩阵，开发了结合3D CNN和BiLSTM的机器学习模型，实现99.57%的入侵者检测准确率和0.4240的综合MAE（位置、速度、碰撞时间预测），验证了基于CSI的ISAC感知在铁路入侵检测中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC作为下一代无线网络的关键范式可高效利用无线资源，3GPP Release 19明确了32个ISAC用例，其中铁路入侵检测具有重要的安全价值——野生动物等入侵者进入轨道会造成严重碰撞风险，需要利用CSI实现物理层感知来检测和追踪移动物体。

Method: 使用3D渲染铁路环境和Sionna无线电仿真器生成22,695个CSI矩阵及对应的真实标签数据；构建结合三维卷积神经网络（3D CNN）和双向长短期记忆网络（BiLSTM）的机器学习模型，用于检测轨道危险区域内的入侵者，并实时估计其相对列车的位置、速度和碰撞时间。

Result: 在合成CSI数据上，模型在平衡测试集上达到99.57%的入侵检测准确率；位置、速度和碰撞时间预测的综合平均绝对误差（MAE）为0.4240。

Conclusion: 结果表明基于CSI的ISAC感知结合机器学习在铁路入侵检测中具有显著潜力，代码库已公开以支持进一步研究与复现。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) combines sensing and communication to efficiently utilize wireless resources and is emerging as a key paradigm for next-generation wireless networks. By leveraging the wide bandwidth, high frequencies, and massive antenna arrays of 5G-Advanced and 6G systems, ISAC enables physical-layer sensing using Channel State Information (CSI). The 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) Release 19 identifies 32 potential ISAC use cases, with particular emphasis on detecting and tracking moving objects. In this work, we address the Sensing for Railway Intrusion Detection use case, where intruders, including wildlife, entering a railway track can pose serious collision risks. We generated 22,695 CSI matrices with corresponding ground truth using a 3D-rendered railway environment and the Sionna radio simulator. We developed a machine learning model combining a three-dimensional Convolutional Neural Network (3D CNN) and Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) network to detect intruders in the track danger zone and estimate their real-time position relative to the train, velocity, and time to collision. On synthetic CSI data, the model achieves 99.57% intruder-detection accuracy on a balanced test set and a combined Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.4240 for position, velocity, and time-to-collision prediction. These results demonstrate the potential of CSI-based ISAC sensing with machine learning for reliable railway intrusion detection. The complete codebase for CSI generation, preprocessing, and model development is publicly available at https://github.com/EdgeIntelligenceLab/6g-isac-railway-intrusion-detection.

</details>


### [182] [Attention, Anomalies! Handling Attention Layers in Unsupervised Federated Outlier Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04753)
*Mihailo Ilić,Miloš Savić,Vladimir Kurbalija,Mirjana Ivanović,Giancarlo Fortino,Dušan Jakovetić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中内存增强自编码器（MemAE）缺乏专门聚合技术的问题，提出了新颖的引导式聚合方法，在非IID数据集和资源受限环境下显著提升了无监督异常检测的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力层已成为现代模型的核心组件，但在联邦学习场景下，MemAE模型缺乏专门的聚合技术。现有方法仅在集中式学习场景中表现良好，需要开发适合MemAE模型的联邦聚合策略。

Method: 作者深入分析了MemAE架构的内在特点，提出了针对性的引导式（guided）联邦聚合方案，专门用于在联邦学习环境中聚合MemAE模型参数，以应对非IID和不平衡数据集分布。

Result: 在非IID数据集上的实验表明，所提出的聚合方案在大量边缘节点和不平衡数据集环境下更加鲁棒，尤其适用于无监督异常检测场景。即使是非常浅层的自编码器也能获得性能提升，适合资源受限环境。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了联邦学习中MemAE模型聚合技术的空白，证明引导式聚合方案能够在分布式、非IID和不平衡环境下提升无监督异常检测性能，并支持浅层模型在资源受限场景中的部署。

Abstract: Attention layers are the backbone of today's most powerful and impactful models. Models with multi-million and billion parameters rely on contextual knowledge provided by attention layers. However, their use goes well beyond just being the core component of large language models. One particularly interesting application is in Memory Augmented Autoencoders (MemAE), specifically for unsupervised representation learning in outlier detection tasks. It was shown that attention helps these models be more effective in centralized learning scenarios. Our work aims to address the lack of specialized aggregation techniques in Federated Learning (FL) when it comes to MemAE models. In this paper we analyze the intricacies of the architecture behind Memory Augmented Autoencoders, and propose novel, guided approaches to effectively aggregate these models in federated scenarios. We demonstrate our approach on non-IID datasets and show that these novel aggregation schemes are more robust when dealing with numerous edge nodes in environments with unbalanced datasets, specifically for unsupervised anomaly detection scenarios. This approach improves the performance of even very shallow autoencoders, allowing them to be used in resource constrained environments.

</details>


### [183] [IMFACT: Counterfactual Explanations for Time Series via Intrinsic Mode Function Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04777)
*Udo Schlegel,Julian Rakuschek,Thomas Seidl,Andreas Holzinger,Tobias Schreck,Javier Del Ser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IMFACT框架，一种基于经验模态分解（EMD）的模型无关反事实解释生成方法，通过将时间序列分解为固有模态函数（IMFs）并逐步替换最近异类邻居（NUN）的IMF分量来生成物理上合理的反事实样本，在两个UCR基准数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡信号（如振动）的类别判别信息集中在特定频段，而传统的反事实分析方法在原始特征空间中扰动信号容易破坏其时序结构，产生物理上不合理的解释结果。因此需要一种在分解空间中操作的方法来保留信号的物理时序特征。

Method: IMFACT框架将输入信号通过经验模态分解（EMD）拆分为多个固有模态函数（IMFs），然后选择特定的IMF分量并用最近异类邻居（NUN）的对应IMF分量进行逐步替换，直到分类器分类结果翻转为目标类别。评估了六种不同的IMF选择策略以及多NUN循环扩展机制。

Result: 在两个UCR基准数据集（FaultDetectionA、FruitFlies）上，基于方差的选择策略配合三个NUN在可靠性和合理性指标上优于两种主流基线方法；而跨三个NUN的循环策略在邻近性（proximity）指标上取得两个数据集上的最佳表现。

Conclusion: 在分解空间而非原始特征空间中生成反事实解释，能够保留振荡信号的时序物理结构，IMFACT框架为时间序列分类器提供了一种更可靠、更合理的反事实解释生成方案。

Abstract: Oscillatory signals, such as vibration, carry class-discriminative information in specific frequency bands; perturbing them in raw feature space for counterfactual analysis easily destroys their temporal structure and produces physically implausible results. In this work, we introduce IMFACT (IMF-based counterfACTuals), a model-agnostic framework for generating plausible counterfactual explanations for time series classifiers that operates in the decomposition space of Empirical Mode Decomposition. An input signal is split into Intrinsic Mode Functions (IMFs), and selected IMFs are progressively substituted with those of a Nearest Unlike Neighbour (NUN) until the classifier flips to the target class. We evaluate six IMF-selection strategies and a multi-NUN cycling extension on two UCR benchmarks (FaultDetectionA, FruitFlies). The variance-based strategy with three NUNs outperforms two prominent baseline techniques on reliability and plausibility metrics, while cycling across three NUNs yields the best proximity across both datasets.

</details>


### [184] [Continual-Learning Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Parameterized Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04778)
*Xujia Chen,Xinyue Hu,Letian Chen,Yi Liu,Wenhui Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CL-PINN，一种基于持续学习的物理信息神经网络，通过贝叶斯优化的主动参数选择、任务级动态损失加权、稀疏物理约束回放及可选参数子网络，在无需观测数据且计算资源受限的条件下，高效学习参数化PDE在整个参数域上的解，相比现有固定采样和网格贪婪基线，在五个基准上实现了更高且更均衡的求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化PINN仍面临训练效率低下、不同参数间精度不均以及对有限采样参数任务过拟合等问题，导致对未采样参数的泛化能力受损，难以在较宽参数域和有限计算资源下部署。

Method: CL-PINN将不同参数下的PDE实例视为相关任务并按序学习，整合了四个关键组件：基于贝叶斯优化的主动参数选择、任务级动态损失加权、稀疏物理约束回放以及可选参数子网络，用于在有限主动任务容量下改善任务分配和知识保留。

Result: 在五个基准（一个连续函数和四个参数化PDE）上的多种子评估表明：贝叶斯选择相比网格贪婪搜索大幅减少目标损失查询次数；稀疏回放有效缓解了对早期任务的遗忘；在规定的资源协议下，CL-PINN总体上比固定采样和网格贪婪基线提供更高且更均衡的求解精度。

Conclusion: CL-PINN提供了一条实用的路径来学习跨物理参数泛化的PDE解，有潜力支持大规模工程参数研究中可复用的物理信息代理模型。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) incorporate governing equations into neural-network training and can approximate PDE solutions without requiring large observational datasets. Parameterized PINNs (ParamPINNs) further take physical parameters as inputs, allowing a single model to represent a family of PDE solutions over a parameter domain. Existing ParamPINNs, however, still face inefficient training, uneven accuracy across parameters, and overfitting to a limited set of sampled parameter tasks, which can impair generalization to unsampled parameters. To address these issues, we propose a continual-learning physics-informed neural network (CL-PINN), which treats PDE instances at different parameter values as related tasks and learns them sequentially. CL-PINN combines Bayesian-optimization-based active parameter selection, task-wise dynamic loss weighting, sparse physics-constrained replay, and an optional parameter subnetwork to improve task allocation and knowledge retention under bounded active-task capacity. It requires no observational data and is designed to solve parameterized PDEs over relatively broad parameter domains under limited computational resources. Multi-seed evaluations on five benchmarks, including one continuous function and four parameterized PDEs, show that Bayesian selection substantially reduces objective-loss queries relative to grid-greedy search, while sparse replay mitigates forgetting of earlier tasks. Under the prescribed within-case resource protocols, CL-PINN generally provides higher and more balanced solution accuracy than fixed-sampling and grid-greedy baselines. CL-PINN offers a practical route toward learning PDE solutions that generalize across physical parameters and has the potential to support reusable physics-informed surrogates for large-scale engineering parameter studies.

</details>


### [185] [Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04788)
*Yi Yang,Cong Qin,Xiaodan Liu,Chishui Chen,Qing Dong,Yan Zhang,Cao Liu,Zhao Yang,Lu Pan,Jiaye Lin,Yi Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation (OCSD)，通过对比结构匹配的Full和Observation-Ablated两种回放视图，消除回放脚手架引起的分数偏移混淆，提取纯化的观察残差来调制token级GRPO更新，在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent通常通过稀疏轨迹级奖励的强化学习训练，缺乏对单个token更新强度的细粒度指导。On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)通过特权回放视图重打分获得稠密token级监督，但存在的混淆问题是：回放支持的信号可能同时包含特权信息和回放脚手架引起的分数偏移，难以将支持归因于特定信息。当未来环境观察作为特权信息时，回放需要重建扩展脚手架，进一步扰动token分数，使问题更加严重。

Method: OCSD对比两种结构匹配的回放视图：完整视图(Full)和观察消融视图(Observation-Ablated)，二者仅在实际未来观察是否存在上不同。通过二者的差值推导出观察残差(observation residual)，从而折扣掉回放脚手架共享的分数变化。该残差用于在高不确定性步骤调制token级GRPO更新，同时保留轨迹级更新方向。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA三个环境上，覆盖三种Qwen3模型规模，OCSD一致性地超越强基线。诊断分析进一步确认校准后的残差与局部环境反馈的对齐度更好。代码已开源。

Conclusion: OCSD有效解决了OPSD中特权信息与回放脚手架效应混淆的问题，通过观察残差实现对token级更新信号的校准，从而提升LLM agent在多个环境下的强化学习训练效果。

Abstract: Large language model agents are commonly trained through reinforcement learning with sparse trajectory-level rewards, which offer limited guidance on how strongly individual tokens should be updated. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) addresses this by re-scoring generated tokens under a privileged replay view to obtain dense, token-level supervision. However, we identify a confounding issue: the resulting support may reflect both the privileged information contained in the replay view and score shifts induced by the replay scaffold, making it difficult to attribute the support specifically to that information. This issue is especially pronounced when future environment observations serve as privileged information, since replaying them requires reconstructing an extended scaffold that itself perturbs token scores. To resolve this confounding, we propose Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation (OCSD), which contrasts two structurally matched replay views, Full and Observation-Ablated, differing only in whether the actual future observation is present, to derive an observation residual that discounts score changes shared by the replay scaffold. OCSD then applies this residual to modulate token-level GRPO updates at high-uncertainty steps, while preserving the trajectory-level update direction. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA across three Qwen3 model scales show that OCSD consistently outperforms strong baselines. Diagnostic analyses further confirm that the calibrated residual aligns better with local environment feedback. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yiy1x/OCSD.

</details>


### [186] [Above-ground Biomass Estimation with Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04792)
*Ghjulia Sialellia,Linus Scheibenreif,Jan Dirk Wegner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全球尺度地上生物量（AGB）估算的地理空间基础模型（GFMs）综合基准测试。研究发现，以预训练权重形式分发的GFMs作为冻结编码器时明显逊于全监督SOTA模型，而预计算嵌入产品（如AlphaEarth Foundations和TESSERA）则表现优异——基于AEF嵌入训练的MLP即可超越SOTA，将AEF嵌入输入SOTA模型并可选结合原始特征则取得最佳效果，且空间和时间泛化能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算卫星影像的AGB对大规模碳储量监测至关重要，但在全球尺度上仍是具有挑战性的回归任务。GFMs作为新兴的机器学习范式有望从地球观测数据中获得通用表征，但其在定量回归任务（如生物量估算）中的效用尚未得到充分探索，因为大多数基准测试侧重于分类和分割任务。

Method: 使用AGBD数据集（一个覆盖多种生物群落和地理区域的机器学习就绪基准数据集），区分两类GFMs：(i) 以权重形式分发的模型——作为冻结编码器在PANGAEA基准框架中评估，共比较11个GFMs；(ii) 以预计算嵌入产品形式分发的模型——评估AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF)和TESSERA。所有模型与全监督SOTA模型对比，评估其地理和时间泛化能力，以及与ESA CCI生物量产品在独立参考数据上的一致性。

Result: 作为冻结编码器运行的GFMs在性能上大幅落后于监督SOTA模型；而预计算的嵌入产品则非常有效。基于AEF嵌入训练的MLP优于在AGBD特征上训练的监督SOTA模型；以AEF嵌入（可选辅以选定原始特征）训练的同一SOTA模型取得最佳总体结果，同时在空间和时间上的泛化表现更好。

Conclusion: GFMs的实用价值取决于其分发形式：预计算嵌入产品（如AEF）在全球AGB估算任务中的有效性显著优于需要用户自行运行的冻结编码器模型。研究为全球碳储量监测提供了一个重要的基准参考，指明了GFM在定量遥感回归任务中的潜力和最佳使用方式。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) from satellite imagery is essential for the large-scale monitoring of carbon stocks, yet it remains a challenging regression task at global scale. Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising machine learning paradigm to derive general-purpose representations from Earth observation data, but their utility for quantitative regression tasks like biomass estimation remains largely unexplored, as most benchmarks emphasize classification and segmentation. Here, we present a comprehensive benchmark of GFMs for global-scale AGB estimation using the AGBD dataset, a machine learning-ready benchmark spanning diverse biomes and geographies. We distinguish two ways in which GFMs reach practitioners: (i) models distributed as weights to be run by the user, which we evaluate as frozen encoders within the PANGAEA benchmarking framework; and (ii) models distributed as ready-to-use, pre-computed embedding products, for which we evaluate AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) and TESSERA. We compare 11 GFMs available on PANGAEA and both embedding products against a fully supervised state-of-the-art (SOTA) model, assess their geographical and temporal generalization abilities, as well as agreement with the ESA CCI biomass product on independent reference data. Our results show that GFMs run as frozen encoders substantially underperform with respect to the supervised SOTA model, whereas pre-computed embedding products prove highly effective. An MLP trained on AEF embeddings outperforms the supervised SOTA model trained on AGBD features, and the same SOTA model trained on AEF embeddings (optionally augmented with selected raw features) achieves the best overall result, while also generalizing better across space and time.

</details>


### [187] [MGSB: Manifold Gated Signature Branch Pressure-Domain Baseline Architecture for Two-Phase Pipeline Flows Under Distributional Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04805)
*Issah Suleiman,Sormeh Serpoosh,Nadine Elkholy,Hicham Ferroudji,Mohammad Azizur Rahman,Matthew Hamilton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Manifold Gated Signature Bias (MGSB)，一种面向多相管道泄漏检测的流型感知架构，通过流型条件特征融合、TT-RoughPath编码器和Mean-Teacher一致性正则化，在分布偏移下显著提升鲁棒性（检测F1=0.930，OOD F1=0.783），验证了显式流型感知建模是工业多相管道泄漏检测的实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相管道泄漏检测模型在实际部署中常因流型变化（如泡状流到段塞流转换）导致的分布偏移而性能退化。现有评估通常在分布内条件下进行，掩盖了这种失稳失效问题，亟需一种对分布偏移鲁棒的泄漏检测架构。

Method: 提出MGSB架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 流型条件特征融合（regime-conditioned feature fusion）实现流型感知的特征处理；(2) TT-RoughPath编码器用于捕获高维时序路径特征；(3) Mean-Teacher一致性正则化增强训练稳定性。采用留一组外（leave-one-group-out）评估策略验证分布外泛化能力。

Result: MGSB在留一组外评估下达到检测F1=0.930，OOD F1=0.783，显著优于CNN-LSTM和全连接基线，尤其在严重特征损坏条件下。消融实验证明架构设计而非训练方法是OOD鲁棒性的主要贡献因素；Mahalanobis距离分析确认留出条件确实属于分布外。

Conclusion: 显式流型感知建模是提升多相管道泄漏检测在分布偏移下鲁棒性的实际有效途径，MGSB架构展示了传感器无关的工业泄漏检测可行性。

Abstract: Leak detection models for multiphase pipelines often degrade when deployed under flow regimes that differ from training. Existing evaluations typically assess performance under in-distribution operating conditions, masking failures caused by regime transitions such as bubble-to-slug flow. We propose the Manifold Gated Signature Bias (MGSB), a regime-aware architecture combining regime-conditioned feature fusion, a TT-RoughPath encoder, and Mean-Teacher consistency regularization to improve robustness under distribution shift. Under leave-one-group-out evaluation, MGSB achieves a detection F1 of 0.930 and an OOD F1 of 0.783, substantially outperforming CNN-LSTM and fully connected baselines under severe feature corruption. Ablations show the proposed architecture, not the training procedure, is the primary contributor to OOD robustness, while Mahalanobis-distance analysis confirms the held-out conditions are genuinely out-of-distribution. These results show that explicit regime-aware modelling is a practical path toward robust, sensor-agnostic leak detection in industrial multiphase pipelines.

</details>


### [188] [Robust Control under Stationary Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04832)
*Konrad J. Mueller,Amira Akkari,Ben Wood,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"平稳模糊性"（stationary ambiguity）概念，通过在仿真器中构造不随时间系统性衰减的潜在参数不确定性，训练出的策略能够在面对潜在因素漂移（如金融市场波动率体制变化）时持续保持鲁棒性，避免策略随观测逐渐"特化"而失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真优化得到的控制策略在真实系统中常常表现不佳，原因之一是：从有限数据估计仿真器参数时，参数不确定性未被体现。在仿真中随机采样参数虽然能引入模糊性，但策略会随观测逐步推断出参数真实值，导致模糊性随时间衰减、策略丧失鲁棒性。这在潜在因素会漂移的真实系统（如金融市场波动率体制变化）中是不可取的。

Method: 提出"平稳模糊性"形式化要求：仿真器应在潜在状态上诱导一个平稳的滤波过程（stationary filter process），即使得模糊性随状态变化但系统性地不随时间衰减。在此基础上构造满足该要求的仿真器，用于训练策略。

Result: 在对冲问题上验证：在平稳模糊性仿真器下训练的策略能够长期保持对潜在因素的鲁棒性，在真实市场数据上表现优异。

Conclusion: 平稳模糊性可作为仿真器设计的一般性原则，指导模型选择、参数随机化方式以及仿真器与策略的初始化设定。除了金融对冲，该方法也有望推广到其他由外生随机过程驱动、存在潜在结构漂移的序贯控制问题。

Abstract: Control policies optimized in simulation can perform poorly in the real system when the parameters $x$ of the simulator are estimated from limited data but the resulting parameter uncertainty is not represented inside the simulation. A common way to incorporate such ambiguity is to simulate each trajectory of the system under a randomly drawn value for $x$. Since the policy cannot observe the drawn value, it must initially choose controls that perform well across many possible parameter values. However, if the policy progressively observes the system, it can often gradually infer the value of $x$, so that ambiguity vanishes. Over time, the policy then specializes to its estimate of $x$ and loses its robustness. This is undesirable in many real systems, where latent factors are expected to shift. In financial markets, for example, a policy hedging a derivative payoff should remain robust to changes in the volatility regime. To induce such continual robustness, we propose training policies in simulators where ambiguity varies with the system's state but does not systematically decay over time. We formalize this requirement as stationary ambiguity: the simulator should induce a stationary filter process over the latent state. We show how to construct such simulators and demonstrate, on hedging problems, that policies trained under stationary ambiguity preserve robustness to latent factors over time, leading to strong performance on real market data. As a modeling principle, stationary ambiguity informs many simulator design decisions: which models make realistic simulators, how their parameters should be randomized, and how simulator and policy should be initialized. While our experiments focus on hedging, stationary ambiguity may also be useful for other sequential control problems driven by exogenous stochastic processes with shifting latent structure.

</details>


### [189] [Training Crossroads for Recurrent Vision Transformers: Recurrence, Neural ODEs, and Deep Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04879)
*Grzegorz Gruszczynski,Pawel Olszowiec,Michal Byra,Grzegorz Stefanski,Alberto Presta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对单块循环视觉Transformer（bViT）在三个训练/推理机制下进行了受控实证研究，发现：FLOPs约束下标准ViT更优，但内存约束下循环ViT在精度-参数量权衡上更好；高层阶求解器并非数值细化而是架构偏差；阶段式深度监督在训练时域外提供优雅的鲁棒性退化而非名义精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ViT的参数量随深度线性增长，而单块循环ViT（bViT）通过重复共享一个块来消除这种增长。已有工作缺乏对bViT在不同训练和推理约束条件下的系统性实证理解，特别是循环优于独立参数化深度的条件、ODE求解器阶数的作用，以及鲁棒性与名义精度之间的权衡。

Method: 固定一个bViT架构，在共享的CIFAR-100协议下进行三个受控实验：1) 在匹配FLOPs和匹配参数量两个维度对比循环深度与独立参数化深度；2) 通过ODE求解器训练残差循环块，考察求解器阶数是数值细化还是架构偏差（其中连续时间类比为状态减法向量场ż=F_θ(z)-z）；3) 采用阶段式深度监督，考察训练时域之外的鲁棒性与名义精度权衡。

Result: 标准ViT在FLOPs为主要约束时仍然更优；循环ViT在内存约束下提供更好的精度-参数权衡。高阶求解器与向量场联合学习时表现为求解器诱导的架构偏差而非数值精度提升，且收益不均匀。阶段式深度监督不提升名义精度，但在远超训练时域时优雅退化，而朴素循环则崩塌至接近随机性能。

Conclusion: 循环ViT的价值取决于资源约束类型；残差循环块的连续时间对应形式应为状态减法向量场而非黑箱包装；高阶求解器的作用是架构层面的偏置而非数值改善；深度监督在名义精度与超时域鲁棒性之间勾勒出一条权衡前沿。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong image-recognition performance, but their parameter count grows linearly with depth when each block is independently parameterized. Single-block recurrent ViTs (bViT) remove this growth by repeatedly applying one shared block. Rather than proposing a new architecture, we fix a bViT and provide a controlled empirical characterization of three training and inference regimes under a common CIFAR-100 protocol, asking: (i)~when does recurrence beat independently parameterized depth---at matched FLOPs or at matched parameter memory? (ii)~when a residual recurrent block is trained through an ODE solver, does solver order act as numerical refinement or as an architectural bias? and (iii)~what does robustness beyond the training horizon cost in nominal accuracy? We find that standard ViTs remain preferable when FLOPs are the primary constraint, whereas recurrent ViTs offer a better accuracy--parameter trade-off under memory constraints. Consistent with the standard view of residual networks as Euler discretizations of ODEs, the continuous-time analogue of a residual recurrent block is the state-subtracted vector field $\dot{z}=F_θ(z)-z$; although known in principle, this distinction is easy to violate when the block is wrapped as a black-box vector field, and we qualify the cost at few accuracy points. Because the vector field is learned jointly with the solver, higher-order solvers act as a solver-induced architectural bias rather than a numerical-accuracy improvement, and their gains are not uniform. Finally, stage-wise deep supervision traces an accuracy--robustness frontier: it does not improve nominal accuracy, but degrades gracefully far beyond the training horizon, where naive recurrence collapses to near-random performance.

</details>


### [190] [Variational Bounds for Perceptron Learning from Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04882)
*Francesco Camilli,Pierluigi Contucci,Federica Gerace,Emanuele Mingione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有限温度连续自旋感知机在混合高斯分布训练数据上的变分方法，通过插值方法结合对数凹性和集中不等式，推导出极限淬火压力的上下极小极大变分界，两个界仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上不同，当优化可交换时两界匹配并确定模型解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知机是经典的自旋玻璃模型，此前研究多集中于二元或离散自旋，对连续自旋且在有限温度下的感知机在非高斯混合数据上的系统性变分框架尚缺乏。本文旨在建立统一的变分框架，同时处理广泛类型的凹效用函数和对数凹可分先验，并为后续物理量（基态能量、训练损失、泛化误差）提供统一计算路径。

Method: 将插值方法与对数凹性和集中估计相结合：对自旋赋予对数凹可分的先验测度，对数据采用高斯混合，效用函数取广义凹函数类。通过插值方法构造辅助系统，推导淬火压力的下界和上界。上下界的差异仅体现在两个变分参数的优化次序上，其余极值由变分势的凹-凸结构控制。

Result: 得到极限淬火压力的上下极小极大变分界；当两个变分参数的优化可交换（commute）时，两界相等并唯一确定模型解；同一变分势给出定点方程作为平稳性条件，并可统一推导基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差。

Conclusion: 该方法为有限温度连续自旋感知机提供了通用而严谨的变分框架，不仅统一了此前针对特定情形的结果，还为计算各物理可观测量提供了系统的途径。两个变分界在优化可交换时的匹配，为确定模型解的充分条件提供了清晰的判据。

Abstract: We introduce a variational approach to a finite-temperature continuous-spin perceptron trained on a Gaussian mixture. The model allows for a broad class of concave utilities and log-concave separable prior measures on the spins. By combining the interpolation method with log-concavity and concentration estimates, we derive lower and upper minimax variational bounds for the limiting quenched pressure. Remarkably, the two bounds differ only in the order of optimization of two variational parameters, while all remaining extrema are controlled by the concave--convex structure of the variational potential. Whenever the two optimizations commute, the two bounds match and identify the solution of the model. The same potential yields the fixed-point equations as stationarity conditions and provides a unified route to the computation of the ground-state energy, training loss, and generalization error.

</details>


### [191] [Consistency-Driven Co-Evolution for Self-Supervised Cross-Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04926)
*Xuehang Guo,Pengyuan Li,Tom Hope,Tirthankar Ghosal,Manling Li,Qingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoCoEvolve是一个旨在提升图表、表格和代码三种表示形式之间一致性的框架。它将跨表示映射从一对多问题重构为显式的一对一对应关系，通过表示间的协议（无额外标注）优化模型，并在训练时（CoCoEvolve@Train）和推理时（CoCoEvolve@Test）分别进行协同演化，同时提供了一套评估套件（CoCoEvolve@Eval）覆盖全部六种跨表示任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表图像、表格数据和可视化代码在多个领域日益重要，但跨表示理解面临三大挑战：表示间关系本质是一对多的、监督信号模糊且成本高、以及模型优化缺乏既有方向自适应又具备表示泛化能力的原则性训练信号。

Method: CoCoEvolve定义显式的一对一跨表示对应关系，使用表示之间的协议（agreement）作为优化目标（无需额外标注）。训练阶段CoCoEvolve@Train在图表-表格-代码循环中进行协同演化；推理阶段CoCoEvolve@Test将同一一致性目标用于测试时协同优化。另提出评估套件CoCoEvolve@Eval，涵盖所有六种跨表示任务。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，CoCoEvolve在训练时和测试时两种设置下均提升了性能。

Conclusion: 通过将一对多映射重构为一对一对应，并以表示间协议作为统一优化信号，CoCoEvolve有效解决了跨表示理解中的监督模糊和优化信号缺乏泛化性的问题，在训练与推理阶段均取得一致性收益。

Abstract: As chart images, tabular data, and visualization code play increasingly important roles across diverse domains, cross-representation understanding across these modalities poses fundamental challenges for AI systems: the relationships across representations are inherently \textit{one-to-many}, supervision is ambiguous and costly, and model optimization lacks a principled signal that is both direction-adaptive and representation-generalizable beyond task-specific objectives. We introduce CoCoEvolve to improve consistency across chart, table, and code representations. Instead of treating cross-representation mapping as a one-to-many problem, we define explicit one-to-one correspondences and optimize models using agreement between representations, without additional annotations. During training, CoCoEvolve@Train performs co-evolution across the chart-table-code cycle, while CoCoEvolve@Test applies the same consistency objective at inference time for test-time co-optimization. We also present CoCoEvolve@Eval, an evaluation suite covering all six cross-representation tasks. Across four benchmarks, CoCoEvolve improves performance in both training-time and test-time settings. Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/CoCoEvolve/.

</details>


### [192] [Optimal Training-Time Scaling in Gradual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04927)
*Zonghuan Xu,Krishna Harish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在渐进适应（gradual adaptation）场景下，随着中间任务数量增加，每个任务的训练时间应如何调整。研究发现当总训练时间Ns_N趋近于τ时，最终学习进度收敛到连续曲线，且最优的每任务训练时间按N^{-1}缩放，即总训练时间保持常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进适应中一个关键的实际问题是：当划分更多中间任务时，每个任务应该训练多久。此前缺乏理论指导，需要理解训练时间分配与任务数量之间的最优权衡关系。

Method: 通过过参数化线性回归任务建模，假设任务平滑变化且共享零损失解。采用理论分析推导连续极限下的学习进度，分析训练时间s_N与任务数N的联合缩放关系（Ns_N→τ），并推导小τ和大τ时的渐进复杂度。

Result: 最终学习进度在小τ时为Θ(τ)，在大τ时为Θ(τ^{-1})，说明训练时间过短或过长都收益甚微。最优每任务训练时间s_N^*=Θ(N^{-1})，即总训练时间Ns_N^*=Θ(1)。在渐转MNIST和Yearbook时间偏移实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: 当路径被划分得更细（任务数增多）时，每个任务应减少训练时间，但总训练时间应保持恒定，这为渐进适应的训练策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In gradual adaptation, how should the training time on each task change as the number of intermediate tasks increases? We study this question for overparameterized linear regression tasks that change smoothly and share a zero-loss solution. With $N$ tasks and training time $s_N$ on each, the final learning progress converges to a continuum curve when $Ns_N\toτ$. The limiting progress is $Θ(τ)$ for small $τ$ and $Θ(τ^{-1})$ for large $τ$, so both very short and very long training produce little progress. It follows that optimal per-task training times scale as $s_N^\star=Θ(N^{-1})$, equivalently $Ns_N^\star=Θ(1)$. Experiments on gradually rotated MNIST and a natural Yearbook time shift are consistent with less per-task training as the path is divided more finely.

</details>


### [193] [SVI-DAG: A Structured Variational Inference Approach to Bayesian Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04930)
*Shrenik Zinage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SVI-DAG，一种基于结构化变分推断的贝叶斯因果发现方法，利用归一化流建模DAG边之间的依赖关系，并通过Stein变分梯度下降在无环性空间中进行节点势更新，从而支持表达性强且多模态的后验学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯因果发现方法存在两个主要局限：一是未能编码边之间的依赖关系，二是缺乏以原则性方式将领域知识作为归纳偏置融入搜索过程的机制。这些问题限制了后验分布的准确近似和不确定性量化能力。

Method: 提出SVI-DAG方法，核心包括：(1)使用结构化变分推断框架，通过归一化流（normalizing flows）建模DAG边之间的依赖关系，支持表达性强且多模态的后验学习；(2)为缓解ELBO优化中的模式寻求（mode seeking）行为并促进模式覆盖，使用Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）在无环性空间中通过核函数更新节点势。

Result: 将SVI-DAG与5种最先进的贝叶斯DAG学习方法进行对比评估，在不确定性量化方面展现出优越性能，同时在结构准确性方面保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SVI-DAG通过归一化流建模边依赖关系并结合SVGD进行后验优化，有效克服了现有贝叶斯DAG学习方法在表达能力、多模态后验学习以及领域知识融入方面的不足，在不确定性量化上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Bayesian causal discovery seeks to determine the posterior distribution of causal theories, which are interpreted as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that explain the observed data. The resulting posterior allows systematic reasoning regarding epistemic uncertainty within these theories. Nonetheless, finding such graphs is difficult due to identifiability problems and limited observational data. Furthermore, precisely approximating posterior over graphs is challenging given vast range of potential DAGs. Recent Bayesian approaches have addressed some of these challenges, yet they remain limited as they fail to encode dependencies between edges, and lack principled ways to incorporate domain knowledge as inductive biases during the search process. To overcome these limitations, we propose SVI-DAG, a structured variational inference approach to Bayesian causal discovery using observational data and prior beliefs that uses normalizing flows to model dependencies between edges, supporting expressive and multimodal posterior learning over DAGs. To mitigate mode seeking behaviour in evidence lower bound optimization and promote mode coverage, we use stein variational gradient descent to update the node potentials using a kernel in acyclicity space. We evaluate SVI-DAG against 5 state-of-the-art Bayesian DAG learning methods and demonstrate superior performance in uncertainty quantification while remaining competitive in terms of structural accuracy.

</details>


### [194] [CheMLFlow: An Open-Source Platform for Cheminformatics and Materials Informatics Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04942)
*Brendan Smith,Susana Lopez-Moreno,Eric Dolores-Cuenca,Sangil Kim,Jose L. Mendoza-Cortes,Nijamudheen Abdulrahiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CheMLFlow是一个开源平台，用于构建和执行端到端、高通量、智能体的科学工作流，涵盖数据采集到报告生成的全流程，模块化设计降低编排成本，支持可扩展、可复现和自动化友好的科学机器学习开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习开发中的常见瓶颈在于研究人员即使只关注某一阶段（如模型训练），也需要组装数据采集、清洗、表征、训练、验证、筛选、解释和报告等完整可复现流程。CheMLFlow旨在通过模块化工作流组件和现成参考管道来减少这种编排开销。

Method: CheMLFlow提供模块化工作流组件、即用型参考管道、标准化产物和评估输出。平台支持可插拔的表征与模型、确定性数据划分、显式运行产物、批量执行和报告生成。其配置驱动的工作流和结构化输出还为编码智能体提供与人类监督下交互实验的实用接口。

Result: 该平台在量子力学、物理化学和生物活性性质预测方面达到了文献水平的基准性能，并通过时间序列数据集的使用案例展示了超越分子化学数据集的应用能力。

Conclusion: CheMLFlow通过配置驱动的模块化设计和结构化输出，为科学软件向智能体辅助实验方向发展提供了实用基础，能够帮助用户构建实验、检查结果并在人类监督下总结发现，同时支撑跨方法和数据集的可复现基准评测。

Abstract: CheMLFlow is an open-source platform for building and executing end-to-end, high-throughput, and agentic workflows for scientific and technological applications. CheMLFlow targets a common bottleneck in scientific machine learning development, where researchers often need to assemble data acquisition, curation, representation, model training, validation, screening, interpretation, and reporting into a reproducible pipeline, even when their primary research contribution concerns only one stage. CheMLFlow provides modular workflow components, ready-to-run reference pipelines, standardized artifacts, and evaluation outputs that reduce orchestration overhead and support benchmarking across methods and datasets. The platform is designed to be extensible, reproducible, and automation friendly, with pluggable representations and models, deterministic splits, explicit run artifacts, batch execution, and report generation. As scientific software increasingly moves toward agent assisted experimentation, CheMLFlow's configuration driven workflows and structured outputs also provide a practical interface for coding agents to help users construct experiments, inspect results, and summarize findings under human supervision. This article describes the system architecture, core workflows, and benchmarks that reach literature performance for quantum mechanical, physicochemical and bioactivity property prediction, and use cases involving time series datasets demonstrating applications beyond molecular chemistry datasets.

</details>


### [195] [SpecRoll: Fast-Slow Verifier-Feedback Adaptation for Speculative Reinforcement Learning Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04962)
*Nhat Minh Pham,Duy Tung Doan,Thi Duyen Ngo,Vinh Van Nguyen,Khac-Hoai Nam Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecRoll 是一种面向强化学习(RL)后训练阶段的投机解码(Speculative Decoding)加速引擎，通过双时间尺度自适应（快速未来token头 + 慢速延迟验证器反馈）保持目标策略的采样分布不变，同时将生成速度提升1.26-2.15倍，端到端速度提升1.21-2.04倍，并在所有15个对比设置中优于FastGRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练显著提升了大语言模型的推理能力，但自回归(autoregressive)的rollout生成是主要效率瓶颈。投机解码可加速生成，但在RL训练中难以应用，因为目标策略不断演化：静态proposer会过时，频繁更新drafter又引入大量开销。因此需要一种既能保持目标分布、又能随策略演化自适应的高效投机rollout引擎。

Method: SpecRoll包含三个核心组件：(1) 轻量级未来token头(future-token heads)生成并行proposals；(2) Reflex模块利用延迟的verifier反馈，在不进行反向传播的前提下执行有界的、轨迹局部的隐藏状态修正（快速路径）；(3) 互补慢路径仅在检测到持续性能退化时才更新头部参数。此外，结合并发感知的稀疏树验证(concurrency-aware sparse-tree verification)和精确目标验证，确保目标rollout分布和GRPO目标不变。

Result: 在5个模型（1.5B到14B）和3个数学推理数据集上，SpecRoll实现了1.26-2.15倍的生成速度提升和1.21-2.04倍的端到端速度提升（对比vanilla GRPO）。在所有15个匹配设置中，SpecRoll在生成时间和端到端时间上均优于FastGRPO，平均端到端增益为1.18倍。消融实验表明快速和慢速两条自适应路径提供了互补的好处。

Conclusion: SpecRoll通过双时间尺度自适应机制有效解决了RL训练中目标策略演化导致的投机解码失效问题，在保持目标采样分布和GRPO目标严格不变的前提下显著加速了RL rollout生成，证明了所提出的快速路径和慢速路径的互补有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but autoregressive rollout generation remains a major efficiency bottleneck. Speculative decoding can accelerate generation, yet applying it during RL is difficult because the target policy continually evolves: static proposers become stale, while frequent drafter updates add substantial overhead. We introduce SpecRoll, a speculative rollout engine that preserves the target model's sampling distribution while adapting at two timescales. Lightweight future-token heads generate parallel proposals, while our proposed Reflex module uses delayed verifier feedback to perform bounded, trajectory-local hidden-state corrections without backpropagation. A complementary slow path updates the head parameters only when sustained degradation is detected. SpecRoll combines these mechanisms with concurrency-aware sparse-tree verification and exact target verification, leaving the target rollout distribution and GRPO objective unchanged. Across five models ranging from 1.5B to 14B and three mathematical reasoning datasets, SpecRoll achieves 1.26-2.15x generation speedup and 1.21-2.04x end-to-end speedup over vanilla GRPO. It also outperforms FastGRPO in both generation and end-to-end time across all 15 matched settings, with an average pairwise end-to-end gain of 1.18x. Controlled ablations show that the fast and slow adaptation paths provide complementary benefits. Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecRoll-26062006.

</details>


### [196] [EvolveNet: Collaborative Harness Evolution for Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04968)
*Jun Nie,Yonggang Zhang,Qianshu Cai,Yiu-ming Cheung,Xinmei Tian,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvolveNet，一种协作式harness进化范式，将经验提取移至数据本地：共享harness广播到各数据本地agent部署，各自在自身工作负载上进化，仅将程序改编结果聚合回共享harness并重新分发，从而实现多路并行的进化搜索。通过scope-typed、evidence-guided的程序聚合机制解决独立修改程序间的冲突问题。在五个基准（文本到SQL、数据科学编码、竞赛编程、软件工程、agentic工作流）上均提升共享harness性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness进化方法假设所有执行经验可以路由到单一优化器，沿顺序轨迹进化一个harness。但真实agent生态系统中，用户、组织和环境生成的经验流是隔离的、无法汇集的，而最有价值的学习经验恰恰是那些无法直接集中化的经验。这打破了现有方法的根本假设，需要一种数据本地化的协作式进化范式。

Method: EvolveNet将经验提取从中心迁移到数据本地：1）共享harness广播到各数据本地agent部署；2）每个部署在自身工作负载上独立进化harness；3）仅将程序改编（adaptations）而非原始工作负载聚合回更新后的共享harness并重新分发。为解决独立修改程序无法像模型参数那样平均、且组合时可能冲突的问题，引入scope-typed（作用域类型化）和evidence-guided（证据引导）的程序聚合机制。

Result: 在五个设置（文本到SQL、数据科学编码、竞赛编程、软件工程、agentic工作流）上，EvolveNet在所有五个设置中均改进了共享harness，且在异构工作负载下增益最大。消融实验表明改进归因于不同agent改编的组成（composition）而非仅从其中选择（selecting among them）。

Conclusion: 通过将聚合边界从原始工作负载转移到学习到的改编，EvolveNet保持工作负载本地化，允许多个进化搜索并发进行且减少序列深度，从而解决了真实agent生态系统中经验无法集中化的核心瓶颈，为harness进化提供了可扩展的分布式范式。

Abstract: The capabilities of an LLM agent depend not only on its model but on the harness: the executable program that constructs context, invokes tools, verifies results, and recovers from failure. Recent work shows that evolving the harness yields persistent improvements without updating model weights. Existing approaches, however, assume that all execution experience can be routed to a single optimizer, which evolves one harness along a sequential trajectory. Real agent ecosystems violate that assumption: users, organizations, and environments generate isolated streams of experience that cannot be pooled, so the experience most worth learning from is exactly the experience that cannot be directly centralized. We introduce EvolveNet, a paradigm of collaborative harness evolution that moves experience extraction to the data. A shared harness is broadcast to data-local agent deployments, each of which evolves it on its own workload. Only the resulting program adaptations are composed into an updated shared harness and redistributed, so that every participating agent inherits operational experience discovered by the others. By shifting the aggregation boundary from raw workloads to learned adaptations, EvolveNet keeps workloads local and allows multiple evolutionary searches to proceed concurrently with reduced serial depth. Because independently modified programs cannot be averaged like model parameters and may conflict when composed, EvolveNet introduces scope-typed, evidence-guided program aggregation. Across five settings spanning text-to-SQL, data-science coding, competitive programming, software engineering, and agentic workflows, EvolveNet improves the shared harness in all five, with the largest gains under heterogeneous workloads, and ablations attribute the improvement to composition of adaptations from different agents rather than to selecting among them.

</details>


### [197] [Stochastic Emulation using Generalized Stratified Sampling for Performance-Based Risk Optimization of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05006)
*Isabela D. Rodrigues,Seymour M. J. Spence,Henrique M. Kroetz,André T. Beck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将广义分层抽样（GSS）与随机多项式混沌展开（SPCE）相结合的新型框架（GSS-SPCE），用于解决SPCE在结构响应极端尾部近似方面的局限性，从而更高效、更准确地支持基于性能的风险优化（PBRO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPCE作为一种随机仿真器，虽然不需要在固定输入条件下对非线性分析进行重复采样，但在精确表示结构响应分布尾部（极端响应）方面存在局限，这直接影响PBRO中概率约束评估的准确性。

Method: 该方法通过GSS方案根据灾害强度将输入空间划分为若干层，在每个层内独立训练SPCE仿真器；每层中估计的条件超越概率通过全概率定理重新组合，以评估概率约束；框架应用于两层钢结构中屈曲约束支撑截面面积的最优设计，以最小化初始建造成本并满足规定的概率性能约束。

Result: 结果表明，GSS-SPCE框架能够准确估计结构响应分布（包括尾部区域），同时显著减少PBRO所需的非线性模型评估次数。

Conclusion: GSS-SPCE框架有效克服了标准SPCE在尾部建模上的不足，在保证精度的前提下大幅提升PBRO的计算效率，为结构在随机荷载下的性能化风险优化提供了一种高效且可靠的元建模途径。

Abstract: Metamodels are instrumental in reducing the computational burden associated with nested reliability analyses and optimization loops in Performance-Based Risk Optimization (PBRO) of structures under stochastic loads. In this context, stochastic emulators are particularly useful because they approximate response distributions while accounting for the intrinsic stochasticity of the simulator. Among these methods, Stochastic Polynomial Chaos Expansion (SPCE) is especially attractive because it does not require replications of nonlinear analyses at fixed input conditions. However, SPCE may present limitations in accurately representing extreme responses in the tails of structural response distributions. To address this limitation, this study proposes a framework that combines Generalized Stratified Sampling (GSS) with SPCE. The GSS scheme partitions the input space into strata according to the intensity of the hazard, improving the representation of extreme responses, while independent SPCE emulators are trained within each stratum. The conditional exceedance probabilities estimated in each stratum are then recombined using the total probability theorem to evaluate the probabilistic constraints. The proposed GSS-SPCE framework is applied to the optimal design of buckling-restrained brace cross-sectional areas in a two-story steel building. The objective is to minimize the initial construction cost while satisfying prescribed probabilistic performance constraints. Results show that the proposed framework accurately estimates structural response distributions, including their tail regions, while substantially reducing the number of nonlinear model evaluations required for PBRO.

</details>


### [198] [Canonical Joint Energy-Based Model on CIFAR-10: failure modes and practical indistinguishability of Predictor-Corrector and SGLD samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05025)
*Dmytro Knopov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对联合能量模型（JEM）进行了系统复现，在无归一化层的WideResNet-28-10上进行两次独立运行，测试了理论上更优的Predictor-Corrector（PC）采样器是否能在无退火噪声调度的情况下保持优势。结果发现PC在三个协议（训练替换、冷启动生成FID、多OOD检测AUROC）上均未展现出相对于SGLD的方法级优势，且两种方法都出现了晚期训练发散和SVHN OOD判别动态依赖运行的问题。作者从理论上解释了这一结果：在固定噪声下PC的预测步退化，其理论保证无法迁移到标准JEM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范JEM训练依赖随机梯度Langevin动力学（SGLD），而理论上更有依据的Predictor-Corrector（PC）采样器此前未在规范模型上接受过系统性复现测试。作者希望验证PC在无退火噪声调度的条件下是否能保留其理论优势。

Method: 在无归一化层的WideResNet-28-10上复现规范JEM，进行两次独立运行，在三种协议下对比PC与SGLD：1）在约130个训练轮次中全程用PC替换SGLD；2）冷启动生成（FID）；3）精炼式多OOD检测（AUROC）。使用分层seed-by-image bootstrap进行统计推断，并进行seed级等价性检验。

Result: 重建达到92.88%测试准确率和buffer-FID 44.46（规范值分别为92.9%和38.40）。发现两种失败模式：通过规范离群buffer机制导致的灾难性晚期训练发散（SGLD和PC各两次运行均出现），以及依赖于运行实例的SVHN OOD判别动态。PC在三个协议上均未展现出优于SGLD的方法级优势：推理时AUROC绝对差异全部低于0.007，FID差异低于0.5；训练协议上AUROC差异的95%置信区间包含零，但两次运行的seed级等价性检验无法建立正式等价。

Conclusion: PC与SGLD在规范JEM上实际不可区分（数据同时与两者等价及存在小方向效应的假设一致）。这一结果在理论上可预期：在固定噪声下PC的预测步在构造上会退化，因此其理论保证无法迁移到不采用退火噪声调度的标准JEM上。

Abstract: Joint Energy-Based Models (JEM) unify classification and generation within a single network and support out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. Canonical JEM training relies on stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics (SGLD); a theoretically motivated alternative, the Predictor-Corrector (PC) sampler, has not previously undergone a systematic replication test on the canonical model. We reproduce canonical JEM on WideResNet-28-10 without normalisation layers on two independent runs and test whether PC retains its theoretical advantage without an annealed noise schedule, across three protocols: PC replacing SGLD throughout the roughly 130 training epochs; cold-start generation (FID); and refinement-style multi-OOD detection (AUROC).
