The reconstruction reaches 92.88% test accuracy and buffer-FID 44.46 (canonical: 92.9% and 38.40). We document two failure modes: catastrophic late-training divergence via the canonical outlier-buffer mechanism (both SGLD runs and, with the same signature, both PC runs), and run-dependent SVHN OOD-discrimination dynamics. No method-level advantage of PC over SGLD is observed on any protocol: at inference the absolute AUROC difference stays below 0.007 across all ten checkpoint-OOD pairs and the FID difference below 0.5; on the training protocol a hierarchical seed-by-image bootstrap gives a 95% confidence interval on the macro-averaged AUROC difference that contains zero, while a seed-level equivalence test with two runs per method cannot establish formal equivalence. The data are consistent both with equivalence and with a small directional effect. This practical indistinguishability is theoretically expected: under fixed noise the PC predictor step degenerates by construction, so its guarantees do not transfer to canonical JEM.

</details>


### [199] [MultiPathFormer: Towards a Foundation Model for Multipath Wireless Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05076)
*Blessed Guda,Kayley Sze,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiPathFormer，一种将多径传播作为预训练对象的自回归无线基础模型。它将每个收发链路表示为连续值路径令牌的有序序列，通过下一路径预测进行预训练，并引入环境RAG机制和首路径码本，在27个环境上预训练后可迁移到未见用户，在多个下游任务上超越现有的基于信道的SOTA基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无线基础模型通常在信道张量上基于子载波、天线或时间的掩码重建进行预训练，但忽视了无线传播的物理特性。这一局限使得模型难以学习到真正反映无线信道物理本质的可复用表征。

Method: 1) 将每个发射-接收链路表示为连续值路径令牌（path tokens）的有序序列；2) 采用自回归架构进行下一路径预测（next-path prediction）预训练；3) 提出环境RAG（检索增强生成）机制，利用环境知识辅助路径统计量估计；4) 引入首路径码本（first-path codebook）增强首个路径的表示；5) 在27个环境上预训练，然后针对具体场景进行微调。

Result: 延迟和功率等路径统计量估计提升高达59%；在未见用户和新环境上具有良好迁移能力；下游任务上超越基于信道的SOTA基础模型：平均定位误差5.57米，top-3波束精度0.914，视距分类准确率0.994，信道估计NMSE为0.561。

Conclusion: 路径级预训练能够学习到可复用的无线传播表征，相对于传统基于信道的预训练方法具有显著优势，为无线基础模型的设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have enabled training of wireless foundation models, which aim to support tasks such as channel estimation, beam prediction, and localization based on wireless signals. Existing wireless foundation models typically pretrain on channel tensors using masked reconstruction over subcarriers, antennas, or time but ignore the physical characteristics of wireless propagation. In this work, we propose to instead use multipath propagation as the fundamental pretraining object. We present MultiPathFormer, an autoregressive foundation model that represents each transmitter-receiver link as an ordered sequence of continuous-valued path tokens and pretrains with next-path prediction. We introduce an Environmental RAG (retrieval-augmented generation) mechanism and a first-path codebook on top of the transformer backbone, leveraging environment knowledge to improve path statistics estimation like delay and power by up to 59%. MultiPathFormer pretrained on 27 environments transfers to unseen users and, after scenario-specific fine-tuning, outperforms training the corresponding models from scratch in new environments. Across downstream tasks, it outperforms SOTA channel-based foundation models, achieving 5.57 m mean localization error, 0.914 top-3 beam accuracy, 0.994 line-of-sight classification accuracy, and 0.561 channel estimation NMSE. These results show that path-level pretraining can learn reusable representations of wireless propagation.

</details>


### [200] [Optimizing What Policies Learn From: Recoverability-aware Rollout Intervention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05080)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Yanfang Ye,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAIL（Recoverability-Aware Intervention Learning），一种在训练时学习如何生成rollout的自适应框架。RAIL将干预选择建模为在线上下文赌博机问题，通过影子到实时（shadow-to-live）流程训练可恢复性控制器，在有限rollout预算下持续提升大语言模型后训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组的无评论强化学习在后训练大语言模型时，对所有任务和轨迹状态分配相同数量的rollout，忽略了不同rollout之间学习信号的差异。已有工作虽开始将rollout生成视为自适应决策，但仍存在两个局限：一，干预策略基于固定启发式，无法随策略变化而调整；二，只决定生成多少rollout，没有显式控制在哪里和如何干预。

Method: RAIL将干预选择建模为在线上下文赌博机（contextual-bandit）问题，训练一个可恢复性控制器（recoverability controller），该控制器基于每次干预产生的改进来学习如何生成rollout。通过影子到实时（shadow-to-live）流程收集干预轨迹，使控制器能够在底层策略演化的过程中持续学习。

Result: RAIL在有效性、自适应性、表达性和效率四个维度上进行了评估。在多种设置下，RAIL在有限rollout预算下持续提升了性能表现。

Conclusion: 可恢复性感知的干预提供了一种有原则的方式，生成更具信息性且冗余更少的rollout，从而在后训练过程中产生更强的学习信号。RAIL相比固定启发式方法能自适应地调整干预策略，是对无评论强化学习后训练范式的重要改进。

Abstract: Critic-free group-based reinforcement learning has become a scalable approach for post-training large language models. However, most existing methods allocate the same number of rollouts to every task and trajectory state, even though some rollouts provide much more useful learning signals than others. Recent work has started to treat rollout generation as an adaptive decision, but two important limitations remain. First, intervention strategies are often based on fixed heuristics and therefore cannot adjust as the policy changes during training. Second, these methods usually decide only how many rollouts to generate, without explicitly controlling where and how to intervene. To address these limitations, we propose Recoverability-Aware Intervention Learning (RAIL), a training-time framework that learns how to generate rollouts based on the improvement produced by each intervention. RAIL models intervention selection as an online contextual-bandit problem and trains a recoverability controller using intervention traces collected through a shadow-to-live procedure. This allows the controller to keep learning while the underlying policy evolves. We evaluate RAIL in terms of effectiveness, adaptivity, expressiveness, and efficiency. Across multiple settings, RAIL consistently improves performance under limited rollout budgets. These results show that recoverability-aware intervention provides a principled way to generate more informative and less redundant rollouts, leading to stronger learning signals during post-training.

</details>


### [201] [Capability-Gated Planning: Cost-to-Goal Discovery and the Limits of Myopic Experiment Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05085)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别出自动化科学发现系统中"建构性动作"（获取认知能力）对myopic信息最大化规划器的结构性局限，证明这类动作在有限视野内与空动作不可区分，导致任意信息最大化规划器在需链式建构的任务上具有无界近似比甚至无法到达目标。作者提出CG-Plan，一种具有能力感知代价启发式的增量重规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动科学发现系统普遍使用myopic评分（如期望信息增益/单位成本或学习到的可信度分数）来决定下一步动作。作者识别出一个根本性缺陷：这些系统无法评估"建构性动作"——即那些本身不立即返回信息，而是为未来动作解锁可能性的动作（如获取仪器、检测、流程、模拟器或抽象）。当通往高置信答案的最短路径需要一系列这样的建构时，因为建构动作在有限视野内不产生任何信息，会被任何产生微小正信息的测量所支配，导致规划器无法启动关键的第一步。

Method: 本文将目标导向的发现形式化为信念空间中的随机最短路径问题，其中建构性实验会改变下游动作图。证明了对任意前瞻深度d都存在使每个myopic信息最大化规划器具有无界近似比的实例，以及相关无法到达目标的实例，机制是"能力不可区分性引理"——在视野内获取能力与支付空动作在观测上难以区分。基于此分析，提出CG-Plan：一种增量重规划器，使用能力感知的代价至目标启发式h = h_cap + h_exp（能力分量+实验分量）。

Result: 证明了能力门控（capability gating）是区别于曲率（子模性）和信息序（自适应间隙）的独立的可达性难度轴。在受控测试平台上，性能差距仅在门控条件下出现，对每个固定视野持续存在，且当近失假设来自数据一致的提议者时产生。CG-Plan在这些门控条件下能有效处理建构链问题。

Conclusion: 自动科学发现系统若仅依赖myopic信息最大化策略，在面对需要建构性动作链的任务时存在根本性的能力盲区。能力获取应当被显式建模为规划目标的一部分，而CG-Plan通过能力感知的启发式设计提供了一种可行的解决方案。这一工作确立了能力门控作为独立的算法难度维度，为设计更鲁棒的自动发现系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Systems that automate scientific discovery must repeatedly decide which experiment to run, which hypothesis to test, which tool to build, and when to stop. Many systems make these decisions by maximizing a myopic score such as expected information gain per unit cost or a learned plausibility score. We identify a structural limitation of this approach. Some actions are constructive: they acquire an epistemic capability (an instrument, assay, pipeline, simulator, or abstraction) whose value lies not in the information returned immediately but in the future actions it makes available. When the least-cost route to a confident answer requires a chain of such constructions, a planner that scores actions only by information obtainable within a bounded horizon cannot value the first construction: it yields no information within the horizon and is dominated by any measurement with positive information, however small. We formulate goal-directed discovery as a stochastic shortest-path problem in belief space in which constructive experiments change the downstream action graph, and prove that for every lookahead depth d there is an instance on which every myopic information-maximizing planner has an unbounded approximation ratio, and a related instance on which it never reaches the goal. The mechanism is a capability-indistinguishability lemma: within the horizon, acquiring a capability can be observationally indistinguishable from paying for a null action. This establishes capability gating as a reachability axis of difficulty distinct from curvature (submodularity) and information order (adaptivity gaps). We introduce CG-Plan, an incremental replanner with a capability-aware cost-to-go heuristic h = h_cap + h_exp. In a controlled testbed, the performance gap appears only under gating, persists for every fixed horizon, and arises when near-miss hypotheses come from a data-consistent proposer.

</details>


### [202] [MALT: Lightweight Curvature-Aware Muon via Diagonal Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05088)
*Tongle Wu,Huanyu Dong,Ying Sun,Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MALT优化器，通过在Muon基础上引入轻量级双侧对角预条件子来显式捕获损失面的曲率几何，减少对曲率各向异性的敏感性，并进一步提出MALTER（自适应步长重缩放版本）增强对随机梯度噪声的鲁棒性。实验证明在GPT-2多规模预训练中优于Muon，且内存和时间开销几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon通过Newton-Schulz迭代对动量矩阵进行正交化来缓解梯度各向异性，但未显式考虑损失面的曲率几何，因此对曲率各向异性仍然敏感。本文旨在通过轻量级预条件方法弥补这一缺陷。

Method: MALT使用双侧对角预条件子（two-sided diagonal preconditioners）近似捕获损失面曲率几何，仅需低内存和低计算开销；对预条件后的动量执行Newton-Schulz正交化后再映射回更新方向，并通过norm grafting控制更新幅度。MALTER进一步引入自适应步长重缩放以增强对随机梯度噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small、Medium和Large的预训练实验中，MALT和MALTER均优于Muon，同时保持了几乎相同的内存占用和墙钟时间。

Conclusion: MALT通过轻量级双侧预条件有效弥补了Muon忽略曲率信息的不足，在保持Muon效率优势的同时提升了优化性能；MALTER进一步增强了噪声鲁棒性。论文还提供了MALT在随机非凸设定下的收敛性保证。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a promising alternative to AdamW for language model pretraining by orthogonalizing momentum matrices using Newton-Schulz iterations. Although Muon mitigates gradient anisotropy, it does not explicitly account for the curvature geometry of the loss landscape and may therefore remain sensitive to curvature anisotropy. We bridge this gap by proposing MALT (Muon Augmented by Lightweight Two-sided Preconditioning), which uses lightweight diagonal preconditioners to reduce the sensitivity of Muon to curvature anisotropy. Specifically, MALT uses two-sided diagonal preconditioners with low memory and computational overhead to approximately capture the curvature geometry of the loss landscape. It orthogonalizes the preconditioned momentum using Newton-Schulz iterations and maps the result back to define the update direction, while norm grafting controls the update magnitude. To improve the robustness of MALT to stochastic gradient noise, we further propose MALTER (MALT with Adaptive stEpsize Rescaling). Convergence guarantees are provided for MALT in the stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on GPT-2 Small, Medium, and Large pretraining show that the proposed methods outperform Muon while maintaining nearly the same memory footprint and wall-clock time.

</details>


### [203] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal Atmospheric Data Assimilation with Latent Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05103)
*Dibyajyoti Chakraborty,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜视频流匹配（latent video flow-matching）的大气数据同化统一方法，使用ERA5再分析数据训练的生成式先验模型，结合后验采样实现滤波、平滑和预报等多种数据同化任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化基于贝叶斯推断更新数值预报模型状态，计算代价高且框架复杂。作者希望提出一种从根本上不同的、统一的方法，能够以生成式建模方式同时处理多种同化任务（滤波、平滑、预报），并直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报。

Method: 使用潜视频流匹配（latent video flow-matching）方法，在ERA5再分析数据（69个变量、8天窗口）上训练生成先验，该先验生成连续的时间轨迹。通过后验采样融合真实观测数据（NOAA综合无线电探空档案和综合地表数据库），改变观测帧即可实现不同的数据同化任务，如滤波和平滑。

Result: 该方法能够直接生成全状态集合预报，且性能与最先进的观测到预报（observation-to-forecast）模型相当。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了生成式模型在大气数据同化中的潜力，通过单一统一框架即可完成滤波、平滑和预报等任务，且性能具有竞争力，为大气数据同化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) uses Bayesian inference to update the state of a numerical forecast model with observed data. In this study, we propose a fundamentally different, unified approach to atmospheric data assimilation. We use latent video flow-matching to sample temporally consistent trajectories from a prior trained using ERA5 reanalysis (69 variables over an 8-day window). We also use posterior sampling to assimilate real observation sources, such as those from the NOAA Integrated Global Radiosonde Archive and the Integrated Surface Database. Because the prior generates a continuous trajectory, it naturally propagates information between observed and unobserved frames. Therefore, we can perform various DA tasks, such as filtering and smoothing, simply by changing the observed frames. Moreover, we generate full-state ensemble forecasts directly from sparse observations, achieving performance competitive with state-of-the-art observation-to-forecast models.

</details>


### [204] [BnBERT-iPET: Sparse Few-Shot Language Modeling for Bengali via Lottery Ticket Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05104)
*Sajib Hossain,Md Kamrus Samad,Anan Ghosh,Labib Imam Chowdhury,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BnBERT-iPET，一种针对孟加拉语的稀疏少样本语言建模方法。通过迭代模式利用训练（iPET）实现少样本学习，并采用彩票假设（Lottery Ticket Hypothesis）剪枝技术达到90%稀疏度，仅保留原始BERT模型10%的边，在下游任务上与Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT和XLM-RoBERTa等大型模型性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练模型（如BERT）在NLP任务中表现出色，但训练成本高昂、碳足迹大，且难以在计算资源有限的设备上部署。对于孟加拉语等资源受限语言，训练复杂模型存在障碍，因此需要一种轻量级、高效的少样本学习方法来降低内存和计算需求，同时保持竞争性能。

Method: BnBERT-iPET结合两种关键技术：(1) 迭代模式 exploiting 训练（iPET），用于从少量样本中有效学习；(2) 彩票假设（Lottery Ticket Hypothesis）剪枝技术，保留原始预训练模型（如BERT）中最重要的10%边（即90%稀疏度），从而获得一个轻量级稀疏模型。

Result: 实验表明，仅保留10%边的BnBERT-iPET模型在孟加拉语标准基准数据集的下游任务上，与Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT和XLM-RoBERTa等更大规模的最先进模型相比表现不相上下，成为强有力的竞争者。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在资源受限语言场景下，结合少样本学习和结构化剪枝，可以大幅压缩模型规模（90%稀疏度）而不显著牺牲性能，为计算资源有限环境下的低资源语言NLP提供了一种可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have shown impressive success in NLP tasks owing to their complex structure and huge number of edges. Achieving state-of-the-art performance in natural language processing with a large pre-trained model such as BERT is expensive and time-consuming, carries a large carbon footprint, and is difficult to realize on machines with minimal computational capability. This creates a barrier to training complex models for resource-constrained languages such as Bengali. However, in a complex neural model, not all edges are equally impactful, and the contributions of some of them can be neglected. Pruning promises to reduce the memory footprint of regular networks, shorten the training time of ever-growing networks, and increase inference efficiency without sacrificing comparable performance. In this work, we introduce BnBERT-iPET, a sparse few-shot language modeling approach for Bengali, and experimentally show that a lightweight few-shot-learned language model retaining only 10% of the edges of an initial model such as BERT can perform neck and neck with much larger models on challenging tasks for a resource-constrained language such as Bengali. By learning from few shots through iterative pattern exploiting training and achieving 90% sparsity with the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis pruning technique, our pruned BnBERT-iPET model proves to be a tough competitor to state-of-the-art language models such as Bangla Electra, Indic-BERT, and XLM-RoBERTa on downstream tasks over standard benchmark datasets of the Bengali language.

</details>


### [205] [Reward Structure Shapes the Interaction Between Episodic Exploration and Neural Memory in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05111)
*Jai Malegaonkar,Rohan Patil,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了部分可观测强化学习中探索奖励与记忆架构之间的交互作用，通过跨三个环境的受控实验揭示了探索奖励与记忆的三种截然不同的交互模式（放大差异、拉平到共同上限、完全无效），并形式化了奖励稀疏性的新概念，证明探索和记忆是互补而非替代关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，探索奖励和记忆架构分别在孤立的环境中评估，二者的交互作用未被量化。同时，标准的稀疏奖励概念混淆了时间信号密度与实际监督内容，无法解释探索奖励在不同任务中为何产生截然不同的效果。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，交叉组合多种情节式探索奖励和多样化神经记忆架构，在三个维度不同的环境中（记忆获取方式不同）进行系统比较。通过受控奖励操作验证模式追踪奖励结构而非密度，并提出基于观察锚定奖励机（observation-anchored reward machines）的形式化框架，区分结构性稀疏与潜在性稀疏。

Result: 相同的奖励信号在三种环境中产生三种不同交互模式：在需要主动发现并无监督保留记忆内容的环境中放大架构容量差异；在内容为单一奖励监督线索的环境中使所有架构收敛到相同的性能上限；在观测流纯日程化时完全无效。密集奖励仅当直接监督所需潜在记忆时才中和探索奖励的作用；小幅可避免的探索惩罚会导致策略收敛到次优静止状态，而两种奖励都能解决该问题。

Conclusion: 探索和记忆是互补而非替代关系：探索奖励负责产生暴露（exposure），记忆架构负责将暴露转化为回报（return）。形式化框架通过保留负担（retention burden）来组织和解释不同任务中探索-记忆交互的模式。

Abstract: In partially observable reinforcement learning, agents face a dual bottleneck: they must explore to encounter rewarding states and retain that experience in memory to optimize their policies. Exploration bonuses and memory architectures are traditionally evaluated in isolation, leaving their interaction unmeasured, and standard notions of sparse reward conflate temporal signal density with what the reward actually supervises. We present a controlled study crossing episodic exploration bonuses with diverse neural memory architectures across three environments that vary how the content of memory is acquired. An identical bonus signal yields three distinct interaction patterns: it amplifies architectural capacity differences where memory content must be actively discovered and retained unsupervised; equalizes architectures to a shared ceiling where the content, once sought out, is a single reward-supervised cue; and is null where the observation stream is purely scheduled. Controlled reward manipulations verify that these patterns track reward structure rather than density: a dense reward neutralizes a bonus only if it directly supervises the required latent memory, and a small avoidable penalty on exploratory actions (leaving the optimum unchanged) induces policy convergence to suboptimal stationary states, which either bonus resolves. We then formalize reward sparsity with observation-anchored reward machines, separating structural sparsity (an automaton reproduces the return without the task-required history) from potential sparsity (the one-step reward misprices local exploratory actions); the resulting vocabulary organizes the three regimes by the retention burden each task exposes. Together, these results show exploration and memory are complements, not substitutes: a bonus induces exposure, and only memory converts exposure into return.

</details>


### [206] [DASyR-LLM: Domain-Aware Symbolic Regression with LLMs for Kinetic Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05120)
*Roberto Aliaga Medina,Paulina Quintanilla,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM引导的符号回归（LLM-guided SR）框架，将大型语言模型嵌入迭代式符号回归算法中，用于自动发现化学/生物过程的动力学模型。LLM在每个迭代中扮演两个角色：对候选模型进行定性物化批判，以及基于SR生成模型和化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。在四个日益复杂的in silico案例（涵盖多相催化和生物过程）中，该框架相比最先进的SR框架将识别真实模型的迭代次数减少了41.7-79.3%，且LLM在超过半数引导运行中直接提出正确模型结构，预测性能（R²>0.98）与基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学模型发现是化学工程的核心挑战，符号回归虽能识别可解释的动力学模型，但通常缺乏领域知识，容易探索物化上不合理的模型。LLM提供了将领域专业知识注入搜索过程的途径，以克服传统SR的局限。

Method: 框架将LLM模块嵌入迭代SR算法中，LLM在每个迭代中执行两个任务：(1)对最佳SR候选模型进行定性物理化学评论；(2)基于SR生成的模型和嵌入的化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。通过四个不同复杂度的in silico案例（多相催化和生物过程系统）评估，并进行消融研究分析SR组件和LLM规模的贡献。

Result: LLM引导框架将识别真实模型的迭代次数较最先进的SR框架减少41.7-79.3%；LLM在超过半数引导运行中直接提出正确模型结构；两种方法在独立验证集上的预测性能相当（所有案例R²>0.98）；消融研究表明SR组件和LLM规模均对性能有贡献，缩减规模的LLM在很大程度上保留了发现效率。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效地将领域知识注入科学模型发现过程，大幅减少寻找真实动力学模型所需的实验迭代次数，为全自动、领域感知的动力学建模流水线铺平了道路。

Abstract: Kinetic model discovery is a central challenge in chemical engineering, as accurate rate expressions are essential for understanding and controlling chemical and biological processes. Symbolic regression (SR) has emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for identifying interpretable kinetic models, but usually operates without domain knowledge, often exploring physicochemically implausible models. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for injecting domain expertise into this search. Here, we introduce an LLM-guided SR framework, embedding an LLM module within an iterative SR algorithm for automated kinetic model discovery. The LLM performs two roles at each iteration: (1) a qualitative physicochemical critique of the best SR candidates, and (2) the proposal of new candidate rate expressions guided by the SR-generated models and embedded chemical knowledge. Our framework is evaluated on four in silico case studies of increasing complexity, spanning heterogeneous catalysis and bioprocess systems. Results show the LLM-guided framework reduces iterations to identify the ground-truth model by $41.7-79.3\%$ versus a state-of-the-art SR framework, with the LLM directly proposing the correct model structure in over half of the guided runs. In practical settings, where each iteration typically requires a new wet-lab experiment, this translates into a substantial reduction in experimental effort. Predictive performance on an independent validation set is equivalent between both approaches, with $R^2>0.98$ in all case studies. Ablation studies indicate that both the SR component and the LLM scale contribute to this performance, with a reduced-size LLM largely retaining discovery efficiency. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively inject domain knowledge into scientific model discovery, paving the way toward fully automated, domain-aware kinetic modelling pipelines.

</details>


### [207] [SSTQ:Privacy-Preserving Vector Quantization via Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05127)
*Adel Javanmard,David P. Woodruff,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ)框架，结合过完备等范数紧框架、坐标子采样和隐私感知一维量化，在不牺牲通信效率的前提下实现局部差分隐私分布式优化。该框架包含Flat Randomized Response和Metric-Aware Laplace两种变体，在理论上达到最优均方误差缩放，并在联邦学习任务上验证了优于现有baseline的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式优化中同时实现局部差分隐私和低通信成本是一个关键挑战。现有的向量量化方法（如vqSGD）依赖高维几何构造，存在维度依赖的不利方差，导致隐私保障与通信效率难以兼顾。

Method: 提出SSTQ框架，核心创新包括：(1) 利用过完备等范数紧框架进行高效的向量表示；(2) 引入坐标子采样以降低计算复杂度；(3) 采用隐私感知的一维量化机制，包含Flat Randomized Response和Metric-Aware Laplace两个变体。两种变体分别适用于不同码本位宽场景（后者更适合高码本位宽）。同时推导了一个隐私感知码本目标函数，将码本相关的MSE缩放从O(4^b)降至O(2^b)，并给出理论上最优的MSE缩放证明，通信开销仅为每位客户端⌈log2 N⌉+b比特（N=Θ(d)为框架大小）。

Result: 理论上，SSTQ在保持码本位宽b下实现了最优的均方误差缩放，每位客户端的通信代价仅为⌈log2 N⌉+b比特；通过隐私感知码本目标将MSE缩放从O(4^b)降低到O(2^b)。实验上，在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST联邦学习任务上，SSTQ相比既有基线展现了更优的效用和通信效率。

Conclusion: SSTQ框架有效地解决了局部差分隐私下分布式优化的通信瓶颈问题，通过紧框架几何结构、子采样和隐私感知量化的组合设计，在隐私保护、通信效率和模型效用之间取得了良好平衡，为隐私保护联邦学习提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Achieving local differential privacy in distributed optimization while maintaining low communication cost remains challenging. Existing vector quantization methods, such as vqSGD, use high-dimensional geometric constructions but incur unfavorable dimension-dependent variance. In this work, we propose Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ), a framework that combines overcomplete equal-norm tight frames, coordinate subsampling, and privacy-aware one-dimensional quantization. SSTQ includes two variants: a Flat Randomized Response version and a Metric-Aware Laplace version, the latter being better suited to higher codebook bit-width regimes. We show that SSTQ achieves optimal mean squared error scaling while using only $\lceil \log_2 N \rceil + b$ bits per client, where $N = Θ(d)$ is the frame size. We also derive a surrogate privacy-aware codebook objective that reduces the codebook-dependent MSE scaling from $O(4^b)$ to $O(2^b)$. Finally, we empirically evaluate SSTQ against established baselines on federated learning tasks using CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, demonstrating favorable utility and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [208] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了不同优化器（梯度下降、Adam等）在低秩矩阵分解中的隐式偏差差异源于线性层的规范对称性。只有规范等变的优化器（如梯度下降、共享标量Adam、Muon、Shampoo）才能继承梯度流的低秩恢复机制；坐标级方法（Adam、RMSProp）因不满足等变性而丧失该偏差。作者提出结构定理刻画记忆无等变规则恰为Gram-决定的左预条件器，并通过迁移定理将梯度流的路径性质推广到公共标量流。实验表明从坐标级到共享标量预条件的单参数族可单调恢复低秩偏差，且在矩阵感知、Transformer及高光谱数据集上验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在分解模型 W=UV^T 上训练时隐式偏向低秩解，但Adam在相同初始化下不具备此性质。作者希望追溯这一差异的根源，理解优化器如何利用损失函数的规范对称性（gauge symmetry）来选择低秩插值解，从而解释不同优化器在隐式正则化上的根本性分歧。

Method: 作者从损失在 (U,V)→(UQ,VQ) 下的规范不变性出发，提出优化器具备低秩机制的充要条件是"规范等变"（gauge-equivariant）。通过结构定理证明记忆无等变更新规则等价于Gram-决定的左预条件器族，并通过迁移定理将梯度流的路径性质推广到公共标量流。实验上构建了从坐标级到共享标量预条件的单参数族以隔离各向异性因素，并在矩阵感知、Transformer和高光谱数据集上系统对比九种更新规则。

Result: （1）梯度下降、动量、共享标量Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足规范等变性，而Adam、RMSProp等坐标级方法不满足；（2）在欠定矩阵感知中，从坐标级到共享标量预条件的单参数族单调恢复低秩偏差，证实各向异性是原因；（3）Muon在等速率更新下可精确恢复低秩目标，但谱尾增长时优势消失（"谱调度"现象）；（4）在Transformer中，Adam在第一步就分离了两个规范等价的初始化，而等变优化器保持浮点精度一致，最终逐头不变量 W_Q^TW_K 的相对Frobenius距离相差56%；（5）在两个高光谱数据集上，相同训练损失下梯度下降在最低采样密度下将留出误差降低43-44%，且有效秩更低。

Conclusion: 基础选择（basis choice）不是优化器的调参细节，而是决定优化器选择何种插值解的关键决策。规范等变性是优化器继承梯度流低秩机制的必要条件，其缺失（即各向异性）导致Adam等坐标级方法丧失隐式低秩偏差。该发现统一了多种优化器在低秩恢复上的表现差异，并为设计具有可控隐式正则化的新优化器提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Kitchen Robotic Manipulation utilizing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04042)
*Myung-Hwan Jeon,Sankalp Yamsani,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于家庭环境下餐具处理任务的模块化感知流水线，集成了开放词汇目标检测、多视图分割、实例感知3D重建和2D-3D特征融合策略，用于6D位姿估计和抓取规划。在自定义厨房数据集上通过模块替换评估出最佳配置（LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer），达到89.12%的ADI，并在真实机器人上无需环境特定重训练即可完成水槽到洗碗机转移和杯子堆叠任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在日常生活环境中部署机器人需要鲁棒且能适应多样化动态条件的感知系统，而现有的感知方案在面对厨房等复杂家庭场景中的杂乱、遮挡条件时缺乏足够的适应性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出一个模块化感知流水线，包括开放词汇目标检测、多视图分割、实例感知3D重建以及2D-3D特征融合，用于6D位姿估计与抓取规划。通过系统性地替换多种视觉和几何基础模型，在自定义厨房数据集上进行广泛评估以确定最优配置。

Result: 最优配置（LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer）在包含杂乱和遮挡条件的20场景厨房基准上取得89.12%的ADI。真实机器人演示验证了该配置无需环境特定重训练即可部署，成功执行水槽到洗碗机转移和杯子堆叠等任务。

Conclusion: 该模块化流水线的适应性和可扩展性得到验证，展示了其作为家庭机器人系统实用框架的潜力，代码和补充材料已公开。

Abstract: Deploying robots in everyday human environments requires perception systems that are both robust and adaptable to diverse, dynamic conditions. In this work, we present a modular perception pipeline for household manipulation tasks, with a focus on dishware handling in kitchen environments. The pipeline integrates open-vocabulary object detection, multi-view segmentation, instance-aware 3D reconstruction, and a 2D-3D feature fusion strategy for 6D pose estimation and grasp planning. Its modular design enables systematic substitution of multiple visual and geometric foundation models, allowing us to identify the best-performing configuration through extensive evaluation on a custom kitchen dataset. The best-performing configuration (LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer) achieves an ADI of 89.12\% on the 20-scene kitchen benchmark with cluttered and occluded conditions. Furthermore, real-world demonstrations confirm that the best configuration can be deployed on physical robots without environment-specific retraining, successfully executing tasks such as sink-to-dishwasher transfer and cup stacking. It validates the adaptability and scalability of the pipeline and highlights its potential as a practical framework for household robotic systems. Our code and supplementary materials are available at https://raivlab.github.io/FM_kitchen .

</details>


### [210] [Interpretable Fuzzy Inference for UAV Target Tracking Using Bounding-Box Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04121)
*Reza Ahmari,Ahmad Mohammadi,Vahid Hemmati,Nicholas Edmond,Hossein Z. Saghazadeh,Olusola Odeyomi,Parham Kebria,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的模糊推理框架，用于从YOLO检测框的低维特征（质心位置、面积、宽高比）生成连续偏航角指令，引导无人机朝向地面移动目标。无需显式几何建模，计算轻量且透明。在VICON动作捕捉环境中6,169个标注样本上验证，Takagi-Sugeno模型实现0.140°的MAE和99.676%的±1°精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机基于视觉引导朝向地面车辆是空地协同机器人领域的关键技术，但可靠连续的偏航估计面临感知不确定性、计算资源受限以及控制可解释性需求等挑战。现有深度学习和几何重建方法通常需要大规模数据集、外部定位或复杂建模假设，降低了透明性和在资源受限平台上的部署适用性，因此需要一种既能保证精度又具备可解释性和低计算成本的方案。

Method: 提出一个可解释的模糊推理框架：(1) 从YOLO检测框中提取低维特征（目标质心位置、面积、宽高比），无需显式几何建模；(2) 使用Mamdani模糊系统作为可解释基线，采用肩-三角-肩（shoulder-triangle-shoulder）输入划分；(3) 提出一阶Takagi-Sugeno模糊模型，每个输入配备3个前件隶属度项，参数从训练集分位数推导，形成紧凑的27条规则结构。评价采用VICON动作捕捉环境中的6,169个标注样本，并进行五次随机训练-测试划分。

Result: 在五组随机train-test划分上，Takagi-Sugeno模型达到：MAE为0.140°±0.003°，RMSE为0.200°±0.008°，最大绝对误差为1.254°±0.121°。阈值内精度：±1°内99.676%±0.270%，±3°和±5°内均100.000%±0.000%。图像平面水平位移与预测偏航符号的方向一致性达90.254%±0.612%。

Conclusion: 该框架具有透明性、数据高效性、计算轻量性，适用于资源受限平台上的实时无人机视觉引导。相比现有深度学习与几何重建方法，在降低数据和计算需求的同时保持了极高的偏航估计精度。

Abstract: Vision-based guidance of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) toward unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) supports cooperative aerial--ground robotics, but reliable continuous yaw estimation from onboard vision remains challenging because of sensing uncertainty, limited computation, and the need for interpretable control. Existing deep-learning and geometric-reconstruction approaches often require large datasets, external localization, or complex modeling assumptions, reducing transparency and deployment suitability on resource-constrained platforms. We present an interpretable fuzzy-inference framework that generates continuous yaw commands from low-dimensional features extracted from YOLO boxes: target centroid location, area, and aspect ratio. No explicit geometric modeling is required. A Mamdani fuzzy system serves as an interpretable baseline using a shoulder--triangle--shoulder input partition. It is followed by a first-order Takagi--Sugeno model with three antecedent membership terms per input, whose parameters are derived from training-set quantiles, yielding a compact 27-rule structure. Evaluation uses 6{,}169 labeled samples from a VICON motion-capture environment. Across five randomized train--test splits, the Takagi--Sugeno model achieves a test-set mean absolute error of $0.140^\circ \pm 0.003^\circ$, a root mean squared error of $0.200^\circ \pm 0.008^\circ$, and a maximum absolute error of $1.254^\circ \pm 0.121^\circ$. Within-threshold accuracies are $99.676% \pm 0.270%$ for $\pm1^\circ$ and $100.000% \pm 0.000%$ for both $\pm3^\circ$ and $\pm5^\circ$. Directional consistency between image-plane horizontal displacement and predicted yaw sign reaches $90.254% \pm 0.612%$. These results show that the framework is transparent, data-efficient, computationally lightweight, and suitable for real-time vision-based UAV guidance toward mobile ground targets.

</details>


### [211] [SiMDex: Mining Similar Egocentric Videos for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04196)
*Nie Lin,Takehiko Ohkawa,Sijin Chen,Ruoshi Wen,Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Zhengming Zhu,Yiming Bao,Yunfei Li,Minjie Cai,Xiao Ma,Wei Xu,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SiMDex是一个基于相似度的数据挖掘框架，将去手操作中VLA后训练的人类数据选择问题建模为推荐问题，采用三层召回-排序-重排流程从约3200万个以自我为中心的人类视频样本中提取任务相关子集，仅使用<5%的数据（约149万样本）将整体成功率从47.7%提升至61.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来以自我为中心的人类视频扩展趋势显著，但并非所有数据都对灵巧操作有益，需要明确哪些数据真正有助于机器人操作，以避免盲目混合数据。

Method: 提出SiMDex框架，将人类数据选择建模为推荐问题，采用三层召回-排序-重排（recall-ranking-re-ranking）流水线，在与形态无关的行动空间中从约3200万样本池中为每个机器人演示提取任务相关子集，无需修改VLA架构或训练流程。

Result: 与使用等量随机采样人类数据的强基线相比，SiMDex仅使用约149万（池中<5%）挖掘样本，将总体成功率从47.7%提升至61.1%，表明选择性数据策展优于无差别数据混合。

Conclusion: 数据质量与选择性相比数量更为关键，针对性的数据挖掘和筛选能够显著优于大规模随机混合数据，为机器人灵巧操作的数据利用提供更有效的范式。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an explosive trend of scaling ego-centric human videos for robot manipulation, yet it remains unclear which data actually benefits dexterous manipulation. We present SiMDex, a similarity-based data mining framework that casts human data selection for VLA post-training in dexterous manipulation as a recommendation problem. For each robot demonstration, SiMDex employs a three-layer recall-ranking-re-ranking pipeline to extract task-relevant subsets from a pool of ~32M egocentric human samples, operating in a morphology-agnostic action space that requires no changes to VLA architecture or training. Against a strong baseline trained with an equal amount of randomly sampled human data, SiMDex uses only ~1.49M mined samples (<5% of the pool) yet improves the overall success rate from 47.7% to 61.1%, showing that selective curation outperforms indiscriminate data mixing.

</details>


### [212] [Feasibility of Embedded Photoplethysmography Sensing in Short-Duration Tactile Interactions With Pocket-Sized Robots Using IMU- and Confidence-Based Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04242)
*Turjja Datta,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究将PPG传感器直接嵌入到口袋大小的陪伴机器人AffectaPocket中，实现触摸交互中的自包含心率监测，通过结合IMU的两阶段滤波管线来抑制运动伪影，并在26名参与者的受试者内研究中验证了其与腕戴式传感器的统计等效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的儿童焦虑缓解陪伴机器人依赖外部可穿戴设备监测生理状态，存在可用性障碍并限制了机器人的自主性。作者希望将生理监测能力直接集成到机器人本体中，使机器人在触摸交互过程中无需外部设备即可实现连续、无感的心率监测。

Method: 将PPG传感器嵌入AffectaPocket陪伴机器人中；采用包含惯性测量单元（IMU）的两阶段滤波管线，第一阶段拒绝高方差（高运动伪影）片段，第二阶段采用基于置信度的平滑算法处理恢复期数据；通过26名参与者的受试者内研究，与常用腕戴式传感器进行对比评估。

Result: 两阶段滤波策略显著降低了平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE），与地面真值测量达到统计学等效（p<0.05）。对短时交互的分析表明，传感器需要在较长时间内保持稳定才能收敛。

Conclusion: 将PPG传感器嵌入陪伴机器人用于自包含心率监测是可行的，运动伪影过滤策略有效提升了测量精度，但传感器在短时交互场景下需要更长的稳定时间才能达到收敛，这提示了其在实际应用中可能需要进一步优化响应时间。

Abstract: Ubiquitous companion robots offer a promising avenue for immediate anxiety relief in children, yet their effectiveness relies on the ability to monitor physiological states continuously and unobtrusively. Current solutions often depend on external wearables, which impose usability barriers and limit the robot's autonomy. This paper investigates the integration of an embedded photoplethysmography (PPG) sensor directly into a pocket-sized companion robot, AffectaPocket, to enable self-contained heart rate monitoring during tactile interaction. We address the significant challenge of motion artifacts inherent in handheld usage by implementing a two-stage filtering pipeline that utilizes an onboard Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) to reject high-variance segments and a confidence-based smoothing algorithm for recovery periods. We evaluated the system against a commonly used wrist worn sensor in a Within-Subjects Study with 26 participants. Our results demonstrate that the filtering strategy significantly reduced the Mean Absolute Percentage Error and achieved statistical equivalence to the ground truth measurements (p<0.05). Analysis of short-duration interactions shows that the sensor requires stability over longer periods to converge.

</details>


### [213] [SAFECAST: Robust Failure Detection for VLA Policies with Contrast-Set Training and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04246)
*Harshitha Rajaprakash,Aditeya Prajapati,Rong Xue,Abrar Anwar,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFECAST通过利用对比集扰动来改善隐藏状态风险探测器的训练与校准，显著提升了视觉-语言-动作策略在部署时分布偏移下的失败检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略在部署时面临多种分布偏移（如杂乱环境、干扰物体、光照变化、新物体、初始状态改变及指令措辞变化），导致策略失败。现有的基于隐藏状态的风险探测器结合功能一致性预测虽能检测滚动失败，但其可靠性高度依赖校准数据与部署条件匹配，这在现实中难以保证。

Method: SAFECAST采用对比集扰动来增强隐藏状态探针的训练和校准过程，通过生成视觉和语言层面的对比扰动数据，使探针能够更好地应对部署时的分布偏移。该方法结合功能一致性预测进行失败检测。

Result: 在真实世界DROID和LIBERO模拟实验中，SAFECAST在多个VLM主干网络上显著优于最先进基线的失败检测ROC-AUC分数。研究发现，同时使用视觉和语言对比集扰动增强数据时收益最大，且使用对比集扰动时，sim-to-real校准能产生比仅使用真实滚动数据更好的探针。

Conclusion: SAFECAST通过对比集扰动有效提升了分布偏移下的失败检测可靠性，特别是在结合视觉与语言双重扰动以及sim-to-real校准策略下表现最佳。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies often fail under deployment-time distribution shifts such as clutter, distractor objects, lighting changes, novel objects, altered initial states, and reworded instructions. Hidden-state-based risk probes combined with functional conformal prediction can detect rollout failures, but their reliability depends on calibration data matching deployment conditions. We introduce SAFECAST, which leverages contrast set perturbations to improve hidden-state probe training and calibration for deployment time shift. SAFECAST statistically significantly improves failure detection ROC-AUC scores over a state of the art baseline in both real-world DROID and LIBERO simulation experiments across multiple VLM backbones. We further find that SAFECAST benefits most when both visual and language contrast set perturbations are used to augment data, and that with contrast set perturbations, sim-to-real calibration leads to better probes than using real rollout data only.

</details>


### [214] [Structured LLM Reasoning for Zero-Shot Human--Robot Coordination Under Hidden Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04309)
*Dong Hae Mangalindan,Anand Gokhale,Francesco Bullo,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于结构化大语言模型（LLM）的零样本人机协作架构，用于具有私有目标视角的协作建筑任务。该架构以Dec-POMDP为理论基础，将决策分解为行动条件化心智理论推断、分层规划、对话理解、行动验证和基于反馈的重规划五个模块。人类参与者实验表明，该方法相比无ToM推断的消融模型及离线多智能体强化学习基线，需要更少的交互步骤并获得更高的信任评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零样本人机协作场景中，机器人缺乏与人类事先联合训练的机会，且面对私有目标视角下的人类意图推断与协作规划问题。传统多智能体强化学习方法需要大量离线训练且难以泛化到新的人类伙伴；而LLM虽然具备推理能力，但直接用于决策可能产生物理不可行的行动。因此需要系统性地分解团队决策问题，将LLM作为棘手的推断与规划计算的可行替代，同时保留传统验证机制保障物理可行性。

Method: 基于Dec-POMDP形式的指导，提出结构化LLM架构，将决策过程分解为五个模块：(i) 行动条件化心智理论（ToM）推断——推断人类伙伴的私有目标；(ii) 分层规划——在高层目标与低层动作间进行规划；(iii) 对话理解——解析人类自然语言指令；(iv) 行动验证——确保物理可行性；(v) 基于反馈的重规划——根据任务反馈动态调整计划。

Result: 在人类参与者实验中，所提方法相比无ToM推断的消融基线以及离线训练的MARL策略，均表现出更少的交互步数和更高的交互后信任评分。

Conclusion: 系统性地分解团队决策问题，将LLM用作棘手的推断与规划计算的可行替代方案，同时保留传统验证机制确保物理可行性，能够同时提升任务协调效率与人类交互体验。

Abstract: We present a structured large-language-model (LLM) architecture for zero-shot human--robot coordination in a cooperative construction task with private goal views. Guided by a Dec-POMDP formulation, the architecture decomposes decision-making into (i) action-conditioned Theory-of-Mind (ToM) inference, (ii) hierarchical planning, (iii) conversation interpretation, (iv) action verification, and (v) feedback-based replanning. We compare the proposed method with an ablation without ToM inference and a multi-agent reinforcement-learning policy trained offline over many goal pairs. In human-participant experiments, the proposed method required fewer interaction steps and yielded higher post-interaction trust ratings than both baselines. These results suggest that systematically decomposing the team decision problem, using LLMs as tractable surrogates for otherwise intractable inference and planning computations, and retaining conventional verification for physical feasibility can improve both task coordination and the human experience.

</details>


### [215] [Design and Flight of an Ion-propelled Micro Hovercraft Leveraging Ground Proximity Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04343)
*C. Luke Nelson,Grant Nations,Mrinmoy Modak,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次展示了离子推进微型悬浮艇（电空气动力学推进的厘米级悬浮艇），利用地面效应实现低空飞行，测得推力效率16 mN/W，有效载荷约1.5克，远超同类机器人一个数量级，开创了新型机器人类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电空气动力学推进具有无噪音、固态结构的优势，适合微型飞行器，但此前效率不足无法实现动力自主飞行。近期研究发现靠近地面时小型大气离子推进器的推力密度和效率可大幅提升，这为开发厘米级悬浮艇提供了新的可能。

Method: 首先对厘米级悬浮艇的设计空间进行经验性探索，系统表征不同几何构型和被动悬浮艇裙边配置下的性能优势与权衡；随后利用实验结果制造可行的样机点设计，制作了一款掌上大小的悬浮艇。

Result: 展示了一款掌上大小、外接电源供电的离子推进悬浮艇：可持续飞行较长时间，经受数十次起降循环，被动稳定以抵抗显著机械扰动，几乎零可闻噪音。测量得推力效率16 mN/W，额外有效载荷近1.5克，超过自身质量约1.6克，整体性能比同类电空气动力学推进机器人高出一个数量级。

Conclusion: 这是文献中首次报道离子推进微型悬浮艇，工作指明了全新类别机器人的发展方向，为未来动力自主飞行和低空悬浮平台铺平道路。

Abstract: Electroaerodynamic propulsion is compelling for use in micro air vehicles due to its silent and solid-state nature, but its limited efficiency has thus far precluded a path towards power-autonomous flight. Recent work has shown that thrust density and efficiency for small-scale atmospheric ion thrusters can be vastly increased when operating close to a ground plane. Here, we explore the design space of centimeter-scale hovercraft, which can leverage this ground effect for low-altitude flight. We first perform an empirical investigation, characterizing the performance benefits and trade-offs for different geometries and configurations of passive hovercraft skirts, then use the results to fabricate a viable point design. We demonstrate a palm-sized hovercraft that, while tethered to an external power source, can fly for extended periods, withstand dozens of takeoff and landing cycles, passively stabilize to reject significant mechanical disturbances, and generate practically zero audible noise signature. The measured thrust efficiency of 16 mN/W and additional payload capacity of almost 1.5 grams above the vehicle's self mass of about 1.6 grams exceeds any similarly sized electroaerodynamically propelled robot by an order of magnitude. This is the first time an ion-propelled micro hovercraft has been shown in the open literature, and our work points the way towards an entirely new class of robot.

</details>


### [216] [Approximate Multi-Objective Search Under Rulebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04398)
*Omar Muhammetkulyyev,Oren Salzman,Tichakorn Wongpiromsarn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RA*pex算法，通过引入epsilon-rule-dominance（epsilon规则支配）概念，在规则手册（rulebook）约束下高效计算epsilon近似最优解的紧凑集合，实现比现有方法快两个数量级的计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人规划通常涉及多个具有复杂优先级关系的目标（如安全、效率和法规遵从）。规则手册将这些关系形式化，允许目标的部分排序，推广了帕累托和字典序支配。然而，计算完整的规则手册最优解集在计算上非常昂贵，亟需一种高效的近似求解方法。

Method: 引入epsilon-rule-dominance（epsilon规则支配）概念，作为规则手册下近似支配的原则性定义；提出RA*pex最佳优先搜索算法，利用维度缩减技术加速多目标搜索，同时通过维护分离的闭合集合（closed sets）并对截断和残差规则集执行支配检查来尊重规则层级结构。

Result: 提供了RA*pex的形式化分析，证明了每个规则手册最优解至少被返回集合中的一个解epsilon-规则支配。实验结果表明显著优于现有方法，计算时间快两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: RA*pex通过epsilon-规则支配和维度缩减的结合，在保证近似最优性保证的前提下，大幅提升了规则手册约束下多目标机器人规划的计算效率。

Abstract: Robotic planning often involves multiple objectives with complex priority relationships, such as safety, efficiency, and regulatory compliance. Rulebooks formalize these relationships, allowing partial ordering of objectives that generalizes both Pareto and lexicographic dominance. Computing the full set of rulebook-optimal solutions, however, is computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we introduce the concept of epsilon-rule-dominance, a principled notion of approximate dominance under rulebooks, and propose RA*pex, a best-first search algorithm that efficiently computes a compact set of epsilon-approximate rulebook-optimal solutions. RA*pex leverages dimensionality reduction, a technique used to speed up existing multi-objective search algorithms, while respecting rule hierarchies by maintaining separate closed sets and performing dominance checks over truncated and residual rule sets. We provide a formal analysis of RA*pex, proving that every rulebook-optimal solution is epsilon-rule-dominated (a generalization of approximate dominance we introduce) by at least one solution in the returned set. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach achieves computation times over two orders of magnitude faster than existing methods.

</details>


### [217] [SCOPE: Field-of-View-Aware Path Planning in Unknown 3D Environments via Safety-Volume Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04420)
*Junbin Yuan,Muqing Cao,Yunwoo Lee,Brady Moon,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，通过在线安全体积认证和观察驱动的规划，在未知三维环境中使用窄视场传感器实现安全导航，在60项随机任务中全部到达目标且几乎不进入未认证空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用安装在机器人本体上的窄视场传感器进行安全导航时，必须在执行前完整观察并验证预期运动的机器人膨胀体积是空闲的。现有方法缺乏对这种在线安全体积认证的系统性规划方法，特别是在未知体素地图中。

Method: SCOPE框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 构建认证图，其顶点对应具有完全已知空闲安全体积的位置；(2) 将乐观路线上第一个未认证点转化为显式观察义务，通过以目标为中心的视点搜索解决，并递归清除中间义务；(3) 认证预览机制与观察感知的轨迹优化后端实现平滑执行。同时证明了在理想单调感知和穷尽有限域图搜索下的条件完备规划性质。

Result: 在三个未知三维环境中的60项随机任务中，SCOPE全部到达目标且保持接近零的未认证膨胀空间进入率。预览机制将平均任务时间减少27%，真实机器人实验在两个代表性场景中验证了完整系统。

Conclusion: SCOPE通过将安全导航问题重构为安全体积认证问题，成功实现了在未知环境下基于窄视场传感器的安全、完备且高效的导航规划，并在仿真和真实机器人上均得到验证。

Abstract: Safe navigation with a body-mounted limited-field-of-view sensor requires the complete robot-inflated volume of an intended motion to be observed and verified free before execution. We formulate this requirement as online safety-volume certification in an unknown voxel map and construct a certified graph whose vertices correspond exactly to positions with fully known-free safety volumes. Based on this representation, we propose SCOPE (Safety Certification through Observation Planning and Execution), a planning framework that decouples optimistic goal-directed guidance from certified execution. SCOPE converts the first uncertified point along an optimistic route into an explicit observation obligation, resolves it through target-centric viewpoint search, and recursively clears intermediate obligations when useful viewpoints are not yet certified-reachable. A certified preview mechanism and an observation-aware trajectory optimization backend enable smooth execution. We prove conditional complete planning: under ideal monotone sensing and exhaustive finite-domain graph search, SCOPE reaches the goal whenever a finite feasible sequence of certified sensing actions exists within its planning primitives. Across 60 randomized tasks in three unknown 3D environments, SCOPE reaches every goal while maintaining near-zero entry into non-certified inflated space. Preview reduces mean mission time by 27%, and real-robot demonstrations in two representative scenarios validate the complete system.

</details>


### [218] [SSC: A Verifiable Structured Representation for Bimanual Manipulation Labelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04425)
*Yupu Lu,Shuang Wu,Sihan Chen,Ruihua Han,Yichen Zhang,Marcus Kalander,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化子任务链（SSC），一种基于状态转移的演示子任务标注表示方法，通过结构化子任务模板（SST）存储核心动作组件、灵活条件、基础运动字段和动作后场景图，支持自然语言渲染、四种状态转移规则校验和欠完整字段补全三种视觉-语言辅助功能，并在BEHAVIOR-1K数据集上验证了13个SOTA视觉语言模型作为验证器的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作演示的子任务标注在自然语言描述与刚性模板格式之间存在矛盾：自然语言易读但语言变异性大导致自动验证困难；刚性模板（如BEHAVIOR-1K的skill_annotation）语言过度分段，损害可读性和标注一致性。需要一种介于两者之间的中间表示形式。

Method: 提出结构化子任务链（SSC），每个演示被表示为结构化子任务模板（SST）序列。每个SST包含核心动作成分（主语、谓语、宾语）、灵活条件（如空间或工具短语等状语修饰）、独立于手臂动作的基础运动字段，以及动作后场景图。在此基础上，SSC支持三种视觉-语言辅助功能：将SST渲染为自然语言、根据四种状态转移规则检查组装后的链、通过查询解析级联补全欠指定字段。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K数据集上（50个任务，每任务3个片段，2357个标注动作单元）实例化该流水线，用于逻辑验证和内容补全，评估了13个选定的SOTA视觉语言模型作为候选验证器，并报告了标注异常。

Conclusion: SSC通过结构化模板在自然语言可读性与自动验证可行性之间取得平衡，为长时程操作演示的子任务标注提供了一种统一的、可验证且可补全的表示框架。

Abstract: Subtask labels decompose a long-horizon manipulation demonstration into shorter semantic segments for policy training and evaluation. Natural language descriptions are easy to read, but their linguistic variability makes automatic verification difficult. Rigid template formats, such as BEHAVIOR-1K's skill_annotation, are linguistically over-segmented, hindering both readability and annotation consistency. We propose the Structured Subtask Chain (SSC), a state-transition representation that bridges these extremes. A demonstration is a sequence of Structured Subtask Template (SST) entries. Each SST stores core action components (subject, predicate, object), flexible conditions (adverbial modifiers such as spatial or instrumental phrases), a base-motion field separate from arm actions, and an after-state scene graph. Built on this format, SSC supports three vision-language assisted functions: rendering SSTs as natural language, checking the assembled chain against four state-transition rules, and completing underspecified fields through a query resolution cascade. We instantiate the pipeline on BEHAVIOR-1K (50 tasks, 3 episodes per task, 2,357 annotated action cells) for logic verification and content completion, evaluating 13 selected state-of-the-art VL models as candidate verifiers and reporting labelling anomalies.

</details>


### [219] [GUARD: Grounding Uncertainty and Ablation-Based Risk Detection for Diffusion-Based VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04510)
*Suhas Hegde,Jitendra Yasaswi Bharadwaj Katta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GUARD是一种测试时故障检测方法，通过在无需修改预训练策略的情况下测量动作对视觉和语言证据的依赖程度，来识别弱接地（weakly grounded）的扩散式视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略行为。它通过构建反事实KV缓存并比较去噪响应，生成包含敏感性、注意力熵、模态偏差和接地效率的诊断流，经在线校准后由轻量级时序分类器处理，在五个策略基准设置中取得最优或次优的RLU-AUC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式VLA策略即使在输出动作与视觉和语言证据的关联很弱时，仍可能生成看似合理的动作，这导致故障难以被及时发现。现有方法需要修改预训练策略或缺乏对多模态证据依赖的深入建模，因此迫切需要一种无需修改预训练策略、能够在推理时准确检测弱接地故障的方法。

Method: GUARD通过以下步骤实现：首先估计最终VLM键值（KV）缓存中token级条目的影响力；然后构建反事实缓存，即删除显著KV条目后比较去噪响应与原始条件；据此推导出包含四个维度的诊断流——敏感性、注意力熵、模态偏差和接地效率；这些指标在线校准后由轻量级时序分类器处理以进行故障判定。

Result: 在LIBERO、SimplerEnv、MetaWorld和PhysicalAI-AV五个任务留存基准上，使用Pi0、SmolVLA和Alpamayo-1.5评估。GUARD在五个未见任务设置中的四个取得了最佳ROC-AUC，在剩余一个设置中排名第二；相比最强的现有运行期监控方法，未见任务平均ROC-AUC提升了5.73个百分点，同时与最佳已见任务平均结果仅相差0.19个百分点。

Conclusion: 直接探测动作头对多模态证据的依赖程度能够提供一种跨策略、跨任务、跨实体和跨领域可迁移的故障检测信号，GUARD在不修改预训练策略的前提下展现出强大的泛化能力和竞争性性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies can generate plausible actions even when their predictions are weakly grounded in the visual and language evidence defining the task. We introduce GUARD, a test-time failure detection method that measures this grounding without modifying the pretrained policy. GUARD estimates the influence of token-indexed entries in the final vision-language model key-value (KV) cache, constructs counterfactual caches by ablating salient KV entries, and compares their denoising responses with the original conditioning. Based on the comparison, we derive GUARD diagnostic stream including sensitivity, attention entropy, modality bias, and grounding efficiency, which are calibrated online and processed by a lightweight temporal classifier. We evaluate GUARD under task-held-out splits across five policy-benchmark settings, using Pi0, SmolVLA, and Alpamayo-1.5 on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, MetaWorld, and PhysicalAI-AV. GUARD achieves the best ROC-AUC on four of five unseen-task settings and ranks second on the remaining setting, improving the average unseen-task ROC-AUC by 5.73 percentage points over the strongest competing runtime monitor while remaining within 0.19 points of the best seen-task average. These results show that directly probing action-head dependence on multimodal evidence provides a transferable failure signal across policies, tasks, embodiments, and domains.

</details>


### [220] [Retrieve in Time, Correct in Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04527)
*Yuze Fan,Yue Cao,Pengjie Gao,Haojia Gao,Guangqiu Guo,Ziyue Zhang,Junbo Tan,Bokui Chen,Zhuo Zou,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RTCF是一种无需训练的测试时校正框架，通过渐进记忆对齐（PMA）检索与当前执行阶段对齐的成功轨迹，并将对齐动作块中低频运动残差以系数裁剪方式迁移到冻结VLA策略输出上，在四个LIBERO套件上将成功率从86.4%提升至88.4%，且校正可在客户端CPU上以每动作块约11ms完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然能生成长时间跨度的动作块，但在长时程操作中容易累积执行误差并受到任务阶段间的视觉混淆影响。现有帧检索方法可能返回进度不对齐的动作，而直接重放或时域融合会破坏策略输出的反应性结构。因此需要一种既能利用成功轨迹中的纠正性证据、又不干扰策略原始反应结构的高效校正方法。

Method: RTCF将"检索什么经验"与"迁移动作的哪部分"相分离。具体包含两大组件：(1) 渐进记忆对齐（PMA）：通过增量更新的单调前沿，将不断增长的视觉执行历史与完整的成功轨迹进行因果对齐，无需阶段标签即可同时识别相关记忆与当前对齐的记忆位置；(2) 低频残差迁移：从对齐动作块中提取运动通道上的系数裁剪低频残差，高频分量和夹爪决策仍保留冻结策略的原始输出，从而避免覆盖策略的反应性结构。

Result: 在四个LIBERO基准套件和每个条件下2000个回合的实验中，RTCF将整体成功率从86.4%提升至88.4%，其中LIBERO-Long从61.6%提升至68.6%。该方法无需参数更新、无需重复VLA推理、不增加GPU资源，校正可在单次策略调用后在客户端CPU上执行，每动作块的中位延迟总和仅10.99ms。

Conclusion: RTCF通过分离检索时域对齐与频域校正，实现了对冻结VLA策略的高效、轻量级测试时修正，显著提升了长时程操作任务的成功率，同时保持极低的计算开销，为无需重新训练即可改善VLA策略性能提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate temporally extended action chunks, but long-horizon manipulation remains vulnerable to accumulated execution error and visual aliasing across task stages. Successful rollouts provide useful corrective evidence, yet current frame retrieval can return progress-misaligned actions,while direct replay or time-domain fusion can overwrite the reactive structure of the policy proposal. We introduce Retrieve in Time, Correct in Frequency (RTCF), a training-free test-time correction framework that improves frozen VLA performance with low model-side overhead.RTCF separates which experience to retrieve from which part of its action to transfer. Progressive Memory Alignment (PMA) causally aligns the growing visual execution history with complete successful trajectories through incrementally updated monotonic frontiers, jointly identifying a relevant memory and the current aligned memory position without stage labels. From the aligned action chunk,RTCF transfers a coefficient-wise-clipped low-frequency residual on motion channels. Higher-frequency components and gripper decisions remain inherited from the frozen policy. Across four LIBERO suites and 2,000 episodes per condition, RTCF raises aggregate success from 86.4% to 88.4% and improves LIBERO-Long from 61.6% to 68.6%.These gains require no parameter updates, repeated VLA inference, or additional GPU resources: correction can be performed on the client CPU after a single policy invocation, and the median latencies sum to only 10.99 ms per action chunk

</details>


### [221] [Static Timing Orchestration for Tree-Structured Robot Control Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04600)
*Wang Xi,Feiran Wei,Mo Deng,Weiheng Lin,Pangkit Fong,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FineMote，一个面向树形结构设备模型的机器人控制固件生成框架，通过调度机制利用编译时信息静态确定执行顺序，满足截止时间和优先级约束，并在真实机器人控制平台上验证了其时序行为和响应性能的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统日益复杂，从结构化描述文件生成控制固件成为降低开发复杂度和提高可维护性的有前景范式。然而机器人描述格式将系统表示为树形结构，这种组织方式引入了影响感知到决策延迟的结构化数据依赖，进而影响控制性能。因此需要一种能够针对树形结构设备模型优化调度机制的固件生成框架。

Method: FineMote框架包含两个核心设计：(1) 将异构的低层控制逻辑对象化，暴露统一的调度单元和执行入口点；(2) 基于生成的对象层级，调度机制利用编译时信息静态确定执行顺序，以最小化运行时开销。论文证明了该机制满足截止时间和优先级约束，并推导了树内决策延迟的上界。

Result: 在真实机器人控制平台上实现的实验结果表明，FineMote改善了系统的时序行为和运行时响应能力，验证了该设计的实际有效性。

Conclusion: FineMote通过面向树形设备模型的编译时调度机制，在减少运行时开销的同时保证了实时约束，有效提升了机器人控制固件的时序性能和响应性，为复杂机器人系统的固件生成提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: As robotic systems become increasingly complex, generating control firmware from structural description files has emerged as a promising paradigm for reducing development complexity and improving maintainability. Existing robot description formats naturally represent robotic systems as hierarchical tree structures, where devices are recursively composed into functional subsystems and eventually into the complete robot. However, such tree-structured organization also introduces structured data dependencies that affect perception-to-decision latency and, consequently, control performance.
  In this paper, we propose FineMote, a control firmware generation framework with a scheduling mechanism tailored for tree-structured device models. The framework objectifies heterogeneous low-level control logic and exposes unified scheduling units and execution entry points. Based on the resulting object hierarchy, the scheduling mechanism exploits compile-time information to statically determine execution order with minimal runtime overhead. We prove that the proposed mechanism satisfies deadline and precedence constraints, and further derive an upper bound on intra-tree decision latency. We implement the proposed framework and evaluate it on real robotic control platforms. The experimental results show improved timing behavior and runtime responsiveness, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of the proposed design.

</details>


### [222] [GASP: GPU-Accelerated Safe Planner for Real-Time Collision-Aware Motion Generation with Latent Trajectory Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04612)
*Colin Merk,Stefanos Charalambous,Peter Dürr,Farshad Khadivar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GASP是一个面向已知环境的GPU加速安全规划器，用于实时碰撞感知的关节空间运动生成。它结合了B样条轨迹参数化与卷积残差神经网络预测内部控制点，并通过条件变分自编码器采样多个轨迹候选，在GPU上并行解码验证，实现近毫秒级推理。在在线运动生成中达到解析级成功率，在机器人乒乓球强化学习重置规划中可约减一半训练碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时机器人运动规划需要在已知环境中实现碰撞感知的关节空间运动生成，但传统轨迹优化方法计算开销大、实时性不足，难以满足高动态任务（如机器人乒乓球）中毫秒级响应的需求。

Method: GASP将夹紧B样条轨迹参数化与卷积残差神经网络相结合，网络负责预测自由内部控制点，解析插入的边界控制点则强制执行初末导数约束。条件变分自编码器用于采样多个轨迹候选，这些候选在GPU上并行解码与验证，构成批量规划器。

Result: 在在线运动生成模块中，GASP达到解析层面的成功率，具备高碰撞感知可行性，且相比基于GPU的轨迹优化大幅缩短推理时间。在机器人乒乓球强化学习重置规划场景中，匹配基线回球率的同时将训练碰撞次数约减少一半。

Conclusion: GASP证明了将学习驱动轨迹生成与GPU并行验证相结合，能够在保证碰撞感知可行性与高质量运动生成的同时实现近毫秒级实时规划，适用于高速动态机器人任务。

Abstract: We present GASP, a GPU-Accelerated Safe Planner for real-time, collision-aware joint-space motion generation in known environments. GASP combines a clamped B-spline trajectory parameterization with a convolutional residual neural network that predicts the free interior control points, while analytically inserted boundary control points enforce initial and final derivative constraints for collision-aware planning under non-stationary conditions. A conditional variational autoencoder samples multiple trajectory candidates, which are decoded and validated in parallel on the GPU, yielding a batched planner for collision-aware coupled joint-space motion with near-millisecond inference. We validate GASP as an online motion-generation module, where it achieves analytical-level success rates with high collision-aware feasibility and substantially reduces inference time relative to GPU-based trajectory optimization. We further deploy GASP as a reinforcement-learning reset planner in competitive robotic table tennis, matching the baseline return rate while roughly halving training-time collisions.

</details>


### [223] [Mind-VLA: Instruction-Aware Spatial Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04633)
*Xingyu Ding,Yuzhong Zhao,Yang Wu,Chaoyang Zhao,Chunhai Zhao,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mind-VLA是一种指令感知的空间表征对齐方法，通过提取语言指令指定的目标物体的三视图及其VAE和VGGT特征，与VLA模型的隐层表征对齐，实现指令感知的3D理解。在LIBERO上达到93.9%，CALVIN上达到4.47，真实机器人目标遮挡任务中平均成功率54%，比最优的指令无关方法高出32个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision-Language-Action (VLA)方法通过将表征与3D场景几何对齐来提升泛化能力，但这类方法本质上是指令无关的——它们统一地对整个场景进行对齐，忽视了语言指令所指定的特定目标物体的3D几何信息。这导致在细粒度操作和目标遮挡等依赖精确目标物体3D理解的任务上表现不佳。

Method: Mind-VLA首先从语言指令中获取指定的目标物体，然后准备该目标物体的三视图（tri-view）并提取对应的VAE和VGGT特征，最后将VLA模型的隐层表征与这些特征进行对齐，从而实现指令感知的3D理解。模型采用紧凑的345M参数骨干网络。

Result: 在LIBERO数据集上达到93.9%，在CALVIN数据集上达到4.47分。在真实机器人目标遮挡任务中达到54%的平均成功率，比参与真实机器人对比的最佳指令无关方法高出32个百分点。

Conclusion: Mind-VLA通过将表征对齐从"全场景统一对齐"转变为"目标物体指令感知对齐"，有效解决了VLA方法在细粒度操作和目标遮挡场景下的不足。代码将公开。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) methods improve generalization by aligning their representations with 3D scene geometry. However, these methods are fundamentally instruction-agnostic: the representations align the entire scene uniformly, neglecting the 3D geometry of the specific target object designated by the language instruction. This causes failures on fine-grained manipulation and target occlusion tasks, where success depends on accurate 3D understanding of the target object rather than the entire scene. To address this, we present Mind-VLA, an instruction-aware spatial representation alignment method for VLA models. Specifically, Mind-VLA first obtains the target object specified by the language instruction, then prepares its target-object tri-view and extracts the corresponding VAE and VGGT features. Finally, the latent representation of the VLA model is aligned with these features to enable instruction-aware 3D understanding. Mind-VLA reaches 93.9% on LIBERO and 4.47 on CALVIN with a compact 345M-parameter backbone. On real-robot tasks with target occlusion, Mind-VLA reaches 54% average success, outperforming the best-performing instruction-agnostic method in real-robot comparison by 32 percentage points. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [224] [Suppression Sticks, Locality Is Fragile: A Closed-Loop Target-and-Control Audit of Task-Vector Negation in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04692)
*Shaoguang Wang,Weiyu Guo,Rushi Dai,Yiren Zhao,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对多任务视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略进行任务向量减法的目标与控制审计，发现减法在10个LIBERO-Goal技能中产生三种不同行为机制，且目标抑制编辑会损害无关控制，表明任务向量减法是一种快速但脆弱的干预手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务向量算术提供了一种闭式方式修改模型，但其在闭环机器人控制中的行为局部性仍不清楚，需要进行系统的目标与控制审计。

Method: 对LIBERO基准的10个Goal技能逐一执行每技能任务向量减法，测量目标抑制率与对照组保留率；在Goal、Spatial、Object和Long-horizon面板上跨连续回归、离散token和流匹配三种动作头进行验证；使用匹配范数对照、多向量组合分析、保留感知梯度基线以及单技能重新学习探针来系统刻画减法行为。

Result: 10个Goal技能中5个呈现目标-控制分离，3个抵抗，2个全局崩溃；5个可抑制目标的保留状态成功率为0%，但平均基线归一化控制保留率仅52%，每个编辑都严重损害至少一个无关控制。不同动作头策略在Goal面板上观察到分离，但Spatial无清晰分离，Object和Long-horizon面板控制崩溃。任务向量余弦均值无法解释这种变异性，匹配范数对照揭示局部符号不对称性，保留感知梯度基线需编辑时数据但减法仅在预计算专家差值下免数据免梯度。

Conclusion: 任务向量减法是一种快速但脆弱的模型编辑干预，在闭环VLA策略中局部性高度不稳定，强调在具身模型编辑中需采用闭环目标-控制评估框架来衡量编辑局部性。

Abstract: Task-vector arithmetic offers a closed-form way to modify a model, yet its behavioral locality remains unclear in closed-loop robot control. We present a target-and-control audit of per-skill task-vector subtraction from multitask vision-language-action (VLA) policies. Across all ten LIBERO-Goal skills, subtraction produces three qualitatively different regimes: target-control separation for five skills, resistance for three, and global collapse for two. On held-out initial states, the five suppressible targets remain at 0% success; however, mean baseline-normalized control retention is only 52%, and each target-suppressing edit materially harms at least one nominally unrelated control. Additional Goal panels show separation across tested policies with continuous-regression, discrete-token, and flow-matching action heads, whereas we observe no clean separation on Spatial and control collapse on the tested Object and Long-horizon panels. Mean task-vector cosine does not account for this variation. A matched-norm control identifies a local sign asymmetry around one Goal anchor, while multi-vector outcomes vary with anchor and scale. Retain-aware gradient baselines provide data-dependent comparators but require removal-time data and optimization; subtraction is data- and gradient-free only at edit time, assuming precomputed expert deltas. Finally, a single-skill relearning probe is consistent with behavioral masking, not certified unlearning. These results characterize task-vector subtraction as a fast but brittle intervention and underscore the need for closed-loop target-and-control evaluation when assessing locality in embodied model editing.

</details>


### [225] [Enabling Urgency-aware Robot Swarm Intralogistics using Smart IoT Tags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04721)
*Youssef Alboraei,Murray Groves,Shane Wen,Wenda Zhao,Senhui Qiu,Mohammud J. Bocus,Robert Piechocki,Sabine Hauert,Kerstin Eder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向仓库物流的去中心化机器人群体系统，每个仓储载体配备IoT标签广播货物紧急度，机器人直接读取广播并根据紧急度与行驶距离权衡选择服务目标，在无需中央调度的情况下实现物品级优先级排序。仿真与实物实验表明，该方法显著提升了紧急物品的优先服务比例，且系统规模越大收益越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库中不同物品的搬运紧急程度不同（如易腐烂商品、药品运输、JIT生产物料需优先交付）。去中心化机器人群体适合无法配置固定自动化基础设施的仓库，但现有群体控制器将所有物品一视同仁，或依赖外部调度器设置优先级，导致紧急物品与普通物品等待时间相同，缺乏物品级别的优先级处理机制。

Method: 每个仓库载体配备超低功耗物联网（IoT）标签，通过蓝牙低功耗（BLE）广播其物品的紧急度。机器人直接读取这些广播，在选择服务哪个载体时同时权衡紧急度与行驶距离，从而在无需中央调度的情况下实现物品级优先级排序。系统在仿真环境中评估，并在真实机器人及带IoT标签的载体上进行验证。

Result: 实物试验中，优先级对齐（即紧急物品先被服务的比例）从0.41提升到0.64，P95交付延迟呈非显著性降低趋势，吞吐量在基线水平的1.2%以内。仿真中收益随系统规模增长：在三个更大的配置下，P95延迟降低5.2%至11.8%，优先级对齐提升41.7%至51.6%。

Conclusion: 将紧急度附加到物品本身，使去中心化群体系统能够在保持低基础设施要求（使群体系统适用于仓库自动化的关键优势）的同时，优先服务时效性强的库存，实现物品级优先排序且无需中央调度。

Abstract: Warehouse items differ in how urgently they must be moved: perishable goods, pharmaceutical shipments, and just-in-time production materials must be delivered sooner than the rest of the stock. Decentralised robot swarms suit warehouses that cannot justify fixed automation infrastructure, but current swarm controllers treat all items alike or rely on an external scheduler to set priorities, so urgent items wait as long as ordinary ones. This paper presents a swarm logistics system in which each warehouse carrier holds an ultra-low-power Internet-of-Things (IoT) tag that broadcasts the urgency of its item over Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE). Robots read these broadcasts directly and weigh urgency against travel distance when choosing which carrier to serve, so prioritisation happens at the item level without central scheduling. The system is evaluated in simulation and validated on real robots and IoT-tagged carriers against a proximity-only baseline. In the physical trials, priority alignment (i.e. proportion of urgent items served first), improved from 0.41 to 0.64, with a nonsignificant trend toward lower 95th-percentile (P95) delivery latency and throughput within 1.2% of the baseline. In simulation, the benefit grew with system size: across three larger configurations, P95 latency fell by 5.2% to 11.8% and priority alignment improved by 41.7% to 51.6%. Attaching urgency to the items themselves therefore allows a decentralised swarm to serve time-critical stock sooner while keeping the low infrastructure requirements that make swarm systems attractive for warehouse automation.

</details>


### [226] [A Vision-based Control Framework for Real-time Autonomous UUV Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04723)
*Erik Tjærand Frøland,Marco Job,Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向水下无人航行器（UUV）的全集成视觉框架，支持实时稳健定位、自主导航和三维建图，在动态且视觉挑战性强的水下环境中实现了相对全局定位，并通过仿真与实船实验验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境动态复杂、视觉条件恶劣，现有方法难以同时实现实时稳健的定位、导航与建图，限制了UUV在关键检查和测绘任务中的自主部署。

Method: 提出一个全集成视觉框架，包含实时定位（支持网具相对定位与全局定位）、自主导航以及连续三维环境建图模块，形成完整pipeline。

Result: 在含地面真值的合成数据集上验证，并在UUV实船上进行自主相对导航实验。结果表明系统达到实时性能且鲁棒性增强，支持视觉驱动的自主导航。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了实时强鲁棒的视觉自主导航能力，能够推动水下机器人在复杂环境中执行关键检查与测绘任务的现场部署。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully integrated vision-based framework for real-time and robust localization, autonomous navigation, and mapping for unmanned underwater vehicles (UUVs) in dynamic, visually challenging environments. The proposed pipeline enables both net-relative and global localization while generating continuous 3D maps of the surroundings in real-time. The framework was validated on synthetic datasets with ground truth and tested onboard an UUV during autonomous net-relative navigation experiments. Results demonstrate real-time performance and enhanced robustness, supporting vision-driven autonomous navigation and enabling the field deployment of marine robots for critical inspection and mapping tasks in complex underwater environments.

</details>


### [227] [A GitOps-Driven Annotation Catalog for Fully Automatic Railway Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04724)
*Martin Köppel,Tobias Cronauer,Zekiye Ilknur-Öz,Sebastian Dubiel,Patrick Naumann,Philipp Neumaier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GitOps的轻量级元数据管理架构，用于轨道交通安全关键应用中的自动列车运行（ATO）AI感知数据集，通过Data-as-Code、CI/CD流水线和静态站点生成实现可追溯、合规且高性能的数据集管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GoA3-GoA4级别的自动列车运行依赖高性能AI感知系统，这些系统需要大规模、高质量、动态标注的数据集。然而现有单体数据目录在元数据管理、来源追踪和标注迭代追踪方面存在巨大的运营开销、开发者工作流集成差和文档漂移等问题，无法满足基础设施和法规要求。

Method: 采用GitOps架构，结合Data-as-Code原则（将数据元数据视为代码托管于Git仓库）、CI/CD流水线（自动化验证、构建和部署），以及静态站点生成（SSG）技术来自动生成高性能的数据集概览页面，形成无缝的开发者中心化工作流。

Result: 该架构实现了完整的可追溯性，强制执行严格的法规合规要求，并自动生成高性能的数据集概览，同时避免了传统单体数据目录的运营开销和文档漂移问题。

Conclusion: GitOps-based元数据管理架构为轨道交通安全关键场景下的AI数据集管理提供了一种轻量、高效、合规且开发者友好的解决方案，能够满足ATO系统的严格要求。

Abstract: Automatic train operation (ATO) at grade of automation 3 and above (GoA3-GoA4) requires robust AI-based perception systems capable of reliably detecting obstacles and railway-specific objects under real-world conditions. The effectiveness of these modern artificial intelligence approaches depends heavily on large-scale, high-quality, and highly dynamic annotated datasets. However, managing metadata, maintaining provenance, and tracking the iterative evolution of these annotations impose significant infrastructural and regulatory requirements. Existing monolithic data catalogs often suffer from massive operational overhead, poor integration into developer workflows, and severe documentation drift. This paper introduces an innovative, lightweight GitOps-based architecture for metadata management. By leveraging Data-as-Code principles, Continuous Integration/Continuous Deployment (CI/CD) pipelines, and Static Site Generation (SSG), the proposed approach establishes a seamless, developer-centric workflow. This ensures an traceability, enforces strict regulatory compliance, and automatically generates a highly performant dataset overview.

</details>


### [228] [Explicit Language Memory for Long-Horizon Planning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04765)
*Houze Xu,Jizhong Li,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带显式语言记忆模块的分层长时程VLA架构，通过将离散时序观测转化为带时间逻辑的连贯文本记忆序列，解耦为高层VLM（负责语义推理）和低层VLA（负责连续控制），解决长时程任务中的跨任务组合泛化差、时间一致性不足、误差累积和语义表征弱化等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在长时程任务中面临四大挑战：稀疏专家示范限制跨任务组合泛化；长时程任务非马尔可夫性导致仅依赖当前观测的策略难以维持时间一致性；闭环纠错能力有限导致执行误差累积；端到端动作微调可能削弱VLM骨干的高层语义表征能力。

Method: 提出分层长时程VLA架构，核心思想是将离散时序观测转换为带时间逻辑的连贯文本记忆序列。系统解耦为高层VLM和低层VLA：高层VLM通过视觉问答训练范式进行语义推理，低层VLA基于子任务指令和视觉观测执行连续控制；高层VLM以上一记忆为上下文锚点递归更新语言记忆和子任务指令，实现长时程执行中的持续时间跟踪和动态纠错。

Result: 在多个仿真环境及真实机器人平台的sim-to-real实验中，显式语言记忆显著提升了VLA模型在复杂长时程任务中的成功率和鲁棒性，同时为决策过程提供了可解释的语义描述。

Conclusion: 显式语言记忆模块能够有效解决长时程VLA任务中的时间一致性和误差累积问题，提升任务成功率与鲁棒性，且决策过程具有可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a unified paradigm for connecting visual perception, language understanding, and robotic control. However, existing VLA models still face major challenges in long-horizon tasks: sparse expert demonstrations constrain cross-task compositional generalization; the non-Markovian nature of long-horizon tasks makes it difficult for policies conditioned only on current observations to maintain temporal consistency; limited closed-loop error correction allows execution errors to accumulate; and end-to-end action fine-tuning may weaken the high-level semantic representations of vision-language model (VLM) backbones. To address these issues, we propose a hierarchical long-horizon VLA architecture with an explicit language-memory module. The central idea is to convert discrete temporal observations into a coherent textual memory sequence with temporal logic. The system is decoupled into a high-level VLM and a low-level VLA: the high-level VLM performs semantic reasoning through a visual question answering training paradigm, while the low-level VLA executes precise continuous control conditioned on subtask instructions and visual observations. The high-level VLM recursively updates both language memory and subtask instructions using the previous memory as a contextual anchor, enabling persistent temporal tracking and dynamic correction during long-horizon execution. We evaluate the proposed method in multiple simulation environments and conduct sim-to-real experiments on a real robotic platform. The results demonstrate that explicit language memory improves the success rate and robustness of VLA models on complex long-horizon tasks while providing an interpretable semantic account of the decision process.

</details>


### [229] [From Transparent Labware Segmentation to Collision Avoidance: A Real-Time Edge-Aware Perception Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04769)
*Shijun Ding,Chen Qian,Weiwei Shang,Junlin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种边缘感知的实例分割框架，利用透明玻璃器皿的边界轮廓作为可靠视觉线索，实现纯视觉感知下的机器人实时避障。框架在单阶段实例分割骨干上增加了轻量级边缘检测分支、边缘引导注意力融合和SimAM模块，并构建了LabGlass-IS数据集（3485张图、21类）。模型在边界F-score上达到97.80%，推理速度7.1ms/帧，参数量仅为最接近精度竞争者的2.85%；通过多视角三角化获取3D位置实现碰撞约束，真实机器人实验中避障成功率达93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明玻璃器皿因折射、镜面反射和缺乏稳定的内部纹理而难以通过常规分割方法处理，但机器人需要在脆弱透明物体周围安全导航。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的深度传感器，要么分割精度不足，因此需要一种高效、可靠的纯视觉方案来识别透明物体的空间位置，实现实时避障。

Method: 在单阶段实时实例分割骨干上增加轻量级边缘检测分支，利用透明物体的边界轮廓作为稳定视觉线索；通过边缘引导注意力融合增强特征表示，并引入无需参数的SimAM注意力模块提升性能；构建了LabGlass-IS真实数据集（3485图、21类）；利用多视角三角化将掩膜质心转换为3D位置，生成保守的包围盒碰撞约束用于机器人控制。

Result: 边界F-score达97.80%，比YOLO提示的FastSAM框架高出18.93个BF点；推理速度7.1ms/帧；参数量仅为最接近精度竞争者的2.85%；真实机器人实验中碰撞避免成功率达93.3%。

Conclusion: 边缘感知的实例分割结合多视角三角化构建的感知-行动管线在透明物体避障任务中展现了良好的可行性，兼顾了精度、速度和轻量化，为机器人在脆弱的透明物体环境中安全作业提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an edge-aware instance segmentation framework that enables real-time robotic collision avoidance with transparent laboratory glassware using purely visual perception. Transparent vessels defy conventional segmentation due to refraction, specular reflection, and the absence of stable interior texture, yet their boundary contours remain comparatively reliable visual cues. Exploiting this observation, we augment a one-stage real-time instance segmentation backbone with a lightweight edge-detection branch, edge-guided attention fusion, and a parameter-free SimAM module, and further construct LabGlass-IS, a 3485-image, 21-category instance segmentation dataset of real laboratory glassware. The enhanced model achieves the highest Boundary F-score of 97.80 among compared methods, outperforming the YOLO-prompted FastSAM framework by 18.93 BF points. Furthermore, it maintains an inference speed of 7.1ms per frame and requires only 2.85% of the parameters of the closest accuracy competitor. Multi-view triangulation of mask centroids further provides 3D positions for conservative bounding-volume collision constraints. Real-robot trials achieve a 93.3% collision avoidance success rate, indicating the feasibility of the proposed perception-to-action pipeline for robot collision avoidance among fragile transparent objects. Our code is available at https://github.com/havishamy/TransYOLO_3D. Our video is available at https://havishamy.github.io/paper-videos/.

</details>


### [230] [Deliberate Before You Fly: Vision-Guided Spatial Deliberation for UAV See-and-Reach Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04825)
*Fanfu Xue,En Yu,Bohang Liu,Hongjun Wang,Yang Yang,Xindi Wang,Jiande Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DBFly是一个面向无人机"视觉-到达"导航任务的视觉语言航点预测框架，通过显式的空间操作决策链、隐式飞行走廊和终端收敛感知的停止策略，解决了现有方法中语义-控制错位的问题，在多个测试集上将成功率平均提升25.07个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机视觉-到达导航方法通常将视觉-语言表征直接映射为动作输出，未显式建模中间细粒度的空间决策，导致语义-控制错位，进而引发不一致的机动行为与不可靠的停止。

Method: DBFly包含三个核心模块：(1) 空间机动决策链，逐步执行目标方向锚定、空间诊断和机动决策，使高层机动意图显式引导连续航点生成；(2) 隐式飞行走廊，将初始目标方向先验转化为持续几何参考，并结合无人机当前位置在线推导走廊状态，为空间诊断与机动修正提供软几何约束；(3) 终端收敛感知的停止策略，通过目标接近度和短时运动收敛性共同刻画终端状态，实现更可靠的近目标停止。

Result: 在可见目标、未见目标和未见场景三组测试集上的大量实验表明，DBFly相比SOTA基线平均提升了25.07个百分点的成功率。

Conclusion: DBFly通过显式空间推理来桥接语义理解与控制执行之间的鸿沟，验证了在无人机语言引导导航任务中以航点预测为中间表征的有效性，为视觉语言导航与低级控制的解耦提供了可行思路。

Abstract: UAV see-and-reach navigation requires an aerial agent to approach a language-specified target visible in its initial view and stop reliably near it. Existing methods typically map vision-language representations directly to action outputs without explicitly modeling intermediate fine-grained spatial decisions. This direct mapping causes semantic-control misalignment, leading to inconsistent maneuvers and unreliable termination. To address this issue, we propose DBFly, a vision-language waypoint prediction framework that introduces explicit vision-guided spatial deliberation before waypoint generation. Specifically, DBFly introduces a spatial maneuver decision chain that progressively performs target-direction anchoring, spatial diagnosis, and maneuver decision, enabling high-level maneuver intent to explicitly guide continuous waypoint generation. DBFly further constructs an implicit flight corridor by transforming the initial target-direction prior into a persistent geometric reference and deriving an online corridor state from the UAV's current position, thereby providing soft geometric guidance for spatial diagnosis and maneuver correction. In addition, DBFly develops a terminal-convergence-aware stopping strategy that characterizes terminal states through both target proximity and short-horizon motion convergence, enabling more reliable stopping near the target. Extensive experiments across seen, unseen-object, and unseen-scene test sets demonstrate that DBFly improves the success rate over the SOTA baseline by an average of 25.07 percentage points. The project homepage is available at https://xuefanfu.github.io/DBFly-Page.

</details>


### [231] [RORA: Realistic Object Reconstruction with Articulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04842)
*Hyesung Lee,Youngseon Lee,Kyutae Lee,Dongjun Lee,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个端到端管道，通过人类辅助的关节建议机制，从单个静态物体视频重建具有准确关节结构的仿真就绪资产，采用3DGS+网格混合表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实到仿真重建工作依赖动态运动扫描或学习式关节化方法，在复杂多关节结构上成功率低，且需捕捉物体运动使重建流程复杂化。

Method: 从单张静态视频输入，进行凸分解和用户分组实现部件分割，将3D高斯绑定到对应网格部件，提出自动关节建议算法从局部边界几何计算候选关节轴供用户高效选择。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility-v0数据集和真实物体上实现精准关节重建，并在Unreal Engine和NVIDIA Isaac Sim中部署实现实时灵巧手操作任务。

Conclusion: 结合3DGS逼真渲染与网格物理交互的混合表示，配合人机协同的关节建议流程，能在无需动态扫描的条件下高效重建仿真就绪的关节化物体资产。

Abstract: Replicating real-world environments into simulation by realistic visual representation like NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an effective strategy to reduce the sim-to-real gap in robot learning. However, implementing object articulation during the real-to-sim process is still a challenging task. Existing motion tracking or learning based articulation methods shows low success rates on complex kinematic structures having multiple joints. Furthermore, those methods require scan of dynamic motion of objects, which makes reconstruction process much complicated. In this work, we propose the first end-to-end pipeline that reconstructs simulation-ready assets with accurate articulation from a single static object video input through suggestion based human-in-the-loop process. Our approach exports a hybrid representation combining 3DGS for photorealistic rendering and mesh-based geometry for physical interaction. In the reconstruction process, our pipeline performs convex decomposition followed by user grouping for intuitive part segmentation, subsequently binding 3D Gaussians to the corresponding mesh parts. An Automatic Joint Suggestion Algorithm then calculates candidate joint axes from local boundary geometries and presents them to users for efficient articulated asset reconstruction. We have shown that our method achieves precise articulation results on partnet-mobility-v0 dataset and real objects. Additionally we presented a potential usage of our framework on robot learning, deploying the reconstructed assets in Unreal Engine and NVIDIA Isaac Sim, demonstrating real-time dexterous hand manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [232] [PRIMAL3: Pathfinding via Reinforcement and Imitation Multi-Agent Learning - Leveraging LaCAM3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04905)
*Chengyang He,Tanishq Duhan,Gadiel Sznaier Camps,Fangyuan Wang,Yuhong Cao,Jiankai Sun,Ge Sun,Mac Schwager,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIMAL3是一个超大规模的多智能体路径规划（MAPF）学习框架，融合了强化学习、拓扑感知通信、LaCAM3引导训练和PIBT动作细化，在拓扑关键状态（如瓶颈、死胡同和持续冲突）下实现智能体的决定性协调，可扩展至城市级别10万智能体规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF学习方法在拓扑关键状态下缺乏有效协调能力，当智能体需要在瓶颈、死胡同和持续冲突区域进行决定性协作时容易失败；同时，大规模场景下学习和推理的扩展性不足。
