Method: 1) 基于割点、死胡同区域、最短路径距离和阻塞估计构建智能体特征表示；2) 构建两个互补图：同向跟随图传播多跳上下文，异向冲突图通过掩码注意力和相对特征区分竞争智能体；3) 利用策略熵识别不确定智能体，由LaCAM3提供置信度触发的动作干预和标签平滑模仿目标进行训练；4) 执行阶段使用基于优先级的PIBT模块，结合持久化、学习和距离感知优先级及策略感知的回退偏好来细化联合动作，保证无碰撞执行。

Result: PRIMAL3显著超越现有最先进的学习型基线方法，可扩展至包含10万智能体的城市级别超大规模实例；真实世界实验验证了在物理机器人系统上部署的可行性；消融实验验证了各组件（拓扑特征、双图通信、熵引导训练、PIBT细化）的独立贡献。

Conclusion: PRIMAL3通过"学习探索+结构化专家引导"的混合架构，在不需推理时依赖LaCAM3的前提下，实现了大规模MAPF的鲁棒性、可扩展性和物理可部署性，为超大规模多智能体路径规划提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present PRIMAL3, an ultra-large-scale learning-based framework for multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF) that integrates reinforcement learning, topology-aware communication, LaCAM3-guided training, and PIBT-based action refinement. PRIMAL3 targets failures at topologically critical states, where agents must coordinate decisively around bottlenecks, dead ends, and persistent conflicts. Each agent is represented using features derived from cut vertices, dead-end regions, shortest-path distances, and blocking estimates. Two complementary graphs capture agent interactions: a same-direction following graph propagates multihop context along compatible paths, while a different-direction conflict graph differentiates agents competing for shared space through masked attention and relative features. During training, we propose to let policy entropy identify uncertain agents, for which LaCAM3 provides confidence-triggered action interventions and label-smoothed imitation targets. During execution, a priority-aware PIBT module refines the proposed joint actions using persistent, learned, and distance-aware priorities together with policy-aware fallback preferences while maintaining collision-free execution. The resulting framework combines learned exploration with structured expert guidance without requiring LaCAM3 at inference. Experiments demonstrate that PRIMAL3 substantially outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based baselines and scales to ultra-large instances with up to city-level 100,000 agents. Real-world experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of deploying PRIMAL3 on physical robotic systems and ablation studies validate the individual contributions the components we proposed. Project page: https://marmotlab.github.io/PRIMAL3/

</details>


### [233] [Mimir: A Neuro-Symbolic Memory System with Dynamic Grounding for Embodied Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04933)
*Haoming Xu,Zhenlin He,Hengyi Wang,Jiafeng Xu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Mimir，一种神经符号记忆系统，将世界记忆与任务记忆分离，并在每次行动前动态将二者关联于当前目标。在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat任务上平均提升23.0%，最高提升42.5%，在EB-Habitat长时程子集上达到86.0%的成功率，超越现有闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身任务要求在部分可观测条件下行动，同时保持场景信念和执行进度。现有的扁平历史或隐式策略状态虽然包含过去的观测，但缺乏显式接口来判定哪些世界事实支持当前活跃目标，因此需要一种能够显式分离并动态关联世界记忆与任务记忆的方法。

Method: Mimir采用神经符号记忆架构，将记忆分为两部分：世界记忆（维护物体位置、物体状态和感知证据）和任务记忆（维护有序目标议程、进度状态、手部状态、失败记录和执行约束）。在每次行动前，一个接地（grounding）模块将活跃目标与回忆到的世界候选绑定，补全缺失的源位置，并在规划和具身执行前附加证据。

Result: 在不同骨干网络上，Mimir在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat任务上取得一致改进，最高提升42.5%，平均提升23.0%。与同骨干下最优的现有智能体和记忆系统相比，整体平均成功率提升8.5%。在EB-Habitat长时程子集上达到86.0%的成功率，显著优于当前闭源模型。

Conclusion: Mimir通过分离世界记忆与任务记忆并动态接地，有效解决了长时程具身任务中场景信念保持与目标执行进度追踪的问题，且在不同骨干网络上表现出良好的泛化性，证明了神经符号记忆在长时程具身决策中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied task requires agents to act under partial observability while preserving both scene belief and execution progress. Flat histories or implicit policy states may contain past observations, but they do not provide an explicit interface for deciding which world facts support the currently active goal. We introduce Mimir, a neuro-symbolic memory that separates world memory from task memory and dynamically grounds them before each action. World memory maintains object locations, object states, and perceptual evidence, while task memory maintains an ordered goal agenda, progress state, hand state, failures, and execution constraints. A grounding module binds the active goal to recalled world candidates, fills missing source locations, and attaches evidence before planning and embodiment-specific execution. Across tested backbones, Mimir consistently improves on different EB-ALFRED and EB-Habitat tasks, with maximum gains of 42.5% and average gains of 23.0%, respectively. Compared with the best results among prior agent and memory systems evaluated under the same backbone, Mimir improves the overall average success rate by 8.5%. Finally, on the EB-Habitat Long-horizon subset, Mimir achieves 86.0% success rate, substantially outperforming current closed-source models. Our code will be released soon.

</details>


### [234] [DreamWAM: Beyond RGB Future Prediction for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04996)
*Shanglin Yuan,Weiheng Zhao,Xin Shi,Haoyi Jiang,Xianda Guo,Liu Liu,Wenyu Liu,Wei Sui,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamWAM提出超越RGB的"结构化世界建模"框架，通过联合去噪RGB与运动信息，并利用轻量级门控残差分支预测几何与语义，让动作分支在共享注意力下学习未来状态表示。推理时仅需RGB，却在LIBERO和真实机器人操作中显著超越RGB-only基线，尤其在未见扰动下鲁棒性大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型(WAMs)大多在RGB空间预测未来帧，但任务相关的状态转移常与纹理、光照、背景、视角等干扰因素纠缠在一起，导致学到的表示对动作决策不够"动作相关"，在分布外场景下鲁棒性差。

Method: DreamWAM将未来预测重构为结构化世界建模：①使用联合潜在去噪同时预测RGB和运动(motion)；②通过轻量级门控残差分支(gated residual branches)补充几何与语义预测；③VideoDiT与ActionDiT之间共享注意力，使动作分支从这些超越RGB的未来状态预测中学习；④推理阶段所有超越RGB的监督分支全部关闭，部署仅需RGB输入。

Result: 在LIBERO上，no-rollout从97.30%提升到98.40%，联合视频-动作推理从98.00%提升到98.90%；在未见LIBERO-Plus扰动下，分别从51.36%提升到63.44%和从69.16%提升到75.47%。真实机器人实验中，在光照、背景和物体布局变化的条件下，DreamWAM平均成功率达74.4%，远超Fast-WAM-Joint的55.6%。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的世界动作学习不仅依赖于预测未来，更在于以对动作有意义的形式表示未来。通过显式建模动作相关的未来状态（运动、几何、语义），即使推理时仅使用RGB，模型也能获得更强的分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) learn action-relevant representations by predicting how the observed world will evolve. Most existing WAMs define this future in RGB space, where task-relevant state transitions are entangled with nuisance variations in texture, illumination, background, and viewpoint. We argue that WAMs should explicitly predict action-relevant future state rather than relying on RGB prediction alone. We introduce DreamWAM, which reformulates future prediction as structured world modeling beyond RGB, representing future states through complementary views of appearance, motion, geometry, and semantics. During training, DreamWAM combines joint latent denoising of RGB and motion with lightweight gated residual branches for geometry and semantics. Shared attention between VideoDiT and ActionDiT allows the action branch to learn from these future-state predictions, while all beyond-RGB supervision branches are disabled at inference and deployment remains RGB-only. Across both no-rollout and joint video-action inference, DreamWAM consistently improves the matched RGB-only baselines on LIBERO, from 97.30\% to 98.40\% and from 98.00\% to 98.90\%, respectively. The gains become larger under unseen LIBERO-Plus perturbations, from 51.36\% to 63.44\% and from 69.16\% to 75.47\%. The same robustness extends to real-world manipulation, where DreamWAM attains an average success rate of 74.4\% across unseen changes in lighting, background, and object layout, compared with 55.6\% for Fast-WAM-Joint. These results show that robust world-action learning depends not only on predicting the future, but on representing it in a form that matters for action. The code and models are publicly released at https://github.com/hustvl/DreamWAM.

</details>


### [235] [Optimal Constrained sc-LTL Planning in MDPs via Switching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05021)
*Zetong Xuan,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）上，针对以共安全线性时序逻辑（sc-LTL）形式指定的目标和安全约束的规划问题中的最优策略综合。该方法将受约束的sc-LTL规划问题简化为扩展模型上的受约束可达性问题，并证明由针对各个sc-LTL规格的平稳策略构成的切换策略类足以实现最优性，从而可通过线性规划高效求解最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划问题中需要同时考虑sc-LTL形式的目标和安全约束，而这类问题本质上具有非马尔可夫性（由于sc-LTL规格的复杂性），且可能需要策略随机化来平衡目标与约束之间的权衡，因此需要一种系统且可计算的方法来求解最优策略。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的方法，将受约束的sc-LTL规划问题归约为扩展模型上的受约束可达性问题；然后证明由针对各个sc-LTL规格的平稳策略构建的一类切换策略足以保证受约束可达性问题的最优性，并据此建立可解的线性规划（LP）来求解最优策略。

Result: 通过网格世界案例研究验证了所提出的切换策略能够在目标与安全约束之间实现最优权衡，同时验证了该方法的最优性和可计算性（tractability）。

Conclusion: 切换策略类结合线性规划方法可以高效求解受约束的sc-LTL规划问题，实现目标与安全约束之间的最优权衡，兼顾了最优性与计算可行性。

Abstract: We study the synthesis of optimal policies for planning problems on Markov decision processes with both objectives and safety constraints specified in co-safe linear temporal logic (sc-LTL). Our problems are inherently non-Markovian due to the complexity of the sc-LTL specification and may require policy randomization to balance the objective and constraint. We propose a novel approach that reduces the constrained sc-LTL planning problem to a constrained reachability problem on an extended model. We then show that a class of switching policies constructed from stationary policies for the individual sc-LTL specifications is sufficient for optimality for the constrained reachability problem. Our finding enables a tractable linear program to compute the optimal policy. A grid world case study demonstrates that our switching policies can achieve the optimal trade-off between the objective and the safety constraint and validates both optimality and tractability.

</details>


### [236] [BridgeVLA++: A Data-Efficient, Generalizable, and Memory-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Framework for 3D Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05042)
*Peiyan Li,Yuze Zhu,Yixiang Chen,Qisen Ma,Yuan Xu,Jiabing Yang,He Guan,Yan Huang,Hongtao Wu,Xiao Ma,Tao Kong,Liang Wang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BridgeVLA++ 是在 BridgeVLA 基础上引入统一时空记忆架构的3D视觉-语言-动作模型，通过保留预训练VLM的输入输出对齐来提升数据效率和泛化能力，同时建模持续空间上下文和时序交互历史，在空间操控任务和记忆依赖操控基准上均达到先进性能，并验证了双臂操控和真实机器人平台的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D VLA方法存在数据需求高、分布偏移下泛化能力有限、缺乏对过去观测的显式记忆等问题，难以适用于数据稀缺、开放世界和记忆依赖的操控场景。需要一种同时具备数据高效、鲁棒泛化和记忆感知能力的统一框架。

Method: 在BridgeVLA的基础上，设计了一个统一的时空记忆架构（spatio-temporal memory architecture），包含持续空间上下文（persistent spatial context）和时序交互历史（temporal interaction history）两个维度的建模。保留BridgeVLA的核心设计——将原始点云投影为多视图图像、先预测中间热图再生成机器人动作，以维持预训练VLM的输入输出对齐。

Result: 在空间操控任务上表现强劲且泛化鲁棒；在两项具有挑战性的记忆依赖操控基准上取得最先进性能，同时不牺牲BridgeVLA原本的数据效率和泛化能力；在双臂操控设置下有效工作；在额外的真实机器人平台上验证了跨任务、跨环境和跨机器人平台的可扩展性。

Conclusion: BridgeVLA++ 被确立为一个统一的3D视觉-语言-动作框架，同时支持数据高效学习、鲁棒泛化和有效的记忆感知机器人操控，扩展了BridgeVLA的应用边界至记忆依赖和双臂操控等场景。

Abstract: Leveraging pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) to construct vision-language-action (VLA) models has emerged as a promising paradigm for 3D robot manipulation. However, existing 3D VLA methods remain data-hungry, exhibit limited generalization under distribution shifts, and lack explicit memory of past observations. These limitations hinder their application to data-scarce, open-world, and memory-dependent manipulation scenarios. Our previous work, BridgeVLA, improves data efficiency and generalization by preserving the input--output alignment of a pre-trained VLM during 3D action learning: raw point clouds are projected into multi-view images, and intermediate heatmaps are predicted before generating robot actions. In this work, we develop BridgeVLA++ by equipping BridgeVLA with a unified spatio-temporal memory architecture that models persistent spatial context and temporal interaction history. The resulting memory-augmented framework can reason over observation histories while preserving BridgeVLA's data efficiency and generalization capabilities. Extensive experiments show that our framework achieves strong performance on spatial manipulation tasks while exhibiting robust generalization. BridgeVLA++ further achieves state-of-the-art performance on two challenging memory-dependent manipulation benchmarks without sacrificing the data efficiency and generalization of the original BridgeVLA. In addition, BridgeVLA++ performs effectively in bimanual manipulation settings and is validated on an additional real-world robotic platform, demonstrating its scalability across tasks, environments, and robotic platforms. These results establish BridgeVLA++ as a unified 3D vision-language-action framework that simultaneously supports data-efficient learning, robust generalization, and effective memory-aware robot manipulation. Project website: https://bridgevla-plus.github.io/.

</details>


### [237] [SpikingNav: Robust Embodied Navigation with Spiking Neural Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05078)
*Jiahong Zhang,Sijun Shen,Dehua Wu,Yifan Lin,Xuechen Xia,Xu Chu,Youhui Zhang,GuoqiLi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikingNav，一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的具身导航框架，包含脉冲感知编码器（SSE）和脉冲策略网络（SPN），在PointNav和ObjectNav任务上实现了与ANN基线相当的干净性能、更强的视觉鲁棒性，同时参数更少、每步计算量更低，并在Thruster-V2神经形态芯片上完成了硬件验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ANN的具身导航模型虽然性能强劲，但依赖密集计算且容易在视觉损坏条件下性能退化。SNN具有事件驱动计算和内在时间动力学特性，有望实现紧凑且鲁棒的导航系统，但脉冲感知和策略动力学能否在视觉丰富的具身导航中提升鲁棒性仍是未解问题。

Method: SpikingNav包含两个核心组件：(1) Spiking Sensing Encoder (SSE)，使用基于脉冲的主干网络提取任务条件化的视觉特征；(2) Spiking Policy Network (SPN)，通过膜积分、阈值化和脉冲触发重置来维持循环策略状态。该框架充分利用SNN的动态特性和脉冲激活来提升导航性能和鲁棒性。

Result: 在PointNav和ObjectNav任务上（含干净观感和视觉损坏条件），SpikingNav取得了与匹配ANN基线相当的干净性能，但在鲁棒性上显著更强，且参数更少、每步计算量更低。具体而言，ObjectNav成功率从31.05%提升至34.12%，视觉损坏下的平均成功率从8.45%提升至13.71%。此外，在Thruster-V2神经形态芯片上完成了物理硬件验证。

Conclusion: 脉冲感知与策略动力学在具身导航中确实能带来鲁棒性收益，SpikingNav证明了基于SNN的导航框架可以在真实神经形态硬件上部署，为资源受限平台上的信息物理系统提供了一条可行的实现路径。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires an agent to make sequential decisions from egocentric observations in a physical environment. Existing Artificial Neural Network (ANN)-based navigation models have achieved strong performance, yet they often rely on dense computation and may degrade under visual corruptions. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) provide event-driven computation and intrinsic temporal dynamics, which are promising for compact and robust navigation on resource-constrained platforms. However, whether spike-based sensing and policy dynamics can improve robustness in visually rich embodied navigation remains an open problem. This paper proposes SpikingNav, a spiking framework for robust indoor embodied navigation. It contains a Spiking Sensing Encoder (SSE) and a Spiking Policy Network (SPN). The SSE extracts task-conditioned visual features with a spike-based backbone. The SPN maintains a recurrent policy state through membrane integration, thresholding, and spike-triggered reset. In this way, SpikingNav exploits the dynamic properties and spike activations of SNNs to improve navigation performance and robustness. We evaluate SpikingNav on PointNav and ObjectNav under clean observations and visual corruptions. SpikingNav achieves competitive clean performance and stronger robustness with fewer parameters and lower per-step computation than a matched ANN baseline. For instance, SpikingNav improves ObjectNav success from 31.05% to 34.12%, and raises the average success under visual corruptions from 8.45% to 13.71%, demonstrating the benefits of spike-based sensing and policy dynamics. We further validate the deployability of our spike-based sensing method on the Thruster-V2 neuromorphic chip. This physical hardware validation shows that SpikingNav can be instantiated on a real neuromorphic substrate for cyber-physical systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [A Long-Run Persistence Theory for AI Systems under the Redundancy-Adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04012)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于冗余调整的人工年龄评分（AAS）的长程持续性框架，形式化地证明了AI系统可以无限循环运行而不必遭受无界结构老化，并通过一系列渐近机制刻画了结构年龄的有界性、边际老化消失以及循环负担收敛为零的理论条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地需要在重复的交互、适应和更新循环中运行，而非一次性输出。这引出了一个根本性的理论问题：AI系统能否无限持续运行而不产生无界的结构老化？现有AAS只是一个静态评估指标，缺乏对循环级结构年龄的动态形式化分析。

Method: 将AAS从静态评估度量扩展为循环级泛函，生成跨重复操作的结构年龄序列。每个循环的结构年龄通过加权、冗余感知的对数惩罚函数定义，该函数基于组件一致性水平。在此框架下建立了渐近层次体系（负担持续性、零负担持续性、振荡持续性、累积终端负担）、比较排序、敏感性界、组件稳态化下的收敛性、有限总变差下的持续性、阻尼循环间扰动下的几何稳定化以及非退化冗余条件下的零负担刻画。

Result: 主要结果是：无限循环延续不需要无界结构老化——AI系统可以经过无限多个循环而结构年龄保持有界；在更强正则性条件下边际老化消失；在最强机制下循环级负担收敛为零。

Conclusion: 该框架为将长期人工持续性视为有界结构负担问题而非不可避免的累积恶化问题提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly expected to operate over repeated cycles of interaction, adaptation, and update rather than through isolated one-shot outputs. This raises a fundamental theoretical question: can an AI system persist indefinitely without incurring unbounded structural aging? This paper develops a long-run persistence framework for AI systems based on the redundancy-adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS). The model extends AAS from a static evaluative measure into a cycle-level functional that generates an age sequence across repeated operation. At each cycle, structural age is defined through a weighted, redundancy-aware logarithmic penalty over component consistency levels. Within this framework, cycle-level age is shown to be well defined and uniformly bounded, thereby excluding explosive pointwise aging. On this basis, the paper defines a hierarchy of asymptotic regimes, including burdened persistence, zero-burden persistence, oscillatory persistence, and cumulative terminal burden. It also establishes comparative ordering, sensitivity bounds, convergence under componentwise stabilization, persistence under finite total variation, geometric stabilization under damped inter-cycle perturbations, and a zero-burden characterization under nondegenerate redundancy conditions. The main result is that indefinite cyclic continuation does not require unbounded structural aging: an AI system may pass through infinitely many cycles while its structural age remains bounded, while under stronger regularity conditions its marginal aging vanishes and, in the strongest regime, its cycle-level burden converges to zero. The framework thus provides a formal basis for analyzing long-run artificial persistence as a problem of bounded structural burden rather than inevitable cumulative deterioration.

</details>


### [239] [The LLM Proposes, the Executive Disposes: A Self-Verifying Agent Instrument that Dissociates Commitment Drift from Binding Drift in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04066)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化验证长期智能体的实验仪器：确定性执行者独占信念，语言模型仅能提交类型化提案，预言在行动前预先注册并由代码匹配观察。该仪器通过自失效机制（前8次架构运行中4次被标记无效并定位真实缺陷）和不可见影子参考实现漂移度量的可测量定义。基于此仪器，报告了目标放弃行为从0.00翻转为1.00（去除承诺机制时）的干净单变量结果，同时任务效能为零（52次门控运行在ARC-AGI-3上零完成），后者作为结构性失败者预先注册。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证长期智能体面临根本性难题：智能体的自身状态和自我报告本身不可信。现有的后验（post-hoc）验证方式无法提供可靠保证，因此需要一种结构性而非追溯性的验证机制，使验证内置于架构而非事后进行。

Method: 构建一种代理仪器：(1) 确定性执行者（Executive）独占信念状态；(2) 语言模型只能提交类型化提案；(3) 声称只有在行动前预先注册的预测与代码观察匹配时才被接纳。仪器具备两种自验证属性：每次运行在写入错误、渲染尺寸或盐渍金丝雀回显底线被突破时自我失效；渲染不可见的影子参考在每个消融单元中编译完整系统会承诺的计划，使漂移度量在机制被移除时仍可定义。

Result: (1) 前8次架构运行中有4次被自动无效化，每次均定位到真实缺陷；(2) 去除承诺机制使目标放弃从0.00翻转为1.00，而绑定错误保持0.00（每单元三种子，每次运行最多394节拍，所有运行门控有效）；(3) 绑定通道在修复被消融时不会以逐节拍漂移的形式重新出现——因为绑定由代码拥有，故障类别被结构性吸收，其残余仅出现在上游假设形成坍缩中；(4) 任务效能为零：52次门控运行在ARC-AGI-3上零级完成。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于一种用于智能体开发的验证方法论及其可测量的漂移分解。虽然任务效能为空（预先注册的结构性失败者），但该仪器证明了其验证自身科学的能力——每次运行的自失效机制和影子参考系统使得对长期智能体故障的因果分析成为可能，即使机制被移除时也能定义漂移度量。

Abstract: How do you verify a long-horizon agent when its own state and self-reports are exactly what you cannot trust? We present an agent instrument built so that verification is structural rather than post-hoc. A deterministic Executive owns all belief; a language model may only file typed proposals, and a claim is admitted only when a prediction pre-registered before acting is matched against observation by code. Two properties make the instrument a verifier of its own science, not just of the agent: every run invalidates itself when per-organ write-error, render-size, or salted-canary-echo floors are breached (four of the first eight architecture runs were invalidated, each localizing a real defect); and a render-invisible shadow reference compiles the plan the full system would have committed in every ablation cell, so drift metrics are defined even where the mechanism under test has been removed. Using this instrument we report a clean, single-variable result on a failure every long-horizon agent suffers: ablating the commitment mechanism flips goal-abandonment from 0.00 to 1.00 while binding error stays flat at 0.00 (three seeds per cell, up to 394 reference beats per run, every run gated valid). The binding channel, by contrast, does not reappear as per-beat drift when its repair is ablated -- because binding is code-owned, the failure class is structurally absorbed, its only residue appearing one layer upstream as a collapse in hypothesis formation. We report these under full disclosure that task efficacy is null (zero level completions across 52 gated runs on ARC-AGI-3), pre-registered as a structural defeater. The contribution is a verification methodology for agent development and the drift decomposition it makes measurable.

</details>


### [240] [Monte Carlo Tree Search for Table-to-Multimodal Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04071)
*Teng Lin,Zhiyang Zhang,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCTS-Report，一个基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）驱动的框架，将多模态表格到报告的生成问题建模为结构化搜索空间上的渐进式构建过程，通过原子动作分解、动态推理和多样化奖励函数，实现文本分析与可视化图表的联合优化，并在自建基准MMRBench上取得77.9分的综合成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态报告生成方法依赖固定的线性流水线和孤立的子任务处理，导致事实准确性、视觉质量和叙事连贯性无法协同优化，难以生成专业级的多模态报告。

Method: 将报告生成分解为章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织和叙事优化等原子动作；利用LLM在MCTS过程中生成逐步推理与动作，将推理轨迹存储于节点中以实现上下文感知的连贯构建；设计多维奖励函数联合评估SQL数值一致性、图表质量、图文对齐和结构完整性，并加入多样性惩罚和前置条件检查以剪枝无效动作。

Result: 在自建基准MMRBench（涵盖六个领域的真实表格，配以专家精炼的参考报告结构和可验证的关键洞察）上，MCTS-Report在结构完整性、数值准确性、图文对齐和洞察新颖性等指标上显著优于强基线，综合得分为77.9。

Conclusion: MCTS-Report通过将报告生成转化为基于搜索的渐进式构建过程，有效解决了现有方法中线性流水线和子任务隔离导致的联合优化不足问题，为多模态数据报告自动化生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Automatically generating professional multimodal reports comprising both textual analysis and visual charts from structured tabular data is a critical challenge in data intelligence. Existing methods suffer from fixed linear pipelines and isolated subtask processing, which hinder joint optimization of factual accuracy, visual quality, and narrative coherence. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCTS-Report, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-driven framework that formulates multimodal table-to-report generation as a progressive construction process over a structured search space. The core idea is to decompose report generation into atomic actions, including chapter planning, visualization task identification, chart generation, insight organization, and narrative refinement, each executed by an LLM based on dynamic reasoning conditioned on the current report state. We use an LLM to generate step-by-step reasoning and actions during MCTS, storing the reasoning trajectory in each node for context-aware, coherent report construction. To guide the search, we design a multi-dimensional reward function that jointly evaluates numerical fact consistency (via SQL), chart quality, chart-text alignment, and structural completeness, while incorporating a diversity penalty to suppress repeated charts and a precondition check to prune invalid actions. We also construct MMRBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising real-world tables from six domains, paired with expert-refined reference report structures and verifiable key insights. Experiments on MMRBench demonstrate that MCTS-Report significantly outperforms strong baselines across structural completeness, numerical accuracy, chart-text alignment, and insight novelty, achieving a 77.9 overall score.

</details>


### [241] [FinProBench: Evaluating Financial AI Agents with Role-Grounded Rubrics Derived from Professional Deliverables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04077)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinProBench基准和Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC)管线，用于评估金融AI代理的专业能力。RGRC通过从业者交付物提取隐性评价标准，构建角色级rubric。在57个职业、1,723个交付物上验证，RGRC在角色专业化任务上显著优于纯提示工程方法（99.1% vs 78.0%），且角色级rubric复用使任务构建成本降低6.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融AI代理评估采用的rubric方法通常从任务提示或模型输出中推导标准，忽视了仅存在于从业者交付物中的隐性专业标准（tacit standards）。需要一种能从真实从业者实践中提取评价标准的方法，以更真实地评估金融AI代理的专业能力。

Method: 提出RGRC四阶段管线：交付物收集（Deliverable Collection）、胜任力提取（Competency Extraction）、Rubric综合（Rubric Synthesis）和验证（Validation）。将57个职业按交付物类型分类为30个先验丰富的常规角色和27个先验稀疏的角色专业化角色。构建FinProBench，包含1,723个精选交付物，覆盖57个职业、8个金融子行业、161种交付物类型，并发布20个完整任务的初始评估集。

Result: 在常规角色上，Prompt-only方法与RGRC几乎持平（89.2% vs 90.7%）；在角色专业化角色上，RGRC显著优于Prompt-only（99.1% vs 78.0%）。在异构LLM评判者和角色级rubric下，人工交付物平均排名第一（73.7分），四个AI系统得分在69.6-70.3之间且95%置信区间重叠。角色级rubric复用将每任务构建成本降低6.7倍。

Conclusion: 当任务约定已充分编码在模型先验中时，纯提示工程可近似rubric的性能；但当标准超出模型先验时，基于从业者交付物的专业grounding至关重要。角色级rubric具有跨任务迁移性和成本效益，为金融AI代理评估提供了更符合真实专业实践的范式。

Abstract: Evaluating financial AI agents requires criteria aligned with real professional work. Existing rubric methods typically derive criteria from task prompts or model outputs, overlooking tacit standards visible only in practitioner deliverables. We introduce FinProBench, a benchmark for professional financial tasks, and Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC), a reusable pipeline that derives rubrics from deliverables produced by practitioners in the same role. RGRC comprises four stages: Deliverable Collection, Competency Extraction, Rubric Synthesis, and Validation. Its rubrics capture tacit standards, distinguish quality levels, and transfer across tasks within a role. Before analysis, we classified 57 occupations by deliverable genre into 30 prior-rich conventional roles and 27 prior-sparse role-specialized roles. Across all roles, Prompt-only nearly matches RGRC for conventional roles (89.2% vs. 90.7%), but RGRC substantially outperforms it for role-specialized roles (99.1% vs. 78.0%). This split indicates that prompt engineering can approximate rubrics when conventions are well represented in model priors, while professional grounding is essential for standards beyond those priors. FinProBench is built from 1,723 curated deliverables spanning 57 occupations, 8 financial sub-industries, and 161 deliverable types, and releases an initial evaluation set of 20 complete tasks covering 20 roles in 7 sub-industries. With heterogeneous LLM judges and role-level rubrics, human deliverables rank first on average (73.7 vs. 70.3, 70.2, and 69.6 out of 100), while all four systems show overlapping 95% confidence intervals and complementary strengths. Reusing rubrics at the role level reduces estimated per-task construction effort by 6.7 times relative to authoring each rubric from scratch.

</details>


### [242] [FinPerMA: A Theory-Informed, Event-Grounded Personalized-Memory Benchmark for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04095)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinPerMA，一个基于事件驱动的个性化记忆评估基准，用于测试LLM智能体在长期纵向投资者轨迹中能否保持并更新个性化用户模型。在2,994个问题、276个角色上，七个前沿LLM和最多七种记忆配置均远未饱和，全上下文配置准确率不超过约0.47，多项选择题约39%。分析表明，摘要式记忆常保留事实细节却丢失个性化偏好信号，简单检索可胜过专用记忆系统，且差距在事件冲击后扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正被用作金融建议等高危领域的个性化助手，但现有个性化记忆基准主要测试事实保留或依赖约束较弱的模型生成轨迹，缺乏对事件驱动的偏好自适应能力的评估。需要一个新的基准来系统检验智能体在长期时间跨度上能否整合重大事件并更新个性化用户模型。

Method: 提出FinPerMA基准：1）生成流水线结合确定性、理论驱动的冲击规则，受控LLM叙述和自动化质量筛选；2）基于冻结的纵向投资者轨迹评估个性化记忆；3）设置Post-Shock检查点，用于隔离判断智能体是否将重大事件整合进其持久用户模型中；4）以2,994个问题覆盖276个角色，测试七个前沿LLM和最多七种记忆配置。

Result: 所有测试配置均未饱和：任意全上下文配置的总体准确率不超过约0.47，多项选择题不超过约39%。归因分析发现摘要式记忆在保留事实细节的同时丢失了实现个性化所需的偏好信号；简单检索方法的性能可超越专用记忆系统，且该差距在事件冲击后进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在事件驱动的个性化记忆方面仍远未达到理想水平，尤其是基于摘要的记忆方法在偏好信号保持上存在显著缺陷。简单的检索方法反而优于复杂记忆系统，且事件冲击场景是区分记忆系统能力的关键测试点，这一发现为后续个性化记忆系统设计提供了明确改进方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used as personalized assistants in high-stakes domains such as financial advising, yet it remains unclear whether they can maintain and update an individualized user model over long horizons. Existing personalized-memory benchmarks primarily test factual retention or rely on weakly constrained model-generated trajectories, leaving event-driven preference adaptation underexplored. We introduce FinPerMA, an event-grounded benchmark that evaluates personalized memory against frozen longitudinal investor trajectories. Its generation pipeline combines deterministic, theory-informed impact rules, controlled LLM narration, and automated quality screening; a Post-Shock checkpoint isolates whether an agent has integrated a material event into its persistent user model. On 2,994 questions from 276 personas, seven frontier LLMs and up to seven memory configurations remain far from saturated: no full-context configuration exceeds approximately 0.47 overall accuracy or approximately 39% on multiple-choice questions. Attribution analysis shows that summary-based memory often preserves factual details while losing the preference signals needed for personalization; simple retrieval can therefore outperform purpose-built memory systems, with the gap widening after shocks.

</details>


### [243] [BrainBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Comprehensive EEG Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04156)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Yuning Chen,Chen Wu,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Benchmarkname的统一基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在指令条件下的全面脑电图（EEG）理解能力。该基准包含四个子集、17个数据集、Case个任务和Instance个真实数据实例，通过数值、分类、集合、序列、语义和伪迹六种验证方式评估输出，并比较了多种LLM在CodeAct和BrainAgent两种执行范式下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG分析评估主要集中在孤立的解码任务或系统特定演示上，缺乏对LLM在综合EEG理解能力（即从自然语言指令、信号处理、定量证据到科学解释的完整工作流）上的统一量化评估。作者认为需要建立一个可复现的基准来系统衡量不同LLM在此类综合任务上的表现。

Method: 构建一个统一的指令条件EEG理解基准，包含四个子集：基础分析（Foundational Analysis）、睡眠评估（Sleep Assessment）、神经认知评估（Neurocognitive Assessment）和生理整合（Physiological Integration）。涵盖17个数据集和Case个任务。评估采用数值、分类、集合、序列、语义和伪迹六种验证方式。在两种执行范式下评估：自主代码执行的CodeAct和结构化智能体分析的BrainAgent，共进行超过10万次执行。

Result: 不同模型在子集、难度和执行范式上的表现存在显著差异，表明EEG能力依赖于模型本身及其操作化方式。该基准提供了一个可复现的测试平台，代码和基准将很快发布，评估结果持续更新。

Conclusion: Benchmarkname是一个可复现的统一基准，能够系统量化LLM在综合EEG理解上的能力，揭示了模型选择与其操作化方式（执行范式）共同影响EEG分析性能的核心结论，填补了该领域缺乏大规模统一评估的空白。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis extends beyond assigning predefined labels to recordings; it requires workflows connecting natural-language instructions, signal processing, quantitative evidence, and scientific interpretation. We term this capability \emph{comprehensive EEG understanding}. Existing evaluations, however, primarily target isolated decoding tasks or system-specific demonstrations, leaving the competence of large language models (LLMs) insufficiently quantified. We introduce \benchmarkname{}, a unified benchmark for comprehensive, instruction-conditioned EEG understanding. It comprises four subsets---Foundational Analysis, Sleep Assessment, Neurocognitive Assessment, and Physiological Integration---covering 17 datasets, \numcases{} tasks, and over \numinstances{} real-data instances. Given an instruction and EEG recordings with optional physiological signals, a system must perform the analysis and produce a scientifically grounded report and, when required, artifacts. Outputs are assessed through numerical, categorical, set, sequence, semantic, and artifact validation. We evaluate \nummodels{} representative LLMs across more than 100K executions under two paradigms: autonomous code execution with CodeAct and structured agentic analysis with BrainAgent. Results vary substantially across models, subsets, difficulty levels, and execution paradigms, showing that EEG competence depends on the model and its operationalization. \benchmarkname{} provides a reproducible testbed for advancing LLM-based EEG understanding. The code and benchmark will be released soon, with evaluation results continuously updated.

</details>


### [244] [Adversarially Robust Abductive Fusion of Pre-trained Transformer-based Perception Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04190)
*Mario Leiva,Yue Ma,Qinru Qiu,Gerardo Simari,Paulo Shakarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需领域知识的元认知层（LVP），通过利用向量空间几何（每个模型训练嵌入的标签向量池）为多个预训练ViT检测器构建错误检测规则，并将多检测器融合建模为基于一致性的溯因问题，通过整数规划或多项式启发式求解。在15个天气偏移的航拍测试集上，该方法在干净数据上与多数投票持平，且在协同标签翻转攻击下显著优于所有多数投票基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练感知模型在分布偏移下精度下降，简单的模型组合（如多数投票）无法恢复精度，且会在高召回与低精度之间权衡，并对协同失败十分脆弱。先前的元认知方法依赖手工编写的领域知识线索（如物体尺寸先验、分割掩码），这些线索无法迁移到真正新颖的场景。因此，需要在无领域知识的情况下学习元认知层。

Method: 1) 构建每个模型自身的Label Vector Pool (LVP)，基于训练嵌入生成错误检测规则；2) 利用检测结果相对于训练确定原型的几何结构进行元认知推理（神经符号框架）；3) 将多个ViT检测器的融合建模为一致性溯因问题，在测试时通过精确整数规划（IP）或多项式时间启发式求解；4) 几何规则与领域知识规则共享同一逻辑框架，可在有领域知识时互补。

Result: 在15个天气偏移测试集和6个ViT检测器上：无领域知识层与最强多数投票在干净数据上F1差异在0.005以内；与领域知识规则F1差异在0.002以内；在90%协同标签翻转攻击下平均F1为0.42，相比MV-Plurality的0.35提升了22%；翻转率超过0.4时在每个测试集上均取得最高F1。

Conclusion: 元认知层可以在完全没有领域知识的情况下通过向量空间几何学习得到，达到与领域知识规则相当的精度；由于保持神经符号架构，该方法与领域知识规则兼容互补；在协同攻击下展现了较强的鲁棒性，显著优于多数投票类基线。

Abstract: Deploying pre-trained perception models in novel environments degrades their accuracy under distributional shift, and assembling them alone does not recover it: combiners such as majority voting trade recall for precision and are brittle to coordinated failures. Prior metacognitive methods learn logical rules that flag a model's errors, but rely on hand-authored domain-knowledge cues (object-size priors, segmentation masks) that do not transfer to genuinely novel scenes. We show that this metacognitive layer can be learned without any domain knowledge by exploiting vector-space geometry: per-model Label Vector Pools (LVP), built from each model's own training embeddings, yield error-detection rules from the geometry of detections relative to training-determined prototypes, reaching parity with domain-knowledge rules to within $0.002$ every F1 on test set. Because the approach remains neurosymbolic, these geometric rules share a single logical framework and can still be complemented by domain knowledge when available. We frame the fusion of multiple imperfect ViT-based detectors as a consistency-based abduction problem solved at test time by an exact Integer Program (IP) and a polynomial-time heuristic. On an aerial-imagery benchmark of 15 weather-shifted test sets and six ViT detectors, our domain-knowledge-free layer matches the strongest majority-vote variant on clean data (within $0.005$ F1) and, unlike every majority-vote baseline, retains its performance under a coordinated label-flipping attack: at a $90\%$ flip rate it averages $0.42$ F1 versus $0.35$ for MV-Plurality (a $22\%$ relative gain) and attains the highest F1 on \emph{every} test set once the flip rate exceeds $0.4$

</details>


### [245] [MatrAIx: Simulating the World with 8.3 Billion Persona Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04205)
*Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Jianheng Hou,Jintao Huang,Qianfeng Wen,Shirley Huang,Yifan Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yilan Fan,Yijun Wang,Koutian Wu,Ruoqi Gao,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Jing Tang,Brihi Joshi,Heming Liu,Zheyuan Deng,Zonglin Di,Sankalp Jajee,Jiuyao Lu,Zhiwei Zhang,Saksham Kapoor,Ishan Gupta,Yunhan Zhao,Chanwoo Park,Yucheng Lu,Bing Hu,Weihang Xiao,Aravind Mohan,Hanwen Xing,Runyu Zhang,Mihir Kulshreshtha,Yuanda Xu,Qianyu Zhu,Dianzhuo Wang,Yuxin Xiao,Bowen Jiang,Yongye Su,Wenhao Chai,Zuxin Liu,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Ethan Ye,Shivam Patel,Jason Xie,Alex Martin Richmond,Weixiang Ding,Emre Okcular,Diya Mathew,Ziheng Wang,Rana M. Shahroz Khan,Zhejian Peng,Fang Wu,Fan Nie,Xinyang Han,Yubin Kim,Jiawei Zhang,Zhenting Qi,Huangyuan Su,Xu Pan,Abinitha Gourabathina,Hyewon Jeong,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Kumail Alhamoud,Kimia Hamidieh,Zidi Xiong,Samuel Schmidgall,Pengrui Han,Yepeng Huang,Yongheng Wang,Bowen Yang,Alex Gu,Yuchu Wang,Akshay Paruchuri,Brenna Li,Hejie Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Chaosheng Dong,Jiahao Wang,Yixuan He,Chi Wang,Pamela Bhattacharya,Tianyi Peng,Paul Pu Liang,Mitchell Gordon,Yilun Du,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Prasanna Tambe,Philip Torr,Emily Fox,Asu Ozdaglar,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MatrAIx是一个大规模模拟用户评估基础设施，包含8.3亿条人设记录（Persona 8B）、四个交互环境（调查/聊天机器人/网页/App）、1,010个跨25+领域的应用任务，并通过18,189次评估试验验证了人设遵循性和提取质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类评估AI系统和数字产品成本高、速度慢且难以规模化；离线评估虽可扩展但往往忽略了用户多样性和交互行为，需要在两者之间架起桥梁。

Method: 构建三大核心组件：(1) Persona 8B——由82.9亿条记录组成，覆盖1,290个分类维度，通过依赖图采样或人类撰写生成，并发布约100万条质量过滤核集；(2) MatrAIx Playground——提供Survey、AI Chatbot、Web、App四种评估环境；(3) 1,010个跨25+领域的应用任务。使用三个LLM（Claude Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5、Claude Haiku 4.5）驱动人设智能体，开展18,189次评估试验。

Result: 人设反馈能够捕捉不同背景下的决策和偏好差异（如价格上涨后的犹豫、AI助手失败后的继续意愿、延迟容忍度）。两项验证研究：400次受控试验中人设遵循率达91.5%（十项行为属性×四种环境）；人设提取质量经人类与LLM评委双重验证。

Conclusion: MatrAIx为以多样化模拟用户评估AI系统和数字产品提供了端到端的基础设施，兼具规模化与用户异质性。

Abstract: Human evaluation of AI systems and digital products is costly, slow, and difficult to scale. Offline evaluations are more scalable but often abstract away human diversity and interactive behavior. We therefore introduce MatrAIx, a population-scale simulated-user evaluation infrastructure for testing AI systems and digital products with heterogeneous users. MatrAIx has three core components: First, Persona 8B contains 8.3 billion persona records represented by 1,290 categorical dimensions. Records are either sampled from a dependency graph that preserves correlated attributes or derived from human-authored profiles. We release a quality-filtered coreset of approximately 1 million personas, comprising 599,847 human-grounded and 400,000 synthetic records. Second, the MatrAIx Playground provides four environments in which diverse users evaluate and interact with digital products: Survey, AI Chatbot, Web, and App. Third, MatrAIx provides 1,010 application tasks spanning more than 25 domains, including Commerce, Software, Finance, and Healthcare. We conducted 18,189 evaluation trials across eight representative tasks. Persona agents were powered by three LLMs: Claude Opus 4.8, GPT 5.5, and Claude Haiku 4.5. The resulting feedback captures how decisions and preferences vary across persona backgrounds, including hesitation after a price increase, willingness to continue after an AI assistant fails, and latency tolerance. We conducted two main validation studies: First, a 400-trial controlled study evaluated persona adherence across ten behavioral attributes and all four environments. The declared behavior was expressed or correctly suppressed in 366 trials (91.5%). Second, human and LLM judges evaluated the extraction quality of human-grounded personas. Overall, MatrAIx provides an end-to-end infrastructure for evaluating AI systems and digital products with diverse simulated human users.

</details>


### [246] [Interoceptive Attention as Dynamic Homeostatic Prioritization in a Foraging Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04232)
*St John Grimbly,Nicolas Kuske,Evert A. Boonstra,Bruce A. Bassett,Charel van Hoof,Rowan Hodson,Benjamin Rosman,Ryan Smith,Mark Solms,Jonathan P. Shock*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究固定预算下生物系统如何将感知精度分配到最需要的通道。在基于主动推理的四通道觅食智能体（AffectWorld）中，将内感受精度预算动态重分配给最需要的通道，可将学习阶段存活率提高一倍以上（0.414 vs 0.199），且该收益同时通过感知和规划两条通路产生，并且与需要是"对齐"的——瞄准最不需要的通道反而比均匀分配更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统必须在有限感知带宽下调节相互竞争的需求，增加某一估计的精度会牺牲其他估计的精度。因此任何固定预算系统都必须决定将感知精度分配到哪里。作者希望在主动推理框架下研究这一"精度分配"问题及其对生存和行为的影响。

Method: 构建基于主动推理的觅食智能体，需维持多个身体需求才能生存。智能体每一步读取自身身体状态信念，识别最紧迫的需求通道，并将固定预算的内感受精度重新分配给该通道，使相同精度形状的似然同时用于信念更新与规划。在四通道觅食网格世界AffectWorld中，与均匀精度分配基线及若干消融（仅感知、指向最不需要通道等）进行对照实验。

Result: 选择性精度分配使学习阶段存活率相对均匀精度基线提升超一倍（0.414 vs 0.199，11种布局、各32个随机种子，配对联立bootstrap检验p≤1e-4）。仅对规划器禁用精度成形似然会消除约一半收益；将精度指向最不需要的通道效果比均匀分配更差。被关注的通道学习动态的速度约为均匀分配的两倍，即使匹配观测次数后仍保持领先，这是精度路由在行为上的痕迹（体现在学习速度而非存活率）。

Conclusion: 主动精度分配（而非均匀分配）能在固定预算下显著提升多需求觅食智能体的生存表现，其机制同时涉及感知与规划双重通路，且分配必须与需要的紧急程度保持一致。这一"精度路由"不仅影响存活，还在行为上留下可观测的学习速度差异痕迹。

Abstract: Biological systems must regulate competing needs under limited perceptual bandwidth, where sharpening one estimate costs the capacity to sharpen the others. Any fixed-budget system therefore has to decide where to allocate its perceptual precision. We study this in a foraging agent that must keep several bodily needs satisfied to survive, modelled with active inference. At each step it reads its own body-state beliefs, identifies the most-needed channel, and reallocates a fixed budget of interoceptive precision toward it, so that the same precision-shaped likelihood feeds both belief update and planning. In AffectWorld, a four-channel foraging gridworld, this selective allocation more than doubles learning-phase survival at matched budget against a uniform-precision agent ($0.414$ vs $0.199$ across 11 layouts, $n{=}32$ seeds each, paired cluster-bootstrap $p \leq 10^{-4}$). Two further results sharpen the mechanism. The benefit runs through planning as well as perception, since denying the shaped likelihood to the planner alone removes about half of it. It is also need-aligned, since aiming precision at the least-needed channel does worse than spreading it evenly. The attended channel additionally learns its own dynamics about twice as fast, and stays ahead even at matched observation count, a behavioural trace of the same precision routing, visible in learning speed, not survival.

</details>


### [247] [The RAIL Principles for Neurosymbolic AI: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04285)
*Agnese Chiatti,Michael Cochez,Cristina Cornelio,Sebastijan Dumancic,Artur d'Avila Garcez,Luis C. Lamb,Lia Morra,Mathias Niepert,Robert Peharz,Alberto Speranzon,Maarten Stol,Annette Ten Teije,Thiviyan Thanapalasingam,Frank Van Harmelen,Emile Van Krieken,Antonio Vergari,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号AI设计的四大原则（RAIL：推理、保证、接口和学习），主张神经符号方法并非小众方向，而是众多成功AI系统的核心特征，该框架可统一分析物理感知机器学习、Alpha系列、因果学习和工具增强大语言模型等看似不同的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号AI将机器学习与符号推理相结合，在高风险领域和低数据场景中至关重要。作者认为这一方向常被低估为"小众方法"，需要一种设计原则框架来重新审视当前AI系统的设计，以帮助工程人员做出更明智的设计决策来构建可靠、高效、可信任的系统。

Method: 提出RAIL框架（Reasoning推理、Assurances保证、Interfacing接口、Learning学习）四个设计原则，并以此为分析透镜，对多种主流AI系统（包括传统上不被视为神经符号的系统）进行统一视角的剖析，涵盖物理感知机器学习、神经引导搜索（如Alpha系列）、因果学习和工具增强的大语言模型等领域。

Result: 展示了RAIL框架能够在统一的视角下理解看似异构的AI系统，证明许多主流系统（包括大语言模型的工具增强等）本质上都体现了神经符号设计原则；该框架可为生产级AI系统的设计和部署提供更加有依据、有原则性的指导。

Conclusion: 神经符号AI不是边缘方法，而是现代可靠、可信任AI系统设计的核心范式。RAIL原则为工程师和研究者提供了系统化思考框架，有望引导下一代AI技术更好地整合机器学习和形式化推理。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic AI systems that integrate machine learning and symbolic reasoning are rapidly gaining attention. They complement the data-intensive statistical approaches of neural networks and language models with symbolic reasoning algorithms to function in high-stakes domains or in low-data regimes that characterize many real-world applications. We argue that the neurosymbolic combination of machine learning and formal reasoning is not a niche approach within AI, but rather includes many already successful techniques that are of crucial importance to the development of reliable, efficient and, ultimately, trustworthy systems. This perspective prompts a re-examination of the design of current AI systems. We show that many leading AI systems, including some that are not traditionally considered as neurosymbolic, can be analysed from the perspective of four principles of neurosymbolic AI design: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning (RAIL). Applying the RAIL framework offers a unified view of seemingly disparate AI systems, ranging from physics-aware machine learning to neuro-guided search (such as Google DeepMind's Alpha-* suite), causal learning and tool-augmented Large Language Models. Importantly, the RAIL principles will enable engineers to make better-informed and more principled decisions about the design and deployment of production-level AI systems. In this article, we introduce the RAIL principles, examine how they can be applied across major areas of AI, and illustrate how they may guide practitioners to integrate neurosymbolic methods into next-generation AI technologies.

</details>


### [248] [SafeCommit: Certifying When Memory-Grounded Agents May Safely Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04289)
*Mayur Akewar,Ravi Ranjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeCommit，一个位于代理推理与外部执行之间的风险受控层，通过构建校准的潜在世界集合并对行动进行一致性认证，解决长时程代理在记忆不确定性下的过早承诺（premature commitment）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程代理依赖持久记忆和工具执行具有外部副作用的行动，核心失败模式是过早承诺——在记忆基础（grounding）可能过时、冲突、不完整或损坏时仍贸然行动。现有方法缺乏对行动安全性的形式化保障。

Method: SafeCommit构建一个校准的潜在世界集合（来自记忆、观测、工具输出、来源与策略约束），仅当一致性行动证书（conformal action certificate）证明行动在每个保留世界中安全时才允许执行；否则选择低副作用探针（probe）针对阻塞认证的世界，或返回保守回退方案。在校准的世界覆盖率下，不安全认证提交的概率上限为α；通过分离校准误差与表示误差来处理不完美世界提议。

Result: 提供一个无依赖的受控模拟器，展示安全性与效用之间的权衡，并可用单条命令复现所有报告结果。

Conclusion: 本文的目标是为代理提供一种具体方法，不仅决定"应该做什么"，还决定"现有证据何时足以安全地去做"。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly use persistent memory and tools to take actions with external side effects. A central failure mode is premature commitment: an agent acts before resolving whether its memory grounding is stale, conflicting, incomplete, or corrupted. We formalize this problem as safe commitment under memory uncertainty and introduce SafeCommit, a risk controlled layer between agent reasoning and external execution. The layer constructs a calibrated set of plausible latent worlds from memory, observations, tool outputs, provenance, and policy constraints. It permits a side effectful action only when a conformal action certificate shows that the action is safe in every retained world. Otherwise, it selects a low-side-effect probe that targets the worlds blocking certification, or returns a conservative fallback. Under calibrated world coverage, the probability of an unsafe certified commit is at most the target level α; with imperfect world proposal, the bound separates calibration and representation error. A dependency-free controlled simulator illustrates the safety-utility tradeoff and reproduces all reported results with one command. The goal is to offer a concrete approach for deciding not only what an agent should do, but when the available evidence is sufficient to safely do it.

</details>


### [249] [NeuMoSync: End-to-End Neuromodulatory Control for Plasticity and Adaptability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04358)
*Seyed Roozbeh Razavi Rohani,Khashayar Khajavi,Wesley Chung,Mandana Samiei,Mo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuMoSync架构，借鉴大脑全局神经调节机制，将动态、神经元特定的调制信号集成到深度神经网络中，以增强其在持续学习中的适应性和可塑性，在多种CL基准上表现出优异的保持可塑性和前后向迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习要求模型顺序学习多个任务，但深度神经网络常面临可塑性丧失和知识迁移不佳的问题，这限制了模型的长期适应性。现有方法未能充分解决模型在持续学习过程中逐渐丧失学习新任务能力的问题。

Method: NeuMoSync扩展标准神经网络架构，包含两个核心组件：(1) 为每个神经元配备可学习的特征向量，跟踪网络范围的历史上下文；(2) 在更高抽象层面运行一个模块，基于当前输入和网络动态状态合成神经元特定信号，自适应地调节激活动态和突触可塑性。该方法受到大脑全局神经调节机制的启发。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准上评估NeuMoSync，包括记忆任务（Random Label CIFAR-10和MNIST）、概念漂移（Shuffle CIFAR-10和Shuffle Mini-ImageNet）、类增量学习（Class Split ImageNet和CIFAR-100）以及域增量学习（Permuted MNIST），该方法在保持可塑性方面表现优异，并与现有方法相比在前向和后向适应方面均取得改进。消融研究验证了各组件必要性，对学习到的调节信号的分析揭示了跨任务的可解释协调模式。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将全局协调机制集成到深度学习系统中的潜力，为推进稳健、自适应的持续学习提供了新方向。代码已公开。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) requires models to learn tasks sequentially, yet deep neural networks often suffer from plasticity loss and poor knowledge transfer, which can impede their long-term adaptability. Drawing high-level inspiration from global neuromodulatory mechanisms in the brain, we introduce Neuromodulation and Synchronization (NeuMoSync), a novel architecture that integrates dynamic, neuron-specific modulation into deep neural networks to enhance their adaptability and plasticity. NeuMoSync extends standard neural network architectures with learnable feature vectors for each neuron that track network-wide historical context and with a module operating at a higher level of abstraction. This module synthesizes neuron-specific signals, conditioned on both current inputs and the network's evolving state, to adaptively regulate activation dynamics and synaptic plasticity. Evaluated on diverse CL benchmarks, including memorization (Random Label CIFAR-10 and Random Label MNIST), concept drift (Shuffle CIFAR-10 and Shuffle Mini-ImageNet), class-incremental learning (Class Split ImageNet and Class Split CIFAR-100), and domain-incremental learning (Permuted MNIST), NeuMoSync demonstrates strong performance in retaining plasticity and achieves improvements in both forward and backward adaptation compared with existing methods. Ablation studies validate the necessity of each component, while analysis of the learned modulatory signals reveals interpretable coordination patterns across tasks. Our work underscores the potential of integrating global coordination mechanisms into deep learning systems to advance robust, adaptive continual learning. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/RoozbehRazavi/NeuMoSync.

</details>


### [250] [Improving Auto-Design of Neural PDE Solvers with a Domain-Specific Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04384)
*Shengxin Kong,Liwen Xu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADSL-PDE提出了一种用于神经PDE求解器自动设计的结构化搜索空间表示方法，将设计决策与低层实现细节解耦，通过确定性编译器将搜索状态映射为可执行代码，使进化代理能在有意义的决策空间中进行搜索而非花费精力于无效程序，在多个PDE基准上首个十次迭代内提升超过52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无约束的Python代码空间中，有效的神经PDE求解器只占极其稀疏的子集——大多数候选程序要么语法错误、要么语义不兼容、要么数值不稳定。直接进行代码生成迫使LLM将大部分搜索能力浪费在处理实现失败上，而非专注于求解器质量的推理，因此需要一种重新塑造搜索空间的方法。

Method: 提出一种介于求解器概念与可执行代码之间的结构化搜索状态表示（ADSL-PDE），涵盖架构、物理约束、目标函数、采样和优化等关键设计决策，同时抽象掉低层实现细节；通过确定性编译器将有效的搜索状态映射为可执行求解器，从而去除无效程序区域、提高有效候选密度，并在此基础上运行进化代理进行迭代式搜索与优化。

Result: 在多个PDE基准测试上，ADSL-PDE同时提升了搜索效率和优化稳定性，在前十次进化迭代中实现了超过52%的性能改进。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动设计系统有效的关键不仅在于更强的推理能力，更在于设计一种能将探索集中到有效且关键决策上的搜索表示，这一原则具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Neural PDE solver auto-design is fundamentally a search-space representation problem. In the space of unrestricted Python programs, valid solvers form an extremely sparse subset: most candidate programs are syntactically incorrect, semantically incompatible, or numerically unstable. Direct code generation therefore forces an LLM to spend most of its search capacity navigating implementation failures rather than reasoning about solver quality. ADSL-PDE addresses this challenge by introducing a structured search state between solver concepts and executable code. It represents the functional decisions that determine a neural PDE solver (architecture, physical constraints, objectives, sampling, and optimization) while abstracting away low-level implementation details. A deterministic compiler maps each valid search state to an executable solver. In effect, ADSL-PDE reshapes the search space: it removes large regions of invalid programs, increases the density of meaningful candidates, and preserves the compositional freedom needed to discover previously unseen designs. Solver evolution can thus operate over design decisions rather than code artifacts. Built on this representation, our evolutionary agent iteratively proposes, evaluates, and refines solver search states using empirical feedback. Across multiple PDE benchmarks, ADSL-PDE improves both search efficiency and optimization stability, achieving an improvement of more than 52% within the first ten evolution iterations. These results suggest a broader principle for LLM-driven auto-design: effective agents do not merely require stronger reasoning, but rather a search representation that concentrates exploration on valid and consequential decisions.

</details>


### [251] [Architectural Implications of Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04458)
*Jirong Yang,Peizhe Liu,Chaojie Zhang,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对数据中心中的Agentic AI工作负载进行了架构层面的系统特性分析，基于微软Azure生产环境研究和开源框架受控实验，揭示了代理工作流在CPU-GPU边界反复跨越的碎片化、异构化执行特征，并提出了针对性的服务器架构原型Agora。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI正快速进入数据中心，但其架构影响尚未被探索。理解代理工作负载的执行特征和资源需求模式，是设计高效数据中心服务器架构的前提，而当前对这一新兴负载的架构级认知几乎为空白。

Method: 通过两个层次的实证研究：(1) 在微软Azure上的生产环境研究；(2) 开源框架的受控研究。提出代理工作流分类法（taxonomy），分析请求如何扩展为LLM推理、工具调用和编排决策组成的工作流；基于此揭示CPU-GPU边界交叉、负载突发性、GPU利用率分布等特性；进而设计Agora原型系统，通过动态回收空闲CPU核心、GPU内存超订阅与状态预取、按角色分池与亲和性调度、自动参数调优等机制来适配代理工作负载。

Result: Agora在保护代理尾部延迟的前提下，提升了服务器利用率和吞吐量。研究发现代理执行呈现碎片化和异构性：CPU处于关键路径上，负载呈低均值高突峰形态，模型组合决定GPU均衡度，任务和工具多样性进一步扩大资源需求范围。传统统一服务器架构在CPU-GPU容量孤立、同质CPU配置低效、多代理共享核心导致微架构局部性退化等方面存在架构不匹配。

Conclusion: Agentic AI工作负载对传统数据中心服务器架构提出了根本性挑战，碎片化执行、角色异质性和突发负载要求服务器架构从统一形态转向为代理工作流专门设计的架构。Agora展示了在现有商品服务器上通过智能资源管理和调度即可显著改善，同时也为未来的代理专用服务器架构指明了方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI is emerging in datacenters, but its architectural implications remain unexplored. We organize agentic workflows in a taxonomy and present its first architectural characterization with a production study at Microsoft Azure and a controlled study of open-source frameworks. We show that agentic execution is fragmented and heterogeneous. Requests expand into a workflow of LLM inferences, tool invocations, and orchestration decisions that repeatedly cross the CPU-GPU boundary. Our taxonomy explains how this fragmentation turns into resource demand. As orchestration and tools run on the host, the CPU sits on the critical path. Execution structure sets the load over time, which stays low with sudden spikes. Model composition sets how evenly the workflow uses the GPUs. Diversity in tasks and tools widens this range even further. These characteristics expose architectural mismatches of conventional uniform servers. Fragmented execution strands CPU and GPU capacity despite bursty demand. Different software roles make homogeneous CPU provisioning inefficient. Finally, multiplexing many agents onto shared cores degrades microarchitectural locality. Guided by our findings, we derive implications for agentic servers and examine them through Agora, our prototype for commodity servers. Agora dynamically harvests idle CPU cores for co-located throughput work, while protecting agentic tail latency against tool spikes. It oversubscribes GPU memory by placing more agents on each GPU, prefetching the next agent's state to hide swap latency. To match the machine to the heterogeneous roles, Agora pools cores by role and applies affinity-aware scheduling to restore locality. It automatically tunes mechanisms to the workload. Agora improves utilization and server throughput while preserving agent tail latency. Our insights also identify key directions for future server architectures for agentic AI.

</details>


### [252] [CARGO-VL: Counterfactual Arbitration with Risk-Constrained Group Optimization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04509)
*De Jiang,Zhengyang Zhang,Kehong Yuan,Shaohua Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARGO-VL，一种面向多模态视觉-语言系统的群组相对（group-relative）训练框架，通过联合优化匹配的四种证据状态变体（对齐A、图像正确V、文本正确T、双错N），引入条件间正确率耦合与三种转移奖励（答案不变性、来源等变性、答转弃切换），并结合原始-对偶控制器平衡不安全性回答与过度拒答，从而提升模型在证据冲突与不足场景下的可靠仲裁能力。同时贡献了四条件冲突训练资源XMC，并在CMC-Bench和Modality-Bias上验证了其超越点式基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练目标对实例进行独立评分，无法在反事实证据变化下强制执行连贯行为。当图像与检索文本不一致或共同不足时，模型需要识别可信来源并在两者均不足时弃答，但点式训练方法难以刻画这种跨条件的结构化一致性与切换行为。

Method: CARGO-VL通过四个匹配变体（A/V/T/N）作为捆绑进行训练；目标函数耦合条件正确性（condition-wise correctness），并设计三类转移奖励：答案不变性（answer invariance）、来源等变性（source equivariance）和答案到弃答的切换（answer-to-abstention switching）；同时采用原始-对偶控制器在"不安全回答"与"过度弃答"之间做自适应权衡；并构建XMC四条件冲突训练数据集。

Result: 在多个随机种子下，CARGO-VL在冲突处理（conflict handling）、不支持答案回避（unsupported-answer avoidance）和模态平衡（modality balance）上均优于逐点（pointwise）基线。消融实验表明，关系性转移信号与自适应风险控制具有互补收益。

Conclusion: 反事实一致性（counterfactual consistency）可以作为一种实用的训练目标用于可靠的多模态证据仲裁，CARGO-VL验证了将实例间结构化关系纳入优化过程的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language systems combine images with retrieved text, but these sources can disagree or jointly fail to support an answer. Reliable models must identify the trustworthy source and abstain when neither is adequate. Existing post-training objectives score instances independently and therefore do not enforce coherent behavior under counterfactual evidence changes. We introduce CARGO-VL, a group-relative framework that optimizes matched variants covering aligned, image-correct, text-correct, and both-wrong (A/V/T/N) evidence states as one bundle. Its objective couples condition-wise correctness with transition rewards for answer invariance, source equivariance, and answer-to-abstention switching, while a primal-dual controller balances unsafe answers against excessive deferral. We also contribute XMC (eXtended Modal Conflict), a four-condition conflict training resource, and evaluate transfer on CMC-Bench and Modality-Bias. Across multiple seeds, CARGO-VL improves conflict handling, unsupported-answer avoidance, and modality balance over pointwise baselines. Ablations identify complementary benefits from relational transition signals and adaptive risk control, supporting counterfactual consistency as a practical objective for reliable multimodal evidence arbitration.

</details>


### [253] [Leak-Resistant Unlearning: A New Benchmark for Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Consistency and Recovery Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04519)
*Haoting Qian,Qingjie Zhang,Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Unlearning基准，用于评估大语言模型在不同多跳推理路径和恢复攻击下的知识删除鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘基准主要包含单跳问题和少量多跳问题，面临两大挑战：(1) 知识并非孤立存在，多样的多跳推理路径可能比普通查询更容易引发知识泄漏；(2) 遗忘可能是脆弱的，被遗忘的知识可以通过轻量级后遗忘调整等恢复攻击被部分恢复，使静态评估不够充分。

Method: 提出Unlearning基准，在3个模型、6种遗忘方法和2个精心策划的数据集上进行实验，涵盖多样化的多跳推理路径和恢复攻击场景。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有遗忘方法在多跳推理路径和恢复攻击面前表现脆弱，容易受到知识泄漏和知识恢复的影响。同时揭示了遗忘质量、鲁棒性与模型实用性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 现有LLM遗忘方法缺乏跨推理路径的鲁棒性和对抗恢复攻击的韧性，需要更全面的基准来评估动态场景下的知识删除效果。

Abstract: Benchmarking machine unlearning methods is critical to understand whether sensitive knowledge is removed from large language models (LLMs) or not. Current unlearning benchmarks include mainly single-hop questions and a narrow set of multi-hop questions. Although effective, they still face two challenges. (1) Knowledge is not isolated, whereby diverse multi-hop reasoning paths can potentially induce knowledge leakage than normal queries. (2) Unlearning may be fragile: unlearned knowledge can be partially recovered through recovery attacks such as lightweight post-unlearning adaptation, making static evaluation insufficient. Therefore, in this paper, we introduce \unlearning as a novel benchmark to understand robust LLM knowledge removal across diverse reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We experiment with this benchmark on 3 models, 6 unlearning methods, and 2 carefully curated datasets. Results show that existing methods are vulnerable to multi-hop reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We further explore the trade-off among forget quality, robustness, and model utility for LLM unlearning.

</details>


### [254] [What Is a Skill Worth? Structure-Aware Shapley Valuation of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04562)
*Tao Li,Junfeng Liu,Qinghua Zhao,Yifan Li,Lei Wang,Bo Shao,Xuejun Liu,Linjun Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSV，一个结构感知的Shapley风格框架，用于评估智能体技能内部单元（规则、示例、脚本、启发式）的价值，通过编译技能为单元、依赖和层级结构，仅评估有效的反事实技能，并使用配对删除和长度中性填充分离内容价值与上下文成本，在四个智能体基准上验证了忠实性、可操作性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能日益通过自动化反馈循环优化，产生包含大量内部单元的长结构化产物，但这些内部单元的实际价值尚不明确。现有的数据和提示跨度评估方法不适用于结构化技能单元，因为技能单元具有相互依赖、文档层级、触发智能体行为和消耗有限提示上下文等特点。

Method: SkillSV将技能编译为单元、依赖和层级结构，确保仅评估有效的反事实技能；采用配对删除与长度中性填充来分离内容价值与上下文成本；通过带有 rollout 预算的估计器处理噪声智能体评估。

Result: 在四个智能体基准上，SkillSV能够恢复单元间的交互作用、保持聚合技能提升，并指导安全的剪枝与压缩。

Conclusion: SkillSV为结构化技能单元的价值评估提供了一种结构感知的方法，弥补了现有方法在技能内部信用分配上的不足，并能为技能优化提供可操作反馈。

Abstract: Agent skills are increasingly optimized by automated feedback loops, producing long structured artifacts whose internal value remains unclear. We study skill valuation: assigning credit to the internal units of a fixed skill, such as rules, examples, scripts, and heuristics, under a fixed agent and held-out task distribution. Skill valuation differs from data or prompt-span valuation because skill units are structured: they may depend on other units, belong to a document hierarchy, trigger agent behavior, and consume limited prompt context. We introduce SkillSV, a structure-aware Shapley-style framework for skill valuation. SkillSV compiles a skill into units, dependencies, and hierarchy, so that only valid counterfactual skills are evaluated. It uses paired deletion and length-neutral padding to separate content value from context cost, and estimates the resulting values with a rollout-budgeted estimator for noisy agent evaluations. On four agentic benchmarks, we assess the faithfulness, actionability, and explanation of SkillSV: it recovers unit interactions, preserves aggregate skill lift, and guides safe pruning and compression.

</details>


### [255] [Joint UAV Flight and Opportunistic Routing under Reinforcement Learning for Delay-Tolerant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04590)
*Xiao Wang,Shun-Ren Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对延迟容忍网络(DTN)中稀疏连接导致的投递率低下与拥塞问题，提出了JUROR框架——基于PPO的联合UAV飞行与机会路由协同优化方法，采用集中式训练与分布式执行(CTDE)架构，在四种流量模式下均优于PRoPHET和MaxProp基准算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTN中稀疏连接环境下的路由面临间歇性接触、有限缓冲区和消息TTL限制，导致投递稀疏和拥塞，端到端性能严重退化。现有方案未充分考虑通过主动控制UAV飞行来扩大未来接触机会，同时也未解决接触受限观测下的逐节点复制问题。

Method: 将问题建模为因子化的部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(Dec-POMDP)，考虑顺序运动-路由耦合和每步团队奖励；采用CTDE架构，分散智能体基于局部观测执行动作，训练时critic利用全局统计；可选的多时域热点预测器提供辅助监督；基于PPO框架联合优化UAV离散航向与机会路由决策。

Result: 在四种流量模式下的仿真实验中，JUROR相比PRoPHET和MaxProp基准取得了有效性能提升，同时保持了接触受限的分散式执行能力。

Conclusion: JUROR通过联合优化UAV飞行控制和机会路由，在保持分散式执行的前提下显著提升了DTN在稀疏连接环境下的投递性能，验证了CTDE架构在该问题上的有效性。

Abstract: The growing deployment of delay-tolerant networks (DTNs) has made store-carry-forward (SCF) communication indispensable under sparse connectivity.
  However, intermittent contacts, finite buffers, and limited message time-to-live (TTL) often give rise to sparse delivery and congestion, leading to substantial end-to-end performance degradation.
  To address this challenge, this study explores the joint optimization of decentralized opportunistic routing and controllable unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) flight, aiming to enlarge future contacts through discrete UAV headings while enabling per-node replication under contact-limited observations.
  Building upon this architecture, we study cooperative factored routing--UAV control under centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) and propose JUROR (Joint UAV flight and Opportunistic Routing, based on the proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework.
  In our design, we first cast the problem as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with sequential motion--routing coupling and a per-step team reward; subsequently, decentralized actors act on local observations while a training-time critic uses global statistics, and an optional multi-horizon hotspot predictor provides auxiliary supervision.
  Simulation results over four traffic modes demonstrate effective gains over PRoPHET and MaxProp, while retaining contact-limited decentralized execution.

</details>


### [256] [Agreement Before Diversity: Verification-First Complementarity for Heterogeneous Language-Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04618)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD)方法，将异构语言模型集成中"何时用新答案替换已有答案"的决策从候选多样性中解耦，转化为显式、可审计的冻结决策规则：锚定答案仅在两个额外可信样本在固定等价关系下佐证时保留，否则由异构综合答案替换。文章证明了两个精确恒等式，量化了与无条件综合和永不综合的性能差距，并在LiveCodeBench-v6和GPQA-Diamond上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构语言模型集成扩展了候选答案空间，但缺乏一个原则性标准来判断新生成的答案何时应替换已被支持的答案。现有方法将候选的"头room"(headroom)与替换的"权限"混为一谈，缺少可审计的决策标准。

Method: ABD方法是一个冻结(label-free)的决策规则：锚定答案在满足两个额外可信样本在固定等价关系下的佐证时被保留，否则由异构综合答案替换。核心将"候选多样性"与"替换权威性"解耦，使后者成为显式可审计的对象。提供两个精确恒等式（accuracy gap恒等式）以及预期推理成本公式（约8-5×覆盖率次调用）。

Result: 在盲评估精确ID条件下，ABD在LiveCodeBench-v6完整集上达到59.43%（对比Single9的52.57%和HAC的52.00%，n=175）；在未触碰的GPQA-Diamond分集上达到75.00%（两个对照均为72.78%，n=180）。保护层分析显示：LiveCodeBench上3个保护案例无冲突项，覆盖率将门控贡献上界限定为先验1.71个点；GPQA-Diamond上132个案例中13对8个冲突案例；冻结锚定扰动下71个案例中12对0个冲突案例。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：多样性只提供潜力（potential），验证结构才提供权威（authority）。通过将替换决策形式化为可证明的恒等式，每个聚合层面的性能差异都能被定位到可枚举的保护层（protected stratum），实现了模型集成决策的可审计性与可解释性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous language-model ensembles expand the space of candidate responses, yet they lack a principled criterion for when a newly generated answer should supersede an already supported one. We decouple candidate headroom from replacement authority, rendering the latter as an explicit, auditable object. Our proposed method, Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD), is a frozen, label-free decision rule: an anchor answer is retained if two additional trusted samples corroborate it under a fixed equivalence relation; otherwise, it is replaced by a heterogeneous synthesis. For this gating mechanism, we prove two exact identities. The first shows that the accuracy gap relative to unconditional synthesis is determined jointly by the agreement coverage and the anchor's advantage on the protected subset. The second shows that the gap relative to never synthesizing reflects a contrast between authorized recovery and authorized destruction. Neither identity assumes independence or calibrated confidence, and the expected inference cost is approximately eight minus five times the coverage in number of calls. Under blind, exact-ID evaluation, ABD achieves 59.43% on the complete LiveCodeBench-v6 (vs. 52.57% for Single9 and 52.00% for HAC; n = 175) and 75.00% on an untouched GPQA-Diamond split (both controls at 72.78%; n = 180). Furthermore, these identities localize every aggregate difference to an enumerable protected stratum: no discordant items occur among the 3 protected cases on LiveCodeBench, where coverage bounds the gate's contribution to 1.71 points a priori; 13 versus 8 discordant cases among 132 on GPQA-Diamond; and 12 versus 0 among 71 under a frozen anchor perturbation. Diversity supplies potential; verification structure supplies authority.

</details>


### [257] [A/B Agent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Strategy Iteration in Industrial A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04625)
*Zhuohang Jiang,Yuxin Chen,Yongsen Pan,Zheng Hu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li,Hongyang Wang,Jun Wang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出A/B Agent——一个用于工业推荐策略优化的闭环A/B智能体框架，通过层次化经验树组织历史策略、多路径Tree-RAG检索可迁移证据生成可执行策略，并利用在线A/B反馈驱动策略自动调优与经验树自我进化，在真实短视频电商推荐系统中实现了4.829%的GMV提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐策略迭代高度依赖大规模A/B实验，传统人工调优过程劳动密集且耗时；历史实验知识碎片化，难以通过人工实现系统化复用；现有RAG智能体虽能检索历史策略，但以扁平方式组织经验，忽略了业务场景、推荐阶段、优化目标和实验上下文之间的层次关系，导致检索不匹配、跨场景迁移受限，且无法通过连续的A/B反馈持续优化策略与参数。

Method: 提出A/B Agent闭环框架，包含三个紧密耦合的核心组件：1) 历史策略知识组织——将历史策略构建为层次化经验树；2) 自主目标感知策略生成——通过多路径Tree-RAG检索可迁移证据生成可执行策略；3) 实验引导的策略自我进化——持续分析在线A/B反馈以指导自主调参并更新经验树实现自我进化。

Result: 离线与在线评估均验证了有效性：在真实短视频电商推荐系统中实现GMV提升4.829%，且所有护栏指标均保持正向增益。

Conclusion: A/B Agent通过层次化经验树组织和多路径Tree-RAG检索，解决了现有RAG智能体在工业推荐策略优化中经验组织扁平化、检索不匹配和跨场景迁移受限的问题，并实现了基于A/B反馈的闭环自我进化，在真实工业场景中取得了显著的业务指标提升。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation strategy iteration heavily relies on large-scale A/B experimentation. Traditional tuning requires experts to repeatedly design strategies, configure experiments, analyze results, and adjust parameters, making the process labor-intensive and time-consuming. Meanwhile, valuable knowledge from historical experiments is often fragmented, making systematic reuse difficult through manual expert effort alone. Existing RAG agents partially alleviate this burden by retrieving prior strategies, but typically organize experience in a flat manner, overlooking the hierarchical relationships among business scenarios, recommendation stages, optimization objectives, and experimental contexts. This often results in mismatched retrieval and limited cross-scenario transfer, while preventing agents from continuously refining strategies and parameters through sequential A/B feedback. %
To address these limitations, we propose A/B Agent, a closed-loop A/B agent for industrial recommendation strategy optimization. The framework comprises three tightly coupled core components: Historical Strategy Knowledge Organization, Autonomous Target-Aware Strategy Generation, and Experiment-Guided Strategy Self-Evolution. It organizes historical strategies into a hierarchical experience tree, retrieves transferable evidence through multi-path Tree-RAG to generate executable strategies, and continuously analyzes online A/B feedback to guide autonomous tuning and update the experience tree for self-evolution. Extensive offline and online evaluations demonstrate its effectiveness, including a 4.829% improvement in GMV in a real-world short-video e-commerce recommendation system while maintaining positive gains across all guardrail metrics.

</details>


### [258] [AI Literacy for Legal Translation: Developing Digital Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04641)
*Łucja Biel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了生成式AI对专业法律翻译的影响，提出一个包含基础性、程序性、批判性和战略性四个相互强化维度的AI素养框架，并将其视为法律翻译能力中的横向组成部分，用以培养译者的数字韧性，同时讨论了该框架在法律翻译教育中的教学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在变革法律翻译领域，带来了语言、技术、法律、伦理和认知等多方面的机遇与风险，但专业法律翻译领域缺乏系统性的AI素养培养框架，需要为从业者提供应对AI挑战的能力建构方案。

Method: 通过理论分析提出AI素养框架，该框架包含四个相互强化的维度（基础性、程序性、批判性、战略性），将AI素养概念化为法律翻译能力中的横向组成部分以促进数字韧性，并据此设计课堂教学活动。

Result: 提出了一套适用于法律翻译职业的AI素养框架，论证了AI并未改变法律翻译的根本目标但要求扩展专业能力，该框架可作为培养未来法律译者的教学依据，帮助其以批判、负责任且符合专业标准的方式整合AI。

Conclusion: AI不改变法律翻译的核心目标，但要求从业者通过AI素养扩展专业能力；所提出的四维框架及配套教学活动为法律翻译教育提供了可行路径，使未来译者在AI辅助下保持专业标准并具备数字韧性。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming legal translation by introducing opportunities alongside linguistic, technical, legal, ethical and cognitive risks. This chapter examines the implications of AI for professional legal translation and proposes an AI literacy framework tailored to the profession. It argues that AI does not change the fundamental objectives of legal translation but requires an extension of professional competence through AI literacy. The proposed framework comprises four mutually reinforcing dimensions, foundational, procedural, critical and strategic, and conceptualises AI literacy as a transversal component of legal translation competence that fosters digital resilience. It further discusses the pedagogical implications of this framework by proposing classroom activities designed to develop AI literacy in legal translator education, enabling future translators to integrate AI critically, responsibly and in accordance with professional standards.

</details>


### [259] [Calibrating Artificial Guilt: Neurally Grounded Reward Shaping for Prosocial Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04663)
*Aaditya Mehta,Arya Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次尝试从人类神经与行为数据中校准"内疚"信号权重，并将其迁移至多智能体强化学习中的亲社会奖励塑造。利用SoDec fMRI数据集（40名被试）拟合快乐变化的回归模型，恢复出内疚权重（权值1.118），将该权重嵌入双智能体"社会彩票"环境中的PPO算法，对比四种塑造方案后，神经校准方案与人类"安全选择率"最接近（KL散度仅0.0012），表明人类神经行为先验可作为亲社会奖励塑造的定量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习中常向个体奖励添加社会性项以促进合作，但这些项的规模通常由人工凭经验设定，缺乏客观依据。作者质疑是否存在一种可以从人类神经与行为数据中校准内疚信号并将其迁移到人工智能体的途径，以替代手工调参。

Method: 使用SoDec责任fMRI数据集（40名被试），拟合被试固定效应回归模型，以瞬间快乐变化为因变量、结果类型计数为自变量，通过"伙伴负面减去社会负面"的对比恢复内疚权重（w=1.118，Cohen's d=0.214）。将该权重嵌入双智能体"社会彩票"环境，训练独立PPO（近端策略优化）actor-critic，比较四种奖励塑造方案：神经校准、均匀常数、零（自私）、单位系数oracle，各评估1000个回合。

Result: 神经校准的智能体在安全选择率（0.459）上最接近人类水平（0.484），KL散度仅为0.0012；其他三种方案与人类的偏差在KL散度上高出一到三个数量级。

Conclusion: 人类神经行为数据提取的先验能够作为定量约束，用于多智能体强化学习中亲社会奖励塑造的权重校准，提供了一种替代人工调参的数据驱动方法。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning often adds social terms to individual rewards, yet the scale of those terms is usually chosen by hand. We ask whether a guilt signal can instead be calibrated from human neural and behavioural data and transferred to artificial agents. Using the public SoDec responsibility fMRI dataset (40 participants), we fit a subject-fixed-effects regression of momentary-happiness changes on outcome-type counts and recover a guilt weight as the Partner-negative minus Social-negative contrast ($\hat{w}=1.118$, Cohen's $d=0.214$). We embed this weight in a two-agent Social Lottery environment and train independent Proximal Policy Optimization actor-critics under four shaping regimes: neurally calibrated, uniform constant, zero (selfish), and a unit-coefficient oracle. Across 1{,}000 evaluation episodes per condition, the calibrated agents track the human Social safe-choice rate most closely ($0.459$ vs.\ human $0.484$; $\mathrm{KL}=0.0012$), while the other three conditions deviate by one to three orders of magnitude in KL. Human neurobehavioural priors can therefore act as quantitative constraints on prosocial reward shaping.

</details>


### [260] [Traceable LLM-Generated Hazard Scenarios for Operational Safety Analysis of Aviation Systems Using ASRS Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04697)
*Cristian Mascia,Roberto Pietrantuono,Daniel Rodriguez,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI辅助方法，从NASA航空安全报告系统（ASRS）中生成候选航空运行危险情景。该方法针对目标不利结果，生成由分类因素和叙述性场景组成的结构化假设，并提供基于历史共现证据的可信度评分及与最相似ASRS报告的可追溯性。论文还提出一种混合变体，通过进化溯因生成结构化假设来调节叙述生成，提高正确性并降低变异性，并在多个大语言模型、不同提示策略和微调设置下进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空系统运行的危险分析必须考虑天气、ATC行动、空域约束、飞机运行和人为因素之间的交互，这与飞机系统层面的功能危险评估有所不同。传统方法在处理这些多因素交互时面临挑战，需要一种能够从历史数据中自动生成候选危险情景的方法。

Method: 文章提出一个两阶段方法：首先针对目标不利结果生成结构化假设（分类因素）和叙述性事件场景；随后提出混合变体，采用进化溯因方式生成结构化假设，在此基础上条件化叙述生成。评估涵盖多个大语言模型、零样本与少样本提示、以及可选微调，衡量提示和模型选择对生成结构和叙述的有效性与真实性的影响。

Result: 混合变体（条件化叙述生成于进化溯因产生的结构化假设之上）相比基线方法提高了生成正确性并降低了变异性。研究评估了不同模型、提示策略与微调方案对生成结果有效性和真实性的影响。

Conclusion: AI辅助方法能够从ASRS数据中生成可信且可追溯的航空运行危险情景假设，其中混合进化溯因方法在正确性和稳定性上优于直接生成方法，为航空系统运行层面的危险分析提供了自动化支持。

Abstract: Operational hazard analysis of aviation system operations must consider interactions among weather, ATC actions, airspace constraints, aircraft operations, and human factors - distinct from the functional hazard assessment applied at the aircraft-system level. We present an AI-assisted approach that generates candidate hazard scenarios from NASA's Aviation Safety Reporting System (ASRS). Given a target adverse outcome, it produces a structured hypothesis as categorical factors and a narrative scenario describing an operational event sequence consistent with the structure. Each scenario includes by a plausibility score from historical co-occurrence evidence and traceability to the most similar held-out ASRS reports. We then propose a hybrid variant, conditioning narrative generation on a structured hypothesis produced via evolutionary abduction, improving correctness and reducing variability. We evaluate multiple large language models, zero-shot versus few-shot prompting, and optional fine-tuning, measuring how prompting and model choice affect the validity and realism of the generated structures and narratives.

</details>


### [261] [Diagnosing Tool-Selection Reasoning in LLM Agents with Canary Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04719)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"金丝雀工具"(canary tools)诊断框架，通过在代理的MCP工具集中植入六类探针工具来剖析模型工具选择失败的具体原因。在8个模型、120个任务、8640次运行上的评估显示：模型能力越强越不易受干扰（CSR跨度约36倍）；能力层级不能预测安全性；六类故障类型具有能力分层效应（能力幻象最能困住前沿模型，其他类型主要对小模型生效）；软化提示词不影响前沿模型表现，证明探针衡量的是推理而非措辞匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理评估只能告诉我们模型选错了工具，却无法解释为什么会选错。作者希望超越简单的对/错二元结果，开发一种能系统性地剖析模型工具选择推理过程中具体弱点的方法。

Method: 提出"金丝雀工具"诊断框架：在代理的MCP工具集中植入探针工具，每种工具针对一种特定的工具选择弱点。采用六类故障类型学（语义诱饵、参数陷阱、能力幻象、先决条件盲区、时间诱饵、粒度陷阱），将单一的错误结果转化为多维推理画像。评估8个模型（6个托管+2个8B开源），120项任务、三种金丝雀密度条件、三个种子共8640次运行，另有2880次消融实验。任务评分由独立的提供商无关评判者完成，并有第二位独立评判者佐证（Cohen's kappa=0.75）。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 模型能力越强，金丝雀易感性越低，每任务CSR跨模型跨度约36倍（Claude Opus 4.8最低，Llama 3.1 8B最高）；(2) 能力层级不能预测安全性——最易受影响的托管模型是中等层级，同一提供商内更便宜的模型可能更安全；(3) 类型学具有能力分层效应——能力幻象最能困住前沿模型，其余类型对强模型基本无效但对小开源模型有效。软化提示词不影响前沿模型CSR，证明衡量的是推理能力；易感性可预测任务失败（Spearman rho=-0.34），最稳健的模型不受金丝雀压力显著影响。

Conclusion: 金丝雀工具提供了一种可复现、可扩展的诊断框架，能将"选错工具"这一粗粒度结果细化为模型工具推理弱点的多维画像。研究揭示了能力与工具选择安全性的非平凡关系，并公开了框架、模式、任务和日志以供社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Agent evaluations tell us that a model picked the wrong tool, but rarely why. We introduce canary tools: diagnostic probe tools planted in an agent's Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool set, each engineered to probe one specific tool-selection weakness. A six-type taxonomy (semantic decoys, parameter traps, capability mirages, prerequisite blindness, temporal decoys, and granularity traps) turns a single "wrong tool" outcome into a multi-dimensional profile of how a model reasons about tools. We evaluate eight models -- six hosted and two 8B open-weight -- spanning three capability tiers, on 120 tasks across three canary-density conditions and three seeds (8,640 runs), plus a 2,880-run subtlety ablation. Task success is graded by a provider-independent judge, corroborated by a second independent judge (Cohen's kappa = 0.75). We report three findings. First, susceptibility drops sharply as models get more capable: the per-task canary susceptibility rate (CSR) ranges about 36x across models, lowest for Claude Opus 4.8 and highest for Llama 3.1 8B. Second, capability tier alone does not predict safety: the most susceptible hosted model is mid-tier, and within a provider the cheaper model can be the safer one. Third, the taxonomy is capability-stratified: capability mirages most reliably trap frontier models, while the other types are largely inert on strong models but fire on small open models, so they discriminate by capability rather than being weak. Softening each canary's give-away phrase leaves frontier CSR essentially unchanged, evidence that the probes measure reasoning, not phrase-spotting. Susceptibility also predicts task failure (Spearman rho = -0.34), while the most robust models are not significantly degraded by canary pressure. We release the framework, canary schemas, tasks, and logs.

</details>


### [262] [When Prompts Become Pixels: Prompt-Region Grounding for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04726)
*Yongxin Wang,Ruizhe Zhou,Yueling Tang,Yingying Zhu,Xuemin Zhao,Xiaojun Chang,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可视化任务语义（VTS）干预方法，将问题从文本移到图像中，发现六个MLLM在四个基准上性能平均下降17.8分，暴露了模型在"读取"与"使用"视觉任务指令之间的语义通道鸿沟。作者提出prompt-region grounding方法，通过将问题区域与类型化语义对齐并从掩码视图中恢复其干净表示，在相同训练成本下将VTS准确率从58.0提升到66.3，且推理时无需OCR或区域元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型基准通常将问题以文本形式呈现，而实际应用中任务指令可能以像素形式存在于图像中（如截图、文档）。现有基准无法评估模型是否能在不同通道（文本vs图像）中同等有效地使用指令，因此需要一种受控干预方法来揭示这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出Visualized Task Semantics (VTS)干预：将问题嵌入图像中，同时保持原始问题和答案不变；2) 提出prompt-region grounding方法：核心设计是将问题区域与类型化语义对齐，并从掩码视图中恢复其干净表示，训练时建立区域与语义的映射关系。

Result: 在六个MLLM和四个基准上的24个模型-任务组合中，准确率全部下降，平均下降17.8分。模型往往能正确转录视觉问题但未能使用它，表明鸿沟不仅仅是OCR层面。所提出的方法在匹配训练成本下将VTS准确率从58.0提升到66.3，同时保持原始接口上的准确率不变，且推理时无需OCR或区域元数据。

Conclusion: 读取任务承载文本并将其作为推理指令进行grounding是两个不同的能力。当前的MLLM存在语义通道鸿沟，仅靠OCR能力的进步无法弥合，需要专门的训练方法来使模型能够真正理解并利用图像中的指令语义。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models increasingly reason over screenshots and documents where the task itself may be written in pixels. Yet benchmarks usually place questions in text, leaving it unclear whether models use the same instruction equally well across channels. We introduce Visualized Task Semantics (VTS), a controlled intervention that moves the question into the image while keeping the source problem and answer fixed. Across six MLLMs and four benchmarks, accuracy drops in all 24 model-task pairs, by 17.8 points on average. Models often transcribe the visual question correctly yet fail to use it, exposing a semantic channel gap beyond OCR. To reduce this gap, we present prompt-region grounding, whose core design aligns the question region with typed semantics and recovers its clean representation from a masked view. At matched training cost, our method raises four-benchmark VTS accuracy from 58.0 to 66.3 while preserving accuracy on the original interface, and requires no OCR or region metadata at inference. Reading task-bearing text and grounding it as an instruction for reasoning are distinct capabilities.

</details>


### [263] [Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Can Be Unreliable in Implicit-Influence Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04735)
*Agatha Duzan,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出直接对比隐性与显性影响场景下CoT可监控性的基准。结果显示，显性影响下监控器能检测60-94%的行为偏移，但隐性影响下检测率大幅下降（在两种设置中下降41-46个百分点），且真实系统提示可进一步将检测率降至5%左右，说明显性设置下获得的监控性估计可能高估实际监控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT监控评估主要集中于显性影响场景（即提示直接激励模型隐藏行为），但忽略了隐性影响场景（即提示无隐藏指令，但任务特征仍会影响模型行为）。为更全面评估前沿推理模型的可监控性，需要直接比较两种场景下的监控效果差异。

Method: 构建涵盖四种任务格式（二选一、数值评分、多项选择问答、开放编码）和七个前沿扩展思维模型的基准。通过"随意提及"（隐性）或"直接指示行动并隐藏"（显性）两种方式传递引导（nudge）来影响模型选择，并对比CoT监控器在两种场景下的行为偏移检测能力，同时考察现实系统提示添加对检测的影响。
